(pariśiṣṭa_1. nakṣatrakalpaḥ)
(0,0.0) śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ || oṃ namo'tharvātmane vāmadevāya śivāya || śrīsarasvatyai namaḥ ||
(1,1.1) atha nakṣatrakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(1,1.2) kṛttikā rohiṇī mṛgaśira ārdrā punarvasū puṣyāśleṣā maghā(ḥ) phālgunī phalgunyau hasta(ś) citrā svāti(r) viśākhe anurādhā jyeṣṭhā mūla(m) pūrvāṣāḍhā uttarāṣāḍābhijicchravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhā śatabhiṣaḥ pūrvaproṣṭhapadottaraproṣṭhapadau revatyaśvayujau bharaṇyaḥ ||
(1,2.1) ṣaṭkṛttikā ekā rohiṇī tisro mṛgaśira ekārdrā dve punarvasū ekaḥ puṣyaḥ ṣaḍāśleṣāḥ ṣaṇmaghāḥ catasraḥ phalgunyaḥ pañca hasta ekā citrā ekā svātirdve viśākhe catasro'nurādhā ekā jyeṣṭhā sapta mūlamaṣṭāvaṣāḍhā eko'bhijittisraḥ śravaṇaḥ pañca śraviṣṭhā ekā śatabhiṣā catasraḥ proṣṭhapadau ekā revatī dve aśvayujau tisro bharaṇyaḥ ||
(1,2.2) iti saṃkhyāparimitaṃ brahma ||
(1,3.1) āgniveśyaḥ kṛttikā rohiṇyānurohiṇī śvetāyinaṃ mṛgaśira ārdrā bārhadgavī vātsyāyanau punarvasū bhāradvājaḥ puṣyo jātūkarṇyo'śleṣā vaiyāghrapadyo maghā(ḥ) pārāśaryau pūrve phālguṇyāvaupaśivyā uttare māṇḍavyāyano hastaścitrā gautamī kauṇḍinyāyanaḥ svātiḥ kāpile viśākhe maitreyyanurādhā kauśikī jyeṣṭhā kautsaṃ mūlaṃ hāritayajñī pūrvāṣāḍhā kāśyapyuttarā śaunako'bhijidātreyaḥ śravaṇo gārgyaḥ śraviṣṭhā dākṣāyaṇī śatabhiṣagvātsyāyanyau pūrve proṣṭhapade āgastyāvuttare śāṅkhāyanī revatī kātyāyan(y)āvaśvayujau mātṛbhyo bharaṇyaḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ kaśyapa ādityaścandramā brahmāṇo nakṣatreṣu ||
(1,4.1) kṛttikā agnidevatyā rohiṇyāṃ tu prajāpatiḥ | saumyaṃ mṛgaśiraṃ vidyānmarutaścātra daivatam ||
(1,4.2) rudrasyārdrāditeḥ punarvasū puṣye vidyādbṛhaspatim | aśleṣā[[ḥ]] sarpadaivatyā maghāsu pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ ||
(1,4.3) bhagas[tu] pūrvayoḥ phalgunyoraryamottarayorapi | haste ca savitā daivaṃ citrā tu tvaṣṭṛdaivatā ||
(1,4.4) svātau tu daivataṃ vāyurindrāgnī tu viśākhayoḥ | anurādhāsu mitro vai jyeṣṭhāyāmindramahādevau ||
(1,4.5) ahirbudhnyaśca mūlasya nirṛtiścātra daivatam | āpaḥ pūrvāsvaṣāḍhāsu viśve devāstathottare ||
(1,4.6) abhijidbrahmadevatyaḥ śravaṇe viṣṇurucyate | śraviṣṭhā vasudevatyā śatabhiṣagvaruṇendrayoḥ ||
(1,4.7) aja ekapāddiśaśca ādityaśca tathottare | revatī pūṣadaivatyā aśvinyāmaśvinau smṛtau ||
(1,4.8) bharaṇyo yamadevatyā devatāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ ||
(1,5.1) kṛttikā maghā mūlaṃ [[ca tathā]] pūrvāṇi dvandvinām | etāni purastādbhāgānyahaḥpūrvāṇi jānīyāt ||
(1,5.2) ārdrāṃ śatabhiṣajaṃ svātimāśleṣā bharaṇīrapi | naktaṃbhāgāni bruvate jyeṣṭhayā saha brahmāṇam ||
(1,5.3) punarvasū viśākhe ca uttarāṇi ca dvandvinām | rātrīmubhayataḥ pakṣau bhajante yā ca rohiṇī ||
(1,5.4) mṛgaśiraḥ puṣyo hastaścitrā tu sahānurādhaiḥ | śravaṇaśca śraviṣṭhāśca revatyaśvayujau nava ||
(1,5.5) etānyupariṣṭādbhāgāni rātrīpūrvāṇi jānīyāt | muhūrto'bhijiducyate ||
(1,5.6) purastādbhāgānyanāgatenopariṣṭādbhāgānyatikrāntena ubhayatobhāgāni vartamāne[[na]] naktaṃbhāgāni samaṃ candreṇa vā ||
(1,5.7) [[ṣaḍa]]nāgatayogīni sthitayogīni dvādaśa | navātikrāntayogīni tathā yogaḥ pradṛśyate ||
(1,6.1) bahūni jātāni graho hinasti kṛttikāsu tiṣṭhannuta dīrghamāyuḥ | ajāvayo mūṣikāśca vyathante viśo brahmaṇaḥ saha mlecchavāṃśca ||
(1,6.2) kaliṅgānāṃ vyathate nanu rājā hiraṇyakārāṃśca nihanti kṛtsnam | ayaskārā lohakārā āhitāgniśca kṛtsnaṃ niyanti sahāgnitaptaiḥ ||
(1,6.3) ayo lohaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ hiraṇyamiśraṃ [yac] ca patanti sāram | kāśāḥ kuśā yacca suvarṇavarṇaṃ yaccāgnivarṇaṃ phalamūlapuṣpam ||
(1,6.4) [ye tatra jātāḥ] sarāṃsi śuṣyantyapayanti nadyaḥ prajā vyathante paśavo mṛgāśca || prajāpaterhṛdaye pīḍyamāne sarvaṃ jagadvyathate sapradeśam ||
(1,6.5) mahābhaumo rājā mariṣyatīti vidyādekāriṇāṃ chavakāminvakāsu | nakṣatrabhāge niyanti yojadhānāḥ tathā hi nūnaṃ triṇavena sṛṣṭam ||
(1,6.6) tathā sa ninye nidhānadarśanāya tatra teṣāṃ sahākṣemaṃ tasya vidyāt | paritya ye pūrvapadān balena uttiṣṭhanti vīryavanto mṛgānām ||
(1,6.7) bhagena devyupayanti divyamārdrābhāge sahino bhavanti | tatra daivānmānuṣyāṃśca punarvasvornakṣatra bhāge niniyoja dhānāḥ ||
(1,6.8) tathā hi nūnaṃ puṣyo bhāga ekadhā brāhmaṇānāṃ tathā vidurnakṣatraveditāraḥ tathā hi nūnam ||
(1,6.9) śatadrayaḥ kaiśikā dakṣiṇārdhā āndhrāśca yogahāsayā pravṛddhāḥ || aśleṣābhāge sahino bhavanti tatra ||
(1,6.10) veṇūn pitṝṃstrirujāhurdevatām | maghābhāge aṣṭame devasṛṣṭam | saṃyujyante devaprasādanena tatra ||
(1,7.1) rājñāṃ rākāyāmatha madrakaikayā manomāpāyanasahaparisunniyojasahāntau | tunyamatha saptamātraṃ pūrvabhāge navake phalgunīṣu ||
(1,7.2) matsyā māgadhāścedayaśca śālvā matsyā ubhe phalgunīṣu | saṃyujyante devaprasādanena tatra ||
(1,7.3) pūrvācāryā icchamānāśca sarve yakṛtkloma saha bhāgena haste | saṃyujyante devaprasādanena tatra ||
(1,7.4) ye pūrvārdhe nijīhate carantaḥ śāntā mṛgā [jantu]paśavo apagāminaste | paśavo bhavatāśca sarve citrāyā bhāge sahino bhavanti || tatrā
(1,7.5) cyutakeśaṃ vāhanaṃ jayārthaṃ kumāryo anaḍvān sahate atra ye | vijñāyate devasṛṣṭaṃ purāṇaṃ svātiṃ bhajante tṛṇavaśca sarve ||
(1,7.6) vṛkṣā vṛkṣamūlamikṣvākavaśca viśākhāyāṃ yojitāḥ sāṃpadena | tasmin gṛhīte bhayameva teṣāṃ daivopasṛṣṭe tu balena kāryam || ye tatra jātāḥ ||
(1,7.7) ye paścārdhe nijīhate caranta āsavo mṛgā uttarārdhāścāndhrāḥ | anūrādhāsu magadhavaṅgamatsyāḥ sarve samagrāḥ sahino bhavanti tatra ||
(1,7.8) pañcaikadhā janapadā bhavanti sayujaḥkāsaubaladādauṣyadhāḥ | bāhlīkā jyeṣṭhā upayanti bhaktyā tatra ||
(1,7.9) ikṣvākūṇāṃ nirmathyā mūlamāhuḥ | tathā vidurnakṣatraveditārastathā hi nūnam
(1,7.10) kāmbojāḥ kālamṛṣāśca krandā ucchuṣmāṇaḥ śvānaścāvadhūmamarkaṭāśca pūrvāṣāḍhā upayanti bhaktyā tatra ||
(1,8.1) viśve devāḥ saha pañcālajyeṣṭhā āpaśca yāḥ pāntu bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyat | uttarāṣāḍhā upayanti bhaktyā tatra ||
(1,8.2) nārkavindā nārvvidālā nasṛṅgāvau naiṣadhā jantavo mataṅgāḥ | abhijitaṃ hārthavijñāya bhejire tatra ||
(1,8.3) pāñcālāḥ śravaṇamupaiti bhaktyā sunvantaścobhe vidvān bhūtā niniyoja devaḥ || pūrvakartā bhūtabhaviṣyakālastathā ni nūnam
(1,8.4) kurūn śraviṣṭhāstathā śivāstathāhurnakṣatrabhāge niniyoja dhānāḥ || tathā hi nūnam
(1,8.5) aṅgādayo janapadā guhāśayā apsu ca ye kṣipanti śatabhiṣaji bheṣajasya bhejire tatra ||
(1,8.6) khaḍgā hastino gavayā varāhā ahīnarā kuntayaścāpi sarve | pūrvau proṣṭhapadā upayanti bhaktyā tatra ||
(1,8.7) uśīnarā uttarayoḥ proṣṭhapadayornakṣatrabhāge niniyoja dhānāḥ | tathā hi nūnam
(1,8.8) āvṛtāḥ śūdrāḥ saha kāravaśca dakṣiṇapūrve yūkabhiḥ saha revatīṃ hārthavijñāya bhejire tatra ||
(1,8.9) acyutakeśaṃ vāhanaṃ ca padārthamucāvacajanapadā mahāntaḥ | aśvayujau hārthavijñāya bhejire tatra ||
(1,8.10) ubhaye kīkaṭāḥ kauśalāśca rahaśca[vo] ye ca prasuptāścaranti bharaṇīḥ sahārthavijñāya bhejire tatra teṣāṃ sahākṣayamasya vidyāt ||
(1,9.1) kariṣyamāṇaḥ saṃgrāmaṃ pratirājena kṣatriyaḥ | brāhmaṇaṃ pūrvamanvicchedvidvāṃsaṃ śāstravittamam ||
(1,9.2) utpātān yastu yān vidyāddivyāntarikṣapārthivān | taṃ vai lipsitumarhati rājā rāṣṭre jijīviṣuḥ ||
(1,9.3) grahāṇāṃ yaḥ sthitiṃ vidyānnakṣatrāṇāṃ ca sāṃpadam | anabhyaktamupāsīta nakṣatrasamatāṃ ca yat ||
(1,9.4) āyudhīyān bibhradrājā kṛttikāsu na riṣyati | taddhi tejasvi nakṣatraṃ bahulaṃ divi rocate ||
(1,9.5) atho hi kṛttikā iti nakṣatraṃ bhānumattamam | āgneyamagninakṣatraṃ rājā hyasmin pravardhate ||
(1,9.6) rohiṇyā[ṃ] sārdhamāsīta rajjupalyāni kārayet | mṛgaśirasyaśvān bibhṛyātsāsya senā na riṣyati ||
(1,9.7) saumyaṃ somasya nakṣatraṃ rājā hyasmin pravardhate | ārdrāyāṃ mṛgayāṃ yāyādamitrebhyaśca hāvayet ||
(1,9.8) punarvasvābhiyuñjīta puṣyenaitāṃ prayojayet | iṣīkāṃ chedayan rājā aśleṣāsu na riṣyati ||
(1,9.9) maghābhiḥ sārdhamāsīta na yāyāducchrayaṃ cana | phalgu dvārāṇi kārayetparicārāṃśca vāhayet ||
(1,9.10) toraṇāni ca saṃhanyuḥ phalakāni ca takṣayet | ..... uttarābhyāṃ ca hāvayet ||
(1,10.1) hastena citrāmākāṅkṣennakṣatrasya parigraham | anekadarśī syāccitrāyāṃ purā svāterabhiplavāt ||
(1,10.2) svātau śiśūnniyojayejjavārthān rathavāhinaḥ | athāsmin [kanyām] upavāsayetkṣipraṃ sā labhate patim ||
(1,10.3) pradatīn kārayan rājā viśākhāyāṃ na riṣyati | lepayetpradatī rājā anūrādhāsu kṣatriyaḥ ||
(1,10.4) jyeṣṭhāyāṃ hastinaṃ paśyedabhiṣekāṃśca kārayet | ..... rājaputrāṃśca yodhayet ||
(1,10.5) mūlena parikhāṃ khānayetpuraṃ citena yojayet | nairṛtaṃ rājanakṣatraṃ vadhyānanena ghātayet ||
(1,10.6) trirātraṃ sārdhaṃ dīkṣayitvā āṣāḍhāsu vrataṃ caret | abhijityabhiyuñjīta śravaṇena cikīrṣatu ||
(1,10.7) śraviṣthābhiḥ sṛjedrasān ||
(1,10.8) śatabhiṣaji bhiṣakkarma bhaiṣajyaṃ cātra kārayet | prācīnaproṣṭhapadayoryāyād...
(1,10.9) uttarābhyāmabhiyuñjīta gṛheṣu revatyāṃ vaset | vi senāṃ kārayedrājā aśvinyāṃ bharaṇīṣu ca ||
(1,11.1) citrāṇi sākaṃ divi rocanāni sarīsṛpāṇi bhuvane javāni | turmiśaṃ sumatimicchamāno ahāni gīrbhiḥ saparyāmi nākam ||
(1,11.2) suhavamagne kṛttikā rohiṇī castu bhadraṃ mṛgaśiraḥ śamārdrā | punarvasū sūnṛtā cāru puṣyo bhānurāśleṣā ayanaṃ maghā me ||
(1,11.3) puṇyaṃ pūrvā phalgunyau cātra hastaścitrā śivā svāti sukho me astu | rādhe viśākhe suhavā anurādhā jyeṣṭhā sunakṣatramariṣṭamūlam ||
(1,11.4) annaṃ pūrvā rāsatāṃ me aṣāḍhā ūrjaṃ dehyuttarā ā vahantu | abhijinme rāsatāṃ puṇyameva śravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhāḥ kurvatāṃ supuṣṭim ||
(1,11.5) ā me mahacchatabhisagvarīya āme dvayā proṣṭhapadā suśarma | ā revatī cāśvayujau bhagaṃ ma ā me rayiṃ bharaṇya ā vahantu ||
(1,12.1) kṛttikā rohiṇī mṛgaśira ārdrā punarvasū puṣyāśleṣā maghāḥ pūrve phalgunyau tannavamamagnirdaśamamahorātre edādaśadvādaśe ||
(1,12.2) etānyevāsmai nakṣatrāṇi śriyaṃ bhūtiṃ puṣṭiṃ prajāṃ paśūnannamannādyaṃ samindhata iti veda
(1,12.3) atha yaṃ kāmayatyetānyevāsmai nakṣatrāṇi śriyaṃ bhūtiṃ puṣṭiṃ prajāṃ paśūnannamannādyaṃ samindhīranniti tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt
(1,12.4) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya [[parisamuhya paryukṣya]] paristīrya barhī rasān barhiṣyādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyāccitrāṇi sākaṃ divi rocanāni svāhetyagnau hutvā raseṣu saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homāṃstata enaṃ prāśayati rasān ||
(1,12.5) evaṃ cedasmai karotyetānyevāsmai nakṣatrāṇi śriyaṃ bhūtiṃ puṣṭiṃ prajāṃ paśūnannamannādyaṃ samindhate ||
(1,13.1) uttare phalgunyau hasta[[ś]] citrā svāti[[r]] viśākhe anūrādhā jyeṣṭhā mūlaṃ pūrvāṣāḍhā tannavamamādityo daśamaṃ [nāmarūpa] pūrvapakṣāparapakṣāvekādaśadvādaśe
(1,13.2) etānyevāsmai = 1,12.2.
(1,13.3) atha yam = 1,12.3.
(1,13.4) prāñcamidhmam = 1,12.4.
(1,13.5) evam = 1,12.5.
(1,14.1) uttarāṣāḍhābhijicchravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhā śatabhiṣakproṣṭhapadau revatyaśvayujau bharaṇyastaddaśamam ... paurṇamāsyamāvāsye dvādaśatrayodaśe
(1,14.2) etānyevāsmai = 1,12.2.
(1,14.3) atha yam = 1,12.3.
(1,14.4) prāñcamidhmam = 1,12.4.
(1,14.5) evam = 1,12.5.
(1,15.1) ṛgvedo yajurvedaḥ sāmavedo brahmavedaḥ śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ niruktaṃ chando jyotiṣamitihāsapurāṇaṃ vākovākya[[m]] idāvatsaraḥ parivatsaraḥ saṃvatsaro daśamaṃ śītoṣṇe ekādaśadvādaśe
(1,15.2) etānyevāsmai = 1,12.2.
(1,15.3) atha yam = 1,12.3.
(1,15.4) prāñcamidhmam = 1,12.4.
(1,15.5) evam = 1,12.5.
(1,16.1) prāṇo apāno vyānaḥ samāna udānaścakṣuḥ śrotraṃ vāṅmanastannavamam ... daśamaṃ nāmarūpe ekādaśadvādaśe
(1,16.2) etānyevāsmai = 1,12.2.
(1,16.3) atha yam = 1,12.3.
(1,16.4) prāñcamidhmam = 1,12.4.
(1,16.5) evam = 1,12.5.
(1,17.1) ajanyajaniryaśo ajanirvarco ajanistejo ajaniḥ saho ajanirmaho ajanirbrahmā ajanirbrāhmaṇavarcasamajaniḥ sarveṣāṃ lokānāṃ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vedānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ sarvāsāṃ sravantīnāṃ janitādhipatirajanirbhavatīti veda
(1,17.2) atha yaṃ kāmayeta sarveṣāṃ lokānāṃ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vedānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ sarvāsāṃ sravantīnāṃ janitādhipatirajaniḥ syāditi tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt ||
(1,17.3) prāñcamidhmam = 1,12.4.
(1,17.4) evaṃ cedasmai karoti sarveṣāṃ lokānāṃ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vedānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ sarvāsāṃ sravantīnāṃ janitādhipatirajanirbhavati ||
(1,18.1) viṣṭhitaśravā vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yatpūrvāhne adhi[[tiṣṭhati vi]]tiṣṭhatyasya puṇyā kīrtirainaṃ puṇyā kīrtirgacchatyupainaṃ puṇyā kīrtistiṣṭhati nāsmātpuṇyā kīrtirapakrāmati kīrtimān prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena bhavatīti veda
(1,18.2) atha yaṃ kāmayeta vitiṣṭhedasya puṇyā kīrtir[[ainaṃ puṇyā kīrtir]] gacchedupainaṃ puṇyā kīrtistiṣṭhennāsmātpuṇyā kīrtirapakrāmetkīrtimān prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena syāditi tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt ||
(1,18.3) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya paristīrya barhī rasān barhiṣyādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyādviṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ svāhetyagnau hutvā raseṣu saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homāṃstata enaṃ prāśayati rasān ||
(1,18.4) evaṃ cedasmai karo[[ti vitiṣṭha]]tyasya puṇyā kīrtir[[ainaṃ puṇyā kīrtir]] gacchatyupainaṃ puṇyā kīrtistiṣṭhati nāsmātpuṇyā kīrtirapakrāmati kīrtimān prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairbhavati ||
(1,19.1) varco vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yanmadhyāhnainaṃ varco gacchatyupainaṃ varcastiṣṭhati nāsmādvarco apakrāmati varcasvī prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena bhavatīti veda
(1,19.2) atha yaṃ kāmayetainaṃ varco gacchedupainaṃ varco tiṣṭhennāsmādvarco apakrāmedvarcasvī prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena syāditi tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt ||
(1,19.3) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya paristīrya barhī rasān barhiṣyādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyādvarco asi varco mayi dhehi svāhetyagnau hutvā raseṣu saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homāṃstata enaṃ prāśayati rasān ||
(1,19.4) evaṃ cedasmai karotyainaṃ varco gacchatyupainaṃ varcastiṣṭhati nāsmādvarco apakrāmati varcasvī prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena bhavati ||
(1,20.1) tejo vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yadaparāhṇainaṃ tejo gacchatyupainaṃ tejastiṣṭhati nāsmāttejo apakrāmati tejasvī prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena bhavatīti veda
(1,20.2) atha yaṃ kāmayetainaṃ tejo gacchedupainaṃ tejastiṣṭhennāsmāttejo apakrāmettejasvī prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena syāditi tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt ||
(1,20.3) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya paristīrya barhī rasān barhiṣyādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyādtejo asi tejo mayi dhehi svāhetyagnau hutvā raseṣu saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homāṃstata enaṃ prāśayati rasān ||
(1,20.4) evaṃ cedasmai karotyainaṃ tejo gacchatyupainaṃ tejastiṣṭhati nāsmāttejo apakrāmati tejasvī prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhanena bhavati ||
(1,21.1) viṣṭhitaśravā vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yatpūrvāhne ||
(1,21.2) varco vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yanmadhyāhne ||
(1,21.3) tejo vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yadaparāhṇe ||
(1,21.4) akāle tvevāprayuktāni bhavanti ||
(1,22.1) viṣṭhitaśravā vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yatpūrvarātre ||
(1,22.2) varco vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yadmadhyarātre ||
(1,22.3) tejo vai nāmaitannakṣatraṃ yadapararātre ||
(1,22.4) svesve kāle [[prayuktāni]] bhavanti ||
(1,22.5) [[yo vai rātriyānvevāprayuktāni bhavanti]] ||
(1,23.1) yo vai ahnaḥ puṇyāhaṃ veda puṇyāhī bhavati puṇyāhamasmai bhavati puṇyāha eva kurute ||
(1,23.2) sūryo vāhnaḥ puṇyāhaṃ tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt ||
(1,23.3) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya paristīrya barhī rasān barhiṣyādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyādviṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ svāhetyagnau hutvā raseṣu saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homāṃstata enaṃ prāśayati rasān ||
(1,23.4) evaṃ cedasmai karoti puṇyāhī bhavati puṇyāhamasmai bhavati puṇyāha eva kurute ||
(1,24.1) yo vai rātryāḥ puṇyāhaṃ veda puṇyāhī bhavati puṇyāhamasmai bhavati puṇyāha eva kurute ||
(1,24.2) candro vai rātryāḥ puṇyāhaṃ tasmādetasminnakṣatra evaṃvidvān kuryāt ||
(1,24.3) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya paristīrya barhī rasān barhiṣyādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyādyadrājānaṃ svāhetyagnau hutvā raseṣu saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homāṃstata enaṃ prāśayati rasān ||
(1,24.4) evaṃ cedasmai karoti puṇyāhī bhavati puṇyāhamasmai bhavati puṇyāha eva kurute ||
(1,25.1) yo vā ahoratrayoḥ puṇyāhaṃ veda puṇyāhī bhavati puṇyāhamasmai bhavati puṇyāha eva kurute ||
(1,25.2) brāhmaṇo vā ahorātrayoḥ puṇyāham
(1,25.3) taṃ pṛcchetkenājiteti
(1,25.4) sa cedbrūyātkartavyamiti tathā kuryāt
(1,25.5) puṇyāhī bhavati puṇyāhamasmai bhavati puṇyāha eva kurute ||
(1,26.1) yāni nakṣatrāṇi divyantarikṣe apsu bhūmau yāni nageṣu dikṣu | prakalpayaṃścandramā yānyeti sarvāṇi mamaitāni śivāni santu ||
(1,26.2) aṣṭāviṃśāni śivāni śagmāni saha yogaṃ bhajantu me | yogaṃ prapadye kṣemaṃ ca kṣemaṃ prapadye yogaṃ ca namo'horātrābhyāmastu ||
(1,26.3) svastitaṃ me suprātaḥ susāyaṃ sudivaṃ sumṛgaṃ suśakunaṃ me astu | suhavamagne svastyamartyaṃ gatvā punarāyābhinandan ||
(1,26.4) anuhavaṃ parihavaṃ parivādaṃ parikṣavam | sarvairme riktakumbhān parā tāntsavitaḥ suva ||
(1,26.5) apapāpaṃ parikṣavaṃ puṇyaṃ bhakṣīmahi kṣavam | śivā te pāta nāsikāṃ puṇyagaścābhimehatām ||
(1,26.6) imā yā brahmaṇaspate viṣūcīrvāta īrate | sadhrīcīrindra tāḥ kṛtvā mahyaṃ śivatamāskṛdhi ||
(1,26.7) svasti no astvabhayaṃ no astu namo'horātrābhyāmastu ||
(1,27.1) dadhyodanaṃ bhuktvā kṛttikābhirabhyudiyātsiddhārtho haiva punarāgacchati ||
(1,27.2) ārṣabhena māṃsena rohiṇyāṃ mṛgamāṃsairmṛgaśirasi rudhiramārdrāyāṃ gṛhapatibhaktaṃ punarvasvoḥ ghṛtapāyasaṃ puṣye sarpirmāṃsairaśleṣāsu
(1,27.3) etāni khalu prāgdvārāṇi nakṣatrāṇi bhavanti ||
(1,27.4) sa yatraiva prācīṃ diśamabhyutthitaḥ śastrahastena vā kaṇṭhahastena vā vadhyaghātena vā sameyānnivartetārvākkhalvetatkrośādūrdhvaṃ krośādavyāghātukamarthasya bhavati ||
(1,28.1) tailena kṛśaraṃ bhuktvā maghābhirabhyudiyātsiddhārtho haiva punarāgacchaty
(1,28.2) āvikairmāṃsairbhuktvā pūrvayoḥ phalgunyorabhyudiyādrasairuttarayoḥ praiyaṅgavaṃ haste citraṃ bhaktaṃ bhuktvā citrayābhyudiyātyāni jyeṣṭhāni teṣāṃ bhuktvā svātāvabhyudiyādapūpān viśākhayor
(1,28.3) etāni khalu dakṣiṇadvārāṇi nakṣatrāṇi bhavanti
(1,28.4) sa yatraiva dakṣiṇāṃ diśamabhyutthitaḥ śayanahastena vāstaraṇahastena vāsandīhastena vā nīvīhastena vā jānuhastena vā sameyānnivartetārvākkhalvetatkrośādūrdhvaṃ krośādavyāghātukamarthasya bhavati
(1,29.1) khalakulairbhuktvānurādhābhirabhyudiyātsiddhārtho haiva punarāgacchati
(1,29.2) jyeṣṭhaṃ bhaktaṃ bhuktvā jyeṣṭhayābhyudiyānmūlairbhuktvā mūlenābhyudiyād[[.... bhuktvā pūrvābhiraṣāḍhābhirabhyudiyād]] rasairuttarābhirnavanītena pāyasaṃ bhuktvābhijityabhyudiyād[[.... bhuktvā śravaṇenābhyudiyād]]
(1,29.3) etāni khalu paścimadvārāṇi nakṣatrāṇi bhavanti
(1,29.4) sa yatraiva pratīcīṃ diśamabhyutthitaḥ pāśahastena vā jālahastena vā matsyabandhena vā sameyānnivartetārvākkhalvetatkrośādūrdhvaṃ krośādavyāghātukamarthasya bhavati
(1,30.1) vidalasūpena bhuktvā śraviṣṭhābhirabhyudiyātsiddhārtho haiva punarāgacchati
(1,30.2) śākaṃ śatabhiṣaji godhā gavyairmāṃsairbhuktvā pūrvayoḥ proṣṭhapadayorabhyudiyādrasairuttarayorgṛhiṇībhaktaṃ bhuktvā revatyābhyudiyādakṣatamāṣairbhuktvāśvinyorabhyudiyāttilataṇḍulān bhakṣayitvā bharaṇībhirabhyudiyād
(1,30.3) etāni khalūdagdvārāṇi nakṣatrāṇi bhavanti
(1,30.4) sa yatraivodīcīṃ diśamabhyutthitaḥ pānahastena vā kiṇvahastena vākṣīveṇa vā sameyānnivartetārvākkhalvetatkrośādūrdhvaṃ krośādavyāghātukamarthasya bhavati
(1,31.1) atha rājño'bhiprayāṇasyānayanti pradakṣiṇamukhaṃ śvetamajaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śuklavāsasam ||
(1,31.2) supratiṣṭhitamavibhrāntaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ śṛṅginaṃ harim | sa cennadati saṃsṛṣṭastāmāhuḥ siddhimuttamām ||
(1,31.3) gajaṃ dhvajaṃ rathaṃ chattraṃ varma yodhānalaṃkṛtān | bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvāṇi praśastānyāyudhāni ca ||
(1,31.4) vāditrāṇi ca sarvāṇi patākā vividhāstathā | śuklāḥ sumanaso lājā akṣatā gaurasarṣapāḥ ||
(1,31.5) phalāni pūrṇapātrāṇi dhūpagandhān jalaṃ tilāḥ | arcayitvā devatāḥ sarvā brāhmaṇān pratipūjya ca ||
(1,31.6) purohitaṃ puraskṛtya suhṛdo mantriṇastathā | evaṃ prayāto labhate vijayaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
(1,31.7) kalyāṇanāmadheyaṃ ca gajamavyaṅgadarśanam | kumārīṃ dadhipātreṇa gṛhītena svalaṃkṛtām ||
(1,31.8) yadi cedadhigo jālmi sūryācandramasorgṛhe | aśvinā rāsabhendreṇa yānaṃ kuryātpradakṣiṇam ||
(1,32.1) prādakṣiṇyamagnergavāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ rājño rathasya [naravāhanasya śakaṭasya] caturyuktasya ṣaḍyuktāṣṭayuktasya ca
(1,32.2) hradasya dakṣiṇāvartasya kumārasyābhyutthitasya ca | manuṣyapūrṇapiṭakasya pṛthivyā utthitasya ca || prabaddhasyaikapaśoḥ
(1,32.3) ulūcī kālaśakuniḥ kṣipraśyeno'tha vartikā | ete dvijāḥ prādakṣiṇyāścāṣaścātra pradṛśyate ||
(1,32.4) krauñcanakulapriyavṛkṣacaityānāṃ nityaṃ vayasām ||
(1,32.5) tiryagnyagvādhipatitaṃ viparītaṃ hīnāṅgāṅgātiriktaṃ vikṛtanagnamuṇḍabaṇḍaścitraśyāmaśyāvadantakunakhijaṭila[[ḥ]] kāṣāyāvikayoś[carmā]brāhmaṇayor
(1,32.6) eteṣāṃ kiṃ ciddṛṣṭvā na gacched
(1,32.7) yadi gacchedarthino yanti cedarthaṃ gacchāniddaduṣo rātim | vavṛjyustṛṣyataḥ kāmamityetāṃ japet ||
(1,32.8) ni ṣajyato dasyūṃśchādayannindreti vā
(1,32.9) stuhi śrutamiti vā
(1,32.10) devīṃ vācamajanayanta devāstāṃ viśvarūpāḥ paśavo vadanti | sā no mandreṣamūrjaṃ duhānā dhenurvāgasmānupa suṣṭutaitu iti gardabhamukhena pratinadati pratilomapratikṛtyāsya
(1,32.11) sarvāśca pāpikā vāco neṣṭā<ḥ> | kuceladarśanaṃ ca | anarthā hiṃsārtham | taddarśanāya | pāpaṃ vā jihīrṣatāṃ siddhiḥ ||
(1,33.1) atha rogaparimāṇāny
(1,33.2) uttarāsvaṣāḍhāsūttarayoḥ proṣṭhapadayormāsam |
(1,33.3) rohiṇyāṃ viṃśatirātram |
(1,33.4) punarvasorūnaviṃśatirātraṃ pūrvāṇi dvandvinām |
(1,33.5) mṛgaśirasi ṣoḍaśarātramabhijiti śravaṇe ca
(1,33.6) ārdrāyāṃ pañcadaśarātramanūrādhāśraviṣṭhābharaṇīṣu ca |
(1,33.7) puṣye dvādaśarātraṃ haste svātau ca |
(1,33.8) śatabhiṣaji navarātraṃ māse vākālaṃ kurute |
(1,33.9) jyeṣṭhāyāṃ mūle cāṣṭarātram |
(1,33.10) revatyāṃ saptarātram |
(1,33.11) kṛttikāśleṣā maghā uttare phālgunyau citrā viśākhe aśvayujau ca saptasveteṣu daśarātram ||
(1,34.1) atha balayaḥ
(1,34.2) śukla upaśurasṛpeśuklosaptamūnmodanaḥ | aṣṭamaḥ palalodano dhānāḥ saktavo'tha śaṣkulī ||
(1,34.3) citraśca kṛkavākuśca śuklaḥ kambūkapiṇḍakaḥ | sarvabījāni mūlāni udapātraṃ ca pāyasau ||
(1,34.4) paśu ghṛtaṃ kaśīkā ca ārdramāṃsāni pāyasau | pathyāyāṃ māṣasaktavaḥ pāyaso'tha tilodanaḥ ||
(1,34.5) sarvatra gandhapuṣpāṇi lājānulepikāstathā | anudvāraṃ ca nakṣatraṃ daivataṃ cātra yojayet ||
(1,34.6) dīpāśca maṇḍale dīptāḥ śuciścāpi baliṃ haret | yo'smin yastvā māturiti vipariharet ||
(1,35.1) saśvetasaktu kaṃsaśca prācīnārthasya maṅgalam | sravaṃ ca māṃsapeśī ca dakṣiṇārthasya maṅgalam ||
(1,35.2) kumārī dadhikaṃsaśca pratyagarthasya maṅgalam | anaḍvān brahmacārī ca udagarthasya maṅgalam ||
(1,35.3) kumārīṃ dadhipātreṇa gṛhītena svalaṃkṛtām | pradakṣiṇāṃ tu tāṃ kuryāddhruvaṃ syātsiddhiriṣyate ||
(1,36.1) senāṃ cedabhyutthitāṃ mandraḥ pratigarjedrājā vāmātyo vā mariṣyatīti vidyāttatra vāruṇīṃ japet | uduttamaṃ varuṇa pāśamiti ||
(1,36.2) senāṃ cedadbhyutthitāṃ dhūmo'nugacchedvijeṣyatīti vidyāt | tveṣaste dhūma ityanumantrayet ||
(1,36.3) senāṃ cedabhyutthitāṃ vāto'nuvāyādvijeṣyatīti vidyādvāta ā vatvityanumantrayet ||
(1,36.4) senāṃ cedabhyutthitāṃ mṛgo vyabhimṛśedarthaṃ tasyā vinaśyatīti vidyāt | mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhā ityanumantrayet ||
(1,36.5) senāṃ cedabhyutthitāṃ pakṣiṇo vyatipateyurmāṃsodanaṃ ca tatra dadyāt | aliklavā jāṣkamadā gṛdhrā ityanumantrayet ||
(1,36.6) senāṃ cedabhyutthitāṃ kapiñjalaḥ prativadedbhadraṃ vadeti tisraḥ kāpiñjalāni stavanāni vadanti ||
(1,36.7) yo abhyu babhruṇāyasi svapantamatsi puruṣaṃ śayānamagasvalam | ayasmayena brahmaṇāśmamayena varmaṇā paryasmān varuṇo dadhad || ityabhyavakāśe saṃviśati | abhyavakāśe saṃviśati ||
(1,37.1) agnirdevo yajvanaḥ kṛṣṇavartmā vaiśvānaro jātavedā rasāgrabhuk | sa nakṣatrāṇāṃ prathamena pāvakaḥ kṛttikābhirjvalano no'nuśāmyatām ||
(1,37.2) prajāpatiryaḥ sasṛje prajā imā devāntsa sṛṣṭvā viniyoj[[y]]a karmasu | sa sarvabhuksarvayogeṣu rohiṇī śivāḥ kriyāḥ kṛṇutāṃ karmasiddhaye ||
(1,37.3) vidyāvido ye abhiśocamānavā arcanti śakraṃ saha devatāgaṇaiḥ | sa no yoge mṛgaśiraḥ śivāḥ kriyāḥ śreṣṭharājaḥ kṛṇutāṃ karmasiddhaye ||
(1,37.4) devaṃ bhavaṃ paśupatiṃ haraṃ kṛśaṃ mahādevaṃ śarvamugraṃ śikhaṇḍinam | sahasrākṣamaśaniṃ yaṃ gṛṇanti sa no rudraḥ paripātu na ārdrayā ||
(1,37.5) ....yā vipraiḥ kavibhirnamasyate dākṣāyaṇī devapurādibhirnṛbhir | sā naḥ stutā prathamajā punarvasuḥ śivāḥ kriyāḥ kṛṇutāṃ karmasiddhaye ||
(1,38.1) yasya devā brahmacaryeṇa karmaṇā mahāsuraṃ tigmatayābhicakrire | taṃ subudhaṃ devaguruṃ bṛhaspatimarcāmi pusyeṇa sahābhipātu mā ||
(1,38.2) yā na[[ḥ]] stutaḥ parihiṇomi medhayā tapyamānamṛṣibhiḥ kāmaśocibhiḥ | jaratkārasūnorṛṣibhirmanīṣibhistā aśleṣā abhirakṣantu noragaiḥ ||
(1,38.3) ye devatvaṃ puṇyakṛto'bhicakrire ye cāpare ye ca pare maharṣayaḥ | arcāmi sūnuryamarājagān pitṝṃśchivāḥ kriyāḥ kṛṇutāṃ ca no maghā ||
(1,38.4) yo yojayan karmaṇā carṣaṇīdhṛto bhūmiṃ ceti bhaga[[ḥ prajāḥ]] prasādayan | taddevatye śivatamāmalaṃkṛte phalgunyorīḍe bhajanaṃ ca pūrvayoḥ ||
(1,38.5) stutaṃ pūrvairaryamaṇaṃ manīṣibhiḥ staumi devaṃ jagati vācamerayan | taddevatye śivatamāmalaṃkṛte phalgunyau na uttare devatātaye ||
(1,39.1) ś[y]āvairyuktaḥ śitipāddhiraṇyayo yasya rathaḥ pathibhirvartate sukhaiḥ | sa no hastena savitā hiraṇyabhugghiraṇyapāṇiḥ savitā [no]'bhirakṣatu ||
(1,39.2) tvaṣṭre namaḥ kṣitisṛje manīṣiṇe bhūtagoptre paramakarmakāriṇe | sā na[[ḥ]] stutā kṛṇutāṃ karmasiddhaye citrāṃ devī saha yogena rūpabhṛt ||
(1,39.3) yaḥ prāṇināṃ jīvayan khāni sevate śivo bhūtvā mātariśvā rasāgrabhuk | dhvajo'ntarikṣasya sa sarvabhūtabhṛdvāyurdevaḥ svātinā no'bhirakṣatu ||
(1,39.4) yāvīḍitāvātmavidbhirmaṇīṣibhiḥ sahitau [yau] trīṇi savanāni sāmagau | indrāgnī varadau namaskṛtau viśākhayoḥ kurvatāmāyuṣe śrīḥ ||
(1,39.5) viśve devā yamṛṣimāhurmitraṃ bharadvājamṛṣitaḥ prasāmavit | taṃ jagatyā gāthayā staumyugraiḥ sa māmanūrādhābhir[bhṛtakaṇvo]'bhirakṣatu ||
(1,40.1) śatakraturyo nijaghāna śambaraṃ vṛtraṃ ca hatvā saritaḥ prasarjata[ḥ] | sa naḥ stutaḥ prītamanāḥ puraṃdaro marutsakhā jyeṣṭhayā no'bhirakṣatu ||
(1,40.2) yā dhārayatyojasātidevapadaṃ mātā pṛthivī ca sā sarvabhūtabhṛt | sā na[[ḥ]] stutā kṛṇutāṃ karmasiddhaye mūlaṃ devī nirṛtiḥ sarvakarmasu ||
(1,40.3) parjanyasṛṣṭāstisṛṇībhirāvṛtaṃ yāstarpayantyabhitaḥ pravṛddhaye | tā[[ḥ]] staumyāpo vāruṇīḥ ... pūrvā āṣāḍhā svadhayāstu yojane ||
(1,40.4) yāstriṃśataṃ trīṃśca madanti devā devanāmno nirmitā[[ṃ]]śca bhūyasaḥ | tā no'ṣāḍhā uttarā vaso viśve [[śivāḥ]] kriyāḥ kṛṇutāṃ suramatāḥ ||
(1,40.5) yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvakṛtsarvabhūtabhṛdyasmādanyanna paraṃ kiṃ canāsti | anirmitaḥ satyajitaḥ puruṣṭutaḥ sa no brahmābhijitā no'bhirakṣatu ||
(1,41.1) sthānācyute sthānamindrāya pātave devebhyaśca ya īrayaṃs[trir] vicakrame | taṃ sviddhi svargaṃ nākapṛṣṭhaṃ viśvaṃ viṣṇurdevaḥ śravaṇenābhirakṣatu ||
(1,41.2) aṣṭau śatāni śvetaketūnāṃ yāni tvaṃ ca sa tvaṃ nijaghāna bhūyasaḥ | anādeśenobhaya[ta]śca vīḍitāḥ śraviṣṭhābhirno'bhirakṣantu vājinaḥ ||
(1,41.3) vājā devī devamṛṇānikākubhāvubhāvājasya natakarmaṇā śivā | tava vrājaṃ staumasi devabhojanau pratyagbhiṣakśatabhiṣakśivau naḥ ||
(1,41.4) śunāsīrau naḥ pramumūtu jihmasau tautau pitṛbhyo dadatu[[ḥ]] stanau śubhau | tau pūrvajau kṛṇutāmekapādajaḥ pratiṣṭhānau sarvakāmābhayāya ca ||
(1,40.5) sarvārthāya kṛṇomi karmasiddhaye gaviṣṭutāyānekakāriṇe namaḥ | so'hirbudhnyaḥ kṛṇutāmuttarau śivau pratiṣṭhānau sarvakāmābhayāya ca ||
(1,41.6) yaṃ mahāhemamṛṣitaḥ prasāmavidbharadvājaścandramasau divākaram | sajuṣṭānāmaśvayujau bhayāya ca sa naḥ pūṣā kṛṇutāṃ revatīṃ śivām ||
(1,41.7) jīrṇaṃ santaṃ yau yuvānaṃ hi cakraturṛṣiṃ dhiyā cyavanaṃ somapau kṛtau | tau naścittibhirbhiṣajāmasya satkarau .... prajāmaśvinyāmaśvinau śivau ||
(1,41.8) yasya śyāmaśabalau rakṣataḥ svadhā duṣkṛtsukṛdvividhā carṣaṇīdhṛtau | tau savitryā ca saviturdharmacāribhiryamo rājā bharaṇībhirno'bhirakṣatu ||
(1,42.1) atha nakṣatrasnānānāṃ vidhiṃ vakṣyāmi sāṃpadam | grahadaivatapūjāṃ ca yeṣu yatra yathāvidhi ||
(1,42.2) nakṣatrayogakālajñaḥ kṛtvā tantraṃ yathāvidhi | yajedgrahān haviṣyeṇa yathoktena ca devatāḥ ||
(1,42.3) praśastalakṣaṇaṃ kumbhaṃ sasaṃbhārajalaṃ budhaḥ | saṃpātābhihitaṃ kṛtvā mantrairvidhimanusmaran ||
(1,42.4) sāvitryā śāntisūktaiśca mahāvyāhṛtibhistathā | apāṃ stotraiḥ pavitraiśca nakṣatrastutibhistathā ||
(1,42.5) nakṣatradaivatānmantrān pratinakṣatramāvapet | kāmyāṃścaivāvapenmantrān karmaliṅgavidhānavit ||
(1,42.6) saṃpātyāthābhimantrya vā nakṣatrasnānakovidaḥ | snāpayedarthinaṃ vāgbhiḥ puṇyābhirabhimantritam ||
(1,42.7) eṣa eva vidhirdṛṣṭaḥ sadasyebhyaśca dakṣiṇā | pūrvamāpyāyayeddehaṃ paścāddadyāttu dakṣiṇām ||
(1,42.8) anena vidhinā snātvā dadyāccaivātra dakṣiṇām | prāpnotyanunayaṃ puṃsaḥ sa vedaphalamaśnute || [prāpnotyprāpṇotyed. (misprint)]
(1,42.9) ātmānaṃ nirmalīkṛtya devāniṣṭvā grahāṃstathā | vidvadbhyo dakṣiṇā deyā dvijānannena tarpayet ||
(1,43.1) kṛttikābhiḥ śirīṣasya aśvatthasya vaṭasya ca | snāpayetpattrabhaṅgena ya icchedrājapūjitam ||
(1,43.2) rohiṇyāṃ snāpayedvaiśyaṃ sarvabījairalaṃkṛtam | akṣatānantaraṃ kṛtvā tathā saubhāgyamarhati ||
(1,43.3) yadā mṛgaśiro yujyettadā snānaṃ vidhīyate | muktāmaṇisuvarṇena dhanārthī tena snāpayet ||
(1,43.4) śrīveṣṭakasarjarasatagarośīrapattrakaiḥ | ārdrāyāṃ vaṇijaḥ snātāḥ sulābhāṃstu labhanti te ||
(1,43.5) punarvasubhyāṃ gomārgādāharedagramṛttikām | gopīṭhe snāpayedgo'rthī kṣipraṃ gomān bhaviṣyati ||
(1,43.6) raktaśālisahasreṇa tāvadbhirgaurasarṣapaiḥ | sahasravīryānantyā ca madayantīpriyaṅgubhiḥ | trīn puṣyān brāhmaṇaḥ snātaḥ pārthivaṃ labhate yaśaḥ ||
(1,43.7) aśleṣāsvāhārayedubhayataḥ kūlamṛttikāḥ | [aśv]ārohaṃ snāpayettena kṣipravāhī bhaviṣyati ||
(1,43.8) maghābhistu tilaiḥ snāyādutpalaiḥ kamalaistathā | tasmiṃstu māse sā kanyā kṣipraṃ ca labhate patim ||
(1,43.9) atha pūrvayoḥ phalgunyoḥ śatapuṣpā priyaṅgavaḥ | madhveva ca tṛtīyaṃ syātsaubhāgyaṃ bhogavardhanam ||
(1,43.10) athottarayoḥ phalgunyorakṣatā gaurasarṣapāḥ | etatsnānaṃ prayuñjīta prajāsthāpanamuttamam ||
(1,44.1) hastena sarvakārūṇāṃ caurāṇāṃ cāpi nityaśaḥ | nadīgiritaṭākeṣu mṛttikāsnānamuttamam ||
(1,44.2) citrāyāṃ citramālyaistu sarvagandhairalaṃkṛtam | yoṣārthī snāpayettena kṣipraṃ sa labhate priyām ||
(1,44.3) svātinā tu gandhaiḥ snāyādutpalaiḥ kumudaistathā | tasmiṃstu māse sā kanyā kṣipraṃ nirvyūhyate tataḥ ||
(1,44.4) khaḍgasya ca viṣāṇena gajasya ṛṣabhasya vā | viśākhābhyāmabhiṣikto [rājā] pṛthivīmabhiśāsayet ||
(1,44.5) anūrādhāsvāhārayedvalmīkaśatamṛttikāḥ | karṣaṇaṃ snāpayettena dhanadhānyena vardhate ||
(1,44.6) jyeṣṭhāyāṃ jyaiṣṭhyakāmaṃ tu abhiṣiñcetpurohitam | rasaiśca miśradhānyaiścābhiṣiktaḥ prāśayedrasān ||
(1,44.7) mūlena sarvatobhadramupaviṣṭā varavarṇinī | śamīpattrasahasreṇa snānātputraṃ prasūyate ||
(1,44.8) atha pūrvāsvaṣāḍhāsu yā snāyādahate paṭe | jātarūpeṇa kalyāṇī bhogaṃ bhuṅkte patipriyā ||
(1,44.9) athottarāsvaṣāḍhāsu yaḥ snāyāccedupoṣitaḥ | mahāhrada uśīreṇa dāsīdāsena vardhate ||
(1,44.10) vacayotpalakuṣṭhaiśca brāhmī siddhārthakaistathā | abhijidbrāhmaṇaḥ snātaḥ pārthivaṃ labhate yaśaḥ ||
(1,45.1) śravaṇena sravantīnāṃ yaḥ snāyātsaṃgameṣu ca | sa saṃgacchati svarṇena hiraṇyena dhanena vā ||
(1,45.2) śraviṣṭhābhirdhanakāmaṃ [tu] snāpayedyatra candanaiḥ | etatsnānaṃ prayuñjāno dhanadhānyena vardhate ||
(1,45.3) śatabhiṣagbhiṣakkāmo'bhiṣiñcecchāntikarmasu | so'bhiṣikto hatapāpmā sarvarogaiḥ pramucyate ||
(1,45.4) [atha] pūrvayoḥ proṣṭhapadayo rocanayāñjanena ca | snātā gajaviṣāṇena rājānaṃ janayetsutam ||
(1,45.5) athottara[yoḥ] proṣṭhapadayoḥ prasannāpadmakaṃ madhu | gandharvaṃ snāpayettena rājavāhī bhaviṣyati ||
(1,45.6) khaḍgasya ca viṣāṇena jalena madhusarpiṣā | revatyāṃ kṣatriyaḥ snāto [rājā] pṛthivīmabhiśāsate ||
(1,45.7) aśvinyāṃ svastikaṃ mālyaṃ madayantīpriyaṅgubhiḥ | rūpājīvāyāstatsnānaṃ saubhāgyaṃ bhogavardhanam ||
(1,45.8) bharaṇībhirbhadramustaistvelāsiddharthakaistathā | snātā patikulaṃ gacchedasapatnamakaṇṭakam ||
(1,46.1) sarvajñaḥ sarvaga śaṃsa nārada prajñānamanyasmādanūnaprajñātsvargasya lokasya dehādyairyātpathibhirupapanno manuṣyaḥ ||
(1,46.2) upoṣitaḥ śuciśīlaḥ puṇyagandho yadā bhavet | prāgastaṃgamanabhojanādyadenaṃ nityamatandritaḥ ||
(1,46.3) samayācārapūrvābhiḥ karmasiddhiḥ praśasyate | [taṃ] kāmadughaṃ svargakāmaḥ paretya pratipadyate ||
(1,47.1) kṛttikābhiḥ pāyasaṃ sarpiṣā saha bhojayet | [taṃ] kāmadughaṃ svargakāmaḥ paretya pratipadyate ||
(1,48.1) rohiṇyāmakṣatairmāṣaiḥ sarpirmiśraṃ sahaudanam | dugdhānnapāna[[ṃ]] maṃheta so'kṣato yamasādane ||
(1,48.2) mṛgaśirasi maṃheta ajāṃ dhenuṃ payasvinīm | sāsmai sarvān kāmān dugdh[v]ā eti pūrvā payasvinī ||
(1,48.3) ārdrāyāṃ kṛsaraṃ dadyāttailamiśramupoṣitaḥ | punarvasubhyāṃ maṃheta madhvapūpāṃstvanuttamān ||
(1,48.4) rukmaṃ puṣyeṇa maṃheta so'kṣato yamasādane | aśleṣā rajataṃ dadyātsaurabheyeṇa preṣitaḥ ||
(1,48.5) sarpānnirhanti pretasya paripanthi sukhādbhayāt | maghābhistu tilān dadyānmadhumiśrān smaran pitṝn ||
(1,48.6) kāmaistatropatiṣṭhanti amī ye somayājinaḥ | [phāṇiteneṣṭakā miśrā dadyātpūrvayoḥ phalgunyormadhunottarayoḥ ||]
(1,48.7) pūrvottarayoḥ phalgunyorduhate madhuphāṇite ||
(1,49.1) bṛhaddhastirathaṃ yuktaṃ hastena tu dadannaraḥ | savituḥ sthānamāpnoti divyāṃ kāmajavāṃ sabhām ||
(1,49.2) citrāyāṃ vṛṣalīṃ dadyātsarvapuṣpairalaṃkṛtām | gandhaiḥ śuśrūṣamāṇastu dhruve sthāne [sugandhiḥ] prapadyate ||
(1,49.3) svātāvekadhanaṃ dadyādyadyadasya priyaṃ gṛhe | asajjamāno gaccheta aśarīro yathā manaḥ ||
(1,49.4) dhenuṃ tu rūpasaṃpannāmanaḍvāhau tu vā vahau | viśākhābhyāṃ madhumanthaṃ prāpayetsthānamuttamam ||
(1,49.5) anūrādhāsu prāvaraṇamannaṃ tu śuci jyeṣṭhāyāṃ ca | dadyāccānnaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo bhakṣairuccāvacaiḥ saha ||
(1,49.6) surā[[ṃ]] mūlena maṃhetābrāhmaṇībhya upoṣitaḥ | mātustenānṛṇo bhavati saṃkarācca vimucyate ||
(1,49.7) udamanthamaṣāḍhāsu pūrvāsu madhunottaram ||
(1,49.8) abhijidduhitaraṃ dadyānmadhuparkapurogamām | uttame brahmaṇaḥ sthāne sarvakāmaiḥ pramodate ||
(1,50.1) kambalaṃ śravaṇe dadyādvastrāntaramupoṣitaḥ | śraviṣṭhābhirvastrayugaṃ gandhān śatabhiṣagbhavet ||
(1,50.2) ajaṃ saṃpacyodanaṃ dadyātpūrvayoḥ proṣṭhapadayoraurabhreṇa sahottarayoḥ ||
(1,50.3) dhenuṃ ca rūpasaṃpannāṃ gaurgṛṣṭiḥ pūrṇadohanīm | revatyāṃ trivatsāṃ dadyācchubhakāṃsyopadohinīm ||
(1,50.4) vastreṇānaḍvāhau [saṃ]baddhvā dadyādaśvayujo<r> naraḥ | daśa varṣasahasrāṇi lomnilomni mahīyate ||
(1,50.5) aṣṭau varṣasahasrāṇi ajadhenvā payo'śnute | daśa varṣasahasrāṇi godhenvā payo'śnute ||
(1,50.6) anaḍvāhaṃ tu yo dadyātsuhṛdaṃ sādhuvāhinam | vīraṃ prajānāṃ bhartāraṃ prāpnoti daśadhenudam ||
(1,50.7) [yadā vatsasya pādau dvau śirascāpi pradṛśyate | tadā gauḥ pṛthivī jñeyā yāvadgarbhaṃ na muñcati ||]
(1,50.8) bharaṇībhiḥ kṛṣṇatilāṃ dadyāt[tila]dhenuṃ payasvinīm | tayā durgāṇi tarati kṣuradhārāṃśca parvatān ||
(1,50.9) nakṣatrāṇāṃ yathā somo jyotiṣāmiva bhāskaraḥ | bhāti divyaṃ divaṃ jyotiḥ pāvakaḥ śuciruttamaḥ ||
(1,50.10) evamuktāṃ nakṣatradakṣiṇāṃ yo dadātīha jīvaloke | [[a]]pahatya tamaḥ sarvaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate | yathā yaṣṭustathādhyetureṣā brāhmī pratiśrutireṣā brāhmī pratiśrutiriti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_1 . [kṛttikārohiṇīmadhye paippalādā mantrāḥ])
(1 ,1.1) oṃ yadrājānaṃ śakadhūmaṃ nakṣatrāṇyakṛṇvata | bhadrāhamasmai prāyacchan tato rāṣṭramajāyata ||
(1 ,1.2) bhadrāhamastu naḥ sāyaṃ bhadrāhaṃ prātarastu naḥ | bhadrāhamasmabhyaṃ tvaṃ śakadhūma sadā kṛṇu ||
(1 ,1.3) yo no bhadrāhamakaraḥ sāyaṃ prātaratho divā | tasmai te nakṣatrarāja śakadhūma sadā namaḥ ||
(1 ,1.4) yadāhuḥ śakadhūma[[ṃ]] mahānakṣatrāṇāṃ prathamajaṃ jyotiragre | tannaḥ śatīmabhikṛṇotu rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ niyacchāt ||
(1 ,1.5) yo'smin yakṣma[[ḥ]] puruṣe praviṣṭa iṣitaṃ daivyaṃ sahaḥ | agniṣṭaṃ ghṛtabodhano'paskanda no vidūramasmatso'nyena samṛcchāt || tasmai prasuvāmasi ||
(1 ,1.6) yastvā māturuta vā pituḥ parijāyamānamabhisaṃbabhūva | na tvadyamadhināsayāma so'nyasmai sayātaiḥ praviṣṭaḥ ||
(1 ,1.7) aliklavā gṛdhrāḥ kaṅkāḥ suparṇāḥ śvāpadāḥ patatriṇo vayāṃsi śakunayo'muṣyāmusyāyaṇasyāmuṣyāḥ putrasyādahane carantu ||
kṛttikārohiṇīmadhye paippalādā mantrāḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_2. rāṣṭrasaṃvargaḥ)
(2,1.1) om | brahmaṇe brahmavedāya rudrāya parameṣṭhine | namaskṛtya pravakṣyāmi śeṣamātharvaṇaṃ vidhim ||
(2,1.2) daivaṃ prabhavate śreṣṭhaṃ hetumātraṃ tu pauruṣam | daivena tu suguptena śakto jetuṃ vasuṃdharām ||
(2,1.3) daivātpuruṣakārācca daivameva viśiṣyate | tasmāddaivaṃ viśeṣeṇa pūjayettu mahīpatiḥ ||
(2,1.4) daivakarmavidau tasmātsāṃvatsarapurohitau | gṛhṇīyātsatataṃ rājā dānasaṃmānarañjanaiḥ ||
(2,1.5) apitā tu yathā bālastathāsāṃvatsaro nṛpaḥ | amātṛko yathā bālastathātharvavivarjitaḥ || arimadhye yathaikākī tathā vaidyavivarjitaḥ ||
(2,1.6) dharmeṇa pṛthivīṃ kṛtsnāṃ vijayiṣyanmahīpatiḥ | vidyālakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ bhārgavaṃ varayedgurum ||
(2,1.7) caturvidhasya karmaṇo vedatattvena niścayam | prajāpatirathaiko hi na vedatrayamīkṣate ||
(2,2.1) atharvabhinnaṃ yacchāntaṃ tacchāntaṃ netaraistribhiḥ | vijñānaṃ triṣu lokeṣu jāyate brahmavedataḥ ||
(2,2.2) atharvā sṛjate ghoramadbhutaṃ śamayettathā | atharvā rakṣate yajñaṃ yajñasya patiraṅgirāḥ ||
(2,2.3) divyāntarikṣabhaumānāmutpātānāmanekadhā | śamayitā brahmavedajñastasmādrakṣitā bhṛguḥ ||
(2,2.4) brahmā śamayennādhvaryurna chandogo na bahvṛcaḥ | rakṣāṃsi rakṣati brahmā brahmā tasmādatharvavit ||
(2,2.5) senāyā rakṣaṇe tasmātsvarāṣṭraparivṛddhaye | śāntyarthaṃ ca mahīpālo vṛṇuyādbhārgavaṃ gurum ||
(2,3.1) gurave pārthivo dadyātkotiṃ varaṇadakṣiṇām | ardhamardhaṃ mahībhāgaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu tribhāgataḥ ||
(2,3.2) evaṃ bhūmipramāṇena koṭibhāgaṃ vinirdiśet | yena vā parituṣyeta gurustatpārthivaścaret ||
(2,3.3) ghnanti daivopasargāśca na ca devo'bhivarṣati | vīrāstatra na sūyante yadrāṣṭramapurohitam ||
(2,3.4) na haviḥ pratigṛhṇanti devatāḥ pitaro dvijāḥ | tasya bhūmipateryasya gṛhe nātharvavidguruḥ ||
(2,3.5) samāhitāṅgapratyaṅgaṃ vidyācāraguṇānvitam | paippalādaṃ guruṃ kuryācchrīrāṣṭrārogyavardhanam ||
(2,4.1) tathā śaunakinaṃ vāpi vedamantravipaścitam | rāṣṭrasya vṛddhikartāraṃ dhanadhānyādibhiḥ sadā ||
(2,4.2) ātharvaṇādṛte nānyo niyojyo'tharvavidguruḥ | nṛpeṇa jayakāmena nirmito'gnirivādhvare ||
(2,4.3) bahvṛco hanti vai rāṣṭramadhvaryurnāśayetsutān | chandogo dhananāśāya tasmādātharvaṇo guruḥ ||
(2,4.4) ajñānādvā pramādādvā yasya syādbahvṛco guruḥ | deśarāṣṭrapurāmātyanāśastasya na saṃśayaḥ ||
(2,4.5) yadi vādhvaryavaṃ rājā niyunakti purohitam | śastreṇa vadhyate kṣipraṃ parikṣīṇārthavāhanaḥ ||
(2,5.1) yathaiva paṅguradhvānamapakṣī cāṇḍajo nabhaḥ | evaṃ chandogaguruṇā rājā vṛddhiṃ na gacchati ||
(2,5.2) purodhā jalado yasya maudo vā syātkadā cana | abdāddaśabhyo māsebhyo rāṣṭrabhraṃśaṃ sa gacchati ||
(2,5.3) palālakamidaṃ sarvamṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃsthitam | sāraṃ sāraparaṃ dhānyamatharvāṅgiraso viduḥ ||
(2,5.4) trayo lokāstrayo devāstrayo vedāstrayo'gnayaḥ | ardhamātre layaṃ yānti vedaścātharvaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(2,5.5) na tithirna ca nakṣatraṃ na graho na ca candramāḥ | atharvamantrasaṃprāptyā sarvasiddhirbhaviṣyati ||
(2,6.1) guruṇā paippalādena vedamantravipaścitā | vardhate dhanadhānyena rāṣṭramevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
(2,6.2) stabdhaṃ nṛśaṃsaṃ pramattaṃ śraddhāhīnamaśāstragam | bhūmikāmo na yāceta dātāramapi pārthivam ||
(2,6.3) sahasrāṇāṃ śataṃ japtvā gāyatryāyājyayājakaḥ | pūyate bhrūṇahāpyevaṃ cāṇḍālānnāda eva ca ||
(2,6.4) sarvadravyaparityāgācchuddhiranyairudāhṛtā | anyaiścaturṇāṃ vedānāmadhītyādyottamā ṛcaḥ ||
(2,6.5) yajanādabhicārādvā kva cidvā mantrakarmaṇi | pūtāneva dvijān prāhuragnikāñcanavarcasa iti ||
iti rāṣṭrasaṃvargaḥ samāptaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_3. rājaprathamābhiṣekaḥ)
(3,1.1) omatha rājaprathamābhiṣeke prakṛtidravyāṇi parīkṣeta ||
(3,1.2) tadyathā ||
(3,1.3) rathasiṃhāsanāsichattracāmaradhvajagajavājivastrālaṃkārasāṃvatsaracikitsakapurohitādīny
(3,1.4) upayuktāni nopayojayet ||
(3,1.5) śmaśānānaladevatāni nihitāni dravyāṇīty
(3,1.6) ācāryapurohitavajramukhata ityāha durmatiḥ sa bhāradvājo
(3,1.7) na hyagnerivopayuktasyopayogo vidyata iti parāśaro
(3,1.8) brahmā brahmāṇḍāgnirivāprameyo
(3,1.9) nānyakulopayukto
(3,1.10) yasyānyakulopayuktaḥ purodhāḥ śāntikapauṣṭikaprāyaścittīyābhicārikanaimittikordhvadehikānyatharvavihitāni karmāṇi kuryātsa tasya pratyaṅgiro bhūtvā hastyaśvarathapadātikaṃ prakṛtimukhebhyo ...
(3,1.11) variṣyanna punaḥ kuryādanyaṃ rājā purohitam | nirmālyamiva taṃ rājā nānyo bhūyaḥ samācaret ||
(3,1.12) svajasraṃ hyagnau heturbhagavato vyādhitapatitonmattābhiśastaprahīṇapradhvastasaṃprasāraṇamṛtvik ||
(3,1.13) tasmātkulīnaṃ śrotriyaṃ bhṛgvaṅgirovidaṃ vinayākṛtiśaucācārayuktamalolupaṃ vrataniyamacāritravṛttalakṣanaguṇasaṃpannaṃ saṃdhivigrahacintakaṃ māhendrajālaprabhṛtikarmādiṣvabhividakaṃ jitasthānāsanaṃ himātapavarṣasahaṃ hrīdhṛtikamārjavaṃ śamadamadayādānaśaktisaṃpannaṃ bṛhaspatyuśanasoḥ sthānākṛtipramāṇaṃ varṇāśrutavapuṣā cānumeyaṃ tejasvinaṃ gambhīraṃ sattvayuktaṃ guruṃ vṛṇīyādbhūpatiriti ||
(3,1.14) madhuparkādyena vidhinā yathārthaṃ saṃpādya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt
(3,1.15) koṭimadhyāttṛtīyaṃ bhāgam
(3,1.16) yathābhūmipramāṇena vā
(3,1.17) hastyaśvaṃ narayānaṃ divyamābharaṇamātapatraṃ hiraṇyaṃ kṣitigodhanadhānyaratnādikaṃ ca gurave dadyād
(3,1.18) yena vā parituṣyeta ||
(3,1.19) abhiṣekaḥ saṃhitāvidhau vyākhyātaḥ ||
(3,2.1) hastyaśvaṃ gurave dadyānnarayānaṃ tathaiva ca | divyamābharaṇaṃ caiva ātapatrādimeva ca ||
(3,2.2) ucchiṣṭārthaṃ na gṛhṇīyānmaṅgalārthaṃ mahīpatiḥ | mantrauṣadhyo na sidhyanti rājā tatra vinaśyati ||
(3,2.3) nātidīrghaṃ nātihrasvaṃ nātisthūlaṃ kṛśaṃ tathā | na ca hīnātiriktāṅgaṃ kva citkuryātpurohitam ||
(3,2.4) hīnādhikāṅgaṃ patitaṃ vivarṇaṃ stenaṃ jaḍaṃ klībamaśaktiyuktam | bhinnasvaraṃ kāṇaṃ virūpanetraṃ dveṣyaṃ ca rājā guruṃ naiva kuryāt ||
(3,2.5) hīnādhikāṅge purarāṣṭrahāniḥ kāṇe jaḍe vāhanakośanāśaḥ | stene tvaśakte ca samastadoṣāḥ klībe vivarṇe nṛpatirvinaśyet ||
(3,3.1) bhinnasvare jāyate gātrabhedo dveṣye gurau vipratipattimāhuḥ | vivarṇanetre patite tu putrānadhvaryuṇā ca nihanti pautrān ||
(3,3.2) kṛṣṇe kośakṣayaṃ vidyādrakte vāhanasaṃkṣayaḥ | piṅgalaḥ pārthivaṃ hanyādrāṣṭraṃ hanyāttu kekaraḥ ||
(3,3.3) bahvṛcaṃ hi niyuñjyādyaḥ paurohitye tu pārthivaḥ | sa tārapaṅke hastīva saha tenaiva majjati ||
(3,3.4) adhvaryuṃ hi niyuñjyādyaḥ paurohitye tu pārthivaḥ | uttitīrṣurivāśmānamādatte svavadhāya saḥ ||
(3,3.5) vadhabandhaparikleśaṃ kośavāhanasaṃkṣayaḥ | karotyetānvaye'vasthāstapoyukto'pi sāmagaḥ ||
(3,3.6) anvayākṛtisaṃpannaṃ tasmādbhṛgvaṅgirovidam | gotrāṅgirasavāsiṣṭhaṃ rājā kuryātpurohitam ||
(3,3.7) makheṣu rāṣṭreṣu pureṣu caiva senāsu rājñāṃ svaniveśaneṣu | ya utpātāstrividhā ghorarūpāstān sarvān śamayedbrahmavedavit ||
(3,3.8) tasmādguruṃ vedarahasyayuktaṃ caturvidhe karmaṇi cāpramattam | śāntaṃ ca dāntaṃ ca jitendriyaṃ ca kuryānnarendraḥ priyadarśanaṃ ca || priyadarśanaṃ ceti ||
iti rājaprathamābhiṣekaḥ samāptaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_4. purohitakarmāṇi)
(4,1.1) omatha purohitakarmāṇi || rājñaḥ prātarutthitasya kṛtasvastyayanasya
(4,1.2) atha purohitaḥ snātānuliptaḥ śuciḥ śuklavāsāḥ soṣṇīṣaḥ savitā prasavānāmiti vyākhyātam ||
(4,1.3) imamindra vardhayetyuktam ||
(4,1.4) pari dhatteti dvābhyāṃ rājño vastramabhimantrya prayacchet ||
(4,1.5) yadābadhnannityalaṃkārān ||
(4,1.6) siṃhe vyāghra iti siṃhāsanam ||
(4,1.7) yaste gandha iti gandhān ||
(4,1.8) ehi jīvaṃ trāyamāṇamityakṣiṇī aṅkte ||
(4,1.9) vātaraṃhā ityaśvam ||
(4,1.10) hastivarcasamiti hastinam ||
(4,1.11) yatte mātā yatte piteti narayānam ||
(4,1.12) khaḍgaṃ cābhimantrayāmīti khaḍgam ||
(4,1.13) khaḍgaṃ cābhimantrayāmi yaḥ śatrūnmardayiṣyati | marditāḥ śatravo'nena vaśamāyāntu te sadeti ||
(4,1.14) paryaṅkamāsanaṃ khaḍgaṃ dhvajaṃ chattraṃ sacāmaram | rathamaśvagajaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dhanurvarma śareṣudhim ||
(4,1.15) āñjanaṃ gandhamālyāni vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca | sarvān śāntyudakenaitānabhyukṣyeccābhimantrayet ||
(4,1.16) dūrvādīnmūrdhni nikṣipya svastyayanairabhimantrayet | abhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivītyabhimantrito brāhmaṇān praṇipatya prāk ||
(4,1.17) yuṣmatprasādācchāntimadhigacchāmīti ||
(4,1.18) tathāstvityukto nirgacchediti ||
(4,1.19) evaṃkṛtyasvastyayano yadevāvalokayati ta sidhyati ||
(4,1.20) tadapi ślokāḥ ||
(4,1.21) asuraiḥ pīḍyamānastu purā śakro jagatprabhuḥ | kārayāmāsa vidhivatpurodhastve bṛhaspatim ||
(4,1.22) sa vṛto bhayabhītena śamanārthaṃ bubhūṣatā | maṅgalāni sasarjāṣṭāvabhayārthaṃ śatakratoḥ ||
(4,1.23) proktāni maṅgalānyaṣṭau brāhmaṇo gaurhutāśanaḥ | bhūmiḥ siddhārthakāḥ sarpiḥ śamī vrīhiyavau tathā ||
(4,1.24) etāni satataṃ puṇyāni saṃpaśyannarcayannapi | na prāpnotyāpadaṃ rājā śriyaṃ prāpnotyanuttamām ||
(4,2.1) atha rājakarmāṇi
(4,2.2) viśvāvasau muhūrte snāto abhiṣekamantrairabhimantritaḥ ||
(4,2.3) anulepanairanuliptaḥ ||
(4,2.4) pūrvoktena vidhinā vastrālaṃkārādibhiḥ
(4,2.5) suvarṇaniṣkaṃ kṛṣṇalaṃ vā vāmahastena saṃgṛhya ||
(4,2.6) yadduḥkṛtaṃ yacchabalaṃ sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ dahatvity
(4,2.7) anena mantreṇa suvarṇaṃ śarīre nighṛṣya dakṣiṇena hastena viprāya dadyād
(4,2.8) dhenuṃ cārogām
(4,2.9) aparimitaguṇān tilān sauvarṇamaye tāmramaye vā pātre sthāpayitvā yadajñānādityabhimantrya viprāya dadyāt ||
(4,2.10) yadajñānāttathā jñānādyanmayā śabalaṃ kṛtam | tatsarvaṃ tiladānena dahyatāmiti hi prabho ||
(4,2.11) bhūmiśca sasyasaṃpannā brāhmaṇe vedapārage | yathāśakti pradeyā hi bṛhaspativaco yathā ||
(4,2.12) sa bhuktvā vividhān bhogān saptasāgaramekhalām | pṛthivīṃ prāpya modeta candravatpṛthivīpatiḥ ||
(4,2.13) annaṃ tu vividhaṃ nityaṃ pradadyātdvijātaye | tūryaghoṣeṇa saṃyuktaḥ kṛtasvastyayanastathā ||
(4,2.14) gṛhadevāṃstu saṃpūjya kāryaścāpyutsavo gṛhe | chattrādīni ca yānāni pūjayedvidhivatsvayam ||
(4,2.15) [puṣpaiśca vividhaiḥ śubhraiḥ phalaiścāpyarcayedbudhaḥ] tasmātsarvāṇi satataṃ dānāni tu mahīpatiḥ | dattvā śraddhānvito viprairvṛto bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ ||
(4,3.1) atha piṣṭamayīṃ rātriṃ caturbhirdīpakaiḥ saha | arcitāṃ gandhamālyena sthāpayettasya cāgrataḥ ||
(4,3.2) namaskṛtvā tato rātrimarcayitvā yathāvidhi | dhūpena cānnapānena stotreṇa ca samarcayet ||
(4,3.3) pāhi māṃ satataṃ devi sarāṣṭraṃ sasuhṛjjanam | uṣase naḥ prayacchasva śāntiṃ ca kṛṇu me sadā | ye tvāṃ prapadyante devi na teṣāṃ vidyate bhayam ||
(4.3.4) rātriṃ prapadye jananīṃ sarvabhūtaniveśanīm | bhadrāṃ bhagavatīṃ kṛṣṇāṃ viśvasya jagato niśām ||
(4,3.5) saṃveśanīṃ saṃyamanīṃ grahanakṣatramālinīm | prapanno'haṃ śivāṃ rātriṃ bhadre pāramaśīmahi ||
(4,3.6) yāṃ sadā sarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca | sāyaṃ prātarnamasyanti sā māṃ rātryabhirakṣatviti ||
(4,4.1) ā rātri pārthivamiṣirā yoṣā trāyamāna ityrātryāṇi ||
(4,4.2) mamobhā mahyamāpa iti sūktābhyāmanvālabhya japet ||
(4,4.3) yo na[[ḥ]] sva iti pañcabhiḥ sarṣapāñjuhuyāt ||
(4,4.4) yo'smin yastvā māturiti dīpena nṛpasyopari triṣparihṛtya praiṣakṛte prayacchet ||
(4,4.5) abhayamityṛcā catasraḥ śarkarāḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ pratidiśaṃ kṣipet ||
(4,4.6) ehyaśmānamā tiṣṭheti pañcamīmadhiṣṭhāpayet ||
(4,4.7) na taṃ yakṣmā aitu deva iti guggulukuṣṭhadūpaṃ dadyāt ||
(4,4.8) yaste gandhastryāyuṣamiti bhūtiṃ prayacchet ||
(4,4.9) dūṣyā dūṣirasīti pratisaramābadhya
(4,4.10) agnirmā pātu vasubhiḥ purastāditi śarkarān pradakṣiṇaṃ pratidiśaṃ kṣipet ||
(4,4.11) bahirniḥsṛtyottareṇa gatvā bāhyenopaniṣkramya suhṛde kuryācchraddadhate kuryāt ||
(4,4.12) naiśamabhayaṃ karma mausalīputraḥ paiṭhīnasiḥ ||
(4,5.1) athāto rātrisūktānāṃ vidhimanukramiṣyāmaḥ ||
(4,5.2) śuciḥ śuklavāsāḥ purohitaḥ ||
(4,5.3) pārthivasya paścimāṃ saṃdhyāmupāsya darbhaiḥ pavitrapāṇī rājānamabhigamya
(4,5.4) piṣṭamayīṃ rātriṃ kṛtvā ||
(4,5.5) annapānadhūpadīpairarcayitvā mālyaiśca ||
(4,5.6) prajvalitaiścaturbhirdīpakairarcayitvā ||
(4,5.7) ā rātri pārthivamiṣirā yoṣeti sūktadvayena rātrimupasthāya ||
(4,5.8) trāyamāṇe viśvajite ahne ca tveti rājānaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ triḥ kṛtvā
(4,5.9) rājaveśmani dvāre visarjayitvā ||
(4,5.10) na taṃ yakṣmā aitu deva iti guggulukuṣṭhadhūpaṃ dadyād
(4,5.11) yaste gandha iti bhūtimabhimantrya
(4,5.12) tryāyuṣamiti rājñe rakṣāṃ kṛtvā ||
(4,5.13) asapatnamiti śarkarāmabhimantryāṅguṣṭhātpradakṣiṇaṃ pratidiśaṃ kṣipet ||
(4,5.14) śāntā dyauriti japitvā rājānaṃ vāsagṛhaṃ nayet ||
(4,5.15) bhūtinā rakṣāṃ kṛtvā niṣkramya
(4,5.16) evamevamaharahaḥ kuryāt ||
(4,6.1) yasya rājño janapade atharvā śāntipāragaḥ | nivasatyapi tadrāṣṭraṃ vardhate nirupadravam ||
(4,6.2) yasya rājño janapade sa nāsti vividhairbhayaiḥ | pīḍyate tasya tadrāṣṭraṃ paṅke gauriva majjati ||
(4,6.3) tasmādrājā viśeṣeṇa atharvāṇaṃ jitendriyam | dānasaṃmānasatkārairnityaṃ samabhipūjayet ||
(4,6.4) nityaṃ ca kārayecchāntiṃ grahaṛkṣāṇi pūjayet | bhūmidohān prakurvīta devatāyataneṣu ca ||
(4,6.5) catuspatheṣu goṣṭheṣu tīrtheṣvapsu ca kārayet | gotarpaṇaṃ ca vidhivatsarvadoṣavināśanam ||
(4,6.6) ya evaṃ kārayedrājā sarvakālaṃ jitendriyaḥ | anantaṃ sukhamāpanoti kṛtsnāṃ bhuṅkte vasuṃdharām ||
iti purohitakarmāṇi samāptāni ||

(pariśiṣṭa_5. puṣyābhiṣekaḥ)
(5,1.1) omatha puṣyābhiṣekasya vidhiṃ vakṣyāmi sāṃpadam | dharmārthakāmasaṃyuktaṃ rājā kuryātpurohitam ||
(5,1.2) sauvarṇarājataistāmraiḥ kalaśaiḥ pārthivairapi | sahasreṇa śatenātha toyagrahaṇamiṣyate ||
(5,1.3) caturṇāṃ sāgarāṇāṃ tu nadīnāṃ ca śatasya tu | abhiṣekāya rājñastu toyamāhṛtya yatnataḥ ||
(5,1.4) ekadvitricaturṇāṃ vā sāgarasya tu pañcamam | oṣadhīsteṣu sarveṣu kalaśeṣūpakalpayet ||
(5,1.5) sahā ca dahadevī ca balā cātibalā tathā | madayantī vacā śvetā vyāghradantī sumaṅgalā ||
(5,2.1) śatāvarī jayantī ca śatapuṣpā sacandanā | priyaṅgū rocano aśīramamṛtā ca sasārikā ||
(5,2.2) aśvatthaplakṣabilvānāṃ nyagrodhapanasasya ca | śirīṣāmrakapitthānāṃ pallavaiḥ samalaṃkṛtān ||
(5,2.3) hemaratnauṣadhībilvapuṣpagandhādhivāsitān | ācchāditān sitairvastrairabhimantrya purohitaḥ ||
(5,2.4) sāvitryubhayataḥ kuryācchaṃ no devī tathaiva ca | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ sūktaṃ ca anuvākādyameva ca ||
(5,2.5) dharaṇī pādapīṭhaṃ syāddūrvāmūlāṅkurān śubhān | tasyopari nyasetpīṭhaṃ haimaṃ raupyamathāpi vā ||
(5,3.1) anaḍudvyāghrasiṃhānāṃ mṛgasya ca yathākramam | catvāri carmāṇyetāni pūrvādārabhya vinyaset ||
(5,3.2) cāturhotravidhānena juhuyācca purohitaḥ | caturdikṣu sthitairviprairvedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ ||
(5,3.3) bilvāhāraḥ phalāhāraḥ payasā vāpi vartayet | saptarātraṃ ghṛtāśī va tato homaṃ prayojayet ||
(5,3.4) gavyena payasā kuryātsauvarṇena sruveṇa tu | vedānāmādibhirmantrairmahāvyāhṛtipūrvakaiḥ ||
(5,3.5) śarmavarmā gaṇaścaiva tathā syādaparājitaḥ | āyuṣyaścābhayaścaiva tathā svastyayano gaṇaḥ ||
(5,4.1) etān pañca gaṇān hutvā vācayeta dvijottamān | hiraṇyenākṣatārgheṇa phalaiśca madhusarpiṣā ||
(5,4.2) puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvāsya ārambhaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ | tiṣyanakṣatrasaṃyukte mahūrte karaṇe śubhe ||
(5,4.3) uccairghoṣa iti tūryāṇyabhimantrya purohitaḥ | sarvatūryaninādena abhiṣikto hyalaṃkṛtaḥ ||
(5,4.4) siṃhāsanaṃ samāruhya pīṭhikāṃ vā yathākramam | cāmarachattrasaṃyuktaṃ pratihāravibhūṣitam ||
(5,4.5) mattadvipacatuṣkaṃ ca caturdikṣu prakalpayet | upaviṣṭastato rājā prajānāṃ kārayeddhitam | akarā brāhmaṇā gāvaḥ strībālajaḍarogiṇaḥ ||
(5,5.1) tatastu darśanaṃ deyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ nṛpeṇa tu | śreṇīprakṛtimukhyānāṃ strījanaṃ ca namaskaret ||
(5,5.2) āśiṣaste hi dāsyanti tuṣṭā janapadā bhuvi | evaṃ prajānurajyeta pṛthivī ca vaśā bhavet ||
(5,5.3) purohitaṃ mantriṇaṃ ca senādhyakṣaṃ tathaiva ca | aśvādhyakṣaṃ gajādhyakṣaṃ koṣṭhāgārapatiṃ tathā ||
(5,5.4) bhāṇḍāgārapatiṃ vaidyaṃ daivajñaṃ ca yathākramam | yathārheṇa ca yogena sarvān saṃpūjayennṛpaḥ ||
(5,5.5) rājyaṃ purohite nyasya śeṣāṇāṃ ca yathākramam | sthānāntarāṇi cānyāni dattvā sukhamavāpnuyāt ||
(5,5.6) dūrvāsiddhārthakān sarpiḥ śamīrvrīhiyavau tathā śuklāni caiva puṣpāṇi mūrdhni dadyātpurohitaḥ ||
(5,5.7) atharvavihito hyeṣa vidhiḥ puṣyābhiṣecane | rājā snāto mahīṃ bhuṅkte śakralokaṃ sa gacchati ||
iti puṣyābhiṣekaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_6. piṣṭarātryāḥ kalpaḥ)
(6,1.1) omathātaḥ piṣṭarātryāḥ kalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(6,1.2) ahatavāsāḥ purastāttalpasya gomayena sthaṇḍilamupalipya
(6,1.3) ahatavastreṇa siṃhāsanamavachādya ||
(6,1.4) yāṃ devāḥ prati nandantīti rātrimāvāhayet ||
(6,1.5) saṃvatsarasya pratimāmiti piṣṭamayīṃ pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvodanmukhīmupaveśayet ||
(6,1.6) chattraṃ hiraṇmayaṃ dadyādāsanaṃ ca hiraṇmayam | dadyācchubhrāṇi vāsāṃsi śubhraṃ caivānulepanam ||
(6,1.7) śubhramannamatathā dadyātprabhūtāṃścaiva modakān | dhūpaṃ ca vividhaṃ nityaṃ pradīpāṃśca prakalpayet ||
(6,1.8) ā mā puṣṭe ca poṣe cetyetābhirupasthāya ||
(6,1.9) rakṣoghnairmantraiḥ sarṣapānabhimantrya
(6,1.10) āvatasta iti japan samantāttalpasyāvakīrya
(6,1.11) ayaṃ pratisara iti pratisaramābadhya śarkarān pratidiśaṃ kṣipet ||
(6,1.12) dhūpaśeṣaṃ rājñe dadyād ||
(6,1.13) abhayapradaṃ karma ||
(6,1.14) paścātsarveṣu koneṣu dvimukhīmekamukhīṃ vā | sarvato vijayāṃ rakṣāmekāṃ vā teṣu caturmukhīm ||
(6,1.15) ekamukhīṃ vā sarvatrāpratirathajapa ityeke
(6,1.16) sarvatra śarkarākṣepaśceti ||
(6,2.1) gṛhītvā piṣṭarātriṃ tu veśmadvāre visarjayet
(6.2.2) vanaspatiraso madhya iti guggulukuṣṭhadhūpaṃ dadyād
(6,2.3) yaste gandha iti bhūtimabhimantrya
(6,2.4) tryāyuṣamiti rājñe rakṣāṃ kṛtvā
(6,2.5) asapatnamiti śarkarānabhimantryāṅguṣṭhād[abhi]pradakṣiṇaṃ pratidiśaṃ kṣipet ||
(6,2.6) śāntā dyauriti japitvā rājānaṃ vāsagṛhaṃ nayet ||
(6,2.7) bhūtinā rakṣāṃ kṛtvā niṣkramya
(6,2.8) evamevamaharahaḥ kuryāditi || piṣṭarātryāḥ kalpaḥ samāptaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_7. ārātrikam)
(7,1.1) oṃ na suṣvāpa purā śakro dānavānāṃ purodhasā | prayuktairauṣadhairyogairmantrāṇāṃ japahomataḥ ||
(7,1.2) praṇipatya bṛhaspatimatharvāṇaṃ puraṃdaraḥ | dānavaiḥ paribhūto'haṃ trāhi māmityuvāca ha ||
(7,1.3) tato'sāvevamuktastu prabhūtabalavardhanam | ārogyadaṃ bhūtikaraṃ kṣudropadravanāśanam ||
(7,1.4) ārātrikaṃ hi kartavyaṃ tasya trātumidaṃ tadā | kṛtvā piṣṭamayaṃ dīpaṃ suvartisnehasaṃyutam ||
(7,1.5) ati nihaḥ prānyāniti dvābhyāmenaṃ pradīpayet | pātre sapuṣpe saṃsthāpya sarṣapāṃśca sahākṣataiḥ ||
(7,1.6) priyaṅguṃ śatapuṣpāṃ ca dūrvāṃ caiva śatāvarīm | sapāpahāriṇīṃ bhūtiṃ tatraiva ca baliṃ nyaset ||
(7,1.7) apsarobhiḥ parivṛto gururgatvā puraṃdaram | prāptasattvaṃ sumanasamāsane prānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
(7,1.8) preto yantvekaśataṃ ca dīpaṃ samabhimantrayet | triḥ paribhrāmayedrājño mantreṇātha sumaṅgalam ||
(7,1.9) śāmyantyasya tato rogā grahā vighnavināyakāḥ | svastyastu nrparāṣṭrāya svasti gobrāhmaṇāya ca ||
(7,1.10) tatastu śaṅkhadhvaninā dīpaṃ gṛhītvā satoyadhārāṃ prayato'pi nirharet | purohito jyotiṣiko'pi vā svayaṃ hitaiṣinī dhātryatharvo (pakāritā ||
(7,1.11) ācāmyātha ha rājānamācamyādau purohitaḥ | brāhmaṇāya yathāśakti rukmaṃ prātaḥ pradāpayet ||
(7,1.12) mūlakarmādikaṃ tasya pañcaguhyakakāritam | bhayaṃ rājño na bhavati tejo vīryaṃ ca vardhate ||
(7,1.13) evaṃ vidhānamakhilaṃ vihitaṃ yathāvadetatsamastaśubhadaṃ gaditaṃ nrpāṇām | naivāpadaḥ samupayānti nṛpaṃ kadā cidārātrikaṃ pratiniśaṃ kriyate tu yasya || ityārātrikaṃ samāptam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_8. ghṛtāvekṣaṇam)
(8,1.1) omatha ghrtāvekṣaṇaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ ||
(8,1.2) prātaḥprātaḥ śaṅkhadundubhinādena brahmaghoṣeṇa vā prabodhito rājā śayanagṛhādutthāyāparājitāṃ diśamabhiniṣkramyopādhyāyaṃ pratīkṣeta ||
(8,1.3) atha purohitaḥ snātānuliptaḥ śuciḥ śuklavāsāḥ krtamaṅgalaviracitoṣṇīṣī śāntigṛhaṃ praviśya devānāṃ namaskāraṃ kṛtvā svastivācanamanujñāpya vinītavadupaviśet ||
(8,1.4) yamasya lokādyathā kalāṃ yo na jīvo'sīti svastyayanaṃ kṛtvollikhyābhyukṣya paristīrya śantātīyena tilān ghṛtāktān juhuyāt ||
(8,1.5) tān hutvā sauvarṇarājatamaudumbaraṃ vā pātraṃ ghṛtapūrṇaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ ghṛtasya jūtiḥ sahasraśṛṅgo yamasya lokāduru viṣṇo vi kramasvetyabhimantryājyaṃ teja iti tadālabhate ||
(8,1.6) ājyaṃ tejaḥ samuddiṣṭamājyaṃ pāpaharaṃ param | ājyena devāstrpyanti ājye lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||
(8,1.7) bhaumāntarikṣadivyaṃ vā yatte kalmaṣamāgatam | sarvaṃ tadājyasaṃsparśātpraṇāśamupagacchatviti ||
(8,1.8) tasmin ghṛtapātrasthaṃ hi sarvamātmānaṃ ca paśyet ||
(8,1.9) dadhnā śiro hṛdayamanvālabhya japet ||
(8,1.10) uccā patantamiti dvābhyām
(8,1.11) sūryasyāvṛtamiti pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya śeṣaṃ kārayedity
(8,1.12) atra ślokāḥ ||
(8,2.1) ayaṃ ghṛtāvekṣaṇasya prokto vidhiratharvaṇā | upāsyo nityakālaṃ tu rājñā vijayakāṅkṣiṇā ||
(8,2.2) etatsamāharetsarvaṃ prayatastu samāhitaḥ | rājā vijayate rāṣṭraṃ naśyante tasya śatravaḥ ||
(8,2.3) dvijottamāya kapilāṃ rājā dadyāttu gāṃ śubhām | āśīrvādaṃ tatastena śrutvā tanmukhaniḥsṛtam ||
(8,2.4) guruṇā vācito yasmāddīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt | putrān pautrāṃśca maitrāṃśca labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||
(8,2.5) āyuṣyamatha varcasyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ śatrutāpanam | duḥsvapnanāśanaṃ puṇyaṃ ghṛtāsyāvekṣaṇaṃ smṛtamiti || iti ghṛtāvekṣaṇaṃ samāptam || śrīsāmba | yamasya lokādadhyābabhūvitha iti ṛcaḥ 6, yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphamiti ṛcaḥ 6, ghṛtasya jūtiriti ṛcaḥ 4 ghṛtāvekṣaṇamadhye paippalādā mantrāḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_9. tiladhenuvidhiḥ)
(9,1.1) omatha tiladhenuṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpapraṇāśanīm | tilāḥ śvetāstilāḥ kṛṣṇāstilā gomūtravarṇakāḥ ||
(9,1.2) tilānāṃ tu vicitrāṇāṃ dhenuṃ vatsaṃ ca kārayet | droṇasya vatsakaṃ kuryāccaturdroṇā tu gauḥ smṛtā ||
(9,1.3) suvāsāyāṃ śucau bhūmau dhūpapuṣpairalaṃkrtā | kṛṣṇājine tu kartavyā bahūnāṃ vāpi kārayet ||
(9,1.4) karṇau ratnamayau kuryāccakṣuṣī dīpakau tathā | ghrāṇe tu sarvagndhāṃstu jihvāyāṃ śāradaṃ ghṛtam ||
(9,1.5) danteṣu mauktikaṃ dadyāllalāṭe tāmrabhājanam | ūdhasi tu madhūśīramapāne ca ghṛtaṃ madhu ||
(9,2.1) hṛdaye candanaṃ dadyājjaṅghayorikṣukāṇḍakam | suvarṇaśṛṅgī raupyakhurī raupyalāṅgūladakṣiṇā ||
(9,2.2) vastrachannā tu dātavyā kāṃsyapātraṃ tu dohanī |
(9,2.3) prajāpatiścetyabhimantrya viprāya dadyāt
(9,2.4) tatra ślokāḥ ||
(9,2.5) dhenuṃ vatsaṃ ca yo dadyādvinātharvābhimantritām | vinānena vidhānena devatvaṃ nopajāyate ||
(9,2.6) viśvarūpāḥ sthitāḥ sarvā dhenavaḥ parikīrtitāḥ | dhenutvaṃ na sa prayāti vinā sūktābhimantritām | niṣphalaṃ naśyate sarvaṃ vinābhimantritaṃ satām ||
(9,2.7) bālatve yacca kaumāre yatpāpaṃ yauvane kṛtam | vayaḥpariṇatau yacca yacca janmāntareṣu ca ||
(9,2.8) yanniśāyāṃ tathā prātaryanmadhyāhnāparāhṇayoḥ | saṃdhyayoryatkṛtaṃ pāpaṃ karmaṇā manasā girā ||
(9,3.1) prasūyamānāṃ yo dhenuṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇapuṃgave | kṛṣṇājinaṃ guḍadhenuṃ ghṛtadhenuṃ tathaiva ca ||
(9,3.2) suvarṇaratnadhenuṃ ca jaladhenuṃ tathā parām | kṣīradhenuṃ madhudhenuṃ śarkarālavaṇaṃ tathā ||
(9,3.3) rasādidhenūḥ sarvānyā anena vidhinā smṛtāḥ | yattu bālye kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yauvane caiva yatkṛtam ||
(9,3.4) mānakūṭaṃ tulākūṭaṃ kanyānṛtagavānṛtam | udake ṣṭhīvitaṃ caiva musalaṃ cāpi laṅghitam ||
(9,3.5) vṛṣalīgamanaṃ caiva gurudārāniṣevaṇam | surāpānasya yatpāpaṃ tiladhenuḥ praśāmyati ||
(9,4.1) yā sa yamapure ghore nadī vaitaraṇī smṛtā | yatra lohamukhāḥ kākāḥ śvānaścaiva bhayāvahāḥ ||
(9,4.2) vālukāntāḥ sthalāścaiva pacyante yatra duṣkṛtaḥ | asipattravanaṃ yatra śālūkāḥ śālmalī tathā ||
(9,4.3) tān sukhena vyatikramya dharmarājāśramaṃ vrajet | svāgataṃ te mahābhāga svasti te'stu mahāmate ||
(9,4.4) vimānametadyogyaṃ te maṇiratnavibhūṣitam | atrāruhya naraśreṣṭha gaccha tvaṃ paramāṃ gatim ||
(9,4.5) mā ca cārabhaṭe dadyānmā ca dadyātpurohite | mā ca kāṇe virūpe ca kuṣṭhivyaṅge tathaiva ca ||
(9,4.6) vedāntagāya dātavyā vedāntagasutāya vā | ekaikasmai ca dātavyā māghamāse tu pūrṇimām ||
(9,4.7) ya imāṃ paṭhate nityaṃ yaścemāṃ śṛṇuyādapi | devalokamatikramya sūryalokaṃ sa gacchati || sūryalokaṃ sa gacchatīti || iti tiladhenuvidhiḥ samāptaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_10. bhūmidānam)
(10,1.1) omatha rohiṇyāṃ sakalāyāmupoṣito brahmā sarvabījarasaratnagandhāvakīrṇaṃ tīrthodakapūrṇakalaśamādāyātisṛṣṭo apāmityabhiṣekamantrairyathoktairdātāramabhiṣiñcati ||
(10,1.2) vratena tvaṃ vratapata iti vratamupaiti
(10,1.3) yācitāradātārāvayācitāśināvadhahśāyinau bhavato
(10,1.4) vratopacāram
(10,1.5) yathāśaktyaikarātraṃ pañcarātraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ vā vrataṃ caritvā
(10,1.6) śvo bhūte tantramājyabhāgāntaṃ kṛtvānvārabhyātha juhuyāt ||
(10,1.7) kāmasūktaṃ kālasūktaṃ puruṣasūktaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ saṃkhyāpūrvikābhiḥ sarva ṛtvijo juhvaty
(10,1.8) atha suvarṇamayīṃ bhūmiṃ bhūmeḥ pratikṛtiṃ gocarmamātrāṃ kṛtvā
(10,1.9) maṇḍapavedyāṃ samānīya vedyuttarato yasyāṃ vedimityupasthāpya
(10,1.10) girayaste parvatā iti parvatānavasthāpya
(10,1.11) hiraṇyarajatamaṇimuktāpravālādibhirupaśobhayedyadadaḥ saṃprayatīriti
(10,1.12) sā mandasāneti nadīḥ kalpayitvā rasaiśca paripūrayed
(10,1.13) apāmagramasi samudraṃ vo'bhyavasṛjāmīti samudrān
(10,1.14) vanaspatiḥ saha devairna āganniti bṛhaspatineti vanaspatīnanyāṃśca
(10,1.15) yajñe tvā manasā saṃkalpayenmanasā saṃkalpayatītha bhavatīha bhavatīha bhavati
(10,1.16) nidhiṃ bibhratī bahudheti namaskārayitvā
(10,1.17) satyaṃ bṛhadityanuvāko ye devā divyekādaśa stheti puṇyāhaṃ vācayet
(10,1.18) saṃsthāpayen
(10,1.19) nava divo devajanenetyabhimantrya brāhmaṇebhyo dadyād
(10,1.20) dāturesāsmai rohiṇī kāmaṃ nikāmaṃ vā dugdha iti
(10,1.21) yathā rohanti bījāni phālakṛṣṭe mahītale evaṃ kāmāḥ prarohante pretyeha manasaḥ sadā
(10,1.22) sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ yatphalaṃ samudāhṛtam | tattatprāpnoti viprebhyo dattvā bhūmiṃ yathāvidhi || dattvā bhūmiṃ yathāvidhīti || iti bhūmidānaṃ samāptam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_11. tulāpuruṣavidhiḥ)
(11,1.1) omathātastulāpuruṣavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmas
(11,1.2) tadudagayana āpūryamāṇapakṣe puṇye nakṣatre śraddhāpreritau grahaṇakāle vā
(11,1.3) ṛtvigyajamānau kḷptakeśaśmaśrū romanakhāni vāpayitvā
(11,1.4) saṃbhārānupakalpya prāktantramājyabhāgāntaṃ kṛtvā
(11,1.5) mahāvyāhṛtisāvitrīśāntiṃ brahma jajñānamiti hutvā
(11,1.6) agne gobhiragne'bhyāvartinnagneḥ prajātamiti saṃpātānudapātrānīyābhiṣekakalaśeṣu ninayed
(11,1.7) athāsyendro grāvabhyāmityabhiṣecayed
(11,1.8) idamāpo yathendro bāhubhyāmityabhiṣecayitvā
(11,1.9) yathoktamañjanābhyañjanānulepanaṃ kārayitvā vāso gandhasrajaścābadhnīyāt
(11,1.10) tulāṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca pavitrairabhyukṣya puruṣasaṃmito'rtha iti saptabhistadārohayed
(11,1.11) acyutā dyauriti catasṛbhiravarohayet
(11,1.12) sūryasyāvṛtamiti pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayitvātmālaṃkārān kartre dadyāt
(11,1.13) sahasradakṣiṇaṃ grāmavaram
(11,1.14) dvijānannena tarpayet
(11,1.15) atha cenniḥsvapakṣeṇa yathā saṃpadyate dhanam | dhātubhiḥ saha taulyamatu vāsobhiśca rasaistathā | vrīhyādisaptadhānyairvā yathāsaṃpadyate gṛhe ||
(11,2.1) sakhaḍgaḥ saśirastrāṇaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ | tapanīyamagare kṛtvā paścāttolyo narādhipaḥ ||
(11,2.2) indreṇedaṃ purā dattamadhirājyāptaye varam | sarvapāpapraṇāśāya sarvapuṇyavivṛddhaye ||
(11,2.3) mahādānātidānānāmidaṃ dānamanuttamam | akṣayyaphaladaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dātṝṇāṃ śreyavardhanam ||
(11,2.4) yatpāpaṃ sve kule jātaistriḥ sapta puruṣaiḥ kṛtam | tatsarvaṃ naśyate kṣipramagnau tūlaṃ yathā tathā ||
(11,2.5) anāmayaṃ sthānamavāpya daivairalaṅghanīyaṃ sukṛtairhiraṇmayaiḥ | suvarṇatejāḥ pravimuktapāpo divīndravadrājati sūryaloke || divīndravadrājati sūryaleketi || iti tulāpuruṣavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_12. ādityamaṇḍakaḥ)
(12,1.1) omatha yaḥ kāmayeta sarveṣāṃ nṛṇāmuttamaḥ syāmiti sa bhāskarāyāpūpaṃ dadyāt
(12,1.2) tasya kalpo
(12,1.3) yavagodhūmānāmanyatamacūrṇena maṇḍalākṛtiṃ saṃśrapya
(12,1.4) pātre kṛtvāpihitam
(12,1.5) saguḍājyasuvarṇaśakalaṃ cvopariṣṭānnidhāyārcayedraktakusumair
(12,1.6) viṣāsahimityabhimantrya brāhmaṇāya nivedayet
(12,1.7) tatra ślokāḥ
(12,1.8) anena vidhinā yastu pūpaṃ dadyāddvijātaye | prayacchetsatataṃ prājñastasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu ||
(12,1.9) ārogyavān varcasvī ca prajāvān paśumāṃstathā | dhanavānannavān śrīmāṃstathā sarvajanapriyaḥ ||
(12,1.10) apamṛtyuśataṃ sāgraṃ nāśayatyavicārataḥ | pradattaṃ sūryalokaṃ ca prāpayetparamaṃ padam || prāpayetparamaṃ padamiti || ityādityamaṇḍakaḥ samāptaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_13. hiraṇyagarbhavidhiḥ)
(13,1.1) omatha hiraṇyagarbhavidhimanukramiṣyāmaḥ sarvapāpāpanodanam
(13,1.2) udagayana āpūryamāṇapakṣe puṇye nakṣatre śraddhāpreritau grahaṇakāle vā
(13,1.3) ṛtvigyajamānau kḷptakeśaśmaśruromanakhau syātām
(13,1.4) atha ṛtvikpragastaṃgamanādaśvatthādaraṇī ādhāyāgne jāyasveti dvābhyāṃ mathyamānamanumantrayate
(13,1.5) tṛtīyayā jātaṃ caturthyopasamādadhāti
(13,1.6) śucau deśe paridhāpya yathoktamañjanābhyañjanānulepanaṃ kārayitvā
(13,1.7) agneḥ prajātaṃ pari yaddhiraṇyaṃ yadābadhnanniti hiraṇyasrajamāgrathya rakṣantu tveti rakṣāṃ kṛtvā
(13,1.8) darbhānāstīryādhahśāyinau syātām
(13,1.9) śvo bhūte'bhijinmuhūrte hiraṇmayaṃ maṇḍalākṛti nābhimātraṃ pātramādhāya sāpidhānam
(13,1.10) sruksruvājyasthālyudapātrālaṃkārāṃśca
(13,1.11) parisamuhya paryukṣya paristīrya barhirudapātramupasādya paricaraṇenājyaṃ paricarya nityān purastāddhomān hutvājyabhāgau cātha juhoti
(13,2.1) hiraṇyagarbhāya svāhā || agnaye svāhā || brahmaṇe svāhā || prajāpataye svāheti hutvā
(13,2.2) taireva namaskāraṃ kārayitvā
(13,2.3) tairevopasthāya sruksruvājyasthālyudapātrālaṃkārāṃśceti
(13,2.4) hiraṇmaye rājānaṃ hiraṇyavatībhiḥ snāpayitvā
(13,2.5) hiraṇyakalaśaistasmin pañcagavyamapa āsicya
(13,2.6) hiraṇyagarbhasūktenāṃhomucena śantātīyena pañcabhiśca nāmabhiḥ saṃpātānānīya
(13,2.7) tathaiva sadasyānasadasyānṛtvijo'nujñāpayed
(13,2.8) rājā hiraṇyagarbhatvamabhīpsatyasmin bhavanto'numanyantāmiti
(13,2.9) tairanuṣṭhitaḥ
(13,3.1) udehi vājinniti dvābhyāṃ praveśayet
(13,3.2) trayastriṃśaddevatā ityabhisaṃdhāya tamanuśāsti
(13,3.3) vācaṃ niyamya pratisaṃhṛtya cendriyāṇi viṣayebhyo manasā bhagavantaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ hiraṇyagarbhaṃ parameṣṭhinaṃ pauruṣaṃ dhyāyasveti
(13,3.4) tatheti tatpratipadyate
(13,3.5) sa saptadaśamātrāntaramasti
(13,3.6) saptadaśo vai prajāpatiḥ
(13,3.7) prajāpaterāvṛta iti vijñāpayet
(13,3.8) tathaiva sadasyānasadasyānanujñāpyotthāpayed
(13,3.9) utthitaṃ hiraṇmayena cakreṇābhinyubjayet
(13,3.10) mā te prāṇa ityuddhared
(13,3.11) uddhṛtaṃ hiraṇyanāmno pravimucya yastvā mṛtyurityapāsyet
(13,3.12) saṃpaśyamānā ityavekṣito brāhmaṇān praṇipatya namaskārya namo hiraṇyagarbhāyety
(13,3.13) atha ha vai hiraṇyagarbhasyeti priyatamāya tata uktam
(13,3.14) brāhmaṇā brūyuruttiṣṭha hiraṇyagarbhānugṛhīto (sīty
(13,3.15) apratirathena hutvā saṃsthāpayed
(13,3.16) apsu te rājan varuṇeti varuṇamabhiṣṭūya snātvā pavitraiḥ pratyetyādityamupatiṣṭhante
(13,4.1) hiraṇyaṃ tava yadgarbho hiraṇyasyāpi garbhajaḥ | hiraṇyagarbhastasmāttvaṃ pāhi māṃ mahato mahāniti
(13,4.2) sūryasyāvṛtamiti pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya
(13,4.3) gṛhānaimīti gṛhān pratipadya
(13,4.4) tvamagne pramatirityagnimupasthāpyāthotsṛjed
(13,4.5) atha dvijebhyo dakṣiṇāṃ daśa sahasrāṇdadyāt
(13,4.6) grāmavaraṃ ca
(13,4.7) sruksruvājyasthālyudapātrālaṃkārāṃścetyanyatsarvaṃ sadasyebhyo
(13,4.8) yāvadvā tuṣyeraṃśtāvadvā deyam || rājā dadyād
(13,4.9) yathākāmaṃ brāhmaṇānannena paricaret
(13,4.10) tatra ślokāḥ
(13,5.1) vedānāṃ pāragā yasya caturṇāṃ brahmavittamāḥ | tuṣṭā yasyāśiṣo brūyustasya yajñaphalaṃ bhavet ||
(13,5.2) brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena sūryo divi virājati | indro'pyeṣāṃ prasādena devānativirājati ||
(13,5.3) hiraṇyadānasya phalamamṛtatvamiti śrutiḥ | śrūyate hyasya dātā yaḥ so'mṛtatvaṃ samaśnute ||
(13,5.4) rājekṣuvākuprabhrtayaḥ purā rājarṣayo'malāḥ | dattvā hiraṇyaṃ viprebhyo jyotirbhūtvā divi sthitāḥ ||
(13,5.5) ya evaṃ saṃskṛto rājā vidhinā brahmavādinā | prajānāmiha sāmrājyaṃ jyaiṣṭhyaṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ ca gacchati ||
(13,5.6) amuṣmin brahmaṇā sārdhamānandamanubhūya vai | jyotirmayaṃ satyalokaṃ nacaivāvartate punaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_14. hastirathadānavidhiḥ)
(14,1.1) omatha hastirathadānānāmanukramaṃ vakṣye
(14,1.2) jātarūpamayaṃ krtvā ekacakraṃ suśobhanam | hastibhiḥ saptabhiryuktamarcayitvā yathāvidhi ||
(14,1.3) atha vā caturbhiryuktaṃ haimaṃ rājatameva vā | aspṛṣṭaṃ dārujaṃ vāpi sarvasaṃbhārapūritam ||
(14,1.4) hastiyugmena saṃyuktaṃ saurabheyayutena vā | bhuṅkte saptaiva janmāni saptadvīpāṃ vasuṃdharām ||
(14,1.5) hastena yukte candramasi paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāyāṃ vā puṇye vā ṛkṣe śuciḥ śucau deśe tantramityuktam ||
(14,1.6) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāyānvārabhyātha juhuyāt ||
(14,1.7) savitre svāhā || pataṃgāya svāhā || pāvakāya svāhā || sahasraraśmaye svāhā || mārtaṇḍāya svāhā || viṣṇave svāhā || prajāpataye svāhā || parameṣṭhine svāhā iti hutvā.
(14,1.8) kanakāśca tilā gāvo dāsī gṛhamahīrathāḥ | kanyā hastī ca vidyā ca mahādāni vai daśa ||
(14,1.9) tasmātsarveṣu dāneṣu anuktavidhikeṣu ca | agniṃ brūma iti sūktamājyatantreṇa homayet ||
(14,1.10) aśvadātā vrajetsvargamaśvārūḍhaśca mānavaḥ | pūjyate devagandharvairapsarokiṃnaraistathā ||
(14,1.11) hastivarcasaṃ prathatāmiti kalaśe saṃpātānānīya yugaṃ yoktraṃ rathamiti sarvaṃ saṃprokṣya ||
(14,1.12) aśrāntasya tvā manasā yunajmīti yojayet ||
(14,1.13) aśrāntasya tvā manasā yunajmi prathamasya ca | utkūlamudvaho bhavo uduhya prati dhāvatāt ||
(14,1.14) yuktāyārthaṃ dadyāt ||
(14,1.15) śyāvairyuktaḥ śitipadbhirhiraṇyayo yasya rathaḥ pathibhirvartate sukhaiḥ | sa no hastena savitā hiraṇyabhugghiraṇyapāṇiḥ savitā no'bhirakṣatu ||
(14,1.16) bṛhaddhastirathaṃ yuktaṃ hastena tu dadannaraḥ | savituḥ sthānamāpnoti divyāṃ kāmajavāṃ sabhām ||

(pariśiṣṭa_15. aśvarathadānavidhiḥ)
(15,1.1) omathāśvarathadānavidhiḥ.
(15,1.2) goṣṭha udakānte śucau vā deśe prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāyānvārabhyātha juhuyāt ||
(15,1.3) vataraṃhā bhava vājin yujyamāna ityetenāśveṣu saṃpātānānīyāśrāntasya tveti samānam
(15,1.4) tvamindrastvaṃ mahendra iti savitre arghaṃ dattvā
(15,1.5) punantu metyātmānamālabhya japed
(15,1.6) rakṣantu tvāgnaya iti yajamānamabhimantrya samṛddhihomānte
(15,1.7) varāṃ dhenuṃ kartre dadyādaśvarathaṃ grāmavaraṃ ceti
(15,1.8) ya evaṃ vidhinā dadyādviduṣe aśvarathaṃ sudhīḥ | jyaiṣṭhyaṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ ca sāmrājyaṃ prajānāmiha gacchati ||
(15,1.9) saptānāṃ lokānāmante jyotirlokamanāmayam | gatvā sa paramānandaṃ bhuṅkte yāvadvibhāvasuḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_16. gosahasravidhiḥ)
(16,1.1) omathāto gosahasravidhiḥ ||
(16,1.2) goṣṭha udakānte śucau vā deśe prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāyānvālabhyātha juhuyāt ||
(16,1.3) ā gāva iti sūktenājyaṃ juhuyāt ||
(16,1.4) mahāvrīhīṇāmaindraṃ caruṃ saumyaṃ ca sahasratamyāḥ payasi śrapayitvā gāva eva surabhava ityetena juhuyāt ||
(16,1.5) pañcānāṃ nadīnāṃ tīrthodakam ||
(16,1.6) paścādagnestīrthodakena pūrṇaṃ kalaśamavasthāpya hiraṇyavarṇā ityabhimantrya saṃ vo goṣṭheneti daśa gāḥ snāpayet ||
(16,1.7) tvaramānanayāḥ samabhyukṣya sahasratamyāḥ sānodanekemamindra vardhaya kṣatriyaṃ ma iti rājānamabhiṣicya ||
(16,1.8) imā āpa iti ṣaḍbhiryothoktamañjanābhyañjanānulepanaṃ kṛtvā ||
(16,1.9) sahasratamīṃ prathamāmalaṃkṛtya ||
(16,1.10) ā gāvo māmupatiṣṭhantāmityupatiṣṭhet ||
(16,1.11) prajāvatīḥ sūyavasāditi ca sarvāḥ pāyayet ||
(16,1.12) priyamaśanaṃ dattvāddhi tṛṇamaghnya iti sahasratamīmālabhya japet ||
(16,1.13) mayā gāvo gopatinā sacadhvamiti mantrānetnārghaṃ dattvā ||
(16,1.14) sahasratamyāḥ puchamaupasaṃgṛhya bhūmiṣṭvā pratigṛhṇātviti japan ||
(16,1.15) sahasratamyāḥ pṛṣṭhato vrajan ||
(16,1.16) sarvāḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskṛtya svastivācya brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya daśa gā dakṣiṇāṃ kartre dadyātsahasratamīṃ vastrayugmamaca ||
(16,1.17) tadapi ślokāḥ ||
(16,2.1) saptājanmānugaṃ pāpaṃ puruṣaiḥ saptabhiḥ krtam | tatkṣaṇādvidhinānena nāśayedgoprado naraḥ ||
(16,2.2) sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ phalaṃ yatparikīrtitam | tadavāpnoti virebhyo gosahasraprado naraḥ ||
(16,2.3) aśvamedhaṃ vṛṣotsargaṃ gosahasraṃ ca yaḥ sutaḥ | dadyānmadīya ityāhuḥ pitarastarpayanti hi ||
(16,2.4) tasmādanena vidhinā gosahasraṃ dadennaraḥ | sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā yāti tatparamaṃ padamiti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_17. rājakarmasāṃvatsarīyam)
(17,1.1) omatha pratisaṃvatsaraṃ rājakarmāṇi krameṇa vakṣyāmaḥ ||
(17,1.2) athāśvayuje māse śuklapakṣasya tṛtīye'hani ||
(17,1.3) haridrāyavānām ||
(17,1.4) rakṣantu tvāgnaya iti catasṛbhī rakṣāmaśvānāṃ baddhvā hastyaśvānāṃ nīrājanaṃ kuryāt ||
(17,1.5) aśvo'si kṣiprajanmāsi ....pradadyātsa viśuddhātmā saptadvīpāṃ vasuṃdharām ||
(17,1.6) hastena yukte candramasi paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāyāṃ vā puṇye nakṣatre śucau deśe || tantramityuktam ||
(17,1.7) prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāyānvārabhāthajuhuyāt ||
(17,1.8) savitre svāhā || pataṃgāya svāhā || pāvakāya svāhā || sahasraraśmaye svāhā || mārtaṇḍāya svāhā || viṣṇave svāhā || prajāpataye svāhā || parameṣṭhine svāheti hutvā kanakānāṃ baddhvā hastyaśvānāṃ pūrvavannīrājanaṃ kuryāt ||
(17,2.1) atha navamyāmaparāhṇe vāhanāni snāpayitvā ahatavāsā brahmā dvādaśamitāṃ vediṃ kṛtvā || tantramityuktam ||
(17,2.2) śāntikṛtyādūṣaṇena vāhanaṃ triḥ prokṣya parīyān ||
(17,2.3) niḥsālāmiti sūktaṃ japan pratyetyābhiṣiñcayedenam ||
(17,2.4) aśvamalaṃkṛtaṃ śabalakaṇṭhaṃ kṛtvopasthāpya dadhyād ||
(17,2.5) evameva maiśradhānyānyudapātrāṇyantarāsu dikṣu ||
(17,2.6) tatraiva devatā yajet || agniṃ vāyuṃ varuṇamaśvināviti ||
(17,2.7) payasi sthālīpākaṃ śrapayitvā ||
(17,2.8) samāstvāgne tvaṃ no agne mā no vidannabhayairaparājitairāyuṣyaiḥ svastyayanairapratiratheneti ca hutvā saṃsthāpya ||
(17,2.9) agnerado'sītyahatavāsobhiḥ prachādya rasaiḥ kumbhānaudumbarān pūrayitvā pratidiśamavasthāpya mamāgne varco abhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī uduttamaṃ varuṇāśvinā brahmaṇā yātamiti juhuyāt ||
(17,2.10) paurṇamāsī prathameti ca juhuyāddundubhimāhanyādityuktam ||
(17,2.11) upa śvāsaya pṛthivīmiti tatraivānumantraṇaṃ ca ||
(17,2.12) sarvāṇi ca vāditrāṇi vāhanāni ca ||
(17,2.13) janasyān praharṣaya pañcamīṃ pratiṣṭhāpayet ||
(17,2.14) na taṃ yakṣmā aitu deva iti guggulakṣṭhadūpaṃ dadyāt ||
(17,2.15) yaste gandhastryāyuṣamiti bhūtiṃ prayacchet ||
(17,2.16) dūṣyā dūṣirasīti pratisaramābadhya ye purastāditi pratidiśaṃ kṣipet ||
(17,2.17) bahirniḥsṛtyottareṇa gatvā bāhyenopaniṣkramya suhṛde kuryācchraddadhate kuryādvāhanānāmabhayaṃ karma ||

(pariśiṣṭa_18. rājakarmasāṃvatsarīyam)
(18,1.1) athāśvayuje māse paurṇamāsyāmaparāhṇe hastinīrājanaṃ kuryāt ||
(18,1.2) prāgudakpravaṇe deśe yatra vā mano ramate ||
(18,1.3) girayaste parvatā ityetayā hastaśatamardhaṃ vā maṇḍalaṃ parigṛhya yābhiryajñamiti saṃprokṣet ||
(18,1.4) tatra ślokāḥ ||
(18,1.5) daśahastasamutsedhaṃ pañcahastaṃ tu vistṛtam | śāntavrkṣamayaṃ kuryāttoraṇaṃ puṣṭivardhanama ||
(18,1.6) śuklaiḥ śuklāmbaradhvajairmālyaiśca paribhūṣitam | kārayeta bile śubhre rasaiśca paripūrite ||
(18,1.7) rasaistvāmabhiṣiñcāmi bhūme mahyaṃ śivā bhava | asapatnā sapatnaghnī mama yajñavivardhanī ||
(18,1.8) imau stambhau ghṛtānvaktāvubhau mā yaśasāvatāt | yo mā kaścabhidāsati tamimau stambhau nirdahatāmiti ||
(18,1.9) ucchrayasva imā yā brahmaṇaspata ityetābhyāṃ suvarṇamālā patākaiḥ stambhau saṃyojya ||
(18,1.10) tasyādhastāccaturhastāṃ vediṃ kṛtvā darbhapavitrapāṇirbaliṃ puṣpāṇi ca dattvā ||
(18,1.11) madhulājāmaśraiḥ svastikasaṃyāvakadadhikṛsarāpūpakāpāyasaghṛtavividhapānabhakṣaphalairagniṃ paristīrya ||
(18,1.12) āpo asmānmātaraḥ sūdayantviti caturaudumbarān kumbhān hradodakena pūrayitvā ||
(18,1.13) pratidiśamavasthāpya dadhyādraudrāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ vāruṇā mantrāḥ ||
(18,1.14) rakṣoghnaṃ krtyādūśaṇaṃ yaśasyavarcasyāni ca hutvauṣadhīḥ samādāya dvihastaṃ maṇḍalamityuktam ||
(18,1.15) tatra ślokāḥ ||
(18,1.16) bṛhatkaṇṭārīkaṇṭakā lāghukaṇṭārikā smṛtāḥ | suvarṇapuṣpī śvetagiri karṇikā hyudisatrā ||
(18,1.17) siṃhī vyāghrī ca hariṇī hyamṛtā cāparājitā | pṛśniparṇī ca dūrvā ca padmamutpalamālinī ||
(18,2.1) tāmanumantrayate ||
(18,2.2) vaiṇavaṃ kaṭakamavasthāpyādadhyāt ||
(18,2.3) dvaipavaiyāghrānaḍuccarma paristīrya ||
(18,2.4) tato yā syādadhidevatā tasyai baliṃ dattvā piṇḍāni ca dadyāt ||
(18,2.5) hastinamācāmayet ||
(18,3.1) yasyāṃ diśi sa ripurbhavati tāṃ diśaṃ gatvā hastinamānayeddhiraṇyena rajatena vajramaṇimuktādibhiḥ śaṅkhena candanena bhadradāruṇayā kuṣṭhena naladena rocanenāñjanena manahśilayā padmakumudotpalair ||
(18,3.2) mamāgne varca iti sūktaṃ dakṣinottaramakhaṃ pratijapet ||
(18,3.3) śeṣeṇa gātrāṇyabhyañjayet ||
(18,3.4) tatra slokāḥ ||
(18,3.5) hastināṃ rakṣaṇe daṇḍaḥ kartavyo vaiṇavo navaḥ | ṣoḍaśāratnimātrastu cāruparvamanaoramaḥ ||
(18,3.6) tena vāraṇān vārayet ||
(18,3.7) dantāgreṣu tṛṇāni kṛtvā yathā havyaṃ vahasi grasati ||
(18,3.8) sujātaṃ jātavedasamityagniṃ prajvālayet ||
(18,3.9) sujātaṃ jātavedasamiti vācayedyathā havyamiti nīrājayitvā ||
(18,3.10) nidhiṃ bibhratīti śālāṃ praveśayed ||
(18,3.11) anapekṣamānāḥ svāni sthānāni vrajanti dīrghāyuṣo balavantaśca bhavanti ||
(18,3.12) gosahasraṃ kartre dakṣiṇā grāmavaraṃ ca ||

(pariśiṣṭa_18 . rājakarmasāṃvatsarīyam)
(18 ,1.1) atha varṣaśataṃ pravardhamāno rājānamabhivardhayiṣyan saṃvatsare janmadine kuryāt || tantramityuktam ||
(18 ,1.2) punantu mā vāyoḥ pūto vaiśvānaro raśmibhiriti pavitraiḥ puṇyāhādīni ca maṅgalairyajamānaṃ ca saṃprokṣya yadābadhnanniti puṣpādyalaṃkāraṃ varjayitvā māhendraṃ caruṃ śrapayet ||
(18 ,1.3) lokapālebhyaśca dvitīyaṃ caruṃ śrapayet ||
(18 ,1.4) mahāmindro ya ojaseti sūktena tṛtīyāyāṃ hyagnau hutvā indrāya svāhetyādi lokapālāṃśceṣṭvā rājānamanvālabhya ādivajjuhuyādarvāñcamindramindraḥ sutrāmā imamindra vardhaya kṣatriyaṃ me śataṃ jīvataḥ sarada iti ||
(18 ,1.5) rakṣantu tvāgnaya iti catasṛbhī rakṣāṃ kṛtvā rocanayālaṃkuryāt | triguṇena sūtreṇa baddhvā ||
(18 ,1.6) manāyai tantumiti sūktena rakṣāsūtre saṃpātaṃ ca kṛtvā ||
(18 ,1.7) dhātā te granthimiti badhnāti ||
(18 ,1.8) uttaratantraṃ hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇā ||
(18 ,2.1) mahānavamyāṃ hastyaśvadīkṣā pratipatprabhṛti navarātram ||
(18 ,2.2) śastrasasvasaṃpātaḥ ||
(18 ,2.3) trtīyāyāṃ hastyaśvavāhagrāmyāśvānāṃ karma saptamyāṃ hastyaśvānāṃ darśanam ||
(18 ,2.4) aṣṭamyāmatha piṣṭamayīmityādi navamyāṃ durgāpūjanam ||
(18 ,2.5) atha vā vanamyāmityādi navamyām ||
(18 ,2.6) athāparājitadaśamyām ||
(18 ,2.7) pūrvāhṇe vijayamahūrte uktaṃ prāsthānikam ||
(18 ,2.8) etāni khalu prāgdvārāṇītyādi ||
(18 ,3.1) atha śravaṇe nakṣatre rājñāmindramahasyeti vyākhyātaḥ ||
(18 ,4.1) atha paurṇamāsyāmaparāhṇe paurṇamāsikaṃ karma ||
(18 ,5.1) athāpāmārgatrayodaśyāṃ śvete muhūrte snānaṃ kṛtvāpāmārgaṃ triḥ paribhrāmayedrājña upari mantreṇa ||
(18 ,5.2) iśānāṃ tvā bheṣajānāmiti tribhiḥ sūktaiḥ pratīcīnaphala iti sūktena vā punaḥ snānam ||
(18 ,5.3) tata ārātrikaṃ paridhatteti dvābhyāmiti samānam ||
(18 ,6.1) atha dīpotsavaṃ pratipadi hastyaśvādikdīkṣāsamānam ||
(18 ,6.2) abhyātānāntaṃ kṛtvā ye'syāṃ prācī digiti ||
(18 ,6.3) mā no devā yaste sarpa ityetaiḥ sūktaistṛṇāni yugatardmanā saṃpātavanti gaṇaṃ ca prātitamitadhānāśane hastyaśvādiyugapattantraṃ samānam || dhenurdakṣiṇā ||
(18 ,7.1) athākṣayyanavamyāṃ rātrau hastyaśvādīnāmanīkānāṃ rathasya parahomaśca ||
(18 ,8.1) atha viṣṇudvādaśyāṃ purohitaḥ paścimāṃ saṃdhyāmupāsya gṛhītadarbho yatra rājānamabhigamya pauṣṭikahomaśca rātrau nīrājanaṃ kṛtvā hastyaśvebhyaśca ||
(18 ,9.1) atha kārttikyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ raivatyāmaśvayujyāṃ vṛṣotsargaḥ ||
(18 ,10.1) athāgrayaṇīpaurṇamāsyāṃ tantraṃ kṛtvāpādagreti dvābhyāṃ rasaṃ saṃpātyābhimantrya rājānaṃ prāśayet || dhenurdakṣiṇā ||
(18 ,11.1) atha pauṣyāṃ paurṇamāsyāmuktaḥ puṣyābhiṣekaḥ ||
(18 ,12.1) atha phālgunyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ rātrau holākā ||
(18 ,12.2) mahānavamyāmuktaprajvalanaṃ nīrājaṃ vā ||
(18 ,13.1) atha grīṣmapratipadyāyuṣyamiti snānaṃ kṛtvāpāṃ sūktairāplutya prakṣiṇamāvrtyāpa upaspṛśyetyuktam ||
(18 ,14.1) atha caitryāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tejovrataṃ trirātramaśnātītyuktam ||
(18 ,15.1) atha madanatrayodaśyāṃ vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca madhyāhne garte vā vāpyāṃ puṣkariṇyāṃ ghate vā sarvagandhān prakṣipya prāktantramabhyātānāntaṃ kṛtvā siṃhe vyāghre yaśo haviḥ prātaragniṃ girāvaragarāṭeṣu divaspṛthivyā ityetaiḥ sūktairudakaṃ saṃpātyābhimantrya rājānaṃ snāpayet || praviśya saṃprokṣyeti ca tantraṃ saṃsthāpayet || dhenurdakṣiṇā ||
(18 ,16.1) atha śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vijaye muhūrte rakṣantu tvāgnaya iti catasṛbhī rakṣābandhanaṃ kṛtvā nīrājanaṃ ca bāhyenopaniṣkramyeti paiṭhīnasiḥ ||
(18 ,17.1) athādityadina ādityamaṇḍalako vyākhyātaḥ ||
(18 ,18.1) atha janmanakṣatre janmanakṣatrayāgahomo vyākhyātaḥ ||
(18 ,19.1) atha rājakarmāṇi pratinakṣatraṃ kartavyānītyāyudhāni khaḍgaprabhṛtīni bibhṛyāditi kṛttikārohiṇyādīni vyākhyātāni ||
(18 ,19.2) indrotsava indramahotsavo vyākhyātaḥ ||
(18 ,19.3) pratidinaṃ grahayāgaḥ || pratidinaṃ nakṣatrayāgaḥ || pratidinaṃ daśagaṇī mahāśāntiḥ ||
(18 ,19.4) pratisthānaṃ kṛttikārohiṇīvyākhyātā nakṣatrasnānāni nakṣatrakṣiṇāśca ||
(18 ,20.1) rājakarma sāṃvatsarīyaṃ hastyaśvādidīkṣā samāptā ||

(pariśiṣṭa_18 . vṛṣotsargaḥ)
(18 ,1.1) atha vṛṣotsargaḥ ||
(18 ,1.2) kārttikeyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ raivatyāmāśvayujyāṃ vā gavāṃ goṣṭhe paurṇamāsatantramājyabhāgāntaṃ kṛtvā rudraraudrābhyāmājyaṃ juhuyāt ||
(18 ,1.3) pūṣā gā anu etu na iti catasṛbhiḥ pauṣṇasya juhuyāt ||
(18 ,1.4) pūṣā gā anu etu naḥ pūṣā rakṣātu sarvataḥ | pūṣā vājaṃ sanotu naḥ ||
(18 ,1.5) pūṣannanu pra gā ihi(iti) yajamānasya sunvataḥ | asmākaṃ stuvatāmuta ||
(18 ,1.6) pūṣan tava vrate vayaṃ pari pūṣā purastāditi ||
(18 ,1.7) indrasya kukṣiḥ sāhasrastveṣa iti ṛṣabhaṃ saṃpātavantaṃ kṛtvā ya indra iva deveṣu iti ṛṣabhasya dakṣiṇe karṇe japet ||
(18 ,1.8) lohitena svadhitina iti vatsatarīmanumantrayate ||
(18 ,1.9) ayaṃ prajānāṃ janitā prajāpatirgavāṃ goṣṭha iha madhyato vasaḥ | vatsatarīṣu apasadane gavāmadhi tiṣṭha paśūn bhavanasya gopāḥ ||
(18 ,1.10) iti maṇḍalāni bhrāmayati
(18 ,1.11) retodhāyai tvātisṛjāmi vayodhāyai tvātisṛjāmi yūthatvāyai tvātisṛjāmi gaṇatvāyai tvātisṛjāmi sahasrapoṣāyai tva7 tisṛjāmi aparimitapoṣāyai tvātisṛjāmīti paryukṣyaikarūpaṃ dvirūpaṃ bahurūpaṃ vā yo vā yūthaṃ chādayati yūthena tejasvinālaṃkṛtenālaṃkṛtamaparājitāṃ diśaṃ niṣkrāmayeyuḥ saha vatsatarībhistantraṃ saṃsthāpayeyur
(18 ,1.12) atha brahmaṇe gāṃ payasvinīṃ dadyātpāyasena brāhmaṇān bhojayitvotsṛjya sarvān kāmānāpnoti akṣayāṇca lokānāpnotīti

(pariśiṣṭa_19. indramahotsavaḥ)
(19,1.1) atha rājñamindramahotsavasya upacārakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(19,1.2) prauṣṭhapade śuklapakṣe ||
(19,1.3) saṃbhṛteṣu saṃbhāreṣu brahmā rājā ca ubhau snātau ahatavasanau surabhisujātānulepanau karmaṇyau vratavantau upavasataḥ
(19,1.4) [vratavantau] śvo bhūte śamnodevyāḥ pādairardharcābhyāmṛcā ṣakṛtvodakaṃ pari vācamācānto
(19,1.5) barhirupakalpayitvā rājānamanvālabhya juhuyāt ||
(19,1.6) arvāñcamindraṃ trātāramindramindraḥ sutrāmā imamindra vardhaya kṣatriyaṃ me hantāya vṛṣa indrasya indro jayāti iti ca hutvā rāṣṭrasaṃvargaiścā
(19,1.7) atha indramutthāpayanti ā tvā ahārṣamantardhruvādyaurviśastvā sarvā vāñchantu iti sarvato'pramattā dhārayerann
(19,1.8) adbhutaṃ hi savanānayetsamutthitaṃ bhavati || yadi prācyāmagnibhayam || yadi dakṣiṇasyāṃ yamabhayam || yadi pratīcyāṃ varuṇabhayam || yadi udīcyāṃ kṣudbhayam || yadi antardeśebhyobhayato vidyād
(19,1.9) agnirmā pātu agniṃ te vasumantamṛcchantviti || yathāsvaliṅgaṃ dvābhyāṃdvābhyāṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ pratidiśamupasthāpayet ||
(19,1.10) gṛdhraścedasminnipatati mṛtyorbhayaṃ bhavati || yadvā kṛṣṇaśakunirantarikṣrṇa patati iti japedyastvā gṛdhraḥ kapota iti antato japet
(19,1.11) sarvatra anājñāteṣu trirātraṃ ghṛtakambalam ||
(19,1.12) śirobhaṅge tu rājānaṃ madhyabhaṅge tu mantriṇam | ādibhaṅge janapadaṃ mūlabhaṅge tu nāgarān ||
