(19,1.13) indrāṭako yadā bhidyādrājakośo vilupyate | rajjuchede parijāte nṛpatistu vinaśyati ||
(19,2.1) sāvitryā abhimantritaṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇamāvartayedrājānamabhibhūryajña iti etaistribhiḥ sūktairanvārabdhe rājani pūrṇahomaṃ juhuyāt ||
(19,3.1) atha paśūnāmupācāram ||
(19,3.2) indradevatāḥ syurye rājño bhṛtyāḥ syuḥ sarve dīkṣitā brahmacāriṇaḥ syur
(19,3.3) indraṃ copasadya yajeran || trirātraṃ saptarātraṃ vā
(19,3.4) trirayanamahnāmupatiṣṭhante havisā ca yajante ||
(19,3.5) āvṛta indramahamiti indra kṣatramiti haviṣo hutvā
(19,3.6) brāhmaṇān svastivācya indramavabhṛthāya vrajanti
(19,3.7) apāṃ sūktairāplutya pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya apa upaspṛśya anapekṣamāṇāḥ pratyetya brāhmaṇān bhaktyā yadīpsitaṃ varapradānaiḥ paritoṣayet ||
(19,3.8) atha haiṣamitikamiti ||
(19,3.9) śvaḥśvo'sya rāṣṭraṃ jyāyo bhavati eko'syāṃ pṛthivyāṃ rājā bhavati na purā jarasaḥ pramīyate ya evaṃ veda yaścaivaṃvidvānindramaheṇa carati iti brāhmaṇam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_19 . brahmayāgaḥ)
(19 ,1.1) oṃ bhārgavaṃ praṇipatyātha bhagavāñśaunako'vadat | brahmayāgavidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ vistareṇa vadasva me ||
(19 ,1.2) paripṛṣṭaḥ sa tena atha atharvā yajatāṃ varaḥ | vidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ pratiṣṭhāya ākhyātumupacakrame ||
(19 ,1.3) sarveṣāmeva yāgānāṃ paramo'yamudāhṛtaḥ | brahmayāgaṃ prayatnena śṛṇu etaṃ tadyathākramam ||
(19 ,1.4) hṛdye manohare śubhre sarvābādhāvivarjite | śleṣmāntakākṣagṛdhrādiparityakte same śubhe ||
(19 ,1.5) maṇḍapaṃ kārayettatra yathoktavidhinā guruḥ | patākātoraṇiryuktaṃ dvāraiśca api pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
(19 ,2.1) abhyukṣya śāntitoyena pañcagavyena vā sakṛt | gomayena pralipya ādau pūjayedvarṇakiḥ pṛthak ||
(19 ,2.2) puṣpaiśca vividhaiḥ śubhraiḥ phalaiśca api arcayedbudhaḥ | tato baliṃ haredrātrau caturdikṣu vidhānavit ||
(19 ,2.3) pradīpān ghṛtapūrṇāṃśca pradadyādvividhān tathā | ttato maṇḍapamadhye tu vartayeddivyamaṇḍalam || [caturaśraṃ caturdvāraṃ vṛttākāramatha api vā] ||
(19 ,2.4) sitacūrṇena tanmadhye likhetpadmaṃ suśobhanam | bahiśca varṇaiḥ śubhrairnānā śobhāṃ prakalpayet ||
(19 ,2.5) madhye padmaṃ tu saṃsthāpya brahmāṇaṃ parameśvaram | brahmajajñānasūktena yathoktamupakalpayet ||
(19 ,3.1) tatha imā āpa ityā dyairyathāvadadhivāsayet | rocanācandanādyaiśca puṣpairdhūpaiśca pūjayet ||
(19 ,3.2) ghṛtapradīpamālyaiśca vastrairbhakṣaiśca śobhanaiḥ | sitacandanakarpūraṃ dadyādvā api hi guggulam ||
(19 ,3.3) pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā nametsarvāṅgakairnaraḥ | dakṣiṇe paścime vā api bhāge vediḥ praśasyate ||
(19 ,3.4) kṛtvājyabhāgaparyantaṃ tataḥ śāntyudakaṃ punaḥ | brahmajajñānasūktena kuryāccaivātra pūjanam | tathaiva raudramantraiśca abhiṣekāya kalpayet ||
(19 ,3.5) hutvābhyātānamantrāṃśca tato rudragaṇena ca | nīlarudraiścaruṃ vidvān vidhinā śrapayedbudhaḥ ||
(19 ,4.1) homayetkutsasūktena ucchuṣmaiśca yathāvidhi | japenmantrān tathāyuṣyānmaṅgalyāṃścāpi yatnataḥ ||
(19 ,4.2) hutvā ca cātanaṃ tatra mātṛnāmagaṇena ca | snāpayetpañcagavyena tathā śāntyudakena ca ||
(19 ,4.3) phalasnānaṃ ca kurvīta yukto maṅgalavādibhiḥ | bandibhirvedavidbhiśca strīsaṇgītairmanoramaiḥ ||
(19 ,4.4) cārucāmarahastābhiścitradaṇḍaiḥ sadarpaṇaiḥ | snāpayedbrahmasūktena raudreṇāpi tathārcayet ||
(19 ,4.5) tataḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā jānubhyāṃ dharaṇīṃ gataḥ | āśāsyeṣṭaphalaṃ tatra yukto maṅgalapāṭhakaiḥ ||
(19 ,5.1) tūryaghoṣeṇa saṃyuktaḥ kṛtasvastyayanastathā | kuryāddundubhinādaṃ tu śaṅkhabheriprapūritam ||
(19 ,5.2) kuryāduttaratantraṃ ca sadasyān vācayettataḥ | bhojayecchaktitastatra brāhmaṇān vedapāragān ||
(19 ,5.3) dīnānāthāndhakṛpaṇān bhakṣabhojyairanekadhā | annapānavihīnāṃśca viśeṣeṇa prapūjayet ||
(19 ,5.4) dattvā ca dakṣiṇāṃ śaktyā dadyādgaṇabaliṃ niśi | gṛhadevāstu saṃpūjyāḥ kāryaścāpyutsavo gṛhe ||
(19 ,5.5) yogino bhojayetpaścādgṛhesu gṛhamedhinaḥ | achedyāstaravaḥ kāryāḥ prāṇihiṃsāṃ ca varjayet ||
(19 ,5.6) bandhanasthāśca moktavyā baddhāḥ krodhācca śatravaḥ | abhayaṃ ghoṣayeddeśe guruṃ ca paripūjayet ||
(19 ,5.7) abhayaṃ sarvato dattvā iṣṭe ca parameśvare | dīrghamāyuravāpnoti kṛtsnāṃ bhuṅkte vasuṃdharām ||
(19 ,5.8) brahmayāgavidhiḥ kṛtsno bhaktānāṃ tu mayoditaḥ | atharvaṇā surendrāya praṇatāya śubhecchayā ||
(19 ,5.9) kṛtābhiṣekaḥ kṛtayāga eṣa kṛtāhhnikaḥ kṛtarakṣaḥ sureśaḥ | atharvaṇo'nugrahamāśu labdhvā triviṣṭapaṃ virarājāsapatnam || iti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_20. skandayāgaḥ ordhūrtakalpaḥ)
(20,1.1) athāto dhūrtakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(20,1.2) caturṣucaturṣu māseṣu phālguṇāṣāḍhakārttikapūrvapakṣeṣu nityaṃ kurvīta ||
(20,1.3) śvo bhūte ṣaṣṭhyāmupavāsaṃ kṛtvā prāgudīcīṃ diśaṃ niṣkramya śucau deśe manohare noṣare maṇḍalaṃ trayodaśaratniṃ kṛtvā madhye maṇḍapasya sarvavānaspatyāṃ mālāṃ kṛtvā ghaṇṭāpatākāsrajaḥ pratisaraṃ ca mālāpṛṣṭhe kṛtvā madhye darpaṇāṃścopakalpayitvā tatra yaṃ vahanti hayāḥ śvetā ityāvāhayet ||
(20,2.1) yaṃ vahanti hayāḥ śvetā nityayuktā manojavāḥ | tamahaṃ śvetasaṃnāhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.2) yaṃ vahanti gajāḥ siṃhā vyāghrāścāpi viṣāṇinaḥ | tamahaṃ siṃhasaṃnāhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.3) yaṃ vahanti mayūrāśca citrapakṣā vihaṃgamāḥ | tamahaṃ citrasaṃnāhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.4) yaṃ vahanti sarvavārṇāḥ sadāyuktā manojavāḥ | tamahaṃ sarvasaṃnāhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.5) yasyāmoghā sadā śaktirnityaṃ ghaṇtāpatākinī | tamahaṃ śaktisaṃnāhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.6) yaśca mātṛgaṇairnityaṃ sadā parivṛto yuvā | tamahaṃ mātṛbhiḥ sārdhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.7) yaśca kanyāsahasreṇa sadā parivṛto mahān | tamahaṃ siṃhasaṃnāhaṃ dhūrtamāvāhayāmyaham ||
(20,2.8) āyātu devaḥ sagaṇaḥ sasainyaḥ savāhanaḥ sānucaraḥ pratītaḥ | ṣaḍānano'ṣṭādaśalocanaśca suvarṇavarṇo laghupūrṇabhāsaḥ ||
(20,2.9) āyātu devo mama kārttikeyo brahmaṇyapitraiḥ saha mātṛbhiśca | bhrātrā viśākhena ca viśvarūpa imaṃ baliṃ sānucara juṣasva ||
(20,2.10) saṃviśasveti saṃveśayet ||
(20,3.1) saṃviśasva varaghaṇṭāpsaraḥstave yatra subhujo hi nirmitāḥ | saṃviṣṭo me dhehi dīrghamāyuḥ prajāṃ paśūṃścaiva vināyakasena ||
(20,3.2) imā āpa iti gandhodakaṃ pādyaṃ dadyāt || pratigṛhṇātu bhagavān devo dhūrta iti || ṣatcaiva hiraṇyavarṇā itīme divyo gandharva iti gandhān yaste gandha iti cemāḥ sumanasa iti sumanasaḥ || priyaṃ dhāturiti... ||
(20,3.3) vanaspatiraso medhya iti dhūpam || yakṣyeṇa te divā agniḥ śukraśceti dīpam || yo viśvataḥ supratīka iti parṇāni ||
(20,3.4) prakṣālya haviṣyupasādayeddadhyodanaṃ kṣīrodanaṃ guḍodanaṃ mudgapayasamiśradhānyamodakāni sarvagandhān sarvarsānudakapūrṇaṃ mūlapurṇaṃ puṣpapūrṇaṃ phalapūrṇaṃ rasapūrṇaṃ copakalpayitvā
(20,3.5) indraḥ sītamityullikhya agne pretyagniṃ praṇīya prajvalya prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya bhaga etamidhmamiti tisṛbhiretamidhmaṃ sugārhaspatya ityupasamādhāya samiddho agniriti samiddhamanumantrayate ||
(20,4.1) bhadramicchanto hiraṇyagarbho mamāgne varcastvayā manyo yaste manyo yaddevā devaheḍanamiti ṣatkāmasūktādayo daśa mahīpataye svāhā ||
(20,4.2) dhūrtāya skandāya viśākhāya pinākasenāya bhrātṛstrīkāmāya svacchandāya varaghaṇtāya nirmilāya lohitagātrāya śalakaṭaṅkaṭāya svāheti hutvā agnaye prajāpataye ye devā divyekādaśa stheti anumataye agnaye sviṣṭakṛta iti ca ||
(20,5.1) śivāgnikṛttikānāṃ tu stoṣyāmi varadaṃ śubham | sa me stuto viśvarūpajsarvānarthān prayacchatu ||
(20,5.2) dhanadhānyakulān bhogān sa me vacanavedanam | dāsīdāsaṃ tathā sthānaṃ maṇiratnaṃ surāñjanam ||
(20,5.3) ye bhaktyā bhajante dhūrtaṃ brahmaṇyaṃ ca yaśasvinam | sarve te dhanavantaḥ syuḥ prajāvanto yaśasvinaḥ ||
(20,5.4) yathendrastu varān labdhvā prītastu bhagavān purā | dehi me vipulān bhogān bhaktānāṃ ca viśeṣata iti ||
(20,5.5) kāmasūktenopahāramupaharet ||
(20,5.6) upahāramimaṃ deva mayā bhaktyā niveditam | pratigṛhya yathānyāyamakruddhaḥ sumanā bhava ||
(20,6.1) sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmi sadyojātāya vai namaḥ | bhavebhave nādibhave bhajasva māṃ bhavodbhaveti bhavāya namaḥ ||
(20,6.2) devaṃ prapadye varadaṃ prapadye skandaṃ prapadye ca kumāramugram | ṣaṇṇāṃ sutaṃ kṛttikānāṃ ṣaḍāsyamagneḥ putraṃ sādhanaṃ gopathoktaiḥ ||
(20,6.3) raktāni yasya puṣpāṇi raktaṃ yasya vilepanam | kukkuṭā yasya raktākṣāḥ sa me skandaḥ prasīdatu ||
(20,6.4) āgneyaṃ kṛttikāputramaindraṃ ke cidadhīyate | ke citpāśupataṃ raudraṃ yo'si so'si namo'stu ta iti ||
(20,6.5) svāmine namaḥ śaṅkarāyāgniputrāya kṛttikāputrāya namaḥ ||
(20,6.6) bhagavān kva cidapratirūpaḥ svāhā bhagavān kva cidapratirūpaḥ ||
(20,6.7) maṇiratnavarapratirūpaḥ || kāñcanaratnavarapratirūpa iti ||
(20,6.8) ete [te] deva gandhā etāni puṣpāṇyeṣa dhūpa etāṃ mālāṃ triḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā ādityakartitaṃ sūtramiti pratisaramābadhnīyāt ||
(20,7.1) ādityakartitaṃ sūtramindreṇa trivṛtīkṛtam | aśvibhyāṃ grathito granthirbrahmaṇā pratisaraḥ kṛtaḥ ||
(20,7.2) dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyamaśubhasya ca ghātanam | badhnāmi pratisaramimaṃ sarvaśatrunibarhanam ||
(20,7.3) rakṣobhyaśca piśācebhyo gandharvebhyastathaiva ca | manuṣyebhyo bhayaṃ nāsti yacca syādduṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam ||
(20,7.4) svakṛtātparakṛtācca duṣkṛtātpratimucyate | sarvasmātpātakānmukto bhavedvīrastathaiva ca ||
(20,7.5) abhicārācca kṛtyātaḥ strīkṛtādaśubhaṃ ca yat | tāvattasya bhataṃ nāsti yāvatsūtraṃ sa dhārayet ||
(20,7.6) yāvadāpaśca gāvaśca yāvatsthāsyanti parvatāḥ | tāvattasya bhayaṃ nāsti yaḥ sūtraṃ dhārayiṣyatīti ||
(20,7.7) anvāyaṃ bhuktvā devaṃ visarjayet ||
(20,7.8) pramodo nāma gandharvaḥ pradoṣo paridhāvati | muñca śailamayātpāpanmuñcamuñca pramuñca ca ||
(20,7.9) [yāvat] imā āpaḥ pavanena pūtā hiraṇyavarṇā anavadyarūpāḥ | tāvadimaṃ dhūrtaṃ pravāhayāmi pravāhito me dehi varān yathoktān ||
(20,7.10) uditeṣu nakṣatreṣu gṛhān praviṣṭo gṛhiṇīṃ paśyetdhanavati dhanaṃ me dehīti ||
(20,7.11) yadbhoktuṃ kāmajātaṃ jagatyāṃ manasā saṃhīhate tattaddvijanmā pinākasenayajamānātkāmamupabhukto bhuktvāmṛtatvaṃ tadvadevābhyupaiti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_21. saṃbhāralakṣaṇam)
(21,1.1) oṃ saṃbhārān kīrtayiṣyāmo yathālakṣaṇasaṃyutān | yaiḥ karma kriyamāṇaṃ hi phalavatsyāddvijanmanām ||
(21,1.2) achinnāgrān kuśānārdrānindranīlasamaprabhān | śuṣkānapi śvetavarṇānāhuḥ śāntikarān budhāḥ ||
(21,1.3) surabhīṇi ca puṣpāṇi susvādūni phalāni ca manoharāṇi vāsāṃsi saṃbhāreṣūpakalpayet ||
(21,1.4) surabhīṇyeva bījāni anyavastūni yāni ca | arghacandanadhūpādi hemaratnādi cottamam ||
(21,1.7 d) gāvaśca dakṣiṇārthaṃ hi payasvinyaḥ sulakṣaṇāḥ |
(21,1.8 b) ṣoḍaśāṣṭau ca catvāraścaturṇāṃ vedavittamāḥ ||
(21,1.5) ṛtvijastu samākhyātā vayaḥśīlaguṇānvitāḥ | dvātriṃśatṣoḍaśāṣṭau vā śāntikārye tathādbhute ||
(21,1.6) sahiraṇyāḥ savastrāśca sālaṃkārāḥ savatsakāḥ | te sadasyā iti proktā vācane yajñakarmaṇi ||
(21,1.7 b) sarve te'pi hyatharvāṇa ṛtvijaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
(21,1.8 d) śuddhātmāno japairhomairvaidikairvītamatsarāḥ ||
(21,2.1) tāmrarājatahaimānāṃ mṛnmayānāmathāpi vā | achidrāṇāṃ savarṇānāṃ kalaśānāṃ ca saṃgrahaḥ ||
(21,2.2) carūṇāmatha pātrāṇāmindhanānāṃ viśeṣataḥ | yavavrīhitila''ādīnāmājyasyāharaṇaṃ tathā ||
(21,2.3) achidrāsphātitāvakrā dīrghaparvāḥ sumadhyamāḥ | śamīdūrvātarūṇāṃ tu jñeyāḥ śāntau śabhāvāḥ ||
(21,2.4) puṣkaratantugovālatruṭisarṣapayavasya tu | ṣaḍguṇitaḥṣaḍguṇito [bhavati] narasyāṅgulamāne ||
(21,2.5) gopucchasadṛśo daṇḍaḥ samaḥ ślakṣṇo manoharaḥ | sruvaśca śāntuke jñeyaḥ sruguktā yajñalakṣaṇā ||
(21,3.1) sauvarṇaḥ śāntike proktaḥ pālaśo vātha khādiraḥ | abhicāre viśeṣeṇa kuryātsruvamayomayam ||
(21,3.2) kāṃsyaṃ yccāṭane kuryādāśvatthaṃ vaśyakarmaṇi | viśeṣeṇa tu vidveṣe sruvo nimbamayaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(21,3.3) pauṣṭike rājataṃ vidyāttāmraṃ ca vijayāvaham | amṛtādau tu vijñeyaścāndanaḥ siddhidaḥ sruvaḥ ||
(21,3.4) sruvāṇāṃ viṣayaṃ jñātvā kīlakānāṃ vidhiṃ tathā | gṛhasyāśmavidhiṃ dikṣu karmasiddhimavāpnuyāt ||
(21,3.5) śleṣmātakārkakaṇṭakikaṭutiktādivarjite | ariṣṭagṛdhrakauśikavihaṃgaiśca vivarjite ||
(21,4.1) gulmavallīlatāyukte hṛdyaiśca madhurairdrumaiḥ | taruṇaiḥ phalavadbhiśca śmaśānāsthivivarjite ||
(21,4.2) mayūracakravākādihaṃsakāraṇḍavādibhiḥ | susvarairnādite deśe hṛdyāṅkurasamanvite ||
(21,4.3) nadītaṭe samudrasya saṃgame vā viśeṣataḥ | anindye digvibhāge ca uttare vāparājite ||
(21,4.4) bhūmiṃ saṃśodhayetkartā prāgudakppravaṇe śubhe | prācīṃ saṃśodhya yatnena maṇḍapaṃ tatra kārayet ||
(21,4.5) navakoṣṭhaṃ samaṃ vāpi hastaiḥ ṣoṣaśabhirmitam | caturaśraṃ caturdvāramekordhvadvārameva vā ||
(21,5.1) tata īśānakoṇe tu snānavediṃ samācaret | daśadvādaśahastaṃ vā yathāvitānameva vā ||
(21,5.2) caturguṇocchrayāścaiva mūlastambhāstu ye tataḥ | upastambhāstu ye pārśve tadardhena prakīrtitāḥ ||
(21,5.3) kumbhāḥ stambhaistathā deyāḥ kāmairdvāraṃ diśāṃ smṛtam | yajamānocchrayaṃ vāpi tadardhena prakīrtitāḥ ||
(21,5.4) kuṇḍākṛti gṛhaṃ kuryāddviguṇaṃ pariveṣtitam | sarvadikṣu plavaṃ caiva kuṇḍasyordhvaṃ na chādayet ||
(21,6.1) parito dvādaśasthūnaṃ catu[ḥ]stambhaṃ tu madhyataḥ | arcitaṃ pūjitaṃ nityaṃ śāntau śāntigṛhaṃ smṛtam ||
(21,6.2) netrādyullocaśobhiṣṭhaṃ nānāvarṇadhvajākulam | raktā pītā ca dhūmrā ca kṛṣṇā nīlātha pāṇḍurā ||
(21,6.3) vicitrā hīndranīlābhā patākāḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ | [aindrāyudhadhūmrakṛṣṇanīlapāṇḍuravarṇakāḥ] ||
(21,6.4) [pītaraktasitāḥ śyāmā patākāḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |] kalaśān ṣoḍaśān tatra upariṣṭānmahādhvajaḥ ||
(21,6.5) vastreṇāchāditān kuryātsahiraṇyān pṛthakpṛthak | maṇimuktāphalaiḥ puṣpairhṛdyaiśca madhuraiḥ phalaiḥ ||
(21,6.6) samantāddikṣu vinyastaiḥ pradīpaiścāpyalaṃkṛtam | dhūpairbalyupahāraiśca jayaghoṣaiśca bandinām ||
(21,6.7) śaṅkhatūryaninādaistu vīṇādundubhisasmitaiḥ | pūjyamāno hi nṛpatiḥ praviśetsapurohitaḥ ||
(21,6.8) tataḥ śāntyudakaṃ kṛtvā cātanenānuyojitam | saṃprokṣya vidhivanmantrairānayedaraṇī tataḥ ||
(21,7.1) mathite'gnau vidhānena śāntyudakena samantrakam | homaṃ kṛtvā yathoktaṃ tu nimittānyupalakṣayet ||
(21,7.2) meghadundubhinirghoṣaiḥ prajvalan sarvatastathā | avyavachinnadīrghārciḥ susnigdhaḥ siddhikārakaḥ ||
(21,7.3) kiṃśukāśokapadmābho nīlotpalanibhastathā | vahniḥ siddhikaro jñeyaḥ saptarātrānna saṃśayaḥ ||
(21,7.4) hutamātre prajvalati vihasanniva dṛśyate | taṃ vidyātsiddhidaṃ vahniṃ padmavarṇanibhaṃ tathā ||
(21,7.5) asnigdhārciḥ sadhūmo yaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇo'pradakṣiṇaḥ | yathoktaviparītastu na vahniḥ syātprayaṃkaraḥ ||
(21,7.6) yasmin prasannatāmeti hūyamāne hutāśane | tatra nityaṃ mahāsiddhirasamāpte vinirdiśet || asamāpte vinirdiśet || iti saṃbhāralakṣaṇaṃ samāptam ||


(pariśiṣṭa_22. araṇilakṣaṇam)
(22,1.1) omathātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi araṇyoścaiva lakṣaṇam | rūpamathā pramāṇaṃ ca guṇadoṣān tathaiva ca ||
(22,1.2) coditān śabdaśāstreṇa ācāryeṇa tu dhīmatā | purā kalpe ca yaddṛṣṭamṛṣibhiścaiva lakṣaṇam ||
(22,1.3) gṛhyāgniṃ parisaṃgṛhya dharmapatnyā sahaiva tu | vaitānikāstataḥ kuryādādhānādyā yathoditāḥ ||
(22,1.4) tithau śubhāyāṃ nakṣatre diśaṃ gatvā tvaninditām | aśvatthāttu śamīgarbhāduktamāharaṇaṃ śruteḥ ||
(22,1.5) śamīvṛkṣe tu yo'śvattho nānyavṛkṣeṇa saṃyutaḥ | madhye mūlaṃ na bāhye tu sa garbhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
(22,2.1) abhāve tu śamīgarbhe aśvatthādeva vāharet | prāpte caiva śamīgarbhe samāropya visarjayet ||
(22,2.2) caturviṃśāṅgulā dīrghā vistareṇa ṣaḍaṅgulā | caturaṅgulocchrayā ca araṇiścottarāraṇiḥ ||
(22,2.3) ā skandhāduraso vāpi iti staudāyanaiḥ smṛtā | bāhumātrā devadarśairjājalairūrumātrikā ||
(22,2.4) cāraṇavaidyairjaṅghe ca maudenāṣṭāṅgulāni ca | jaladāyanairvitastirvā ṣoḍaśeti tu bhārgavaḥ ||
(22,2.5) śiraḥpramāṇe nābhau tu caturviṃśatikaiva hi | śaunakādibhirācāryairetanmānaṃ prakīrtitam ||
(22,3.1) tasyāstu piṇḍaḥ ṣaḍbhāge caturbhāge tu vistare | caturaśrā ca ślakṣṇā ca chidragranthivivarjitā ||
(22,3.2) klinnā bhinnāgnisaṃspṛṣṭā sphuṭitā vidyutā hatā | anyaiśca doṣaiḥ saṃyuktā varjanīyā prayatnataḥ ||
(22,3.3) śirogranthirhareccakṣuśchidrā patnīvināśinī | klinnā vināśayetputrān sphuṭitā śokamāvahet ||
(22,3.4) ūrdhvaśuṣke na kartavyā kṛṣṇe rūkṣe tathaiva ca | ubhe apyekavṛkṣe ca araṇiścottarāraṇiḥ ||
(22,3.5) tatpramāṇā tadardhā vā bhūyasī vā yathecchayā | anenaiva tu manthavyo na kuryādyonisaṃkaram ||
(22,4.1) yonisaṃkarasaṃkīrṇe mahān doṣaḥ prapadyate | sa yajñastāmaso nāma phalaṃ tatra na vidyate ||
(22,4.2) piṇḍe tvayanaviṣuvau pṛthutve ṛtavaḥ sthitāḥ | ardhamāsāśca dīrghatve kālaścātra pratiṣṭhitaḥ ||
(22,4.3) yajamāno'raṇiriti vadantyeke vipaścitaḥ | tatpradhānāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā yajñaścāpi tathaiva hi ||
(22,4.4) prathame mūlaṣaḍbhāge pādau jaṅgheti kīrtyate | dvitīye jānunī ūrū tṛtīye śroṇirucyate ||
(22,4.5) caturthe jaṭharaṃ sāṅgaṃ grīvā caiva tu pañcame | ṣaṣthe śiraḥ samākhyātamaṅgānyetāni nirdiśet ||
(22,5.1) mathite pādajaṅghe ca piśācaḥ saṃprajāyate | jānunośca tathā corvo rākṣastvaṃ prayāti hi ||
(22,5.2) śroṇyāṃ ca sarvakāmā[ḥ] syurjaṭhare kṣuttathā smṛtā | urasyamitrā grīvāyāṃ mṛtyuḥ śirasi vedanā ||
(22,5.3) śroṇyāmevāta aicchanti nirdoṣā kīrtitā yataḥ | tathā vittapaśūn putrān svargamāyuḥ priyaṃ sukham ||
(22,5.4) prathamaṃ manthanaṃ śroṇyāmādhāne ca viśeṣataḥ | itarāṇi yatheṣtaṃ hi grīvāṃ sarvatra varjayet ||
(22,5.5) trīṇyaṅgulāni tyaktvādau tathā catvāri cāntataḥ | madhye devāḥ sthitāstasmādvahniṃ tatraiva manthayet ||
(22,6.1) anulomā bhavedyoniḥ pārśvabhedo na vidyate | ānulomyena mathitaḥ sarvān kāmān prayacchati ||
(22,6.2) mūlādaṅgulamutsṛjya trīṇitrīṇi ca pārśvayoḥ | devayonistu vijñeyā tatra mathyo hutāśanaḥ ||
(22,6.3) [mūlāttyaktvāṅgulānyaṣṭau agrāttu dvādaśaiva hi | antare devayoniḥ syāttatra mathyo hutāśanaḥ] ||
(22,6.4) vedā yajñāya saṃbhūtā yajñā agnau pratiṣṭhitāḥ | araṇyorjāyate cāgnistayostasmātpradhānatā ||
(22,6.5) araṇyardhena caiva syuḥ khādirau cātrapīḍakau | netraṃ tu ṣaḍguṇaṃ cātrāduttaro'ṣṭāṅgulaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(22,7.1) aṣṭāṅgulaḥ pramanthaḥ syāccātraṃ syāddvādaśāṅgulam | ovīlī dvādaśāṅgulā eṣa yajñavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(22,7.2) vyāmamantraṃ vadantyeke anye hastatrayaṃ viduḥ | trivṛtaṃ muṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ yajñavṛkṣajavalkalaiḥ ||
(22,7.3) cātre ca bhāskarāḥ proktā uttare vasavastathā | netre devagaṇāḥ sarve viṣṇuścaiva tu pīḍake ||
(22,7.4) araṇiryā dvitīyā tu uttarā sā prakīrtitā | tatraikadeśaṃ saṃgṛhya uttaraḥ sa ca kīrtitaḥ ||
(22,7.5) prāṅmukhodaṅmukho vāpi bhrāmakaḥ paścimāmukhaḥ | pāṇibhyāṃ pīḍanaṃ śubhramuttarāgraṃ pragṛhya tu ||
(22,8.1) mūle mūlaṃ tu saṃyojya cātrasyaivottarasya ca | araṇyupari saṃsthāpya pīḍakena tu pīḍayed ||
(22,8.2) netreṇa bhrāmayeccātraṃ yāvadvahniḥ prajāyate | sarvairhi devaiḥ saṃpannastena sarvasukhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(22,8.3) brahmādyairdaivataiścaiva vidvadbhiśca tapodhanaiḥ | eṣa yantraprayogastu dṛṣṭo yajñārthahetubhiḥ ||
(22,8.4) yajamānena manthavyaḥ svaśākhāśrotriyeṇa vā | tanmantrena dvijāgryairvā smṛtametaddhi manthanam ||
(22.9.1) (atha) yadyaraṇī jīrṇe syātāṃ jantubhirmanthanena vā || samānīte nave araṇī āhṛtya śve bhūte darśenāṣṭvā tasmin pūrve śakalīkṛtya gārhapatye prakṣipyoparyagnau dhārayan japati ||
(22,9.2) udbudhyasvāgne pra viśasya yonyāṃ devayajyāyai voḍhave jātavedaḥ | araṇyoraraṇī saṃ carasva jīrṇāṃ tvacamajīrṇayā nirṇudasvety
(22,9.3) ājyaṃ saṃskṛtyāhavanīye manasvatīṃ juhoti || [putrārthī śrāvayet] ||
(22,9.4) mano jyotirjuṣatāmājyasya vichinnaṃ yajñaṃ samimaṃ dadhātu | yā iṣṭā uṣaso yā aniṣṭāstāḥ saṃ cinomi haviṣā ghṛtena svāheti agnaye'gnimate'ṣṭākapālaṃ puroḍāśaṃ nirvapati || śarāvodanaṃ sadakṣiṇaṃ dadāti || sā prakṛteṣtiḥ saṃgṛhyate ||
(22,10.1) prasaṅgenaiva kathitamagnimanthanamatra vai | araṇyoścāṅgasaṃbhūtaṃ netraṃ cātraṃ ca pīḍakaḥ ||
(22,10.2) ya idaṃ dhārayiṣyati araṇyoriha lakṣaṇam | na tasya durlabhaṃ kiṃ cidloke paratra ca ||
(22,10.3) putrārthī śrāvayennityamacirāllabhate sutam | śrutaśīlaṃ vṛttavantaṃ dīrghāyurvipulāṃ prajām ||
(22,10.4) etadevaṃ samākhyātaṃ pippalādena dhīmatā | dvijānāṃ bālavṛddhānāṃ puraścaraṇamuttamam ||
(22,10.5) adhītyaitacca dehānte paraṃ brahmādhigacchati | na tasya mṛtyurna jarā nidrā vyādhirna caiva hi | kṣutpipāsābhayaṃ nāsti brahmabhūtaḥ sa tiṣṭhati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_23. yajñapātralakṣaṇam)
(23,1.1) omathāto yajñapātrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yonireva ca | rūpaṃ tathā pramāṇaṃ ca kramenaiva prakathyate ||
(23,1.2) camasagrahapātrāṇi homapātrāṇi yāni ca | yajñavṛkṣāstathā śākhā brahmavede pradarśitāḥ ||
(23,1.3) pitṛpiṇḍeṣu darvyādyamagniṣvāttaṃ ca yājñikam | sāyaṃhomeṣu nityāni tathā naimittikāni ca ||
(23,1.4) bilvākṛtiścaruḥ proktastāmro vā mṛnmayo'pi vā | grīvāyāṃ mukhavistīrṇaścarusthālīti kīrttitaḥ ||
(23,1.5) kuśasyāmrasya vā parṇairveṇorvā balbajasya vā | catuṣkoṇārdhavītaṃ ca loke śūrpaṃ taducyate ||
(23,2.1) asiḥ khaḍgaṃ ca nistriṃśaḥ paryāyāḥ parikīrttitāḥ | tadākṛṭyeva yadrūpaṃ yajñe sphyaṃ ca vadanti tam ||
(23,2.2) idhmocchrayamardhakhātaṃ khātenaiva tu vistaraḥ | madhye hīnaṃ tathordhvāgraṃ vāraṇaṃ tadulūkhalam ||
(23,2.3) sthūlatvānmuṣṭimātraṃ ca skandhamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ | vāraṇaṃ musalaṃ caiva adhastāllohaveṣṭitam ||
(23,2.4) sruvastu mūladaṇḍaśca bilaṃ cāṅguṣṭhaparvaṇaḥ | samavete pṛthagbhūte bilārdhe daṇḍavṛttatā ||
(23,2.5) vaikaṅkatī dhruvā proktā sarvayajñeṣu yā smṛtā | tathāgnihotrahavaṇī sruvaścāpi tathā smṛtaḥ ||
(23,3.1) mūladaṇḍā tvagbilā ca puṣkaraṃ caturaṅgulam | puṣkarāddviguṇaṃ cāgraṃ gajoṣṭhaṃ paripaṭhyate ||
(23,3.2) [netrādikaraṇairhīnaṃ nāsikābhyāṃ dvijaistathā | dvyaṅgulaḥ khātā ca bilādaṅgulaṃ caiva piṇḍikā | vṛttā vā caturaśrā vā sādhastācchobhanā smṛtā] ||
(23,3.3) ardhāṅgulaṃ pṛthutvena bilabāhyaṃ samantataḥ | bilaṃ vṛttaṃ sruco madhye daṇḍasthaulyaṃ bilārdhataḥ ||
(23,3.4) caturviṃśatyaṅgulaṃ daṇḍaṃ vadantyeke manīṣiṇaḥ | saptatriṃśadaṅgulāni sā srukcaiva prakīrtitā ||
(23,3.5) bhinnā viśīrṇā vakrā ca klinnā ca sphuṭitā tathā | suṣirā granthibhiryuktā cakṣurādivināśinī ||
(23,4.1) dagdhaśeṣe'rdhaśuṣke ca vidyutā caiva pātite | unmūlye patite bhagne manasāpi na cintayet ||
(23,4.2) śubhanakṣatrātithiṣu śubhāṃ gatvā diśaṃ budhaḥ | sruvārthaṃ pātayedvṛkṣaṃ prātaḥ prāgraṃ ca saumyavāk ||
(23,4.3) mṛgo hariṇaruruśca kṛṣṇapṛṣṭhaśirastathā | yattasya carma tvakcaiva tatkṛṣṇājinamucyate ||
(23,4.4) vāmamuṣṭigṛhītāstu prachidyante sakṛtkuśāḥ | paraśunāsinā vā tatsakṛdāchinnamucyate ||
(23,4.5) aṅguṣṭhaparvāgramukhaṃ darvyākṛti tu mekṣaṇam. vaikaṅkate pālāśe vā prādeśastu pramāṇataḥ ||
(23,5.1) alābu vaiṇavaṃ vāpi dārvyaṃ vaiṇavameva vā | akṣāvamaṇḍalau proktau yathā dṛṣṭaṃ pura rṣibhiḥ ||
(23,5.2) cakrābhyāṃ kāṣṭhasaṃghātaiḥ śilpibhiścaiva yatkṛtam | loke prasiddhaṃ śakaṭamagniṣṭhaṃ yājñike vidhau ||
(23,5.3) ājyaṃ ghṛtaṃ vijānīyānnavanītaṃ susaṃskṛtam | sauvīrādyañjanaṃ caiva atha vā daivikaṃ tathā ||
(23,5.4) abhyañjanaṃ ca tatpraktaṃ tilatailaṃ ca yadviduḥ | āsanaṃ kaśipu proktaṃ kāyasthaṃ copabarhaṇam ||
(23,5.5) yavodarairaṣṭabhistu aṅgulaṃ paripaṭhyate | carutviṃśatyaṅgulaṃ tu yājñikairhasta ākṛtaḥ ||
(23,6.1) hastamātraṃ sruvaḥ khaḍgaṃ sakṛdāchinnameva ca | bāhumātrā juhūḥ proktā dhruvā barhistathaiva ca ||
(23,6.2) tāmraścaiva sruvaḥ proktaḥ khaḍgaṃ khādirameva ca | pālāśī ca juhūḥ kāryā idhmāścaiva viśeṣataḥ ||
(23,6.3) grahāḥ pātrāṇi camasā daṇḍayūpāsanāni ca | vṛkṣeṣu yājñikeṣu syuryathālābheṣu nānyataḥ ||
(23,6.4) samidhaḥ prādeśamātryo nityahome prakīrtitāḥ | samillakṣaṇadṛṣṭāni pramāṇāni yathākramam ||
(23,6.5) śamyaśvattha[ḥ] palāśaśca khādiro'tha vikaṅkataḥ | kāśmaryodumbaro bilvo yajñavṛkṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
(23,7.1) eṣāmalābhe vṛkṣāṇāmanye grāhyāstu yājñikaiḥ | yajñāṅgakārye draṣṭavyāḥ samidarthaṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
(23,7.2) yavavrīhimahāvrīhiprayaṅgūṇāṃ hi taṇḍulāḥ | śyāmākataṇḍulatilā āsādyāḥ śruticoditāḥ ||
(23,7.3) sāyaṃhomeṣu yaddravyaṃ prātarhomeṣu tadbhavet | bhinnadravyahutaṃ yattu na hutaṃ tasya tadbhavet ||
(23,7.4) udite'nudite caiva samayādhyuṣite tathā | kṣudhākāle tathāpykeke pakṣahomaṃ tu kārayet ||
(23,7.5) yāyāvarāṇāṃ munibhiḥ pakṣahomastu taiḥ smṛtaḥ | yathā kathaṃ cidvacanaṃ śrutyuktaṃ dvija ācāret ||
(23,8.1) āturaḥ pathikaścaiva rājopadravapīḍitaḥ | pakṣahomaṃ tadā kuryānnistīrya satataṃ caret ||
(23,8.2) caturdaśagṛhītaṃ tu sakṛdunnayate haviḥ | ekā samitsakṛddhomaḥ so'rdhamāsāya kalpate ||
(23,8.3) caturdaśaguṇaṃ kṛtvā srucā pātreṇa pūrvavat | evaṃ gārhapatye ca dakṣiṇāgnau ca juhvati ||
(23,8.4) pūrvā hutvāhutīḥ sāyaṃ vyuṣṭāyāmapare'hni | etenaiva vidhānena juhvati prātarāhutīḥ ||
(23,8.5) ṛṣibhiśca purā dṛṣṭamāpatkāleṣu sarvataḥ | araṇyośca samāropya śrutidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ||
(23,9.1) homārtheṣvetaddraṣṭavyamāhitāgnigṛheṣvapi | tatprayojanamātraṃ tu na doṣaḥ sūtakeṣu ca ||
(23,9.2) sadyaḥśaucādikaṃ proktaṃ sūtakaṃ ca dvijātibhiḥ | svayaṃhomīti vacanānna doṣaḥ śruticodanāt ||
(23,9.3) vratināṃ sattriṇāṃ caiva mahārājāhitāgnayaḥ | eṣāṃ doṣo na vidyeta sāyamprātaḥ kriye sthite ||
(23,9.4) pālāśyaḥ samidho'doṣā nityaṃ home prakīrtitāḥ | atha vā kauśikoktānāṃ yajñiyānāṃ mahīruhām ||
(23,9.5) aṅgulatrayamāvartya ucchraye'pyaṅgulatrayam | puroḍāśapramāṇaṃ tu sarvatra kathitaṃ nṛṇām ||
(23,10.1) ṣoḍaśāṅgulamāvartya tribhāgaṃ cottaramṛju | dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi sthānaṃ dakṣiṇāgneḥ prakīrtitam ||
(23,10.2) aṣṭāviṃśatyaṅgulāni gārhapatyaṃ prakīrtitam | āhavanīyam [catur]viṃśatiścaturaśraṃ tu kārayet ||
(23,10.3) aṅgulāni tu ṣaṭtriṃśaddhnvākṛtyā tu kārayet | dakṣiṇāgnestu vai kuṇḍaṃ vidvadbhijparikathyate ||
(23,10.4) īśānyāṃ diśi sabhyasya gārhapatyavidhānataḥ | sabhyaṃ necchanti śālāgnau māhakiḥ kauśikastathā ||
(23,10.5) maudāyanāstathecchanti śaunakeyāstathaiva ca | mantādeva tathā proktaṃ dravyaṃ yatra na dṛśyate ||
(23,10.6) ājyaṃ tatra vijānīiyāddhomastatra sruveṇa ca | abhyukṣaṇaṃ haviḥkarma kartavyaṃ vajrapāṇinā ||
(23,10.7) kuśahastena kartavyā japahomapitṛkriyāḥ | yajñe caiva aṅgabhūtāśca pātramantrahavirdvijāḥ | carutbhiśca kriyāḥ sarvāścāturhotraṃ taducyate ||
(23,11.1) yājñikāstu vadantyanye caturbhiryacca hūyate | brahmaṇādhvaryuhotṛbhyāṃ tribhiragnicaturthakaiḥ ||
(23,11.2) durbhikṣe cākule bhaṅge ṛtvijāṃ cāpyasaṃbhave | ekaścāturhotraṃ kuryādāpastambe prapaṭhyate ||
(23,11.3) ṛtvijāṃ ca7 pyasāmnidhye adhvaryustatpaṭhetsvayam | asthānapaṭhite kuryurṛtvigityādi coditam ||
(23,11.4) khāte lūne tu yaccoktaṃ saṃskāraśrutihetubhiḥ | dravyāṇāṃ yajñakḷptyarthaṃ kuryātpūrveṇa saṃgraham ||
(23,11.5) pātrāsādaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca prokṣaṇena vivarjitam | ubhayoścaiva kurvīta pākayajñeṣṭikarmavat ||
(23,12.1) kṛṣṇājinaṃ tilā darbhā mantrā ājyaṃ dvijottamāḥ | doṣo na vidyate hyeṣāṃ yathārthaṃ saṃniyojayet ||
(23,12.2) ājyaṃ dhūmahavirjvālā paripākaḥ sphuliṅgakaiḥ | dāvāgnikāṣṭhasaṃsparśe agnerdoṣo na vidyate ||
(23,12.3) japādhyāyatapodānaiḥ sopavāsaiḥ sahomakaiḥ | śrāddhādipitṛkāryaisca na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
(23,12.4) pitṛbhrātṛsapatnaiśca patitonmattaṣaṇḍhakaiḥ | jātyandhamūkabadhirairna doṣaḥ parivedane ||
(23,12.5) atyantakāminā caiva patnīhīnena caiva hi | eṣāmanujñāmādāya kuryādvaitānikī[ḥ] kriyāḥ ||
(23,13.1) raudrarākṣasapaiśācānāsurāṃścābhicārikān | mantāṃśca pitṛkarmaivaṃ kṛtvālabhyodakaṃ spṛśet ||
(23,13.2) sruksruvaśca dhruvā khaḍgaṃ musalolūkhalaṃ caruḥ | udakenaiva soṣṇena saṃprakṣālya viśudhyati ||
(23,13.3) pātraṃ grahāśca camasā haviḥ śūrpaṃ kuśāsanam | somaspṛṣṭaṃ ca yadbhāṇḍaṃ vāriśaucena śudhyati ||
(23,13.4) vedoktaṃ sarvamantoktaṃ śaunakena mahātmanā | avaśyaṃ taddvijaiḥ kāryaṃ śreyaskāmaistu nityaśaḥ ||
(23,13.5) pātrānāṃ tu prasāṅgena yadanyatparikīrtitam | sāyaṃ prātastu homāṅgaṃ purā dṛṣṭaṃ maharṣibhiḥ ||
(23,14.1) guruṇā bhāṣitenaiva yājñikānumatena ca | sadāpadiṣṭadravyāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ parikīrtitam ||
(23,14.2) nityaṃ ye'nusmariṣyanti yajñapātreṣu lakṣaṇam | rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ phalaṃ prāpsyanti te dhruvam ||
(23,14.3) pippalādena mahatā samākhyātamidaṃ śubham | brāhmaṇānāṃ hitārthāya putraśiṣyahitāya ca ||
(23,14.4) niṣkāmo vā sakāmo vā vedoktaṃ yaḥ samācaret | niṣkāmasya tu muktiḥ syātsakāmaḥ phalamaśnute ||
(23,14.5) niṣkāmeṇa tu yatkiṃ citkartavyamiti vaidikam | tatsarvaṃ muktidaṃ jñeyaṃ parāparaparaṃ sukham ||
(23,14.6) na śokastasya no vyādhirna mṛtyurna jarā tathā | na kṣudhā na pipāsā ca amṛtātmā sa tiṣṭhati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_24. vedilakṣaṇam [+ agnivarṇalakṣaṇam])
(24,1.1) omatha rṣiputrikāyāṃ tu sphutaṃ sarveṣu karmasu | lakṣaṇaṃ hyagnivarṇānāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathākramam ||
(24,1.2) vāstukarmaṇyathotpāte paternīrājane vidhau | sarvanakṣatrahomeṣu grahātithyavidhau tathā ||
(24,1.3) yātrodyāne vivāheṣu cūḍopanayaneṣu ca | sarveṣu cāgnihomeṣu vahnivarṇānnibodhata ||
(24,1.4) mānenādhyardhaśīrsaṇyā trimadhyā ṣaṇmukhā smṛtā | caturaśrā cakartavyā vediḥ śāntīṣṭikarmasu ||
(24,1.5) eṣā vai viparītā ca kāryā ghoreṣu karmasu | karmaṇāmanurūpāṃ tu vediṃ vakṣyāmyataḥ param ||
(24,1.6) yathāvartanagocarmacakratalpeṣu saṃmitā | saṃmṛjya prokṣya saṃstīrya vidhivaccopaśobhayet ||
(24,1.7) ślakṣṇāḥ samāhitāḥ sarvāḥ prāgudakpravaṇāḥ śubhāḥ | saṃmṛjya prokṣya saṃstīrya vidhivaccopaśobhayet ||
(24,1.8) dakṣiṇena tu yā vakrā yājñikaṃ sā vināśayet | yā ca vakrottareṇa syādyajamānaṃ vināśayet ||
(24,1.9) purastātpṛṣṭhato vāpi madhyato viṣamā ca yā | puramantaḥpuraṃ cāpi nāyakaṃ ca hinasti sā ||
(24,1.10) eṣā saṃkṣepataḥ proktā vediḥ sāmānyalakṣaṇā | viśeṣatastu teṣveva karmasvevābhidhāsyate ||
(24,2.1) prācīṃ saṃśodhayedbhūmiṃ yajñavāstu yathoditam | samitkuśāgnivarṇānāṃ lakṣaṇajño bhavedguruḥ ||
(24,2.2) tatastu yatnavān samyagagnāvupasamāhite | agnivarṇān parīkṣeta yathovācośanāḥ kaviḥ ||
(24,2.3) śabdaṃ varṇaṃ ca gandhaṃ ca rūpaṃ snehaṃ prabhāṃ gatim | sparśaṃ cāpi parīkṣeta agnāviti viniścayaḥ ||
(24,2.4) svāhākārāvasāne tu svayamutthāya pāvakaḥ | haviryātrābhilaṣati tadvidyādarthasiddhaye ||
(24,2.5) vṛṣavāraṇameghaughanemidundubhiniḥsvanaḥ | mṛṇālapadmadūrvābhakumudotpalagandhamuk ||
(24,3.1) tathā mahātmā stanayan vāhakumbhanibhadyutiḥ | saṃhitajvālanikaraḥ pāvakaḥ pāpanāśanaḥ ||
(24,3.2) kuraṇṭākṛtigokṣīrahemāruṇataḍitprabhaḥ | protphullotpalakundendukumudābhotpaladyutiḥ ||
(24,3.3) huto'pi saṃjvalatyeva snigdho viprasya dakṣiṇaḥ | lelihānaḥ pramuditaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇo'rthasidhaye ||
(24,3.4) viśālamūlo hyamalo nīlaḥ pṛthulamadhyamaḥ | pradīptāgro'malatato jvālāmālākulo'nalaḥ ||
(24,3.5) pradakṣiṇaḥ prasannārcirarciṣmānarcitadyutiḥ | arcanīyaśca nṛpaterarcito havyavāhanaḥ ||
(24,4.1) paristaraṇayogācca yajñakāṇḍaparicchadam | śāntiveśmārdhvadīptārcirṛtvijaścānulimpati ||
(24,4.2) prahasanniva śabdena dyotayanniva tejasā | kṛtapuṇyasya nṛpaterhūyamāno hutāśanaḥ ||
(24,4.3) karmaṇo'vabhṛthe yasya haviṣo'nte ca pārthivam | sugandhābhiradhūmābhiḥ śikhābhiḥ saṃspṛśanniva ||
(24,4.4) arcibhirjvālabhāraiśca pradahan dviṣatāṃ diśaḥ | vidhūmaḥ kuṇḍalī yaḥ syādanulomaśca siddhaye ||
(24,5.1) kuraṇṭahemāruṇaśaṅkhakundamuktāvalīndupratime hutāśe | samasvane siṃhavṛṣairgajendrabalāhakaughasvanadundubhīnām ||
(24,5.2) viśālamūle pṛthule ca madhye jñeyānale saṃprati pīḍitāgre | mṛṇālapadmānilatulyagandhe trisāgarāntā vasudhā nṛpasya ||
(24,5.3) ātaptapadmānilatulyagandhe trisāgarāntā vasudhā nṛpasya | tasyārthabāndhavavatī sakalā mahīyaṃ vīryāṃśujālavivarāhatarājaśabdā ||
(24,5.4) vibhrājate tvakhilarāgayuto hutāśo hastāvṛtaṃ kathayatīva jayaṃ nṛpasya | sūryāṃśubhiḥ kṛtavighaṭṭanahemapadmakiṃjalkacūrṇanikarāruṇatāmalāṃśuḥ ||
(24,5.5) kṣīrodaśuktipuṭagarbhavikīrṇamuktāsaṃghātapāṇḍurarajo rathanemighoṣaḥ | dātā nṛpāya vasudhāṃ tu himāṃśumaulijyotsnāvikāśitasamudrajalāṃ hutāśaḥ ||
(24,6.1) lakṣmīpradoharamṛṇālakapālaśubhrasnigdhānuvṛttaśikharaprakṛtiśca yatra | vaiśvānaro jvalati yasya viśuddhamūrtiḥ sa prāpnuyānnṛpaśatādhipatāṃ narendraḥ ||
(24,6.2) bālārkabodhitasaroruhagarbhagandhastoyāvalambijaladastanitābhirāmaḥ | rājño dadāti vasudhāṃ hutabhuggajendradantāgrakampitamahārṇavavīcivṛkṣām ||
(24,6.3) yasyāṃ vasantaravimaṇḍalatulyabimbo vedyāṃ nṛpasya paripūrṇamarīcijālaḥ | tasyānalākalitasāgaratoyavastrā mrāgaikṣaveniravanirvaśamabhyupaiti ||
(24,6.4) yasyānalo jvalati kāñcanatulyagauro prajvālyamānavapurutpalakośagandhaḥ | āyāti tasya bhavanaṃ hyalidīptimālā savrīḍasūryakiraṇā kṣitipālalakṣmīriti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_25. kuṇḍalakṣaṇam)
(25,1.1) oṃ śaunakaṃ tu sukhāsīnaṃ bhārgavaḥ paripṛcchati | kuṇḍaṃ kasmin bhavetkīdṛkkasyāṃ vā diśi kiṃ phalam ||
(25,1.2) sa tasmā upasannāya ācaṣṭe bhārgavāya tu | kuṇḍaṃ yasmin bhavedyādṛgyasyāṃ vā diśi yatphalam ||
(25,1.3) caturaśraṃ śaphākṛtyā ardhacandraṃ trikoṇakam | vartulaṃ pañcakoṇaṃ ca padmābhaṃ saptakoṇakam ||
(25,1.4) kuṇḍānyetāni pūrvādidikṣu aṣṭasu vinirdiśet | digdevatānāṃ cāṣṭānāṃ karmaṇi svamsvamādiśet ||
(25,1.5) caturaśraṃ tu pūrvasyāmaindreṣvapi ca karmasu | śaphākṛti tadāgneyyāmāgneyeṣvapi karmasu ||
(25,1.6) ardhacandraṃ tu yāmyāyāṃ yāmyeṣvapi ca karmasu | nairṛtyāṃ ca trikoṇaṃ syādabhicāravidhau śubham ||
(25,1.7) vāruṇyāṃ vartulaṃ jñeyaṃ vāruṇyeṣvapi karmasu | vāyavyāṃ pañcakoṇaṃ tu vāyavyeṣvapi karman ||
(25,1.8) uttarasyāṃ tu padmābhaṃ saumyeṣvapi ca karmasu | aiśānyāṃ saptakoṇaṃ tu raudreṣvapi yathoditam ||
(25,1.9) sarvakarmasu vijñeyaṃ kuṇḍaṃ padmanibhaṃ tu yat | caturaśraṃ tu sarvatra samaṃ syādvijayāvaham ||
(25,1.10) sarvaśāntikaraṃ kuṇḍaṃ padmākāraṃ viśeṣataḥ | śāntike caturaśraṃ ca pauṣṭike vartulaṃ tathā ||
(25,1.11) abhicāre trikoṇaṃ ca vaśyādau cārdhacandrakam | ṣaṭkoṇaṃ māraṇādau ca vidveṣe cāṣṭakoṇakam ||
(25,1.12) mekhalā sarvakuṇḍeṣu dvādaśāṅgulamiṣyate | caturaṅgulamānena pūrvāpūrvasamucchritā ||
(25,2.1) na cetpurastāddhīnaṃ syātsukhaṃ yajñaḥ samāpyate | yattu dakṣiṇato'hīnaṃ tadrājñastvabhayapradam ||
(25,2.2) yathāhīnaṃ paścimena bhavettatsiddhidaṃ śubham | uttareṇa samamyatsyāttadrājño rājyavardhanam ||
(25,2.3) madhye samaṃ ca sukhadaṃ digvidikṣu samaṃ tu yat | tadrājajanasaṃpatkṛtsarvāṃścārīn vināśayet ||
(25,2.4) na cetpurastādityādi ya eṣa kathito vidhiḥ | maṇḍapānāṃ gṛhāṇāṃ ca māne śāntikaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(25,2.5) hastakuṇḍaṃ sadāhome ayute dvikaraṃ smṛtam | lakṣahome catuṣpāṇi koṭyāmaṣṭakaraṃ smṛtam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_26. samillakṣaṇam)
(26,1.1) oṃ samidhāṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi pramāṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śubham | tathāśubhaṃ ca tattvena yathāphalavibhāgataḥ ||
(26,1.2) yajñakarmaṇi kartavyā[ḥ] śāntike pauṣṭike tathā | prādeśamātrīḥ samidhaḥ proktāḥ sarveṣu karmasu ||
(26,1.3) tiryagyavodarāṇyaṣṭāvūrdhvā vā vrīhayastrayaḥ | aṅgulasya pramāṇena prādeśo dvādaśāṅgulaḥ ||
(26,1.4) ata ūrdhvaṃ na kartavyā nāpi hrasvā na cordhvataḥ | na vakrā caiva kartavyā nāpi granthisamanvitā ||
(26,1.5) ūrdhvatastu yato vakrā citradadruvicarcikāḥ | karoti yāge kṣipraṃ tu tasmāttāṃ parivarjayet ||
(26,2.1) dviphalā piṇḍavarjāpi triphalā vāpi yā bhavet | ṣaṭphalā saptaphalā yā caturaṅgaṃ vinaśyati ||
(26,2.2) sapattrapuṣpasamidhaḥ kalpayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ | pauṣṭikaṃ karma kurvīta sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
(26,2.3) pattrapuṣpayutā yāstu śāntadrumasamudbhavāḥ | samidho goṣṭhamadhye tu praśastāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
(26,2.4) [athāparaṃ pravakṣyāmi samidhāṃ caiva lakṣaṇam] ||
(26,2.5) viśīrṇā dvidalā hrasvā vakrā caiva dvidhāgrataḥ | kṛśā ca dīrghā sthūlā ca karmasiddhivināśinī ||
(26,2.6) [yadyatra lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ yasmin kāle yathāvidhi | tatra tenaiva siddhiḥ syādviparīte tathā bhayam] ||
(26,2.7) samāhṛtānāṃ samidhāṃ tāsāṃ caiva phalaṃ śṛṇu ||
(26,2.8) viśīrṇāyuḥkṣayaṃ kuryāddvidalā rogadā bhavet | abhimukhagatamātrā sadyo hrasvā nivartayet ||
(26,3.1) durbhagaṃ kurute vakrā sthānabhraṃśaṃ dvidhāgrataḥ | kṛśā sarvavināśāya dīrghā nāśayate śriyam ||
(26,3.2) sthūlā tu kurute vighnaṃ sarvakārye dvijasya tu | ataḥ pramāṇaṃ vividhaṃ phalaṃ cāpi tathā śṛṇu ||
(26,3.3) latāpallavasaṃjātā dvādaśāṅgulakalpitā | kṣīrāktā śāntike home hotavyā tu viśeṣataḥ ||
(26,3.4) kevalaṃ muktisiddhyarthaṃ ghṛtāktā[m] homayeddvijaḥ | daśāṅgulapramāṇāṃ hi homayenmantrakarmaṇi ||
(26,3.5) navāṅgulā tu kartavyā tailābhyaktābhicārike | aṣṭāṅgulā vibhūtyarthe ghṛtadadhnā tu homayet ||
(26,4.1) kevalaṃ madhusaṃyuktā saptāṅguladalīkṛtā | uccāṭane praśastā sā dvidalā ca na śāntaye ||
(26,4.2) vidveṣe kaṭutailāktā dvidalā tu ṣaḍaṅgulā | sarvato granthihīnā tu viprāṇāṃ syātsamicchubhā ||
(26,4.3) avakrāgranthisaṃyuktā kṣatriyāṇāṃ jayāvahā | madhye tu granthisaṃyuktā vaiśyānāṃ bhūtisādhanī ||
(26,4.4) trayāṇāmapi vakṣyante yā grāhyāḥ samidhaḥ smṛtaḥ | nātyārdrā nātiśuṣkā vā naiva coṣarasaṃbhavāḥ ||
(26,4.5) na dagdhā na kṛmidaṣṭāḥ sarvadoṣavivarjitāḥ | samidhāmindhanānāṃ ca tulyān vṛkṣān bravīmyataḥ ||
(26,4.6) śuṣukairyā indhanaiḥ pūrvaṃ yajñavṛkṣamayaiḥ śubhaiḥ | ārdrāṇi homayeccaiva śuṣkaiḥ kalahakarmaṇi | śuṣkāṇi hīndhanāni syuḥ samidhastu yathoditāḥ ||
(26,5.1) puṣṭikāmaḥ palāśasya gṛhṇīyācchāntikarmaṇi udumbarasya vittārthī vaṭāśvatthasya rājyadhīḥ ||
(26,5.2) śrīkāmo bilvavṛkṣasya kadambasya tathaiva ca | vidveṣaṃ kaṭukaiḥ kuryātkaṇṭakairmaraṇaṃ bhavet ||
(26,5.3) kakubhaṃ kaṭabhaṃ vṛkṣaṃ kauvirālaṃ tu kauhakam | vaṃśaṃ vibhītakaṃ śigruṃ vidyāduccāṭane hitān ||
(26,5.4) stambhane sarvasainyānāṃ vijayārthe jayaṃ diśet | apāmārgeṇa saubhāgyamāyuṣkāmo hi dūrvayā ||
(26,5.5) punnāgacampakau vṛkṣā ye cānye kṣīriṇaḥ śubhāḥ | yadyatra lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ yasmin kāle yathāvidhi ||
(26,5.6) tatra tenaiva siddhiḥ syādviparīte tathā bhayam | arkaḥ palāśo madhuko nyagrodhodumbarastathā ||
(26,5.7) plakṣo'śvattho gomayānikuśāśca samidhaḥ kramāt | yathākrameṇa samidha ādityādigraheṣu ca ||
(26,5.8) śataṃ sahasraṃ lakṣaṃ vā gāyatryā paramāhutiḥ | hūyamānaṃ tu yatkiṃ citkṛtānnaṃ yadi vā tilāḥ ||
(26,5.9) grahanakṣatrapīḍāyāṃ sāvitryāpi hutaṃ hutam | eṣabhedo mayākhyātaḥ śubhasya tvaśubhasya ca ||
(26,5.10) yathoktametadyaḥ kuryātsa sarvaphalamāpnuyāditi ||

(pariśiṣṭa_27. sruvalakṣaṇam)
(27,1.1) oṃ sauvarṇarājataistāmraiḥ kāṃsyairdraumaistathāyasaiḥ | sruvaiḥ sarvaguṇopetaiḥ karma kuryādyathākramam ||
(27,1.2) sauvarṇai rājatairyajñe tāmraiḥ śāntikapauṣṭike | kāṃsyena rudhiraṃ māṃsaṃ nānyajjuhuvīta kiṃ cana ||
(27,1.3) sarve yajñe prayoktavyā varjayitvāyasaṃ sruvam | āyasaṃ khādiraṃ caiva abhicāre prayojayet ||
(27,1.4) adhunvaṃścaiva juhuyātsruveṇāsphuṭitāhutim | dhunvan hi hanti putrān tu rākṣasā sphuṭitāhutiḥ ||
(27,1.5) nānyatkiṃ cidabhidhyāyeduddhṛtyānyata āhutim | taddaivatamabhidhyāyedāhutiryasya hūyate ||
(27,2.1) sruve pūrṇe japenmantramuttānaṃ śāntike karam | śāntike pauṣṭike caiva varjayettu kanīnikām ||
(27,2.2) nātidīrgho nātihrasvo nātisthūlaḥ kṛśastathā | aṣṭāviṃśatyaṅgulaḥ syātkaniṣṭhāgrapramāṇataḥ ||
(27,2.3) dīrgho hinasti rājānaṃ hrasva rtvijaṃ vināśayet | sthūlaḥ sasyopaghātāya kṛśaḥ kṣayakaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(27,2.4) gopucchāgrākṛtirdaṇḍo maṇḍalāgra[m] śiro viduḥ | aṅguṣṭhāgrapramāṇena nimnaṃ śirasi khānayet ||
(27,2.5) etallakṣaṇamuddiṣṭaṃ sruvasya phalabhedataḥ | gopathena yathāśāstramuddhṛtaṃ śruticodanāt | sruveṇa kurute karma hastenāpi tathā śṛṇu ||

(pariśiṣṭa_28. hastalakṣaṇam)
(28,1.1) oṃ yadā juhoti hastena dakṣiṇenetareṇa vā | tadā vakṣye vidhiṃ tasya śreyasī syādyathāhutiḥ ||
(28,1.2) yathā naśyati caivāsya karma guhyamajānataḥ | tathāhaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi gopathaḥ pāṭhamicchatām ||
(28,1.3) kuśabalbajamauñjāṃ vā kṛtvā veṣṭimanāmikām | homakarma tataḥ kuryātspṛṣṭvā vāmena dakṣiṇam ||
(28,1.4) na riktapāṇirjuhuyānnānipātitajānukaḥ | anipātitajānośca harantyāhutī rākṣasāḥ ||
(28,1.5) uddhṛtya samidho'nnaṃ vā pañcabhirjuhuyādbudhaḥ | śanaiśca nirvapedannaṃ madhye'gnau susamāhitaḥ ||
(28,2.1) gṛhakarmaṇi yajñe vā tathā pañcabhireva tu | śāntike pauṣṭike vaiva varjayettu kanīnikām ||
(28,2.2) tisṛbhirjuhuyādannaṃ na tilānnaiva taṇḍulān | yadābhicārikaṃ kiṃ cittasmin kāle prayojayet ||
(28,2.3) vāmenābhicārannityaṃ tribhiraṅgulibhiḥ samaiḥ | nirdiṣṭaṃ tisṛbhiḥ śūlaṃ tena śatruṃ nipātayet ||
(28,2.4) apasavyena hastena savyaṃ yadi juhoti tat | savyena cāpasavyaṃ tu [savyaṃ yadi juhoti tat] ||
(28,2.5) abhicārastu tatproktaḥ....ṣarvaśāntiṃ gamiṣyati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_29. jvālālakṣaṇam)
(29,1.1) oṃ bṛhaspatiṃ sukhāsīnamātmavidyāparāyaṇam | praṇipatya mahartvijaṃ nāradaḥ paripṛcchati ||
(29,1.2) kathayasva mahāprājña sarvaśāstraviśārada | aśubhaṃ yacchubhaṃ vāpi lakṣaṇaṃ pāvakasya tu ||
(29,1.3) sa pṛṣṭastena tatsarvamācacakṣe mahāmatiḥ ||
(29,1.4) hūyamānoyadā vahnirṛjujvālaḥ pradṛśyate | snigdhaśca kiṃśukābhaśca siddhistatra vinirdiśet ||
(29,1.5) yatra bālārkavarṇābhaḥ karmaṇyagniḥ pradṛśyate | śāntiṃ labhete tatrāśu yajamānapurohitau ||
(29,1.6) aśokakusumābho'pi kāñcanābhastathaiva ca | śāntiṃ karotyākālena hūyamāno hutāśanaḥ ||
(29,2.1) adhūmo jvalate kṣipraṃ kṛtvāvartaṃ pradakṣiṇam | tadā śāntiṃ vijānīyādviparīte tathā bhayam ||
(29,2.2) śvasate garjate caiva visphuliṅgāḥ samantataḥ | prāyaścittiṃ tadākuryādyaduktaṃ kauśikena tu ||
(29,2.3) athāpyāliṅgate bhūmiṃ bhramate ca samantataḥ | aśubhaṃ kathayettatra hotre'sau pāvakaḥ svayam ||
(29,2.4) kapilaḥ piṅgalastāmro raktaḥ kāñcanasaprabhaḥ | śubhakṛtpāvako jñeyo viparīto bhayāvahaḥ ||
(29,2.5) yadāgnau lakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ cidaśubhaṃ tu pradṛśyate | hotā kleśamavāpnoti śāntiṃ tatra prayojayet ||

(pariśiṣṭa_30. laghulakṣahomaḥ)
(30,1.1) oṃ śaunakaṃ tu sukhāsīnaṃ gautamaḥ paripṛcchati | lakṣahomasya yatpuṇyamāhutīnāṃ ca devatāḥ ||
(30,1.2) tasmai yathāvadācaṣṭe śaunako jñānalocanaḥ | śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā lakṣahomaṃ yathāvidhi ||
(30,1.3) agnyāgārasya yā bhūmistāṃ pravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ | śuddhākṣārā samā snigdhā yā ca pūrvottaraplavā ||
(30,1.4) abhasmāsthyaṅgāratūṣā praśastā parikīrtitā | pramāṇaṃ bāhumātraṃ tu jaṅghāmātraṃ dviratnikam ||
(30,1.5) caturaśraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ tulyaṃ sūtreṇa dhārayet | brāhmaṇā vedasaṃpannā brahmakarmasamādhayaḥ ||
(30,1.6) upoṣya caikarātraṃ ca gāyatryā ayutaṃ japet | upoṣya caiva gāyatryā japeyurayutaṃ budhāḥ ||
(30,2.1) te śuklavāsasaḥ snātāḥ sragbhirgandhairalaṃkṛtāḥ | nirāhārāstathā dāntāḥ saṃtuṣṭāḥ sajitendriyāḥ ||
(30,2.2) kauśamāsanamāsīnāḥ prayuñjyurhomamuttamam | ullikhya cādbhirabhyukṣya saṃskṛtya vidhipūrvakam ||
(30,2.3) agne praihyagninā rayimityupasthāpya pāvakam | kuryādupasamādhāya samāstvāgne samāhitaḥ ||
(30,3.1) lakṣahoma॰॰kute pūrṇe dhenuṃ dadyātpayasvinīm | anaḍvān kāñcanaṃ vastraṃ tuṣyeyuryena vā dvijāḥ ||
(30,3.2) yavaistu vipulān bhogān dhānyairāyuṣyameva ca | tilairhutvā tu tejasvī āyuḥ kīrtiṃ ca vardhate ||
(30,3.3) ādityaloko'rkamayī pālāśī soma āpyate | āśvatthī viṣṇulokasya brāhma audumbarī tathā ||
(30,3.4) anenaiva vidhānena hūyate'tra hutāśanaḥ | hutvaitāṃścaturo lakṣān brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
(30,3.5) yāvajjīvati kartāsau tāvatputrān dhanaṃ śriyam | pūrṇe kāle vimānena nīyate paramaṃ padam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_30 . bṛhallakṣahomaḥ)
(30 ,1.1) omatha kāṅkāyano bhagavantamatharvāṇaṃ papraccha || bhagavan kena vidhānena koṭihomaṃ lakṣahomamayutahomaṃ vā prārambhamāṇaḥ kathamṛtvijo vṛṇīte kathaṃ ca kuryustasmai sa hovāca ||
(30 ,1.2) brāhmaṇo vā rājā vā vaiśyo vā grāmo vā janapado vā śrīkāmaḥ śāntikāmaḥ koṭihomaṃ lakṣahomamayutahomaṃ vāhaṃ kariṣyāmīti tasyā samāpterbhavadbhiramāṃsāśibhirbrahmacāribhirhaviṣyabhugbhirbhavitavyam
(30 ,1.3) taistatheti uktaḥ kuṇḍamekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ caturhastamaṣṭahastaṃ vā [samastahastaṃ vā dīrghaṃ vā] daśahastaṃ khānayettathā ca bādariḥ ||
(30 ,1.4) lakṣahome tu kartavyamaṣṭahastaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | dvihastaṃ vā prakurvīta caturhastamathāpi vā ||
(30 ,1.5) kuṇḍaṃ vā daśahastaṃ tu dvivistāraṃ tathottaram | na cetpurastāddhīnaṃ syātsukhaṃ yajñaḥ samāpyate ||
(30 ,1.6) atha dakṣiṇato hīnaṃ yajamānabhayaṃkaram | paścimena vihīnaṃ syādyajñasyāsiddhidaṃ dhruvam ||
(30 ,1.7) uttareṇa vinirdiṣṭaṃ rājño rājyaharaṃ bhṛśam | madhye vihīnaṃ yadkuṇḍaṃ prajākṣayakaraṃ viduḥ ||
(30 ,1.8) sraktihīnaṃ tu yatkuṇḍaṃ tadapyaśubhadaṃ bhavet | dvādaśāṅgulamānena mekhalākṣetramucyate ||
(30 ,1.9) mekhalātrayamuddhiṣṭamubhayaṃ caturaṅgulam | caturaṅgulamānena pūrvāpūrvasamucchritā ||
(30 ,1.10) prathamā sāttvikī proktā dvitīyā rājasī smṛtā | tṛtīyā tāmasī jñeyā mekhalā vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ ||
(30 ,1.11) caturdaśāṅgulāṃ tatra yoniṃ kurvīta sādhakaḥ | aṣṭāṅgulaṃ bhavedvṛttaṃ nirvāhastu ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ ||
(30 ,1.12) gajoṣṭhasadṛśākāra prājāpatyā ca sā ciduḥ | evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena kuṇḍaṃ lakṣaṇalakṣitam ||
(30 ,1.13) sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvatastu samāhitaḥ | kuṇḍaṃ siddhikaraṃ jñeyamāyuḥkīrtivivardhanam ||
(30 ,1.14) tasmādyatnena kuṇḍaṃ khātvādbhirabhyukṣya purastādagnerādityādīn grahān pratiṣṭhāpyottarataḥ kṛttikādīni nakṣatrāṇi svāsu dikṣu lokapālān sarvāsu kuṇḍasraktiṣvagnyādidevān ratiṣṭhāpya
(30 ,1.15) teṣāṃ pratimantramāvāhanādikaṃ kṛtvā vilīnapūtamājyaṃ gṛhītvā sruvaṃ srucaṃ ca saṃmṛjya pratapyedhmamupasamādhāyānvārabdhāya vāstoṣpatyādibhiścaturbhirgaṇaiḥ śāntyudakaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ kartāramācāmayati ca saṃprokṣati ca ||
(30 ,1.16) atha samidho ghṛtāktāstilān vā svāhākāravatīḥ saṃkhyāvatyo gāyatryā mahāvyāhṛtibhirvā sarva ṛtvijo juhvati ||
(30 ,1.17) śrīparṇamiśrāḥ śrīkāmasya śamīparṇamiśrāḥ śāntikāmasya karīrasaktumiśrā vṛṣṭikāmaysa badarādiphalamiśrāḥ paśukāmasya ||
(30 ,2.1) aharahaḥ karmaṇo'pavargādatha pūrṇāyāṃ koṭyāṃ lakṣe vāyute vānvārabdhe yajamāne niśi mahābhiṣEkaṃ kṛtvā vasordhāraṃ juhvati ||
(30 ,2.2) audumbarīṃ srucaṃ śuraḥpramāṇāṃ brāhmaṇasya lalātapramāṇāṃ kṣatriyasya skandhapramāṇāṃ vaiṣyasyāpramāṇāṃ janapadasya
(30 ,2.3) teṣāmante saraṇārthaṃ nimnaṃ khānayitvoṣṇodakena prakṣālyājyamānīya vasordhārāṃ vaiśvānaraṃ prāpnoti ||
(30 ,2.4) tadyadājyadhānīṃ ca vaiśvānaraṃ prāpnoti athoccārayati ||
(30 ,2.5) ojaśca me kṣatraṃ ca me ye agnayo amo devavadhebhyo bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ prāṇāya nama iti hutvā arghaṃ pradāya vastraṃ lodhraṃ mālyaṃ phalādīni bhājane kṛtvā namaste astu paśyata iti svāhākāreṇāgnau prakṣipya yamyaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate so'smai kāmaḥ samṛdhyate ||
(30 ,2.6) samṛddhihomādi samānaṃ svastyayanāni japetpuṇyāhaṃ vācayedgobhūtilasuvarṇaṃ vāsaśca rtvigbhyaḥ saṃpradāya praṇipatya visarjayet ||
(30 ,2.7) tasminnahani vyatīte yadi strī mālyahastā śvetacandanāniliptā śvetapuṣpāṇyādāya prayacchedgaurasarṣapān pāṇyādhāre vā gṛhītvā prāsādamārohayetkuñjaraṃ vā pramattamaśvaṃ śvetaṃ vā parvataṃ govṛṣaṃ vā yānaṃ yuktaṃ vājibhiryadyārohetsvapnakāle samastasiddhiṃ vidyānmanaso yāmabhīṣṭām ||
(30 ,2.8) tasmāttāṃ rātrīṃ prayataḥ svapet || svapnaṃ dṛṣṭvā rtvigbhyo nivedayet || paro'pehi yo na jībo'si vidma te svapna yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphamiti rājamukhamabhimantrya yathāgataṃ gaccheyustadapi ślokāḥ ||
(30 ,2.9) divyāntarikṣabhaumeṣu adbhuteṣu na saṃśayaḥ | koṭihomaṃ viduḥ prājñā lakṣaṃ vāyutameva vā ||
(30 ,2.10) avijñātaṃ ca yatpāpaṃ sahasā caiva yatkṛtam | tatsarvaṃ lakṣahomasya karaṇāddhi vinaśyati ||
(30 ,2.11) tasmātsarveṣu kāryeṣu śāntikeṣu viśeṣataḥ | yaḥ kuryātprayato nityaṃ na so'narthān samaśnute ||

(pariśiṣṭa_31. koṭihomaḥ)
(31,1.1) oṃ devāśca ṛṣayaścaiva pīḍyamānā mahāsūraiḥ | mṛtyunā vyādhibhiścaiva brahmāṇamidamabruvan ||
(31,1.2) karmaṇā kena deveśa mṛtyurvyādhiśca jīyate | aiśvaryaṃ prāpyate vāpi sthānaṃ ca paramaṃ prabho ||
(31,1.3) evamukto mahātejā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ | pratyuvāceśvaraḥ sarvān viprān devagaṇaiḥ saha ||
(31,1.4) śṛṇudhvaṃ prayatāḥ sarve prāpyate yena karmaṇā | aiśvaryamāyurārogyaṃ putrā vijaya eva ca ||
(31,1.5) savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha | ye japanti sadā tebhyo na bhayaṃ vidyate kva cit ||
(31,2.1) tayā homaśca kartavyaḥ satataṃ siddhimicchatā | yavaistilaiḥ samidbhiśca vrīhibhiḥ sarṣapaistathā ||
(31,2.2) atha cenmahatīṃ siddhiṃ prārthayedhvaṃ surottamāḥ | purodhasā kārayadhvaṃ koṭihomaṃ mahāphalam ||
(31,2.3) yādṛśaṃ kṛtavān pūrvamatharvā tryambakasya tu | tādṛśena vidhānena koṭihomaḥ prayujyate ||
(31,2.4) mahattvaṃ prārthayamānaḥ śarvo'tharvāṇamabravīt | kuruṣva mama tatkarma mahattvaṃ yena labhyate ||
(31,2.5) aiśvaryamāyurārogyaṃ sthānaṃ ca paramaṃ prabho | putrā lakṣmīryaśo medhā balaṃ pauruṣyameva ca ||
(31,3.1) evamukto mahātejā atharvā mantradarśavit | gāyatrīṃ tapasā yuktāmṛcaḥ padamitīti ha ||
(31,3.2) [ṛcaḥ padaṃ mātrayeti mantre vijñāyate hi sā |] gāyatrī vai tripādbrahma viśvarūpā ca saṃsthitā ||
(31,3.3) prāṇadā sarvabhūtānāṃ dhāraṇī yāpi nityaśaḥ | iti niścitya manasā koṭihomaṃ prayojayat ||
(31,3.4) samidbhiḥ śāntavṛkṣasya gāyatryā susamāhitaḥ | tato mahattvamagamadaiśaryaṃ paramaṃ tathā ||
(31,3.5) mahādeva iti cāsya nāma lokeṣu viśrutam | upadravāśca ye ke cidupaghātāstathaiva ca | sarve'sya praśamaṃ yātāḥ skandaṃ putraṃ ca labdhavān ||
(31,4.1) tataḥ prītastu bhagavāñśaṃkaraḥ paryapṛcchata | atharvāṇaṃ mahāprājñaṃ koṭihomasya ko vidhiḥ ||
(31,4.2) caturviṃśākṣaraṃ brahma tripādaṃ lokadhāraṇam | sāvitraṃ tena homo'yaṃ kṛto me koṭisaṃmitaḥ ||
(31,4.3) vidhiṃ cāsya pravakṣyāmi sarvalokahitāya vai | yatprayogādbhavecchāntirvṛddhiśca paramā nṛṇām ||
(31,4.4) upadraveṣu bhūtānāmāpatsu vividhāsu ca | koṭihomaḥ prayoktavyaḥ ketūnāṃ darśane tathā ||
(31,4.5) upasargabhaye caiva paracakrabhaye tathā | anāvṛṣṭibhaye caiva koṭihomaṃ prayojayet ||
(31,5.1) ārambhaṃ tasya kurvīta śukle cāpi tithau śubhe | muhūrte vijaye caiva tithichidrāṇi varjayet ||
(31,5.2) rohiṇyāṃ vaiṣṇave tvāṣṭre pauṣṇe maitrottareṣu ca | abhijitpuṣyasaumyeṣu kuryātpuṇyeṣu vā budhaḥ ||
(31,5.3) atha cettvarate kartuṃ koṭihomaṃ mahāphalam | puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvāsya ārambhaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ ||
(31,5.4) aṣṭahastaṃ tu nirdiṣṭaṃ koṭihomasya khātakam | tasyaivārdhapramāṇena lakṣahome vidhīyate ||
(31,5.5) trirātropoṣito brahmā kṛtvā śāntiṃ caturgaṇīm | prokṣayetkarmasiddhyarthaṃ vāstu śāntyudakena tu ||
(31,5.6) mahāśāntividhānena nirmathyāgniṃ samāhitaḥ | tāvatkuryādbudhaḥ sarvaṃ yāvanno nairṛtaṃ kṛtam ||
(31,6.1) tataḥ prabhāte bahuśa uddhṛtyāgniṃ samāhitaḥ | nirmathya hāvayettatra samidho brāhmaṇān bahūn ||
(31,6.2) śataṃ sahasraṃ koṭiṃ vā viṃśatirdaśa vā dvijāḥ | juhuyuḥ śāntavṛkṣasya samidho ghṛtasaṃyutāḥ ||
(31,6.3) svayaṃ vāpi yajedbrahmā savitāraṃ dinedine | pākayajñavidhānena mantraścātra viṣāsahim ||
(31,6.4) śāntikāmo yavaiḥ kuryāttilaiḥ pāpāpanuttaye | samidbhiḥ sarvakāmastu bilvaiḥ prāpnoti kāñcanam ||
(31,6.5) labhate śriyamagryāṃ tu padmaistejo ghṛtena tu | dadhnā tu labhate putrān payasā brahmavarcasam ||
(31,7.1) haviṣyabhojino dāntāḥ śuklāmbaradharāstathā | hāvakā niyatāḥ sarve bhaveyurbrahmacāriṇaḥ ||
(31,7.2) āraṇyamupayuñjānaḥ payasā vāpi vartayet | phalāhāro'pi vā brahmā koṭihomaṃ samācaret ||
(31,7.3) pratyekaṃ caiva hotṝṇāṃ dātavyā dakṣiṇā tataḥ | niṣko aśvo gaurvāsaśca hiraṇyaṃ vāpi śaktitaḥ ||
(31,7.4) yaścaivāpi bhavedbrahmā prayoktā sarvakarmaṇām | sarvasvaṃ tasya deyaṃ syāddhiraṇyaṃ vāpi tatsamam ||
(31,7.5) koṭihome samāpte tu yajedgrahagaṇān budhaḥ | kārayedamṛtāṃ caiva ghṛtakambalameva ca ||
(31,8.1) kāryāmṛtā mṛtyubhaye ghṛtakambalasaṃyutā | paracakrāgame tvaindrī ghṛtakambala[m] eva ca ||
(31,8.2) raudrī sarvādbhutotpattau ghṛtakambalasaṃyutā | [salilā salilakṣaye ghṛtakambala eva ca ||]
(31,8.3) ghṛtakambalapṛṣṭhā ca salilā salilakṣaye | yenayena tu kāmena koṭihomaṃ prayojayet ||
(31,8.4) āmnātaṃ tatra yatkarma tadante kārayedbudhaḥ | eṣa eva vidhirdṛṣṭo abhicāre vidhīyate ||
(31,8.5) pratilomayātra homaḥ sāvitryā tilasarṣapaiḥ | ārambhaṃ tasya ghoreṣu nakṣatreṣu dineṣu ca ||
(31,8.6) kārayetkṛṣṇapakṣasya tithichidreṣu sarvadā | maghāśleṣā tathā mūlaṃ revatyārdrā ca sarvadā ||
(31,9.1) darbhārthe tu śarān kuryādghṛtārthe tailamucyate | svāhākāre tu phaṭkāro veṇyādyāḥ syuścaturdaśa ||
(31,9.2) cāṇḍālāgnau citāgnau vā sūtikāgnāvathāpi vā | hāvayedghoravṛkṣāṇāṃ samidhastailasaṃyutāḥ ||
(31,9.3) raktoṣṇīṣī raktavāsāḥ kṛṣṇāmbaradharo'pi vā | juhuyādvāmahastena samidho dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
(31,9.4) khādirāgnau madhūcchiṣṭe kṛtvā pratikṛtiṃ ripoḥ | tāpayetpratilomāṃ tu sāvitrīṃ manasā japet ||
(31,9.5) kaṇṭhe śūlārpitāṃ kṛtvā tāpayettu dinedine | yāvacchatrurvaśaṃ yāti vilīnāyāṃ vinaśyati ||
(31,10.1) evaṃproktavidhānena koṭihomasya śaṃkaraḥ | prītimānucyate yena tacchubhaṃ bhautikaṃ dadau ||
(31,10.2) atharvā bhautikaṃ labdhvā śiṣyebhyastatpunardadau | śubhaṃ mokṣakaraṃ puṇyaṃ priyaṃ paśupatervratam ||
(31,10.3) etajjñātvā tu yaḥ samyakkoṭihomaṃ prayojayet | sarvān kāmānavāpnoti brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
(31,10.4) yastvimaṃ śrāvayedvidvān paṭhate caiva sarvadā | koṭihomaphalaṃ labdhvā rudraloke mahīyate ||
(31,10.5) gopathātpāṇineyāya madhye nṝṇāṃ pramodinām | hitārthamuddhṛto granthaḥ koṭihomastu viśrutaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_32. gaṇamālā)
(32,1) oṃ bhūstatsavituḥ śaṃ no devīḥ śāntā dyauḥ śaṃ na indrāgnī śaṃ no vāto vātu uṣā apa svasustama iti śāntigaṇaḥ ||
(32,2) kṛtyāpratiharaṇo dūṣyā dūṣirasi īśānāṃ tvā samaṃ jyotiruto asyabandhukṛdye purastātsuparṇastvā yāṃ te cakruḥ pratīcīnaphalo yadduṣkṛtamayaṃ pratisaro yāṃ kalpayantīti kṛtyāpratiharaṇāni || iti kṛtyāgaṇaḥ ||
(32,3) cātanaḥ stuvānamidaṃ havirye'māvāsyāmupa prāgānniḥsālāmarāyakṣayaṇaṃ śaṃ no devī pṛśniparṇyā paśyati tāntsatyaujāstvayā pūrvaṃ purastādyukto antardāve juhuta prāgnaye rakṣohaṇamityanuvākaścātanāni || iti cātanagaṇaḥ ||
(32,4) mātṛnāmā divyo gandharva ā paśyatīmaṃ me agne yau te māteti mātṛnāmāni || iti mātṛgaṇaḥ ||
(32,5) vāstoṣpatīya āśānāmāśāpālebhya ihaiva dhruvāmṛdhaṅmantro yonimuta putraḥ pitaramindrasya gṛho'sīti catasro dive svāhāśamavarma me pṛthivyai śrotrāyeti dhanvānīti dve ūrjaṃ bibhraditi ṣaṭsatyaṃ bṛhadityanuvāko vāstoṣpatīyāni || iti vāstugaṇaḥ ||
(32,6) pāpmahā vi devā jarasāvṛtamapa naḥ śośucadaghamava mā pāpmanniti pāpmahā || iti pāpmahā gaṇaḥ ||
(32,7) takmanāśano jarāyujaḥ prathamo yadagnirudagātāṃ daśavṛkṣa muñca kṣetriyāttvā hariṇasya raghuṣyado muñcāmi tvā bhavāśarvau manve vāṃ yo giriṣu dive svāhāgnistakmānamagnerivāsyāva mā pāpmantsṛjāva jyāmiva varaṇo vārayātā imaṃ yavaṃ vidradhasya balāsasya namo rūrāyeti dve śīrṣaktiṃ śīrṣāmayamiti takmanāśanāni || iti takmanāśanagaṇaḥ ||
(32,8) duḥsvapnanāśanāni dauṣvapnyaṃ daurjīvityaṃ paro'paihi yo na jīvo'si paryāvarte duṣvapnyādyatsvapne annamaśnami yo na stāyaddipsati yo naḥ suptāṃ jāgrato yanme manso duṣvapnyaṃ kāma svapnaṃ suptvā vidma te svapneti trayaḥ paryāyā duḥsvapnanāśanāni || iti duḥsvapnanāśanagaṇaḥ ||
(32,9) āyuṣyo yathā dyauḥ prāṇāpānāvojo'si tubhyamevākṣībhyāṃ te muñcāmi tvota devā āvatasta upa priyamantakāya mṛtyava ā rabhasva prāṇāya namo viṣāsahimityāyuṣyāṇi || iti āyuṣyagaṇaḥ ||
(32,10) varcasyo ye triṣaptā asmin vasu prātaragniṃ hastivarcasaṃ siṃhe vyāghre yaśo haviryaśasaṃ mendro girāvaragarāṭeṣu yathā somaḥ prātaḥsavane yacca varco akṣeṣu yena mahānaghnyā jaghanamiti varcasyāni || iti varcasyagaṇaḥ ||
(32,11) svastyayano amūḥ pāre pātaṃ na indrāpūṣaṇā tvaṣṭā me daivyaṃ yena soma namo devavadhebhyo'bhayaṃ mitrāvaruṇāvupa prāgātsahasrākṣo'namitraṃ no adharādyamomṛtyurbṛhaspatirnaḥ pari pātu tyamū ṣu trātāramindraḥ sutrāmā sa sutrāmā ā mandrairindra marmāṇi te varma me dyāvāpṛthivī aindrāgnaṃ varma girayaste yatte madhyaṃ yāste prācīrmā naḥ paścāditi svastyayanāni || iti svastyayanagaṇaḥ ||
(32,12) abhayaḥ svastidā viśāṃ brāhmaṇena paryuktāsi na tā arvā reṇukakāṭo abhayaṃ mitrāvaruṇāvabhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī asmai grāmāya hataṃ tardaṃ pūṣemā āśā indraḥ sutrāmā maitaṃ panthāṃ svastidā viśāṃ patirnama te ghoṣiṇībhya ā te rāṣṭramidamucchreyo yata indra bhayāmaha ityabhayāni || ityabhayagaṇaḥ ||
(32,13) aparājito vidmā śarasya mā no cidannadārasṛtsvastidā saṃśitaṃ me tvayā manyo yaste manyo etā devasenā avamanyurnirhastaḥ pari vartmānyabhibhūrindro jāyatyabhi tvendretyaparājitāni || ityaparājitagaṇaḥ ||
(32,14) śarmavarmā yaḥ sapatna itaśca yadamutaścāpendra dviṣato yūyaṃ naḥ pravata imamagna āyuṣe tisro devīruruvyacā no indrasya śarmāsīti uttamāṃ varjayitvā yena devā asurāṇāmanaḍudbhyastvamiti dve tanūṣṭe vājin vājasya nu prasave devānāṃ patnīradhi brūhi rakṣohaṇaṃ vājinaṃ ye srāktyaṃ varma me dyāvāpṛṭhivī aindrāgnaṃ varma bahulaṃ varma mahyamayaṃ mitraḥ pṛthivyodakrāmadaspatnaṃ purastāditi śarmavarmā || iti śarmavarmā gaṇaḥ ||
(32,15) devapurā ye purastādbrahma jajñānaṃ sahasradhāra evāgnirmā pātu agniṃ te vasuvantaṃ mitraḥ pṛthivyodakrāmadapa nyadhuḥ pauruṣeyaṃ vadhaṃ jitamasmākamiti devapurīyaḥ || iti devapurīyagaṇaḥ ||
(32,16) rudro ye'syāṃ prācī digitirudragaṇaḥ || iti rudragaṇaḥ ||
(32,17) raudro rudra jalāṣabheṣaja ye'syāṃ prācī diguditastrayo akraman bhavāśarvau manve vāṃ brahma jajñānamanāptā ye sahasradhāra eva grīṣmo hemanto anaḍudbhyastvaṃ mahyamāpo vaiśvānaro yamo mṛtyuryāṃ te rudra yo agnau rudro bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ bhavāśarvāvidaṃ brūmo yaste sarpo vṛścikastasmai prācyā diśo antardeśāditi raudragaṇaḥ || iti raudragaṇaḥ ||
(32,18) citrāgaṇo mā no vidannadārasṛtsvastidā viśāmamūhoāre aghadviṣṭā agne yatte tapa iti pañca sūktāni rudra jalāṣabheṣaja ye'syāṃ prācī digvi devā uta devā agnermanva itiprabhṛtīni mṛgārasūktānyuttamaṃ varjayitvāpa naḥ śośucadaghaṃ pṛthivyāmagnaye mamāgne vrahma jajñānamanāptā ye sahasradhāre savitā prasavānāṃ nava prāṇān pātaṃ nastvaṣṭā me yena soma namo devavadhebhyo'bhayaṃ mitrāvaruṇāvupa prāgātsahasrākṣaḥ siṃhe vyāghre'bhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī grīṣmo hemanto'naḍudbhyastvaṃ mahyamāpo vaiśvānaro yamo mṛtyurabhi tvendra viśvajittrāyamāṇāyai imaṃ me agne viṣāṇā pāśān śakadhūmaṃ somārudreti dve bṛhaspatirnastyamū ṣu trātāramindraḥ sutrāmā ā mandrairindra marmāṇi te antakāya mṛtyavā ā rabhasvāyaṃ pratisaro'yaṃ me varaṇo bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ prāṇāya namo'gniṃ brūma ityarthasūktaṃ satyaṃ bṛhaditi dve prathame girayaste yatte madhyaṃ yāste prācīrmā naḥ paścādgrīṣmaste bhūme varṣāṇyupasthāste bhūme mātaḥ sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho jātavedā mā pra gāma patho yo yajñasya tasmai prācyā diśo antardeśāditi paryāyaścitrāgaṇaḥ || iti citrāgaṇaḥ ||
(32,19) patnīvanto aditirdyauḥ sinīvāli kuhūṃ devīmiti trīṇi sūktāni patnīvantaḥ || iti patnīvantagaṇaḥ ||
(32,19 ) svasti mātra indra juṣasvāyā viṣṭhā śive te stāṃ pādābhyāṃ te saṃ te śirṣṇo vatso virāja ityekā uccā patantamiti dve bhūyānindro viṣāsahiṃ sahamānamityādityagaṇaḥ ||
(32,20) śaṃ no devī śaṃ na indrāgnī śaṃ no vāto vātu śāntā dyauḥ pippalādiśāntigaṇaḥ || iti pippalā[di]śāntigaṇaḥ ||
(32,21) gane yaditi pañca sūktāni pañcāpatyāni bhavanti pāñcajanyāni bhavanti pāñcāpatyo gaṇaḥ || iti pañcāpatyagaṇaḥ ||
(32,22) ambayo yanti śambhumayobhubhyāṃ brahma jajñānamasya vāmasya yo rohita udasya krtavo mūrdhāhamiti dve sūkte viṣāsahimiti salilagaṇaḥ || iti salilagaṇaḥ ||
(32,23) ye triṣaptā iti viśvakarmā gaṇaḥ ||
(32,24) aghadviṣṭā devajātā śaṃ no devī varaṇo vārayā[tai] pippalī vidradhasya yā babhrava iti gaṇakarmā gaṇo bhaisajyaśca bhavati || iti bhaiṣajyagaṇaḥ ||
(32,25) ayaṃ te yonirā no bhara dhītī vā ya ityarthasūktamutthāpano gaṇaḥ || ityutthāpanagaṇaḥ ||
(32,26) ambayo yanti śambhumayobhū hiraṇyavarṇā niḥsālāṃ ye agnayo brahma jajñānamityekaita devā mṛgārasūktānyuttamaṃ varjayitvāpa naḥ śośucadaghaṃ punantu mā sasruṣīrhimavataḥ pra sravanti vāyogpūtaḥ pavitreṇa śaṃ ca no mayaśca no'naḍudbhyastvaṃ mahyamāpo vaiśvānaro raśmibhiryamo mṛtyurviśvajitsaṃjñānaṃ no yadyantarikṣe punarmaitvindriyaṃ śivā naḥ śaṃ no vāto vātu agniṃ brūmo vanaspatīniti śāntigaṇaḥ || iti sarvaiḥ sūktaiḥ kauśikoktabṛhacchāntigaṇaḥ ||
(32,27) ambayo yanti śambhumayobhū hiraṇyavarṇā uta devā yadyantarikṣe punarnaitvindriyaṃ śivā naḥ śaṃ no vāto vātvagniṃ brūmo vanaspatīniti śāntātīyo laghuśāntigaṇaḥ ||
(32,28) ye triṣaptā mamāgne varcaḥ prātaragniṃ girāvaragarāṭeṣu divaspṛthivyā hastivarcasaṃ siṃhe vyāghre yaśo haviryaste gandha iti tisṛbhirvarcasyagaṇaḥ ||
(32,29) yā asurā manuṣyā mā no vidannamo devavadhebhya ityabhayagaṇaḥ ||
(32,30) bhūto bhūteṣviti rājānamabhiṣekagaṇaḥ ||
(32,31) [ya] āśānāmāśāpālebhyo agnermanva iti sapta sūktāni yā oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīrvaiśvānaro na ā gamacchumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī yadarvācī[na]magniṃ brūmo vanaspatīniti muñcantu nā bhavāśārvā yā devīryanmātalī rathakrītamityetāścatasro varjayitvā aṃholiṅgagaṇaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_33. ghṛtakambalam)
(33,1.1) oṃ brahmaṇe brahmavedāya namaskṛtvā svayaṃbhuve | ghṛtakambalaṃ pravakṣyāmi brahmaṇo nigado yathā ||
(33,1.2) bṛhaspatirmahendrāya cakāra ghṛtakambalam ||
(33,1.13) athendro ha vā asuraiḥ parājitaścintāmāpede
(33,1.4) taṃ savitābravītprāyaścittaṃ kuruṣvāpratiruddho bhaviṣyasīti
(33,1.5) tamindro'bravīt || bhagavan tvamevāpratiruddhaḥ prāyaścittaṃ kurviti ||
(33,1.6) sa prāyaścittamakarot
(33,1.7) puṇye nakṣatre bārhaspatye muhūrte'bhijityaudumbaraṃ kumbhaṃ droṇena sāḍhakena pūrayitvā tasminneva vāsaprabhṛtayaḥ oṣadhayo darbhaprabhṛtayaśca bilvagaurasarṣapāścetyetān saṃbhārān saṃbhṛtya ghṛtakumbhaṃ barhiṣyādhāyaitairgaṇairājyaṃ juhuyāt ||
(33,1.8) pra pateta ityekenāṅgādaṅgācchamalamavalikhya sapatnaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ hṛdaye imarmaṇi vāsināvidhya gomayena kāṣāyeṇa vācchādya śānterapratighātakaṃ karma tato jyeṣṭhaṃ ghṛtakambalaṃ brahmaṇaḥ putramakarot
(33,1.9) tasya ha vā etasya ghṛtakambalasya sāvitrīgaṇaśarīrasya śantātīyaḥ śiraḥ triṣaptīyo mukhaṃ rudraraudrau cakṣuṣī ghṛtaliṅga āsyaṃ nairṛto jihvā dantoṣṭhāvabhayāparājitau kṛtyādūṣaṇacātanau śrotre śarmavarmasvastyayanau bāhū mātṛnāmavāṣṭoṣpatyau pādau....pāyuśca bhaiṣajyaṃ nyāyaḥ prāṇāpānāviti mīmāṃsata ity
(33,1.10) eṣa ha vai jyeṣṭho ghṛtakambalo brahmaṇaḥ putro'parājitagaṇeneṣṭvendro'surānajayanmṛtyumalakṣmīmarātiṃ duḥsvapnadurbhūtānyajayad
(33,1.11) yathā caivaṃvidvān ghṛtakambalaṃ kurute sarvakāmānāpnoti sarvavyādhirahito bhavati brahmalokamavāpnotīti brāhmaṇam ||
(33,2.1) yadā sarvamidaṃ vyāptamasurairnāvaśeṣitam | sthātuṃ devāḥ parābhūtāste'tharvāṇamupāgatāḥ ||
(33,2.2) karmādyekaṃ kuruṣva tvaṃ yadbhṛgvaṅgirasormatam | asurāṇāṃ vadhārthāyetyuktaḥ kartātha so'bhavat ||
(33,2.3) paracakropasṛṣṭasya rājño vijayamicchataḥ | pratiruddhasya vā bhūyaḥ śrīkāmasyecchataḥ śriyam ||
(33,2.4) prādurbhāvādbhutānāṃ ca grahāṇāṃ vigrahe tathā | śaṅkamāno'bhicārādvā kārayedghṛtakambalam ||
(33,2.5) ghṛtamātrā tu vijñeyā māgadhaprasthasaṃmitā | śatāni pañca droṇānāṃ palaikaśatameva vā ||
(33,3.1) [sarvapāpapraṇāśāya sarvakāmārthasiddhaye | sarvarogakṣayārthāya prayojyo ghṛtakambalaḥ] ||
(33,3.2) ghṛtapramāṇaṃ vakṣyāmi māṣakaṃ pañcakṛṣṇalam | māṣakāṇi catuhṣaṣṭiḥ palamekaṃ vidhīyate ||
(33,3.3) dvātriṃśatpalakaṃ prasthaṃ māgadhaiḥ parikīrtitam | āḍhakaṃ tu catuḥprasthaṃ caturbhirdroṇamāḍhakaiḥ ||
(33,3.4) droṇapramāṇaṃ vijñeyaṃ brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā | dvādaśābhyadhikairnityaṃ palānāṃ pañcabhiḥ śataiḥ ||
(33,3.5) [ghṛtamātrā tu vijñeyā māgadhaprasthasaṃmitā | śatāni pañca droṇānāṃ palānāṃ vā śatottare] ||
(33,3.6) ghṛtadroṇaśatenokta eko droṇavarastathā | yathāśakti prayuñjīta ghṛtaṃ kṛtvātha bhāgaśaḥ ||
(33,3.7) caturbhāgo'bhiṣekāya caturbhāgastu hūyate | bhāgo deyaḥ sadasyebhyaḥ kartā bhāgena yujyate ||
(33,4.1) puṣye prayogaṃ kurvīta prājāpatye'tha mārute | vaiṣṇave pūṣadaivatye uttareṣvatha vā triṣu ||
(33,4.2) taptakṛchrāvasāne vā sarvakṛchrasya cāntataḥ | yasmin vā snātakā brūyustatra kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||
(33,4.3) pākayajñavidhānena kṛtvopakramaṇaṃ budhaḥ | niśākāle bahirgrāme kuryādagniniveśanam ||
(33,4.4) yajeta nirṛtiṃ tatra kṛṣṇavāsāścatuṣpathe | yathoktaṃ nairṛtairmantrairhavirbhiśca yathākramam ||
(33,4.5) tṛtīyena tu sūktena nivedya balimantataḥ | yathāvyāvartane caiva yaduktaṃ tatsamācaret ||
(33,5.1) tataḥ snātaḥ śuklavāsāḥ prāśya śāntyudakaṃ śuciḥ | paryukṣyopasamādhāya ghṛtasaṃskāra iṣyate ||
(33,5.2) pūrvaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ sāvitryā tadanantaram | śāntiśca brahma jajñānaṃ brahma bhrājaditīti ca ||
(33,5.3) agne gobhiragne'bhyāvartinnagne jātavedaḥ saha rayyā punarūrjeti ||
(33,5.4) agnimīḻe purohitamagna ā yāhi vītaye | bṛhaspatirna ityekā bṛhaspate yuvaṃ tathā ||
(33,5.5) etairājyaṃ ca juhuyātsaṃpātānānayedghṛte | kṛtyādūṣaṇamantraiśca kuryācchāntyudake vidhim ||
(33,5.6) saṃpātānānayettatra cātanairmātṛnāmabhiḥ | vāstoṣpatyairvāṣṭoṣpatāvānayetsamadūṣaṇam ||
(33,5.7) nidhāya havirāsādya ghṛtakumbhaṃ susaṃskṛtam | ghṛtabhāgau tu yāvanyau pūrveṇāgnernidhāpayet ||
(33,5.8) darbhādīn tu vāsādīṃśca saṃbhārān gaurasarṣapān | bilvaṃ ca kumbhe nidhāyāpareṇāgnernidhāpayet ||
(33,6.1) sāvitraḥ śantātīyaśca kṛtyādūṣaṇa eva ca | abhayāparājitāyuṣyā varcasyaśca tataḥ paraḥ ||
(33,6.2) saṃsaktīyaḥ suṣuptīyaḥ svastyayanaḥ śarmavarma ca | cātano mātṛnāmāni bhaiṣajyaṃ nyāya eva ca ||
(33,6.3) ghṛtaliṅgau tathā raudrau saṃpātānānayedghṛte | gaṇānteṣu yathāśakti brāhmaṇān svastivācayet ||
(33,6.4) yo'sminnakṣībhyāṃ te sahasrākṣaṃ brahma jajñānam | brahma bhrājadudagādidamāpastathāpaśca ||
(33,6.5) abhiṣiñcetsarvamantrairāyuṣyairabhayaistathā | mṛṇmayaścātra bhavati dveṣyasya ca parākṛtiḥ ||
(33,6.6) abhiṣiñcetsarvamantrairāyuṣyairabhayaistathā | mṛṇmayaścātra bhavati dveṣyasya ca parākṛtiḥ ||
(33,6.7) tasyopariṣṭādabhiṣicya kuryānmātrātalekhanīm | aṅgādaṅgādathānyena pra pateti catasṛbhiḥ ||
(33,6.8) bhrātṛvyahamiti vaiśvānaroyantyavasānena | dyāvādinā paryāyeṇa samalaṃkṛṭamullikhet ||
(33,6.9) dviṣantaṃ me parāvadvi dviṣantaṃ nirdahantu me | bhrātṛvyatāniti dvābhyāṃ paryāyāśca trayaḥ parāḥ ||
(33,6.10) anvālabhya tu kartāramupaviṣṭa udaṅmukhaḥ | svapnatakmāṣṭanayanaiḥ saubhāgyairvarmabhistathā ||
(33,6.11) rudraraudraparairmantrairājyahomo vidhīyate | srāktyaṃ vā yadi vāśvatthamaudumbaramathāpi vā ||
(33,6.12) śaṅkhaṃ ca maṇimābadhya pratisarairabhimantrayet | anvārabhyābhiṣekaṃ tu raudrābhyāṃ juhuyāttataḥ ||
(33,7.1) yatte vāsa [iti] paridhānaṃ yathoktaṃ paridhāpayet | rocanā guggulu ghṛtamabhyañjanamathāñjanam ||
(33,7.2) tata etairalaṃkṛtya īkṣayetādarṣe mukham | daśa gā dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādvṛṣabhaṃ ghṛtameva ca ||
(33,7.3) brāhmaṇān svastivācyātha prāṅmukhaḥ saṃviśettataḥ | rakṣohaṇam [ity] anuvākaṃ japetkartātha ṛtvijaḥ ||
(33,7.4) śarmavarmaitaduktaṃ snātasya rakṣobhyo'bhayaṃkaramiti ||
(33,7.5) na rākṣasā na gandharvā na piśācā na cāsurāḥ | krūrāḥ puruṣamarmajñā na hiṃsanti ghṛtārcitam ||
(33,7.6) siddhābhiṣeko naiśaśca ghṛtakambalameva ca | lakṣaḥ puṣyābhiṣekaśca pradhānāvabhṛtastathā ||
(33,7.7) mahāśāntitrayastriṃśattatra ṣaṭprastaraiḥ saha | niyatānyevadṛchāyā kartavyā bhūtimicchatā ||

(pariśiṣṭa_34. anulomakalpaḥ)
(34,1.0) oṃ namo'tharvavedāya ||
(34,1.1) akṣarāni vilomāni na svaraṃ pratilomayet | pratyārambhanighāteṣu sthānānyanyāni nirdiśet ||
(34,1.2) yakārastu takārānto antyasvaritasaṃjñitaḥ | sa tūdāttaḥ sa evādau dakāraḥ śiṣyate guruḥ ||
(34,1.3) dakārātsvaryate nīcamupodātte pra yojayet | anyāni tu yathāpūrvamudāttapracitāni tu ||
(34,1.4) prathamo'ṣṭākṣaro'rdharco dvitīyaḥ ṣoḍaśākṣaraḥ | ādāvante ca vijñeyā vyāhṛtiścāpyatharvaṇām ||
(34,1.5) raktavarṇā vilomā ca yamena parikīrtitā | sarvaśatruvināśāya sarvakarmārthasiddhaye ||
(34,1.6) mṛtyuśca devatā cāsyā nichannaṃ chanda ucyate | svāhākāre tu phaṭkāraḥ koṭihome vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(34,1.7) anulomāṃ vilomāṃ vā gāyatrīṃ yaḥ paṭhetsadā | sarvārthāstasya sidhyanti na cānarthān samaśnute ||
(34,1.8) tyā da co pra naḥ yo yo dhi || hi ma dhī sya va de rgo bha yaṃ ṇī re rva tu vi tsa ta t ||

(pariśiṣṭa_35. āsurīkalpaḥ)
(35,1.1) oṃ kaṭuke kaṭukapattre subhage āsuri rakte raktavāsase | atharvaṇasya duhite aghore aghorakarmakārike ||
(35,1.2) amukaṃ hanahana dahadaha pacapaca mathamatha | tāvaddaha tāvatpaca yāvanme vaśamānayasi svāhā ||
(35,1.3) śayyāvasthitāyāstāvajjapedyāvatsvapiti || prasthitāyā gatiṃ daha svāhā || upaviṣṭāyā bhagaṃ daha svāhā || suptāyā mano daha svāhā prabuddhāyā gṛdayaṃ daha svāheti ||
(35,1.4) athāta āsurīkalpamupadeśādatharvaṇaḥ | nāsyāstithirna nakṣatraṃ nopavāso vidhīyate ||
(35,1.5) ghṛtādidravyasarveṣu āsurī śatajāpitā | pattrādyavayavaścāsyā jikaiṣā cānuyāyinī ||
(35,1.6) hantukāmo hi śatrūṃśca vaśīkurvaṃśca bhūpatīn | āsurīślakṣṇapiṣṭājyaṃ juhuyādākṛtiṃ budhaḥ ||
(35,1.7) arkendhanāgniṃ prajvālya chittvāstreṇākṛtiṃ tu tām | pādāgrato'ṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyādyasya vaśyasau ||
(35,1.8) ghṛtāktayā strī vaśinī pālāśāgnau dvijottamāḥ | guḍāktayā kṣatriyāstu vaiśyāstu dadhimiśrayā ||
(35,1.9) śūdrāstu lavaṇamiśrai rājikāṃ piṣṭayedbudhaḥ | ā saptāhātsarva ete āsurīhomato vaśāḥ ||
(35,1.10) kaṭutailena trisaṃdhyaṃ kulocchedaṃ karoti hi | śunāṃ tu lomabhiḥ sārdhamapasmārī tribhirdinaiḥ ||
(35,1.11) nivṛttiḥ kṣīramadhvājyairlavaṇena tu sajvarī | arkaidhaḥsamidagnau tu kāryo visphoṭasaṃbhavaḥ ||
(35,1.12) teṣāmupaśamaṃ vidyātsurāśvaryā ghṛtena ca | arkakṣīrāktayāṛkāgnāvakṣiṇī sphoṭayeddviṣaḥ ||
(35,1.13) gatāsumāṃsaṃ tasyaiva nirmālyaṃ citibhasma ca | eṣāṃ cūrṇena saṃspṛṣṭo hāsyaśīlo'bhijāyate ||
(35,1.14) ajākṣīrāktayā homāttasya mokṣo vidhīyate | tagaraṃ kuṣṭhamāṃsī ca tasyāḥ pattrāṇi caiva hi ||
(35,1.15) etaiḥ ślakṣṇaistu saṃspṛṣṭaḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ paridhāvati | tasyāḥ phalāni mūlāni surabhīhastimedasā ||
(35,1.16) sūkṣmāttaddravyasaṃsparśādanudhāvatyacetasaḥ | achidrapattrāṇyasita uśīraḥ sarṣapāstathā ||
(35,1.17) etacśūrṇātpūrvaphalaṃ ghṛte caivāparājayaḥ ||
(35,2.1) kusumāni manahśilāpriyaṅgutagarāṇi ca | gajendramadasaṃyuktaṃ kiṃ kurvāṇastvakṛdvaram ||
(35,2.2) yāśca striyo'bhigacchanti tā vaśāḥ padālepataḥ | sapuṣpāṃ tāṃ samādāya añjanaṃ nāgakeśaram ||
(35,2.3) anenāktābhyāmakṣibhyāṃ yamyaṃ paśyetsa kiṃkaraḥ | añjanaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ devījaṃ kāṣṭhameva ca ||
(35,2.4) māṇsī ca sarvabhūtānāṃ saubhāgyasya tu kāraṇam | tatsamidhāṃ lakṣahomānnidhānaṃ paśyate mahat ||
(35,2.5) sarpirdadhimadhvaktapattrāṇāṃ vṛddhaputrī sahasrataḥ | rājyaṃ tu labhate vaśyaṃ tatpattratrisahasrataḥ ||
(35,2.6) svarṇasahasrasyāptistu tatpuṣpāṇāṃ tu lakṣataḥ | sahasrajāpācca tadvadudake kṣīrabhakṣiṇaḥ ||
(35,2.7) vāripūrṇe'tha kalaśe lokeśīpallavān kṣipet | snānādalakṣmyā mucyeta sauvarṇakalaśe'pi tu ||
(35,2.8) vināyakebhyaḥ snānato daurbhāgyāccaiva durbhagāt | pṛṣṭhataścānudhāvanti saṃspṛṣṭa udakena tu ||
(35,2.9) uśīraṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ mustā tatpattrasarṣapāḥ | cūrṇenābhihatastūrṇamīśvaro'pi vaśo bhavet ||
(35,2.10) tulasībhūmahādevīcūrṇaspṛṣṭastathā vaśī | rājābhayaṃ sureśvarīmārjanādbhāraṇāttathā ||
(35,2.11) na syāttasyādbhutaṃ kiṃ cinna kṣudropadravastathā | nānaiśvaryaṃ nāprajatvaṃ yasya devyāsurī gṛhe ||

(pariśiṣṭa_36. ucchuṣmakalpaḥ)
(36,1.1) oṃ nama ucchuṣmebhyaḥ
(36,1.2) ....śikhāṃ devīṃ prapadye śaṃkarāyaṇīm | sarvārthasādhanīṃ vibhvīṃ sarveśīṃ brahmacāriṇīm ||
(36,1.3) te iṣṭakākārakarālamaticaturmukham || caturvidhaistu rūpaṃ dhyānam ||
(36,1.4) śive jaṭile brahmacāriṇi stambhani jambhani mohani huṃ phaṭnamaḥ svāhā ||
(36,1.5) ātmarakṣā ||
(36,1.6) prācyāṃ diśīndro rājā devānāmādhipatyaṃ kurute | taṃ devaṃ bhagavantaṃ sagaṇaṃ sānucaraṃ saparivāraṃ saśirāḥ praṇipatya vijñāpayati | vajreṇa praharaṇenemāṃ diśaṃ vidiśaṃ ca sarvakalikaluṣamaśubhaṃ praśamayoṃ namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,1.7) dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi yamo rājā pretānāmādhipatyamiti | daṇḍena praharaṇeneti ||
(36,1.8) pratīcyāṃ diśi varuṇo rājāpāmādhipatyamiti | pāśena praharaṇeneti ||
(36,1.9) udīcyāṃ diśi kubero rājā yakṣāṇāmādhipatyamiti | gadayā prahareṇeti ||
(36,1.10) dhruvāyāṃ diśi vāsukī rājā nāgānāmādhipatyamiti | daṃṣṭrayā praharaṇeneti ||
(36,1.11) ūrdhvāyāṃ diśi somo rājā nakṣatrāṇāmādhipatyaṃ kurute | taṃ devaṃ bhagavaṃ sagaṇaṃ sānucaraṃ saparivāraṃ saśirāḥ praṇipatya vijñāpayati || tejasā praharaṇenemāṃ diśaṃ vidiśaṃ ca sarvakalikaluṣamaśubhaṃ praśamayoṃ namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,1.12) athocchuṣmahṛdayam || dyurudyuru daradara vidārayavidāraya milimili namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,1.13) ucchuṣmaśikhā || śive jaṭila iti prathamaḥ ||
(36,1.14) kurukuru murumuru mahā muñca mahā muñca viduvidu namaḥ svāhā || iti kavacam ||
(36,1.15) oṃ namo mahāpiṅgalāya siṃhanādanādine namaḥ svāhā || ityastramantraḥ ||
(36,2.1) eṣāmucchuṣmarudrāṇāmataḥ kalpo nigadyate | atharvavedodbhavānāṃ tithyṛkṣādyayogataḥ ||
(36,2.2) grāme vāthāpyaraṇye vā pracareta yathāvidhi | sadyaḥsiddhikarā hyete ucchuṣmāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
(36,2.3) ātmarakṣāṃ diśāṃ bandhaṃ śikhābandhaṃ ca sarvadā | etaireva yathāyogamādau kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||
(36,2.4) khadirasyodumbarasya tathā bilvapalāśayoḥ | dadhisarpirmadhuyujāṃ śāntānāṃ vāpi bilvataḥ ||
(36,2.5) samitsahasratritayaṃ hutvā śāntirgavāṃ bhavet | tīkṣṇāsṛgviṣayuktānāṃ phaṭkāraśca vināśane ||
(36,2.6) prayogādapyasiddhiścettatkarmedaṃ samārabhet | ucchuṣmarūpī bhakṣayaṃstīkṣṇaḥ saktūdakāni tu ||
(36,3.1) abhīṣṭāṃ vā striyaṃ gatvā dhyātvā vā reta utsṛjet | mūtrāṃ purīṣaṃ cotsṛjya gokaṅkālādhirohaṇam ||
(36,3.2) kṛtvā mantraṃ niśi japedyāvadgośṛṅgataścaret | jvālābhaṅgaṃ tatastasya karmasiddhiṃ samādiśet ||
(36,3.3) [dadhimadhughṛtāktānāmiti śeṣaḥ] ||
(36,4.1) saptakṣīrāñjaligrāsaḥ sruvo hyasmin praśasyate | kṣīraṃ tenātha juhuyāddhanakāmasya nityaśaḥ ||
(36,4.2) ghṛtena tejaskāmasya āyuḥkāmasya dūrvayā | kukusaṃ tumbaraṃ vāpi vidyāduccāṭakarmaṇi ||
(36,5.1) brāhmaṇaṃ tu vaśīkartuṃ śālipiṣṭamayīṃ tanum | kṛtvā catuṣpathaṃ gatvā gṛhītvā śastramuttamam ||
(36,5.2) aṣṭottarasahasreṇa kṛtvā tadabhimantraṇam | aṣṭāṅgaṃ tena tāṃ chitvā mantrato vaśamānayet ||
(36,5.3) aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā prakṛte juhuyādbudhaḥ | brāhmaṇīṃ tu vaśīkartuṃ kuryānmāṣamayīṃ tanum ||
(36,6.1) sarpirdadhimadhvaktānāṃ lājānāmāhutīḥ śubhāḥ | kanyākāmo'ṣṭasahasraṃ hutvā kanyāmavāpnuyāt ||
(36,6.2) api vā piṣṭamayyāḥ prāgjuhuyātsaṃdhyayāhutīḥ | darbheṣīkāṃ vābhimantrya tadgṛhe niścalāṃ nyaset ||
(36,6.3) tāvadudvejayetsā tu vajrabhūtā hi tadgṛham | kanyāyāḥ sādhanīyāyās[tu] yāvallābhastato bhavet ||
(36,7.1) pradhānamanyaṃ vā kiṃ cidvaśīkartuṃ narottamam | samidhaḥ khadirādīnāmaudumbaryaśca homayet ||
(36,7.2) śmaśānakhaṭvāṅgamayīṃ homayenmantrasādhane | palāṇḍulaśunaprasthaṃ hutvā mastaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
(36,7.3) śirīṣāṅgamayīṃ rājño balistrimadhureṇa tu | brāhmaṇe pāyasamayīṃ kṣatriyasya viṣāṇikām ||
(36,7.4) vaiśyasya sādhane homyāścūrṇaiḥ surabhisaṃskṛtāḥ | catuṣpathe tu śūdrasya padminyutkaraṇena tu ||
(36,8.1) likhitvā nāma saṃgṛhya karāgrāṅgulipīḍitām | śiraḥpīḍā jvaraḥ śūlaṃ vimatiḥ svastyasaṃgatiḥ ||
(36,8.2) balyādyā vā prayoktavyā brāhmaṇādicatuṣṭaye | evaṃ satyabhicāraśca caturṇāmapi darśitaḥ ||
(36,8.3) liṅgaṃ vā rājasarṣapaiḥ samālikhyātha dhūpayet | gaurairarghaṃ tathā dadyānmriyate sāpyasaṃśayam ||
(36,8.4) abhakṣabhakṣo hyasvāsthyaṃ sarvarogaprakopanam | niḥsaṃjñatā piṇḍapāto japāvṛttyā bhavanti hi ||
(36,8.5) ekādaśaṃ na japtavyaṃ kulotsādastato bhavet ||
(36,9.1) oṃ namo mahāpiṅgalāya trivṛte trivṛte namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.2) namaḥ sarāntitevatevasu trivṛte trivṛte triparvaṇe triśīrṣāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.3) namaḥ kaṭavikaṭakaṇṭemāṭe pāṭale vikale asauryāsau asauryāsau pṛthivīṣṭakā iṣṭakājinātyūnyo saugaluṃtigaluṃtekaṭamasi kaṭapravṛte pradviṣa rudra raudreṇāveśayāveśaya hanahana dahadaha pacapaca mathamatha vidhvaṃsayavidhvaṃsaya viśveśvara yogeśvara maheśvara namaste'stu mā mā hiṃsīḥ huṃ phaṭnamaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.4) kālāya karālāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.5) kṛtāntāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.6) amoghāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.7) aghorāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.8) anivartāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.9) bhagāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.10) bhagapramathanāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.11) vṛṣabhāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.12) indranetrāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.13) suvarṇacūḍāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.14) hāhāhīhī namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.15) namastīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrāya bhīṣaṇāya sahasrapādāyānantaśīrṣāya vāmanāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.16) mahāvaktrāya piṅgalanetrāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.17) khanakhanāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.18) ghanaghanāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.19) ghusughusāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.20) alepāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.21) paśave namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.22) mahāpaśupataye namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.23) ucchuṣmāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,9.24) ucchuṣmarudrāya namaḥ svāhā ||
(36,10.1) eṣāṃ krameṇa kṛtyāni vakṣyamāṇāni yojayet | aṣṭasahasrābhijaptamanyaddravyaṃ tu homataḥ ||
(36,10.2) śatrornāmnā lavaṇasya sahasramaṣṭakādhikam | hutvā dhanāyuṣorhānirjvareṇa sa ca śuṣyati ||
(36,10.3) kṣipraṃ śāntirbhavettasya kṣīrahomāttu tāvataḥ ||
(36,11.1) kaṇaiḥ puttalikāṃ kṛtvā gośṛṅgeṇārghadhūpabe | aṣṭasahasrābhijaptaṃ madanasya tu kaṇṭakam || tenāṣṭādaśavedhāttu mūlakṣīrānnivartanam ||
(36,12.1) dadhnā ca madhusarpirbhyāṃ ttrivarṇaiḥ sarṣapairhutaiḥ || gaurairaṣṭasahasreṇa japtairāveśayedripūn ||
(36,13.1) dadhyādyabhyaktalājānāṃ homādaṣṭasahasrataḥ || nāśayetsatatajvaraṃ dvitīyādiṃ ca dūrataḥ ||
(36,14.1) parijapya darbheṣīkāṃ kumbhakārādiveśmasu | nyastvā pākaṃ surāpākaṃ kaivartādi vināśayet ||
(36,15.1) akṣataistaṇḍulaiḥ kṛtvā pratidehaṃ suśobhanam | saṃsthāpya dhānyarāśau taṃ candanāduru dāhayet | baliṃ trimadhuraṃ dattvā syātsa rāśiścaturguṇaḥ ||
(36,16.1) khādiraṃ kīlakaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ tailāktaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam | parijaptaṃ grāmamadhye nikhanetsadya udvaset ||
(36,16.2) mahāpātakadoṣeṇa grāmī nirdhanatāṃ vrajet | kṣīreṇa kīlakasnānātkuryāttuṣṭastu śāntikam ||
(36,16.3) kṣīrasyāṣṭasahasraṃ ca juhuyāttadanantaram ||
(36,17.1) kalāpamantrāṃ guṭikāṃ tannāmnā gavyamāṃsataḥ | mahāpātakasaṃbandhājjāyate'sya dhanakṣayaḥ ||
(36,18.1) trivarṇasarṣapairhomātsaha trimadhureṇa tu | saṃpadyate sutastasya medhāvī śrutadhārakaḥ | taddhomātke cidicchanti unmattatvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
(36,19.1) tilā dūrvā trimadhuraṃ homato vyādhinigraham | taṇḍulaprakṣepaśca ||
(36,20.1) tryaktodumbarasamidho dogdhrī dhenvaṣṭakapradāḥ | ekāhaṃ bhaikṣabhugbhūtvā māsāṣṭakayutasya vā ||
(36,21.1) prādeśāntaṃ bilvavṛkṣaṃ mūlaśākhāsamanvitam | kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ sāyaṃ hutvā tu rukmabhāk ||
(36,22.1) samidhāṃ vaitasīnāṃ tu agnāvarkendhanāddhute | ahorātrikahomātsyātparjanyo bahuvarṣadaḥ | lakṣatrayaṃ bhaikṣāhāro japtvā karmaitadārabhet ||
(36,23.1) dugdhāktān sarṣapān hutvā tasmādbhasma mukhe kṣipet | sarveṣu vyavahāreṣu sa bhavatyaparājitaḥ ||
(36,24.1) śastraṃ japtamupādāya raṇe grasto na jīyate | khanakhanāyeti mantraḥ pūrvasevārtha ucyate ||
(36,24.2) uttarasyā viśeṣādvā cedānīmata uttaram | khādiratryaktasamidhāṃ pūrvasevā sahasrataḥ | atasīsamidhāmevaṃ medhāvī viduṣāṃ prabhuḥ ||
(36,25.1) gocarmamātraṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ gomayenopalepayet | tatrāgniṃ trikapāleṣu jvālayitvā praṇamya ca ||
(36,25.2) śirasā vānareṇātha mukhavādyaṃ tu kārayet | yatra tacchrūyate tatra āgacchanti varastriyaḥ ||
(36,25.3) damṣṭrāghaṇṭāninādāstu jvālāmukhabhayānakāḥ | yattvaṃ kāmayase putra tatsarvaṃ dadmahe vayam ||
(36,25.4) iti bruvatyaḥ sarvāstā yatra homaḥ kṛto bhavet | tadbhasmanā tu saṃspṛṣṭāśchāgalyaḥ suprabhāvataḥ ||
(36,26.1) lakṣajāpottaraṃ gatvā nadīmudadhigāminīm | vālukāsthaṇḍile liṅgaṃ tanmayaṃ tajjasadmani ||
(36,26.2) padmāṣṭaśatamāhṛtya pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ | udake nābhimātre ca suprabhātaṃ punarjapet ||
(36,26.3) tato māṇḍaliko rājā dīnārāṇāṃ gavāṃ śatam | praṇamya śraddhayā tasmai dadyāduddhara māmiti ||
(36,27.1) lakṣajapādabādhyastu paśūnāṃ damṣṭriśṛṅgiṇām | itareṣāṃ paśūnāṃ tu lakṣatritayavardhanam ||
(36,28.1) saṃjaptaśivanirmālyadānādunmattatāṃ vrajet || śamāya candanaṃ dadyāttriṣvetaṃ mantrasaṃskṛtam ||
(36,29.1) samādhinānumantritaṃ gośṛṅgamarimandire | nikhātaṃ sadya evainaṃ mandiraṃ paridīpayet ||
(36,30.1) tīkṣṇatailaṃ kaṭu proktaṃ darvī grāmasruvastathā | trimadhuraṃ tvatra vijñeyaṃ madhusarpistilātmakam ||
(36,30.2) saṃmukhaṃ mānasaṃ dhyāyañśubhaṃ karma prayojayet | vimukhaṃ bhañjanādau tu naraḥ karmaṇi siddhibhāk ||
(36,30.3) aṣṭottarastrisāhasro homo hāsya prakīrtitaḥ | kīlakāstrādi yaccānyattatsahasrābhimantritam ||
(36.col) ityucchuṣmakalpaḥ samāptaḥ || iti pariśiṣṭānāṃ pūrvārdhaṃ samāptamiti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_37. samuccayaprāyaścittāni) [resemblesin forṃ the 13 hadhyāya of the Kauśikasūtra]
(37,1.1) oṃ bhṛgvaṅgirorūpadhāriṇe śivāya namaḥ || atha yatraitadapahanyamāne musalaṃ patati tadghoraṃ bhavati tadapyetadṛcoktam ||
(37,1.2) ulūlhalānmusalaṃ patitaṃ hinasti patnīṃkule jyeṣṭham | kṛṣīḥ prajāḥ paśavaḥ saṃviśeante yathendrasṛṣṭaṃ prapateta vajramiti ||
(37,1.3) tadvajraṃ śāntyudakena saṃprokṣya arātīyorityullikhya yattvā śikva iti prakṣālya barhiṣyādhāya juhoti ||
(37,1.4) vajraḥ patitastu varaṃ hinasti taṃ tvā vayamapahanma ghoram | sa naḥ śivo'stu dviṣatāṃ vadhāya sapatnānme dviṣato hantu sarvān ||
(37,1.5) yadvatprajāḥ papanayaddhastādyadi volūkhalāt | sapatnānme paripāhi māṃ tvevaṃ paripāhi naḥ ||
(37,1.6) yadyantarikṣe yadi vāsi soccairvajraḥ sṛṣṭo yadi vā pārthivairuta | mantrāḥ prayuktā vitatā mahānto'ghoro vajro musalaprapātaḥ ||
(37,1.7) vajro'si sapatnaheti tisraḥ ||
(37,1.8) vajro'si sapatnahā tvayādya vṛtraṃ sākṣīya | tvāmadya vanaspate vṛkṣāṇāmudayuṣmahi ||
(37,1.9) sa na indrapurohito viśvataḥ pāhi rakṣasaḥ | abhi gāvo anūṣatābhi dyumnaṃ bṛhaspate ||
(37,1.10) prāṇa prāṇaṃ trāyasvāso asave mṛḍa | nirṛte nirṛtyā naḥ pāśebhyo muñca iti ||
(37,1.11) tyamū ṣu trātāramindraḥ sutrāmā sa sutrāmā ā mandrairindra marmāṇi te varmaṇā chādayāmīti ulūkhalamusale saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homānulūkhalamannenaiva pūrayitvā pratipravartayedulūkhalamusalaṃ vasanaṃ ca gāṃ ca kartre dadyātsā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,2.1) atha yatkāko'bhimṛśati tanmṛtyumāśaṅkyaṃ bhavati || tadapyetadṛcoktam ||....antakāya mṛtyava ā rabhasva prāṇāya nama iti svāheti agnau hutvā sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,3.1) athāta ājyasthālī cyavate pracalati vā kā tatra prāyaścittiḥ || saṃnatimahāvyāhṛtisāvitrīkūṣmāṇḍyaḥ sa sarvābhirjuhuyātsā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,4.1) atha pavitram <cet> praṇaśyeta karmamadhyātpramādataḥ | anyacchittvā mantrayeta karmaśeṣamupakramet | ātmendriyasamāyuktaṃ tena mantreṇa kārayet ||
(37,4.2) vāyoḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa yanme chidraṃ punarmaitvindriyaṃ mā na āpo medhāṃ mā no medhāṃ mā naḥ piparidaśvineti saṃnatibhirājyaṃ juhuyādvyāhṛtibhiśca gāṃ ca kartre dadyātsā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,5.1) atha yasyāsamāpte karmaṇi barhirādīpyeta tatastannirvāpya juhuyāt ||
(37,5.2) yadagnirbarhiradahadvedyā vāso apāṃ tataḥ | tvameva no jātavedo duritātpāhi tasmāt ||
(37,5.3) nirdagdhā no amitrāstu yathedaṃ barhistathā | amitrāṇāṃ śriyaṃ bhūtiṃ tāmeṣāṃ pari nirjahi ||
(37,5.4) yatkāmāste juhumastanno astu viśāmpate | ye devā yajñamāyānti te no rakṣantu sarvataḥ ||
(37,5.5) avadagdhaṃ duḥsvapnyamavadagdhā arātayaḥ | sarvāśca yātudhānyaḥ ||
(37,5.6) mā tvā dabhan yātudhānā mā bradhnaḥ śamyumicchata | darbho rājā samudriyaḥ pari naḥ pātu viśvataḥ ||
(37,5.7) ato'nyadbarhirupakalpyodakena saṃprokṣya punaḥ stṛṇāti ||
(37,5.8) idaṃ barhiramṛteneha siktaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ haritaṃ te stṛṇāmi | tadvai purāṇamabhinavaṃ stṛṇīṣva vāsaḥ praśastaṃ prati me gṛhāṇetyanyena ca barhiṣābhiprachādaye ||
(37,6.1) āhutyāṃ tu gṛhītāyāṃ hutocchiṣṭaṃ pramādataḥ | tamāhutiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya śaṃ no devīrityācamya brahmāparamityardharcenemāṃ hutvā brahmajyeṣṭheti hutvā sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,7.1) keśakīṭāvapannā cecchaṃbhuvāya svāheti bhasmani hutvā havirutpūyānyāṃ juhuyātsā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,8.1) atha ceccalitadantaṃ patitadantaṃ vopanayettatra prāyaścittamāha gṛhe vā barhiḥ [vā] pitā vācāryo vā dvādaśarātraṃ dīkṣeyātāṃ kartā trirātraṃ gaurasarṣapasarpiḥpayobhiḥ snātaḥ prayataḥ śuciḥ śuklavāsāḥ paurṇamāsaṃ tantramājyabhāgānte sāvitrīmanuyojayettena śāntyudakenainamācāmayati saṃprokṣati ca ||
