(37,8.2) sāvitrī śāntirbrahma jajñānaṃ ye triṣaptā agniṃ brūma āyuṣyavarcasyasvastyayanābhayāparājitaśarmavarmabhirjuhuyāttaṃ saṃpātya yaḥ śramāttapaso yo vetasaṃ yo bhūtamūrdhvā asyedāvatsarāya yadyantarikṣe punarmaitvindriyamityāplāvayati sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||
(37,9.1) naśyeccenmadughamaṇiḥ śāmyedvāgnirvivāhajaḥ | atyadbhutaṃ dvayamidaṃ dampatyostu vināśanam ||
(37,9.2) pūtudārumaṇistatra bandhyo mantrāśca mādughāḥ | pūtudāru na vindyāccedyavaṃ tatra niyojayet ||
(37,9.3) āyuṣmantau suprajasau suvīrau dhātā pūṣā draviṇe nau dadhātu | vimuñcatāṃ śamalaṃ kilbiṣaṃ nau dīrghamāyuśca savitā kṛṇotviti śāntyudakenāṅguliṃ saṃprokṣya badhnīyāt
(37,9.4) samidho'bhyādadhyādupatiṣṭeta saṃnatibhirvyāhṛtibhirjuhuyādgāṃ ca kartre dadyātsā atra prāyaścittḥ ||
(37,10.1) omatha yasya tantre'praṇīto'huto'gnirupaśāmyati || punastvādityā ityagniṃ prāṇīya saṃnatibhirvyāhṛtibhiḥ samāstvāgne'bhyarcateti ca sūktābhyāṃ juhuyātparisaṃkhyāhomāṃśca ||
(37,11.1) atha yatraitadvivāhagnirupaśāmyati agnipraṇayanamantraiḥ prājāpatyaṃ praṇīya prāktantraṃ praṇīya yaddevā yadvidvāṃso'pamityamapratītaṃ yaddhastābhyaṃ yadadīvyannityetaiḥ sūktairājyaṃ juhuyātsamidho'bhyādadhyādupatiṣṭheta vāsaḥ kartre dadyāt ||
(37,12.1) atha yatraitatkālātītāsu kriyāsvatīta uttarāyaṇe ājyabhāgānte yanme skannaṃ yadasmṛtītyanumantrayettasmai prācyā diśo antardeśāditi paryāyānekaviṃśatiṃ juhuyātsaṃskārātīte ca karmaṇi ||
(37,13.1) atha yatraitatprāṇīto'gnirupaśāmyati || yadyantarikṣe punarmaitvindriyaṃ punastvādityā ityagniṃ praṇīya prajvālya mamāgne varca iti samidhamādhāya śeṣaṃ karma samāpayet ||
(37,14.1) atha yasyopayāmo'vapateddhastātsa yanme upayāma ityādadīta,
(37,14.2) yanme upayāmo'pataddhastādya āyuṣā pariṣkṛtaḥ | tamahaṃ punarādade ||
(37,14.3) punarindraḥ punarbhagaḥ punarme brahmaṇaspatiḥ | brahma jīvitu dādity[ādadīta]
(37,14.4) yanme chidraṃ yadasmṛtīti juhuyāt ||
(37,15.1) yanme sruvo'pataddhastādityupayāmena vyākhyātam ||
(37,16.1) mekhalādīni cetplaveran punarupanayeta || vimocanīyān homān hutvānyaṃ brāhmaṇamanūcānamupaveśyodapātraṃ cāparājitena niṣkramya vāso yajñopavītādi dattvābhyukṣyācamyāpāṃ sūktaiḥ pavitraiśca saṃprokṣya priyaṃ mā kṛṇu deveṣviti yajñopavītaṃ dattvā vimṛgvarīṃ mā naḥ paścāditi dvābhyāṃ prāṅmukha upaviśya mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ sāvitrī śāntisūktaṃ brahma jajñānaṃ yadasmṛtyanumatiḥ sarvamiti juhuyādabhyātānaiśca ||
(37,17.1) atha yatraitanmekhalā prapatati jīrṇā vā syāttāṃ sāvitryoddhṛtyāntaṃ kṛtvā śāntyudakena saṃprokṣya mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ sāvitrī śāntisūktaṃ brahma jajñānaṃ ye triṣaptā idāvatsarāya ghṛtena tvāgniṃ brhūma iti ||
(37,18.1) caturthyāmahutāyāṃ yadi ghaṭodakaṃ naśyeta tathaiva punarānīya śaṃ no devīriheta devīrityanumantryāmbayo yantyāpo hi ṣṭhā śaṃ no devīriti saṃpātya vyāhṛtyā saṃnatyā ca juhuyātāpo bhṛgvaṅgirorūpamapāṃ puṣpamityudakumbhamabhimantrayet || vāso dakṣiṇā ||
(37,19.1) atha yasyāsamāpte karmaṇyudapātraṃ pravarteta tadanumantrayate
(37,19.2) yadudapātraṃ pravartate brahmaṇāsthāpitaṃ mahat | sthānāccyutaṃ pravartitaṃ tanme vahatu kilbiṣam || ityāsthāpayati ||
(37,19.3) pūraṇena pūrayitvā punaḥ pūrṇamityetayā |
(37,19.4) punaḥ pūrṇamidaṃ pātraṃ brahmaṇāsthāpayāmasi | viśvais[tad] devairabhiṣṭutam ||
(37,19.5) ūrjaṃ puṣṭaṃ dadhātu no rāyaspoṣaṃ śriyamāyuḥ | mayi karma samṛdhyatāmiti ||
(37,20.1) atha cetprabhajyeta bhūmirbhūmimagādityanumantryānyataramāhṛtya yadyantarikṣe punarmaitvindriyamityanumantrya vaiśvānaro na ūtaya udenaṃ vaiśvānaro raśmibhiriti juhuyātsā tatra prāyaścittiḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_38. brahmakūrcavidhiḥ)
(38,1.1) oṃ brahmakūrcavidhiṃ puṇyaṃ saṃkṣepādvacmyasaṃśayam | pāvanānāṃ paraṃ yo hi pāvanaṃ tapasāṃ tapaḥ ||
(38,1.2) snātvā śuciḥ śucau deśe gomayenāvasecite | vastreṇa saṃhite cāpi sitapuṣpaiḥ prapūjite ||
(38,1.3) ahorātrositaḥ kṣāntaḥ pavitrātmā prapāvanaḥ | śuklavāsāḥ sugandhiḥ prāgupaviṣṭaḥ kuśāsane ||
(38,1.4) gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam | āharettāmrapātre tu śakṛnmūtraṃ tvabhūgatam ||
(38,1.5) gomūtraṃ nīlavarṇāyāḥ kṛṣṇāyā gomayaṃ tathā | payastu tāmravarṇāyāḥ śvetāya āhareddadhi ||
(38,1.6) kapilāyā ghṛtaṃ grāhyamalābhe syāttu pañcamam ||
(38,2.1) gomūtraikapalaṃ dadyādaṅguṣṭhāgraṃ tu gomayam | kṣīrasya sapta dadhnastu trīṇyekaikaṃ ghṛtāmbhasoḥ ||
(38,2.2) gāyatryāhṛtya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam | ā pyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ||
(38,2.3) tejo'si śukramityajyaṃ devasya tvā kuśodakam | saptapattrāstu ye darbhā achinnāgrāḥ samāyutāḥ ||
(38,2.4) taiḥ samuddhṛtya hotavyaṃ devatābhyo yathākarmam | agnaye svāhā somāya prajāpataya ityapi ||
(38,2.5) bṛhaspate ati yadidaṃ viṣṇuritīti ca | mānastokena gāyatryā etaiśca juhuyāttataḥ ||
(38,2.6) praṇavena praṇavenaiva pibecca praṇavena tu | hotavyaṃ praṇavenaiva pibecca praṇavena tu ||
(38,3.1) madhyamena palāśasya padmapattreṇa vā pibet | api vā tāmrapātreṇa hutaśeṣaṃ viśuddhaye ||
(38,3.2) yattvagasthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati dehinām | brahmakūrco dahetsarvaṃ pradīpto'gnirivendhanam ||
(38,3.3) trayodaśyādicaturṣu triḥsnānākṣārabhojanam | pañcadaśyāṃ pañcagavyaṃ sopavāsaṃ mahāphalam ||
(38,3.4) abhojyābhakṣyaśūdrānnabhakṣaṇe vedavikraye | pratigrahe kālamantrahīne home dyumaithune ||
(38,3.5) bālatve yatkṛtaṃ caiva yuvā vṛddhavayāstathā | mātāpitṛkṛtaṃ caiva tatkṣaṇādeva nirdahet ||
(38,3.6) māsemāse prayuñjānodevalokamavāpnuyāt | ardhamāseardhamāse ca ṛṣīṇāṃ lokamuttamam ||
(38,3.7) ṣaḍrātre caiva ṣaḍrātre brahmalokamanāmayam | aharahaḥ prayuñjānaḥ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati ||
(38,3.8) anena vidhinaiveṣṭvā devatarpaṇapūrvakam | brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ hyetatpavitraṃ paramaṃ hitam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_39. taḍāgādividhiḥ)
(39,1.1) oṃ pippalādaṃ mahāprājñamidamūcurmaharṣayaḥ | bhūmikhātasya śuddhyarthaṃ vidhiṃ prabrūhi tattvataḥ ||
(39,1.2) kūpavāpītaḍāgeṣu puṣkariṇyāṃ ca veśmasu | ahorātroṣito bhūtvā tataḥ karma samārabhet ||
(39,1.3) śāntyudakena tadbrahmā pūrvoktaṃ yadagastinā | pari prāgāditi dvābhyāmabhimantrayate tataḥ ||
(39,1.4) carutantreṇa vidhinā caruṃ bhaumaṃ tu nirvapet | prāktantramājyabhāgāntaṃ satyaṃ bṛhaditi smṛtam ||
(39,1.5) havirājyaṃ samidhaśca upasthānaṃ yathākramam | kūpādīnāṃ samīpe tu japenmantrān samāhitaḥ ||
(39,1.6) ambayo yantyāpo hi ṣṭhā śaṃ no devīriti trayam | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ punantu mā sasruṣīrhimavato'psu te ||
(39,1.7) japtvā tu dhenumānītāṃ pibantīmanumantrayet | ā gāva iti sūktena tārayettu tathaiva gām ||
(39,1.8) kūpavāpītaḍāgānāṃ samīpe cābhimantrayet | upa hvaye sūyavasātkartre dadyāttu gāṃ śubhām ||
(39,1.9) kāmasūktena gṛhṇīyātkarma saṃsthāpayettataḥ | hiraṇyarajatādīnāṃ matsyādīn kārayedbudhaḥ ||
(39,1.10) sauvarṇau kūrmamakarau rājatau matsyamudgarau | tāmrau kulīrakarkaṭāvāyasaḥ śiśumārakaḥ ||
(39,1.11) śāntyudakaṃ pañcagavyaṃ tasminneva jale kṣipet | kartṛdātārau snāyetāṃ drupadādiva śumbhanī ||
(39,1.12) brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ | samyagvidhānametaddhi kūpādeḥ saṃprakīrtitam | puṇyaṃ karma dvijātīnāṃ svargasyākṣayyamicchatām ||

(pariśiṣṭa_40. pāśupatavratam)
(40,1.1) omatha pāśupatavratādeśo
(40,1.2) nāśrotriyāya nācaritavedavratāya nākṛtavapanāya dadīta ||
(40,1.3) māsadvitricatuṣpañcasaṃvatsaradvādaśasaṃvatsaraparimitaṃ naiṣṭhikaṃ vā
(40,1.4) athāsyāyatanāni ||
(40,1.5) mahādevāyatane'pāṃ samīpe ||
(40,1.6) giriguhāyāṃ gavāṃ goṣṭhe'gnyāgāre vā
(40,1.7) nadīnāṃ bahūnāṃ pratiśraye
(40,1.8) anusavanam ||
(40,1.9) bhasmanā snānaṃ raudrahomasnapanaṃ ca sarpiḥkṣīragandhodakair
(40,1.10) gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpodanapāyasayāvakalājādi pradakṣiṇāntaṃ ca ||
(40,1.11) nivedya nirmālyagandhahārīhāsagītavādanādyupahārān
(40,1.12) dakṣiṇena tṛtīyamupatiṣṭhate
(40,1.13) kaṭakakeyūradhāriṇe namo vṛṣāya namo vṛṣabhadhvajāya namo
(40,1.14) vānaraṃ te mukhaṃ raudramanindyaṃ śubhaṃ paśumevājananevājanakaṃ ghoraṃ jīvaṃ jātyameva rukmaṃ dadāmīti ekavāsā vivāsā vā virāgāṇi vastrāṇi dadīta ||
(40,2.1) gocarmamātraṃ sthaṇḍilamupalipya gomayenollikhyābhyukṣyāgne prehītyagniṃ praṇīyopasamādhāya paristīrya brahmāṇaṃ kalpayitvā nānyadevatādiśi rudrasya dakṣiṇodapātraṃ sthāpayitvā mahāvyāhṛtibhiragnyāyatane nidhāya rudramāvāhayati ||
(40,2.2) rudraṃ kruddhāśanimukhaṃ devānāmīśvaraṃ param | śvetapiṅgalaṃ devānām <mahādevam> prapadye śaraṇāgataḥ ||
(40,2.3) yasya yuktā rathe siṃhā vyāghrāśca viṣamānanāḥ | tamahaṃ pauṇḍarīkākṣaṃ devamāvāhaye śivamityāvāhyābhyarcya ||
(40,2.4) na taṃ yakṣmaitu deva iti gugguluṃ dhūpaṃ ca dadyāt ||
(40,2.5) tatpuruṣāya vidmahe mahādevāya dhīmahi | tanno rudraḥ pracodayāt ||
(40,2.6) tasmai devāya vidmahe mahādevāya dhīmahi | tanno rudro'numanyatām || iti rudrasāvitrīṃ japtvā ||
(40,2.7) yo agnau rudra ityanumantrayennamo astu yāvadāvāhane devadevasyāvāhayāmyahamiti ||
(40,2.8) pramardane sarvāsuravināśāya oṃ phaṭkāraṃ karoti ||
(40,2.9) nivedane'hamamukaṃ nivedayāmīti jaṭī muṇḍī pañcaśikhī vā ||
(40,3.1) brāhmaṇo ha vā ahamamukasagotro bhagavato maheśvarasya vrataṃ cariṣyāmīti vācayitvā ||
(40,3.2) tato'sya mauñjīṃ prayacchati || sāvitryā tu daṇḍaṃ pālāśaṃ bailvamāśvatthaṃ vā asiṃ lakuṭaṃ khaṭvāṅgaṃ paraśuṃ vā ||
(40,3.3) aghorebhyo'tha ghorebhyo'ghoraghoratarebhyaśca | sarvataḥ śarvaśarvebhyo namaste rudra rūpebhya ityādau śarvaṃ namaskṛtyopaviśyājyaṃ niratiśāyitvedhmānādīpayatyantara iti
(40,3.4) idhmā jātavedasaḥ samiddhasya tebhyo vardhayasva prajayā paśubhiḥ śriyā gṛhairdhaneneti ||
(40,3.5) yavāghārau ājyabhāgau juhuyād
(40,3.6) vāyave svāhā || śarvāya rudrāya svāhā || paśupataye bhīmāya svāhā || śāntāyādhipataye devāya svāheti ||
(40,3.7) evameva patnīnāṃ tūṣṇīmadhipasya juhuyād
(40,3.8) evaṃ sarveṣu vratanivedaneṣu vrātapatīrjuhoti ||
(40,3.9) vratena tvamityubhayīruhamiti pañcabhī raudrān homān hutvā homāvasānena bhasmanā snānaṃ karoti ||
(40,4.1) bhasmasnānam [tāvad] grahīṣyāmi sarvapāpapraṇāśanam | bhasmasnānena rudro hi snāto'bhūtpūta ātmanā ||
(40,4.2) bhasmanā snāyate rudro viṣṇuḥ snāyate bhasmanā | tena snānena snāmyahaṃ yena snāto maheśvaraḥ ||
(40,4.3) yena snātā umā devī rudro bhartā maheśvaraḥ | yena snātā gaṇāḥ sarve yena snātā dvijātayaḥ ||
(40,4.4) yena snātā śivaḥ śarvaḥ śaṃkaraśca vṛṣadhvajaḥ | snātāni sarvabhūtāni gaṅgāyamunayāgame ||
(40,4.5) snāto'haṃ sarvatīrtheṣu nadīprasravaṇeṣu ca | vāruṇāgneyasaumyānāṃ bhasmanā snānamuttamam | tena snānena snāmyahaṃ yena snāto maheśvaraḥ ||
(40,5.1) bhūtistu piṅgalo babhrurbhūtirviṣṇuḥ sanātanaḥ | bhūtirbrahmā mahendraśca bhūtirdevāḥ saha rṣibhiḥ ||
(40,5.2) bhūtirme'lakṣmīṃ nirṇudedbhūtirme śriyamāvahet | bhūtirma āyuṣā vittaṃ varco brahma prayacchatu ||
(40,5.3) bhasmanā caranto nityaṃ dhyāyinaḥ paricintakāḥ | yānti pāśupataṃ sthānaṃ punarāvṛttidurlabham ||
(40,5.4) vācā tu yatkṛtaṃ karma manasā ca vicintitam | alakṣmīścāpadduḥsvapnaṃ bhasmanā tatpraṇaśyatu ||
(40,5.5) mokṣaṇaṃ mokṣakāle ca bhasmaśeṣaṃ visarjayet | mukto'haṃ sarvapāpebhyo rudralokaṃ vrajāmyaham ||
(40,6.1) etatsnānaṃ vāruṇaṃ parvasu śarīralepena yathākāmaṃ parvasūpavaset ||
(40,6.2) strīśūdraṃ nābhibhāṣeta ||
(40,6.3) tadā sāvitrīṃ japet ||
(40,6.4) yadi bhāṣeta tadā rudrasāvitrīṃ japet ||
(40,6.5) kamaṇḍalukapāle bhinne bhūmirbhūmimagādityapsu praveśayet ||
(40,6.6) retaḥskande yanme retastejasā saṃniṣadya dehātpraskandetpunarna bhavāya | tadagnirvāyuḥ... ...api ceyaṃ pṛthivī kañcakhanteti ||
(40,6.7) samyakkva citkaroti
(40,6.8) vratamupādhyāyāchando vartayet ||
(40,6.9) tata udīkṣaṇam ||
(40,6.10) vrātapatīrjuhoti ||
(40,6.11) samāso'haṃ vratasviṣṭakṛta iti hutvādityābhimukhastiṣṭheta ||
(40,6.12) yanme duruktaṃ durhutaṃ durdhyātaṃ durvicintitam | tanme bhagavānīśānaḥ sarvaṃ tvaṃ kṣantumarhasi ||
(40,6.13) navonavo bhavasi jāyamāna ityapsu pravāhayed
(40,6.14) ye śraddhayedaṃ paśupatervrataṃ caranti | teṣāṃ madhu viśakṣe he dadate na punargamanaṃ madhurivādyehaiva ca | te rudrā viratau paśupatisāyujyaṃ gaccha<n>ti
(40,6.14) tadeṣa ślokaḥ ||
(40,6.16) vilīnapāśapañjarāḥ samāptatattvagocarāḥ | prayānti śaṃkaraṃ paraṃ patiṃ vibhuṃ sadāśivam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_41. saṃdhyopāsanavidhiḥ)
(41,1.1) omathātaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(41,1.2) prāṅvodaṅvā grāmānniṣkramya śuciḥ śucau deśe guptatīrthāyataneṣu vā
(41,1.3) suprakṣālitapāṇipādavadanaḥ prāgvīrāsanenopaviśya jīvā sthetyācamyāpo hi ṣṭhetyabhyukṣya prāṇāyāmān kṛtvācamyottiṣṭhan dakṣiṇahastasthā āpo ayojālā ityapa utsṛjedbahudhā ||
(41,1.4) hariḥ suparṇa iti prātar
(41,1.5) udu tyaṃ citraṃ devānāmiti madhyaṃdine
(41,1.6) atha sauramantrān yathākāmaṃ japed
(41,1.7) udvayaṃ tamasasparīti ca
(41,1.8) udghedabhi śrutāmaghamityastamita āsīnaḥ
(41,1.9) sāvitryante vāñjalayo japaśca ||
(41,2.1) athordhvajānurāsīna iti vīrāsanī
(41,2.2) pratyutthāyābhayaṃ paścādabhayaṃ purastādityupatiṣṭhate
(41,2.3) tiṣṭhan prātaḥ prāṅmukha
(41,2.4) āyātu varadetyāvāhya
(41,2.5) gāyatraṃ chandaḥ savitā devatā viśvāmitra ṛṣir
(41,2.6) yathākṣaraṃ daivataṃ rūpaṃ ca manasi samādhāya mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ saṃdhāya gāyatrīṃ japet ||
(41,2.7) aṣṭakṛtva ekādaśakṛtvo dvādaśakṛtvaḥ pañcadaśakṛtvaḥ śatakṛtvaḥ sahasrakṛtva iti ||
(41,2.8) aṣṭakṛtvaḥ prayuktā gāyatrī gāyatreṇa chandasā saṃmitā bhūlokamabhijayati ||
(41,2.9) ekādaśakṛtvaḥ prayuktā traiṣṭubhena chandasā saṃmitāntarikṣalokamabhijayati ||
(41,2.10) dvādaśakṛtvaḥ prayuktā jāgatena chandasā saṃmitā divaṃ lokamabhijayati ||
(41,2.11) pañcadaśakṛtvaḥ prayuktā pañcadaśena vajreṇa saṃmitā brahmalokamabhijayati ||
(41,2.12) śatakṛtvaḥ prayuktā śataparvaṇā vajreṇa saṃmitā sarvāṇllokānabhijayati ||
(41,2.13) sahasrakṛtvaḥ prayuktāgniṣṭomāptoryāmādīnāṃ kratūnāṃ phalamavāpnoti ||
(41,3.1) ato yathākāmaṃ japitvā paśyema śaradaḥ śatamindra jīvetyāśiṣaḥ prārthayate ||
(41,3.2) stutā mayā varadeti visṛjyodīrāṇā uta sūryasyāvṛtamasapatnaṃ purastādyasmātkośāditi yathārthamupatiṣṭhate ||
(41,3.3) ya imāṃ na vindanti nādhīyate saṃdhyākāle nopāsate te hyaśrotriyā bhavantyanupanītāḥ kriyāhīnāśchedanabhedanabhojanamaithunānyabhicarantaḥ ||
(41,3.4) saṃdhyākāle hyajapantaḥ śvasūkarasṛgālakukkuṭasarpayoniṣu varṣasahasrāṇi jāyante ||
(41,3.5) samāstasyaivopajāyante ||
(41,3.6) tasmādyathoktāṃ sāyaṃ prātaḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīta ||
(41,3.7) araṇyacarito guptaḥ śuklabrāhmaṇakarmasu | prāyeṇa labhate lokān yathoktāṃstāṃ samācaran ||
(41,3.8) sāyaṃ saṃdhyāmupāsīta kṛtavīrāsano dvijaḥ | kṛtottānastathā prātaḥ prāñjaliḥ susamāhitaḥ ||
(41,3.9) etadvīrāsanaṃ sthānaṃ brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā | dvijānaṃ bālavṛddhānāṃ puraścaraṇamuttamam ||
(41,3.10) sāyaṃ prātastu yaḥ saṃdhyāmaskannāmupatiṣṭhate | sa tayā pāvito devyā brāhmahaḥ pūtakilbiṣaḥ ||
(41,3.11) na sīdetpratigṛhṇānaḥ pṛthvīmapi sasāgarām | ye cāsya viṣamāḥ ke ciddivi sūryādayo grahāḥ ||
(41,3.12) te cāsya saumyā jāyante śivāḥ sukhakarāḥ sadā | sthānaṃ vīrāsanaṃ caeṣāṃ pṛthivī ca pradakṣiṇā | agnihotraṃ hutaṃ caiṣāṃ ye vai saṃdhyāmupāsate ||
(41,4.1) ardhāstamita āditye ardhodite divākare | gāyatryāstatra sāṃnidhyaṃ saṃdhyākālaḥ sa ucyate ||
(41,4.2) bhūmyādityāntaraṃ yastu chādayeccaturaṅgulam | tāṃ tu saṃdhyāṃ parāṃ vidyācchāyāsaṃbhedane pare ||
(41,4.3) yāvantastu karādbhraṣṭāḥ patanti jalabindavaḥ | bhūtvā vajrāṇi te sarve patanti hyasureṣu vai ||
(41,4.4) tato vibhāvasusteṣāṃ prītātmāpyāyate varam | yairahaṃ mokṣito vipraisteṣāṃ loko tathā mama ||
(41,4.5) gāyatryā akṣamālāyāṃ sāyaṃ prātaḥ śataṃ japet | caturṇāṃ khalu vedānāṃ samagraṃ labhate phalam ||
(41,4.6) saṃdhyāṃ ye nopatiṣṭhanti brāhmaṇyaṃ nopapadyate | upapadyeta vā bhūyo yadi syustīrthamṛtyavaḥ ||
(41,4.8) ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvāddīrghāṇyāyumṣyadhārayan | tasmāddīrghāmupāsīta samicchan vṛddhimāyuṣaḥ ||
(41,5.1) tadyathāgnirdevānāṃ brāhmaṇo manuṣyāṇāṃ vasanta ṛtūnāmevaṃ gāyatrī chandasām ||
(41,5.2) tadyathā gāyatrī katyakṣarā katipadā kiṃ vāsyā gotraṃ kiṃ vāsya rūpaṃ kīdṛśaṃ tasyāḥ śarīraṃ bhavati ||
(41,5.3) yadvai bhūḥ sa ṛgvedo yadbhuva iti so'tharvaveda iti
(41,5.4) tadyathedamakṣaramomityakṣaraṃ tatparamaṃ śamityakṣaraṃ guhyaṃ tatparamaṃ pavitram ||
(41,5.5) ādityo vai sāvitryādityena saha sāvitrī stauti suvati prātaḥ prasuvati tasmātsāvitrīt<v>am ||
(41,5.6) akṣaradaivataṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
(41,5.7) prathamamāgneyaṃ dvitīyamāśvinaṃ tṛtīyaṃ saumyaṃ caturthaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ sāvitraṃ pañcamaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ pauṣṇaṃ saptamaṃ mārutamaṣṭamaṃ bārhaspatyaṃ navamaṃ maitraṃ daśamaṃ vāruṇamekādaśamaindraṃ dvādaśaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ vasūnāṃ trayodaśaṃ caturdaśaṃ rudrāṇāṃ pañcadaśamādityānāmaditeḥ ṣoḍaśaṃ vāyavyaṃ saptadaśamaṃ bhaumamaṣṭādaśamekonaviṃśamāntarikṣaṃ divyaṃ viṃśaṃ digdevatāni catvāryakṣarāṇi ||
(41,6.1) atha yatpūrvāṃ saṃdhyāmupāste tadgāyatryāḥ śirastena pṛthivīṃ jayati ||
(41,6.2) atha yanmadhyāhne tīkṣṇaṃ rudrastapati <tad> dvitīyaṃ śirastenāntarikṣaṃ jayati ||
(41,6.3) atha yadastamiyāttattṛtīyaṃ śirastena divaṃ jayati ||
(41,6.4) tasyā oṃkāraḥ śiraḥ saha vyāhṛtibhirdarbhāḥ keśā oṣadhīvanaspatayo lomāni cakṣuṣī sūryācandramasau vidyuddhasitaṃ viṣṇuvaruṇau urasī rudro hṛdaye paurṇamāsī cāmāvāsyā ca stanau ahaśca rātrī ca pārśve
(41,6.5) daśa diśaḥ kukṣī sarvajñānāni vyākaraṇamudaraṃ pṛthivī śroṇī vāyuḥ sthānaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ nakṣatrāṇi śrīsarasvatīrūpā padakramamantrabrāhmaṇakalpaśarīrā sāvitrī gotreṇa brahmadeyā bhavati brahmadeyā bhavatīti brāhmaṇam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_42. snānavidhiḥ)
(42,1.1) atha snānavidhiṃ puṇyaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ nibodhata | yena snātā divaṃ lokaṃ prāpnuvanti dvijottamāḥ ||
(42,1.2) saritsu vā taḍāge vā devakhāte hrade'pi vā | gartaprasravaṇe vāpi puṇyaṃ snānaṃ samācaret ||
(42,1.3) pārakye tu tāḍāge hi snānaṃ naiva vidhīyate | taḍāgakarturduṣkṛtairlipyate snānamācāran ||
(42,1.4) saritāṃ sarasāṃ caiva aprāptau nirjharasya ca | uddhṛtya caturaḥ piṇḍān snāyāttu parakhātake ||
(42,1.5) anvīkṣyamāṇaḥ pārśvāni kuśahastaḥ samāhitaḥ | dvijo madhyaṃdinādarvāgarogī snānamācaret ||
(42,1.6) yatte bhūma iti mṛdaṃ saṃgṛhya rcā samāhitaḥ | yaste gandha iti tribhirmṛdhirātmānamālabhet ||
(42,1.7) aghadviṣṭeti sūktena dūrvāṃ śirasi vinyaset | agramagramityetayā gomayenānulepayet ||
(42,1.8) agramagraṃ carantīnāmoṣadhīnāṃ vanevane | yanme rogaṃ ca śokaṃ ca tanme tvaṃ nuda gomaya ||
(42,1.9) praṇavādyā vyāhṛtīstu gāyatrīṃ ca śiroyutām | paṭhettriranavānaṃ hi prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate ||
(42,1.10) ambayo yantītyādīni trīṇi sūktāni hi kramāt | hiraṇyavarṇā yadado vāyoḥ pūtaḥ punantu mā ||
(42,2.1) vaiśvānaro raśmibhiśca tathāpsu ta iti smṛtam | etaiḥ saṃplāvayetsūktaistato'ghamarṣaṇam ||
(42,2.2) apo divyāśca saṃ māgne idamāpaḥ śivena mā | yadāpo naktamiti ca etatsyādaghamarṣaṇam ||
(42,2.3) yadāpo naktaṃ mithunaṃ cakāra yadvā dudroha duritaṃ purāṇam | hiraṇyavarṇāstata utpunantu pra mā muñcantu varuṇasya pāśāt ||
(42,2.4) sarasvatīṃ gayāṃ gaṅgāṃ naimiṣaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca | smṛtvā tīrthānipuṇyātni avagāhejjalaṃ tataḥ ||
(42,2.5) gacchataḥ snānakāryāya pitaraḥ saha daivataiḥ | pṛṣṭhataḥ tvanugacchanti tatsamīpaṃ jalārthinaḥ ||
(42,2.6) āśāṃ tyaktvā nivartante vastraniṣpīḍanena tu | tasmānna pīḍayedvastramakṛtvā pitṛtarpaṇam ||
(42,2.7) utsāhaṃ varuṇaḥ snāne agnirhotuḥ śriyaṃ haret | āyuṣyaṃ bhuñjato mṛtyustriṣu maunamataścaret ||
(42,2.8 d) snānavastre mṛdastisraḥ pradadyācchuddhihetunā | 8 b | snātvā paryukṣya vāso'nyacchodhye jaṅghe mṛdā punaḥ ||
(42,2.9) vastraniṣpīḍatoyena apavitrīkṛte hi te | uttirya vastraṃ niṣpīḍya japedādhyātmikāni tu ||
(42,2.10) adhyātmamasyavāmīyaṃ kautsaṃ kauṣmāṇḍikaṃ tathā | japtvātharvaśiraścaiva bhavāśarvīyameva ca ||
(42,2.11) prāṇāśca bhagavān kālaḥ puruṣo manyureva ca | ucchiṣṭo rohito vrātya etānyādhyātmikāni tu ||
(42,2.12) saṃvatsareṇa yatpāpaṃ kṛtaṃ ghoramavistaram | japtvaitāni tataḥ pāpānmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
(42,2.13) viṣāsahiṃ manasā hi japtvā gāyatrīṃ ca tathā tridivaṃ prāyāti | paribhraṣṭastridivāttapaḥkṣaye jātismaratvaṃ punareva vindate ||

(pariśiṣṭa_43. tarpaṇavidhiḥ)
(43,1.1) omatha tarpaṇavidhimanukramiṣyāmaḥ ||
(43,1.2) snātopasparśanakāle'vagāhya devatāstarpayati ||
(43,1.3) vasūnāṃ namo
(43,1.4) brahmaṇe namo
(43,1.5) vaiśravaṇāya namo
(43,1.6) dharmāya namaḥ
(43,1.7) kāmāya namo
(43,1.8) lokāya amo
(43,1.9) devāya namo

(43,6.1) yāṃ kāṃ citsaritaṃ gatvā kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm | ekaikasya tilairmiśrān dadyāttrīnudakāñjalīn ||
(43,6.2) ā yāteti hi tisṛbhiḥ pitṝnāvāhayettataḥ | udīratāmiti tisṛbhiḥ pitṛbhyo dadyāttilodakam ||
(43,6.3) nābhimātre jale sthitvā cintayenmanasā pitṝn | tathā mātāmahebhyaśca śucau deśe'tha barhiṣi ||
(43,6.4) parā yātetyetayā pitṝṃstṛptān visarjayet | mano nvā hvāmahītyevaṃ pañcabhirmana upāhvayeta ||
(43,6.5) etaddhi tarpaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ svayamuktaṃ svayaṃbhuvā | śraddhadhānaḥ samācaṣṭe brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_44. śrāddhavidhiḥ)
(44,1.1) omathātaḥ śrāddhavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(44,1.2) catuṣprakāraṃ śrāddhaṃ bhavati ||
(44,1.3) nityamābhyudayikaṃ kāmyamekoddiṣṭaṃ ceti ||
(44,1.4) tatra nityamamāvāsyāyām ||
(44,1.5) ābhyudayikaṃ mātṛpūrvakaṃ puṃsavanādiṣu saṃskāreṣu ||
(44,1.6) kāmyaṃ tithidravyabrāhmaṇasaṃyoge ||
(44,1.7) ekoddiṣṭaṃ saṃcayanaprabhṛtyā sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt ||
(44,1.8) tatra nitye yugmān daive brāhmaṇānupāmantrayet ||
(44,1.9) ābhyudayike ubhayatra yugmān ||
(44,1.10) yavaistilārthā ṛjavo darbhāḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt ||
(44,1.11) kāmyaṃ tu nityavat ||
(44,1.12) athaikoddiṣṭaṃ tūṣṇīṃ yāvaduktam ||
(44,1.13) nāgnevaṃ na daivamayugmānbrāhmaṇān ||
(44,1.14) dakṣiṇāmukha upaviśya pitryeṇopacaryaikaṃ pavitramekamudapātramapratyāvṛttimāsanaṃ nāmagotreṇaikaṃ piṇḍametatte annamiti ||
(44,2.1) śve'dyeti vā śrāddhaṃ kariṣyāmīti brāhmaṇānupāmantrayet ||
(44,2.2) trīn pañca sapta vā na prasajyeta vistara iti vacanāt ||
(44,2.3) prāṅmukhān viśvedevānudaṅmukhān pitṝn ||
(44,2.4) vedavedāṅgavidaḥ pañcāgniranūcāno'vyavahārī śrotriyastriṇāciketastrimadhustrisuparṇī chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago'tharvaśiraso'dhyetā saṃdhyāsnāyī devapitṛsadāhniko mātṛpitṛśuśrūṣurbhṛgvaṅgiroviddharmaśāstraviditi ||
(44,2.5) prayato'parāhṇe śuciḥ śuklavāsāḥ ||
(44,2.6) svāgatenābhyarcyācamanīyaṃ kṛtvā dattvā brāhmaṇānupasaṃgṛhyopaveśayed
(44,2.7) daive pitrye ca sadarbheṣvāsaneṣu
(44,2.8) tato'nujñāpayeddevān pitṝṃścāvāhayiṣyāmīti
(44,2.9) āvāhaya saumyāste santvityanujñātaḥ pūrvaṃ devānāvāhayedviśve devāsa ā gateti ||
(44,2.10) viśve devāsa ā gata śṛṇutā ma imaṃ havam | edaṃ barhirni ṣīdata iti ||
(44,2.11) ā yāteti pitṝnāvāhayedācyā jānvityupaveśayetsaṃ viśaṃtviti saṃveśayediti ||
(44,3.1) yajñopavītī sāvitryodapātramabhimantrya
(44,3.2) viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ pādayamarghyamācamanīyamiti brāhmaṇahasteṣu ninayet ||
(44,3.3) trīṇyudapātrāṇi kalpayedgandhamālyatilairmiśrāṇi kṛtvā'
(44,3.4)'udīratāmiti tisṛbhirudapātrāṇyanvṛcaṃ sapavitreṣu brāhmaṇahasteṣu ninayet ||
(44,3.5) prapitāmahebhyaḥ pitāmahebhyaḥ pitṛbhyaśceti dattvā
(44,3.6) gandhamālyadhūpāñjanādarśapradīpasyopaharanam
(44,3.7) sarvānnaprakāramādāyāgnau kariṣyāmītyanujñāpya kuruṣvetyanujñāto darbhairdakṣiṇāgrairagniṃ paristīrya juhuyādagnaye kavyavāhanāyeti tisṛbhir
(44,3.8) hutaśeṣaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyād
(44,3.9) aṅguṣṭhamupayamya pradakṣiṇaṃ daive prasavyaṃ pitrya idaṃ viṣṇuriti japejjānuṃ niṣadya bhūmāv
(44,3.10) atastilairmāṃsaiḥ śākairyuṣaiḥ kṛsarāpāyasāpūpairlājairbhakṣairikṣuvikāraiḥ pānairmadhunā ghṛtena dadhnā payasā caiva prabhūtamṛṣṭato'nnaṃ dadyādanasūyaḥ ||
(44,4.1) pavitrapāṇirdharbheṣvāsīno madhu vātā iti japet
(44,4.2) pavitraṃ dharmaśāstramapratirathaṃ prāṇasūktaṃ puruṣasūktamupaniṣadamanyadvādhyātmikaṃ kiṃ cit
(44,4.3) tṛptāñjñātvānnaṃ prakīrya dattvā cāpaḥ sakṛtsakṛdannaṃ ye'gnidagdhā iti vikiram ||
(44,4.4) darbhirāstīrya dyuaurdarvirakṣiteti tisṛbhiḥ sarvānnaprakāramuddhṛtyājyena saṃnīya trīn piṇḍān saṃhatānnidadhāty
(44,4.5) etatte pratatāmaheti dakṣiṇataḥ patnībhya idaṃ vaḥ patnyā itīdamāśaṃsūnāmidamāśaṃsamānānāmityannena prasavyaṃ parikiranam || ye dasyava ityulmukenābhipariharaṇam
(44,4.6) ekoddiṣṭe tvekaṃ piṇḍamekamudapātramācamyopotthāya etaṃ bhāgametaṃ sadhasthāḥ śyeno nṛcakṣā iti ca śrāddhaṃ dattvābhimantrayeccheṣam
(44,4.7) anujñāpyācamanīyaṃ dattvā puṇyāhaṃ vācayeddakṣiṇāṃ ca dattvā yathāśaktyudapātraśeṣaṃ sapavitreṣu brāhmaṇahasteṣu ninayet
(44,4.8) putraṃ pautramityekamāpo agnimiti dvitīyaṃ yuktābhyāṃ tṛtīyaṃ putraṃ pautramityekayodapātramiti kauśikaḥ ||
(44,4.9) prapitāmahebhyaḥ pitāmahebhyaḥ pitṛbhyo mātulamātāmahebhyo nirdiṣṭaṃ tebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ sapatnīkebhyaḥ svadhāvadakṣayyamastvakṣayyamastviti vrāhmaṇavacanam ||
(44,4.10) dātāro no'bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ saṃtatireva ca | śraddhā ca no mā vyagamadbahudeyaṃ ca no'stvity
(44,4.11) evaṃ varān vācayitvā namo vaḥ pitara ityevamādi mano nvā hvāmahītyevamantaṃ samānaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñena madhyamapiṇḍapradānaṃ ceti ||
(44,4.12) vājevāje'vata vājino no dhaneṣu viprā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ | asya madhvaḥ pibata mādayadhvaṃ tṛptā yāta pathibhirdevayānaiḥ ||
(44,4.13) iti brāhmaṇān hasteṣu gṛhītvotthāpya pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryād
(44,4.14) eṣa śrāddhavidhiranena vidhinā putrān paśūn dhānyaṃ hiraṇyamāyuśca labhate ya evaṃ vedeti ca brāhmaṇam ||
(44,4.15) māhakiḥ kauśikācca māhakiḥ kauśikācceti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_45. agnihotrahomavidhiḥ)
(45,1.1) omagnihotram ||
(45,1.2) sāyamārambhaḥ prātarapavargaḥ ||
(45,1.3) nāntareṇānyatkuryāt ||
(45,1.4) prātarārambhamityeke ||
(45,1.5) yajñapātrāṇi prakṣālyāgnihotraṃ śrapayet ||
(45,1.6) śrapyamāṇaṃ cedviṣyandettadadbhirupaninayet
(45,1.7) tadanumantrayate pṛthivīṃ turīyamityetābhiḥ
(45,1.8) pratyānīyodagudvāsya barhirudapātramundayati paryukṣya ||
(45,1.9) ṛtaṃ tvā satyena pariṣiñcāmīti hoṣyan ||
(45,1.10) satyaṃ tva rteneti hute ||
(45,1.11) gārhapatyādāhavanīyamudakadhārāṃ ninayati || amṛtamasyamṛtamamṛtena saṃdhehīti ||
(45,1.12) āhavanīyaṃ paryukṣya gārhapatyaṃ prāpyāṅgārānavaloḍya carusthālyā saṃsparśayati ||
(45,1.13) nirūḍhaṃ japatyubhayamiti pratyūḍhamiti pratininayati
(45,1.14) sruvaṃ srucaṃ ca pratitapati niṣṭaptaṃ rakṣo niṣṭaptā arātayaḥ pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātaya ity
(45,1.15) adbhirabhyukṣya carusthālyāṃ sruveṇa sruci gṛhītamiti samānam
(45,1.16) srucamādāya <mukhasaṃmitāmud> gṛhyāhavanīyamabhikrāmatīdamahaṃ yajamānaṃ svargaṃ lokamunnayāmīti ||
(45,1.17) barhiṣi srucaṃ nidhāya samidhamādadhāti ||
(45,1.18) agnijyotiṣaṃ tvā vāyumatīṃ prāṇavatīṃ svargyāṃ svargāyopadadhāmi bhāsvatīṃ svāheti <sāyam || sūryajyotiṣamiti prātaḥ> ||
(45,1.19) samidhaṃ pradīptāmabhijuhoti ||
(45,1.20) sajūrdevena savitrā sajū rātryendravatyā juṣāṇo agnirvetu svāheti sāyam ||
(45,1.21) sajūrdevena savitrā sajūruṣasendravatyā juṣāṇaḥ sūryo vetu svāheti prātar
(45,1.22) āhutirudayahome'gnereva
(45,1.23) jyotiṣmānudetyāyaṃtanatāmiti ||
(45,1.24) prajāpate na tvadetānyanya iti manasaivobhayatra prājāpatyottarābhutīrhutvā
(45,1.25) sruvaṃ trirudañcamunnayati rudrān prīṇāmīti
(45,1.26) barhiṣi sruvaṃ nidhāyonmṛjya
(45,1.27) pitryupavītaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇataḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhāṃ karomīti ||
(45,2.1) hutamagnihotraṃ sarveṣvityeke ||
(45,2.2) carusthālyāḥ sruveṇa ||
(45,2.3) iha puṣṭiṃ puṣṭipatirdadhātviha prajāṃ janayatu prajāpatiḥ | agnaye gṛhapataye rayimate paśupataye puṣṭipataye svāheti gārhapatye ||
(45,2.4) agnaye'nnādāyānnapataye svāheti dakṣiṇāgnau hutvā ||
(45,2.5) manasaivobhayatra prajāpateścarusthālī
(45,2.6) srucaṃ sruvaṃ barhiṣyādhāyottarato'gnerupaviśya prāśnāti ||
(45,2.7) prāṇān prīṇāmītyupaspṛśya garbhān prīṇāmīti dvitīyaṃ viśvān devān prīṇāmītyantataḥ sarvam
(45,2.8) aprakṣālitayodakaṃ srucā ninayati sarpetarajanān prīṇāmīti
(45,2.9) barhiṣā prakṣālya sarpapuṇyajanān prīṇāmīti dvitīyam ||
(45,2.10) gandharvāpsarasaḥ prīṇāmīti apareṇāhavanīyamudakaṃ tṛtīyam ||
(45,2.11) sapta rṣīn prīṇāmīti srucaṃ sruvaṃ ca pratitapati
(45,2.12) dakṣiṇānnayāmīti rātrau srugdaṇḍamavamārṣṭi ||
(45,2.13) prātarunmārṣṭi ||
(45,2.14) ityuktaṃ samidādhānam
(45,2.15) agnyupasthānam || rātriṃ rātrimaprayātaṃ bharanta iti ||
(45,2.16) yathākālaṃ sāyamsāyaṃ gṛhapatirayaṃ no agniriti dve
(45,2.17) gārhapatyapaścāddugdhānnasyāgnihotraśrapaṇī vidhīyate ||
(45,2.18) yajña te veda pṛṣṭhamityetayālabhyābhimantrayate ||
(45,2.19) ukhāyaṃ sravantyāṃ sa mardakarmabhyo'nyasyāṃ dṛḍhatarāyāṃ pratyāsicya sutā deveṣvityanumantrayate ||
(45,2.20) evaṃ sarvāsūkhāsu somakalaśamahāvīre vā
(45,2.21) atha yasyāgnihotradhenvādi vyāpadyeta | tatra yathādevataṃ juhuyādapratibhāve vyāhṛtibhiḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_46. uttamapaṭalam)
(46,1.1) atha vedavratasyādeśanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(46,1.2) sāṃvatsarikaṃ vedavratam ||
(46,1.3) kalpānāṃ ṣāṇmāsikam ||
(46,1.4) romanakhāni dhārayettriṣavaṇaṃ tu snāyāddhaviṣyamaśnīyānna tu naktamaṇūnmāśānmasūrāṃstu ||
(46,1.5) daṇḍamathitamuddhṛtasnehaṃ nāśīyāt ||
(46,1.6) daṇḍakamaṇḍaludhāraṇaṃ vāsaścāthorṇam
(46,1.7) śirasvrataṃ ca sāṃvatsarikaṃ vedavratenaiva vyākhyātam ||
(46,1.8) atha pramāṇāni vakṣyāmo
(46,1.9) yamānāṃ mitrasya mṛgārthasyākṣīrākṣārabhojanamayugmamāchādanamanantarhitā śayyā
(46,1.10) mṛgārtheṣvaviśeṣeṇa yameṣu sarvameva śamīdhānyaṃ na bhuñjītā
(46,1.11) athopasamādadhāti ||
(46,2.1) samāstvāsmai kṣatrāṇyetamidhmamagnirbhūmyāmiti tisṛbhirmamāgne varca iti sarvasūktenāyuṣyairvarcasyaiḥ svastyayanairabhayairaparājitaiḥ śarmavarmabhiścopasamādadhāti ||
(46,2.2) vrataṃ nivedya vrātapatībhiḥ samidho'bhyādadhyād
(46,2.3) āṅgirasān samāsān hutvā bhārgavairviparyastāmāṅgirasīm
(46,2.4) vedādibhirvedottamaiḥ vargādibhirvargottamaiḥ padādibhiḥ padottamaiḥ kāṇḍādibhiḥ kāṇḍottamairanuvākādibhiranuvākottamairmahatkāṇḍairviśeṣeṇa sūktādibhiḥ sūktottamair
(46,2.5) atraitānyaṣṭarcaprabhṛtīni vyākhyātāni ||
(46,2.6) brahmajyeṣṭheti ekā kāmojajñe kāmastaditi hutvā
(46,2.7) kalpavrate viśeṣo vacanakarmasu brāhmaṇaṃ śrāvayet ||
(46,2.8) keśībrāhmaṇaṃ ca
(46,2.9) yeṣu vrataviśeṣaḥ syānna tānmantrānudāharetsamāsavatsa hotavyaḥ purāṇārthaṃ vijānatā ||
(46,3.1) agnimīḻe purohitaṃ yajñasya devamṛtvijam | hotāraṃ ratnadhātamam ||
(46,3.2) tacchaṃ yorāvṛṇīmahe gātuṃ yajñāya gātuṃ yajñapataye | daivī svastirastu naḥ svastirmānuṣebhyaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ jigātu bheṣajaṃ śaṃ no astu dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade ||
(46,3.3) iṣe tvorje tvā vāyava sthopāyava stha devo vaḥ savitā prārpayatu śreṣṭhatamāya karmaṇa āpyāyadhvamaghnyā indrāya bhāgam [ūrjasvatīḥ payasvatīḥ] prajāvatīranamīvā ayakṣmā mā va stena īśata māghaśaṃso rudrasya hetiḥ pari vo vṛṇaktu dhruvā asmin gopatau syāta bahvīryajamānasya paśūn pāhi ||
(46,3.4) dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣaṃ jiṣṇoraśvasya vājinaḥ | surabhi no mukhā karatpra ṇa āyumṣi tāriṣat ||
(46,3.5) agna ā yāhi vītaye gṛṇāno gavyadātaye | ni hotā satsi barhiṣi ||
(46,3.6) eṣa sya te dhārayā suto'vyo vārebhiryavane maditavyam | krīḍan raśmirapārthivaḥ ||
(46,4.1) ye triṣaptā vasyobhūyāya ye triṣaptā marmāṇi te vasyobhūyāya ye triṣaptā ye diśāṃ marmāṇi te yāṃ devā vasyobhūyāya ye triṣaptāḥ samānāṃ māsāmā te nayatvā arjanyasya ye diśāṃ kṛtyākṛtaṃ valaginamakṣitāste marmāṇi te viṣamevendraṃ mitraṃ vaśāṃ devā yāṃ deyāḥ sūrya enamannādyena yaśasā pra budhyasvāhnā pratyagvasyobhūyāya ||
(46,5.1) ye triṣaptāḥ śaṃ na āpo yathā vāto yadi no gāṃ haṃsyapendra dviṣataḥ putramattu samānāṃ vṛṣāyamāṇaḥ sūryamṛtaṃ paripāṇamasyā harāmiye krimaya
(46,5.2) ā te nayatu parṇo'sīndraputre viśvāhā te gosaniṃ vyasmai mitrāvaruṇāvā parjānyasya svapna svapnābhikaraena devānāmasthi mahāntaṃ kośaṃ yo antarikṣeṇopa śreṣṭhā ahameva vāta ivāva bādhe ye diśām
(46,5.3) ardhamardhenaivā mahānarvāñcamindraṃ śīrṣāmayamupahatyāmaśvasyāśna indrasya varūthaṃ hiraṇyavarṇā subhagā atrainānindrodāyurudbṛhatā mano
(46,5.4) devo devāya sadyo jātastastuvannagnirivaitu śataṃ ca me yadyekādaśo'si nāsya dhenuriṣuriva digdhā na varṣamacyutacyudetā devasenā
(46,5.5) gandhāribhyaḥ sarveṣāṃ ca krimīṇāṃ tatastatāmahāḥ prajāpate śreṣṭhenāśvinā brahmaṇāgne svāhā ghṛtādulluptaṃ tārṣṭāghīrayaṃ lokaḥ kṛtyākrtaṃ valaginam ||
(46,6.1) dive cakṣuṣe ayaṃ nastriṃśaddhāma mā no hāsiṣuryatkiṃ cedamahaṃ jajāna yāvadaṅgīnaṃ yaste'ṅkuśastānūṣṭe vājinnāñjanasya dvādaśadhā
(46,6.2) abhyañjanamakṣitāste yāvanto mā bradhnaḥ samīcīryadi vāsi bṛhaspatirno yo naḥ śapātsūyavasādyaṃ devā yathā śepo namaskṛtya marmāṇi te agneḥ śarīram
(46,6.3) prati cakṣvāprajāstvamito jaya [yatpratyāhanti] viṣameva yāste śivā etaṃ vo yuvānaṃ jyotiṣmato lokān saṃ te śīrṣṇa indraṃ mitraṃ prabhrājamānāmāre abhūd
(46,6.4) etamidhmamakāmo dhīro vaśāṃ devā namaste ghoṣiṇībhyaḥ prāṇa mā madyanmātalī apsu stīmāsu yāṃ devā bhūme mātaḥ pratyañcamarkamūrdhvāyai tvā yadi hutām
(46,6.5) sūrya enaṃ yo yajñasyābodhyagniḥ kṛṣṇāyāḥ putro annādyena yaśasā brahmāparaṃ pra budhyasvainaṃ śraddhā ahnā pratyaṃ śakvarī stha vasyobhūyāya ||
(46,7.1) vratairbhṛgvaṅgiraḥproktairānupūrvyādvidhānataḥ | upasanne guruḥ kuryādvratavidbrahmacāriṇi ||
(46,7.2) grāmādyathoktaṃ bāhyena sthaṇḍile'gniṃ praṇīya tu | nityenopasamādhāya saṃskṛtya juhuyāddhaviḥ ||
(46,7.3) aupagavyā vyāhṛtayaḥ sāvitrī śāntireva ca | vrātapatyaḥ samāsau ca tanmantrāṃhomucaiḥ saha ||
(46,7.4) kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ kāmasūktaṃ ca brahmavatyo'tha devatāḥ | vratādeśe tathotsarge homaḥ kṣīrodanaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(46,7.5) utsarge kāmasūktaṃ ca pūrvāṃ cāhutimuddharet | nivedya vrātapatyo'nte idāvatsara eva ca ||
(46,8.1) āvartanāntaṃ sāmānyaṃ vyāhṛtyādyubhayorapi | etatsāmāsikaṃ proktamādeśoddīkṣaṇaṃ budhaiḥ ||
(46,8.2) vrateṣu dakṣiṇā dhenuḥ kalyāṇī taruṇī ca yā | anaḍvān vā dhuraṃdharastatsamaṃ vāpi kāñcanam ||
(46,8.3) sāvitryā atha vedasya kalpānāṃ tadanantaram | mailaṃ mailottaraṃ caiva ṣaṣṭhaṃ saṃmitamucyate ||
(46,8.4) ṣaḍvrataṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vidyāttrivrataṃ kṣatriyaṃ viduḥ | dvivratastu bhavedvaiśya etadācāryaśāsanam ||
(46,8.5) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānamagnirmā goptā o citsakhāyamita eta udāruhan dhanurhastādetadā roha candramā apsviti ||
(46,9.1) omāṅgirasānāmādyaiḥ pañcānuvākaiḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.2) ṣaṣṭhāya svāhā ||
(46,9.3) saptamāṣṭamābhyāṃ svāhā ||
(46,9.4) nīlanakhebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.5) haritebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.6) kṣudrebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.7) paryāyikebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.8) prathamebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.9) dvitīyebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.10) tṛtīyebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.11) upottamebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.12) uttamebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.13) uttarebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.14) ṛṣibhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.15) śikhibhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.16) gaṇebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.17) mahāgaṇebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.18) sarvebhyo'ṅgirobhyo vidagaṇebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,9.19) pṛthaksahasrābhyāṃ svāhā ||
(46,9.20) brahmaṇe svāhā ||
(46,9.21) brahmajyeṣṭhā saṃbhṛtā vīryāṇi brahmāgre jyeṣṭhaṃ divamā tatāna | bhūtānāṃ brahmā prathamo'tha jajñe tenārhati brahmaṇā spardhituṃ kaḥ ||
(46,10.1) ātharvaṇānāṃ caturṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.2) pañcarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.3) ṣaḍarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.4) saptarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.5) aṣṭarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.6) navarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.7) daśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.8) ekādaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.9) dvādaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.10) trayodaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.11) caturdaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.12) pañcadaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.13) ṣoḍaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.14) saptadaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.15) aṣṭādaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.16) ekanaviṃśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.17) viṃśatiḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.18) mahatkāṇḍāya svāhā ||
(46,10.19) tṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.20) ekarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.21) kṣudrebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.22) ekānṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.23) rohitebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.24) sūryābhyāṃ svāhā ||
(46,10.25) vrātyābhyāṃ svāhā ||
(46,10.26) prājāpatyābhyāṃ svāhā ||
(46,10.27) viṣāsahyai svāhā ||
(46,10.28) maṅgalikebhyaḥ svāhā ||
(46,10.29) brahmaṇe svāhā ||
(46,10.30) brahmajyeṣṭhā saṃbhṛtā vīryāṇi brahmāgre jyeṣṭhaṃ divamā tatāna | bhūtānāṃ brahmā prathamo'tha jajñe tenārhati brahmanā spardhituṃ ka iti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_47. varṇapaṭalam)
(47,1.1) oṃ varṇān pūrvaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ prākṛtā ye ca vaikṛtāḥ | śrutinirvacanātsarve vivadante vivṛttiṣu ||
(47,1.2) vṛttiḥ karaṇaṃ varṇānāṃ karṇayostu śrutiryathā | śrutipradeśādvimatastadyathā varṇa ucyate ||
(47,1.3) samānasaṃdhyakṣarāṇi sparśā antaḥsthā ūṣmāṇaḥ | etairanye na dṛśyante etāvān varṇasaṃcayaḥ ||
(47,1.4) [yeṣāṃ ca śrutisaṃhitāyāṃ vibhāge asadṛśī syāt || tāneva varnān virudrādaye ca pradise kṛtaḥ] ||
(47,1.5) ye varṇābhyāṃ śrūyante ca yeṣāṃ nāsti vibhāgataḥ | mānasāṃścāpi saṃgrāhyānupadeśena vartayet ||
(47,1.6) krāntān bahirnidhānasya yamāṃścānanunāsikān | savyākṣepaṃ tato varṇānupadeśena vartayet ||
(47,1.7) iti varṇāḥ svarāḥ proktāsteṣāmādyāścaturdaśa | samānākṣarāṇyucyante śeṣaḥ saṃdhyakṣarāṇi tu ||
(47,1.8) anavarṇasvaro nāmi kādayo vyañjanaṃ smṛtam | pañcaviṃśatirādyaiṣāṃ sparśā vargāśca pañcakāḥ ||
(47,1.9) catvāro yādayo'ntaḥsthāḥ śādirūṣmāṣṭako gaṇaḥ | ayogavāhā vartante teṣāmādyāḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ ||
(47,1.10) visarjanīyo'nusvāro jigvāmūlīya [ityadhaḥ] | upadhmānīya ityete catvāro'tha pare yamāḥ ||
(47,1.11) nāsikyābhinidhānau ca vidyate yena pūraṇam | pañcaṣaṣṭiriyān vāco rāśiryo vedalokayoḥ ||
(47,1.12) mukhanāsike ye varṇā ucyante te'nunāsikāḥ | samānāsyaprayatnā ye te savarṇā iti smṛtāḥ ||
(47,1.13) hrasvo'varṇaparastasya savarṇasya ca vācakaḥ | hrasvottarastu dīrgho'pi tasmāttasmāttasyaiva vācakaḥ ||
(47,1.14) vargāntarastu vargādirvargasya grāhako mataḥ | vargāṇāṃ ca yathāsaṃkhyaṃ prathamāditvamiṣyate ||
(47,1.15) akāreṇocyate'tastu kāro yasmātparo bhavet | tasya tadgrahaṇaṃ bodhyaṃ kakāro'tra nidarśanam ||
(47,1.16) vyañjanaṃ ghoṣavatsaṃjñamantaḥsthā haḥ parau yamau | trayastrayaśca vargāntyā agoṣaḥ śeṣa ucyate ||
(47,1.17) śaṣasāśca yamau dvau ca dvitīyāḥ prathamāśca ye | aghoṣā vyañjanaśeṣaṃ ghoṣavaddṛśyate budhaiḥ ||
(47,1.18) ataḥ sthānāni varṇānāṃ kaṇṭho'varṇahakārayoḥ | vosarjanīya ai au ca svādyayormātrayoḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(47,1.19) śeṣastālvoṣṭhayorbodhyaḥ sa yathāsaṃkhyamiṣyate | dvisthānaṃ yamayoścāpi vargāntyānāṃ ca śiṣyate ||
(47,1.20) jihvāmūlāmṛvarṇasya kavargasya ca bhāṣyate | yaścaiva jihvāmūlīya ḻavarṇaśceti ca smṛtaḥ ||
(47,2.1) tālveyaśacavargāṇāmivarṇasya ca bhāṣyate | mūrdhā sthānaṃ ṣakārasya ṭavargasya tathā mataḥ ||
(47,2.2) dantā lasatavargāṇāmuvarṇastvoṣṭhya ucyate | upadhmānīya okāro vaḥ pavargaśca tathā matāḥ ||
(47,2.3) nāsikye nāsikā sthānaṃ tathānusvāra iṣyate | yamā vargottamāścāpi yathoktaṃ caiva te matāḥ ||
(47,2.4) rephasya dantamūlāni pratyagvā tebhya iṣyate | iti sthānāni varṇānāṃ kīrtitāni yathākramam ||
(47,2.5) apara āha | hanumūle tu rephaḥ syāddantamūleṣu vā punaḥ | pratyagvā dantamūlebhyo mūrdhanya iti cāpare ||
(47,2.6) uraḥstho ghoṣo visṛṣṭaḥ kaṇṭhadeśe nihanyate | tato nādo vitiṣṭhate tasya vikṛtirakṣaram ||
(47,2.7) pūrvābhiraṇumātrābhiḥ kaṇṭhyaṃ saṃsevyate'kṣaram | uttarābhistu mātrābhirmukhavikṛtirucyate ||
(47,2.8) api prayogasya hetoḥ saṃyogaḥ saha dhāryate | avyavahito'kṣareṇa nānāvarṇaḥ svarodayaḥ ||
(47,2.9) dvitīyeṣu tathoṣmaṇāṃ tṛtīyeṣu ca ghoṣāṇām | caturtheṣu ghoṣoṣmaṇāmupadeśena vartayet ||
(47,2.10) uttameṣu tu nāsikyamantaḥstheṣu ghoṣaṃ smṛtam | hakārasya ghoṣoṣmāṇāvupadeśena vartayet ||
(47,3.1) ṛkārākṣarayo rephamaṇumātra sarva... | svaritasya dvaidhībhāve upadeśena vartayet ||
(47,3.2) ṛvarṇadeśasaṃdeho'svaraḥ syātsyādanantaram | paro vā yadi vā pūrvo rephameva tu viddhi tam ||
(47,3.3) akāraśca ikāraśca ukāra ṛkāra eva ca | hrasvadīrghaplutāḥ sarve ḻavarṇe nāsti dīrghatā ||
(47,3.4) ekāraśca tathāikāra okāra aukāra eva ca | dīrghamātraplutāsteṣāṃ saṃjjā saṃdhyakṣarāṇi ca ||
(47,3.5) udāttaścānudāttāśca svaritāḥ kampitāśca ye | anunāsikāstathā śuddhā dṛśyate hrasvatā budhaiḥ ||
(47,3.6) varṇāḥ pañcaṣaṣṭiḥ svarā dvāviṃśatiḥ samānākṣarāṇi caturdaśa aṣṭau saṃdhyakṣarāṇyekonaviṃśatirnāminastricatvāriṃśadvyañjanānisparśāḥ pañcaviṃśatiścatvārastvantaḥsthāḥ catvāro yamāścāṣṭāvūṣmāṇo'yogavāhā daśāyogavāhā daśa ||

(48,100) sasniḥ | sasniḥ | alātṛṇaḥ | kvaṇan kuṇāruḥ | dānavaḥ | udadhiḥ | [siriḥ |] vṛtraḥ | parvataḥ | camasaḥ | ahiḥ | abhram | balāhakaḥ | dṛtiḥ | odanaḥ | vṛṣandhiḥ | vṛtraḥ | kośaḥ | asuraḥ | meghasya ||50 ||
(48,101) <adriḥ |> adriḥ | grāvā | gotraḥ | valaḥ | aśnaḥ | purubhojāḥ | valiśānaḥ | aśmā | giriḥ | vrajaḥ | caruḥ | varāhaḥ | śambaraḥ | rauhiṇaḥ | raivataḥ | parighaḥ | [pāṇighaḥ |] uparaḥ | upalaḥ | sānau | rudraḥ | parvatasya ||51 ||
(48,102) gauḥ | gauḥ | gaurī | gāndharvī | gabhīrā | gambhīrā mandrā | mandrājanī | [vāṇīḥ] | vāśī | vāṇī | vāṇīcī | vāṇaḥ | paviḥ | bhāratī | dhamaniḥ | meḍiḥ | sūryā | sarasvatī | nivit | svāhā | vagnuḥ | upabdiḥ | kākuḥ | māyuḥ | jihvā | ghoṣaḥ | ślokaḥ | śabdaḥ | svaraḥ | svanaḥ | ṛk | hotrā | gīḥ | gāthā | gaṇaḥ | dhenā | gnāḥ | vipā | nanā | kaśā | dhiṣaṇā | nauḥ | akṣaram | mahī | aditiḥ | śacī | [tsaghīḥ |] anuṣṭup | [śabdaḥ |] rasaḥ | [vasā | madhu | kaśā |] virāṭ | vācaḥ ||52 ||
(48,103) ojaḥ | ojaḥ | pājaḥ | śavaḥ | śardhaḥ | tvakṣaḥ | bādhaḥ | nṛmṇam | taraḥ | taviṣī | śuṣmam | śuṣṇam | dakṣaḥ | vīḍu [tu] | cyautnam | dyumnam | indriyam | sahaḥ | vayaḥ | vadhaḥ | vargaḥ | majmanā | pauṃsyāni | dharṇasi | syandrāsaḥ | draviṇam | balasya ||53 ||
(48,104) vidyut | vidyut | nemiḥ | paviḥ | vajraḥ | sṛkaḥ | [yataḥ |] vadhaḥ | arkaḥ | śambaḥ | kuliśaḥ | kutsaḥ | sāyakaḥ | trapuṣī | vajrasya ||54 ||
(48,105) raṇaḥ | raṇaḥ | vivāk | vadanuḥ | vikhādaḥ | bhare | krandaḥ | āhāvaḥ | sam[an]īke | mamasatyam | nemadhitiḥ | saṅkā | samanam | spṛdhaḥ | pṛtsu | samatsu | samaraṇe | samohe | saṃkhye | vṛtratūrye | samarye | āṇau | prataraṇe | [maṃtasā |] samanīke | [khāya | sene |] khale | khaje | pauṃsye | mahādhane | pṛtanā | jyeṣṭhaḥ | saṃgrāmasya ||55 ||
(48,106) [khare | svāram | suṣṭi |] nu | nu | makṣu | dravat | oṣam | jīrāḥ | jūrṇiḥ | śūrtāḥ | śūghanāḥ | śībham | tṛṣu | tūyam | tūrṇiḥ | ajiram | bhuraṇyuḥ | āśu | prāśu | tūtujānaḥ | tūtujiḥ | tujyamānāsaḥ | ajrāḥ | sācīvit | dyugat | tājat | taraṇiḥ | vātaraṃhā | kṣiprasya ||56 ||
(48,107) niṇyam | niṇyam | apīcyam | sasvaḥ | <hiruk |> [tatra | tattanta | tāyatam |] antarhitasya ||
(48,108) <svaḥ |> svaḥ | pṛśniḥ | nākaḥ | gauḥ | viṣṭap | iṣṭam | nabhaḥ | divaḥ | antarikṣasya ca | parāṇi tadāyatanānām ||57 ||
(48,109) [hiruk | hiruk |] āke | parācaiḥ | āre | parāvate | iti dūrasya ||58 ||
(48,110) vibhāvarī | vibhāvari | sūnarī | [bhāvatī | sunarī] bhāsvarī | ahanā | dyotanā | śvetyā | aruṣī | sūnṛtāvarī | uṣasaḥ ||59 ||
(48,111) vastoḥ | vastoḥ | bhānuḥ | vāsaram | svasarāṇi | ghraṃsaḥ | gharmaḥ | ghṛṇiḥ | divā | dinam | dive<dive> | dyavidyavi | ahnaḥ ||60 ||
(48,112) dīdhitayaḥ | gabhastayaḥ | vanam | usrāḥ | vasavaḥ | marīcayaḥ | sapta ṛṣayaḥ | sādhyāsaḥ | suparṇāsaḥ | mayūkhāḥ | raśmīnām ||61 ||
(48,113) khedayaḥ | khedayaḥ | kiraṇāḥ | gāvaḥ | abhīśavaḥ | [raśmīn |] raśmīnāṃ ca ||62 ||
(48,114) āryaḥ | āryaḥ | rāṣṭrī | niyutvān | inaḥ | īśvarasya ||63 ||
(48,115) saṃyogaḥ | saṃyogaḥ | āśuśukṣaṇiḥ | jahā | śitāma | mehanā | mūṣaḥ | mandū | īrmāntāsaḥ | [vājarāndhyam |] kāyamānaḥ | vidradhe | tugvani | [nodhāt | nadaḥ |] cyavanaḥ | kaśyapaḥ | nū cit | akūpārasya | aprāyuvaḥ | rajaḥ | juhure | krāṇā | viṣuṇaḥ | jāmiḥ | jasuriḥ | cayase | andhaḥ | dugdham | āhanaḥ | nadaḥ | arkaḥ | sacā | cit | pavitram | pṛthujrayāḥ | kāṇukā | adhriguḥ | āṅgūṣaḥ | āpāntamanyuḥ | śmaśā | vājagandhyam | [jarādhya |] pākasthāmā kaurayāṇaḥ | vrandī | niṣṣapī | kṣumpam | nicumpuṇaḥ | [majāyema | dhṛruḥ |] joṣavākam | kuṭasya | kepayaḥ | salalūkam | askṛdhoyuḥ | niśṛmbhāḥ | [dhruvadrakṣam |] upalaprakṣiṇī | upasi | savīmani | vidathāni | śrāyanta iva | amūraḥ | vijāmātuḥ | amavān | amīvā | amatiḥ | riśādasaḥ | ānuṣak | girvaṇāḥ | amyak | yādṛśmin | śurudhaḥ | apratiṣkutaḥ | dvibarhāḥ | urāṇaḥ | javāru | tatanuṣṭim | ilībiśaḥ | [irāviṇaḥ |] kiyedhāḥ | turīpam | pratadvasū | diviṣṭiṣu | dūtaḥ | ṛcīṣamaḥ | anarśarātim ||64 || anarvā | [anarvā | cāṇḍā | vālhā] jaḍhavaḥ | bakuraḥ | [vaktāraḥ] bekanāṭān | abhi dhetana | sadānve | parāśaraḥ | karūḍatī | danaḥ ||
(48,116) ikṣuṇā | kīlālam | vijāmni | doṣā | [aṣṭamartyaḥ] ||65 || jyeṣṭham | [jyeṣṭham | asipakva |] viśvāhā | vivasavān | vāte | [tanyantaḥ | vrālma | kāmpīvakaṃsam | jasyatyam | jalālī |] andhaḥ | vipaśyan | ayā ciṣṭhā | [āṃsā | rantu | tamāyīvayaḥ |] śamopyāt | gulphaḥ | biṣkale khargalā | pratodaḥ | vedaḥ | [yatrāsmannataḥ | radhraḥ | cikriḥ | nuluḥ |] pucchadhau | [suniḥ |] apāṣṭhaḥ | medī | [jyenā |] maryaḥ | [saptaghnetaḥ |] vālini | yātāram | [ruṣaṃkiḥ | siktaḥ |] sagaṇāḥ | [muḍimnānā | liṅgakāḥ | nādinā |] malvaḥ | amnaḥ | [juguḥ |] nīlāgalasālā | ailabaḥ | [daridraḥ |] nīlalohitaḥ | śvāpadaḥ | kunakhī | kurīram | [upasaḥ |] tāduri | [kamatha | rumathā | sarvartebhyaḥ | idam | adhvaryuḥ | dyumnī | kuvitaḥ | damnanā |] duroṇe | [parektauti] | titau | [utpavādhata |] kimīdī | vāmasya | ekacakram | amatiḥ | sumatiḥ | [dayate | dayanti | vrīhi | vṛtte] ||66 || īḍe | īḍe | kṣayati | tapati | rajati | anekārthāḥ ||
(48,117) prapitve | abhīke | prāptasya ||
(48,118) tiraḥ | sataḥ | aprāptasya ||
(48,119) tvaḥ | nemaḥ | ardhasya ||
(48,120) ṛkṣāḥ | stṛbhiḥ | iti nakṣatrāṇām ||
(48,121) vamrībhiḥ | upajihvikā | sīmikānām ||
(48,122) rambhaḥ | [rathaḥ |] pinākam | iti daṇḍasya ||
(48,123) śepaḥ | vaitasaḥ | iti puṃsprajananasya ||
(48,124) [paraṃgativilīke] | iti strīprajananasya ||
(48,125) anena | anayā | [panasya]]
(48,126) maki | hvakir] | iti pratiṣedhasya ||
(48,127) varūtham | [asagram] | carmaṇo'rutsāhasya ||
(48,128) paṇiḥ prakalavidvaṇijaḥ ||
(48,129) śvaghnī | kitavasya | akṣadhūrtasya ||
(48,130) [mṛṇyaḥ] | sīmikasya ||
(48,131) kuṭasya | kuliśaḥ ||
(48,132) agniḥ | jātavedāḥ | vaiśvānaraḥ | draviṇodāḥ | vanaspatiriti sūktabhāñji ||
(48,133) idhmaḥ | [viṣṇuḥ |] tanūnapāt | narāśaṃsaḥ | devīrdvāraḥ | uṣāsānaktā | daivyā hotārā | tisro devīḥ | tvaṣṭā | vanaspatiḥ | svāhākṛtaya iti nipātabhāñji ||
(48,134) indraḥ | viṣṇuḥ | somaḥ | parjanyaḥ | ṛtuḥ | agnāyī | pṛthivī | iḍā | bhṛgavaḥ | atharvāṇa iti saṃstavikāstasyaikavadbahuvatstrīvacca ||
(48,135) vahanam <ca haviṣāmāvāhanaṃ ca> devānāṃ yacca dāeṣṭiviṣayikaṃ tadasya karma ||
(48,136) ayaṃ lokaḥ | vasantaḥ | prātaḥsavanam | gāyatrī trivṛdrathaṃtaramiti tadbhaktīni ||67 ||
(48,137) vāyuḥ | varuṇaḥ | indraḥ | rudraḥ | parjanyaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ | brahmaṇaspatiḥ | vāstoṣpatiḥ | kṣetrasya patiḥ | kaḥ | yamaḥ | apāṃ napāt | mitraḥ | viśvakarmā | manyuḥ | tārkṣyaḥ | dadhikrāḥ | sarasvān | agniḥ | asunītiḥ | vājaḥ | kutaḥ | vātaḥ | ṛtaḥ | mṛtyuḥ | dhātā | vidhātā | purūravāḥ | gandharvāḥ | anaḍvān | prāṇāḥ | stambhaḥ | vrātya iti sūktabhāñji ||
(48,138) prajāpatiḥ | candramāḥ | somaḥ | induḥ | aditiḥ | dhenavaḥ | ahirbudhnya iti nipātabhāñji ||
(48,139) sarasvatī | vāk | aditiḥ | urvaśī | gauḥ | dhenuḥ | saramā | uṣā | indrāṇī | pṛthivī | dasya | godhukasā | virāṭ | aghnyā | sinīvālī | kuhūḥ | anumatiḥ | rākā | yamī | saraṇyūḥ | pathyā | rodasī | devapatnyaḥ | marutaḥ | rudrāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | aṅgirasaḥ | bhṛgavaḥ | atharvāṇa iti saṃstavikāstasyaikavadbahuvatstrīvacca ||
(48,140) snehānupradānaṃ vṛtravadho yā cakā ca balakṛtistadasya karma ||
(48,141) antarikṣalokaḥ | grīṣmaḥ | madhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | triṣṭuppañcadaśaḥ | bṛhaditi tadbhaktīni ||68 ||
(48,142) ādityaḥ | savitā | bhagaḥ | sūryaḥ | pūṣā | viṣṇuḥ | keśī | viśvānaraḥ | vṛṣākapiḥ | kālaḥ | brahmacārī | rohita iti sūktabhāñji ||
(48,143) dadhyaṅ | atharvā | yamaḥ | aja ekapāt | manuḥ | vivasvān | dakṣaḥ | aryamā | vaiśvānaraḥ | suparṇa iti nipātabhāñji ||
(48,144) uṣāḥ | sūryā | vṛṣākapāyī | sādhyāḥ | vasavaḥ | ādityā | sapta ṛṣayaḥ | vājinaḥ | viśve devā iti saṃstavikāstasyaikavadbahuvatstrīvacca ||
(48,145) <rasādānam> raśmibhiśca rasādhāraṇaṃ yacca <pravalhitam> tadasta karma ||
(48,146) asya lokaḥ | varaṣās| tṛtīyasavanam | jagatī | saptadaśaḥ | vairūpāmiti tadbhaktīni ||
(48,147) eteṣāmeva lokānāmṛtuchandaḥstomapṛṣṭhānāmānupūrveṇa bhaktiśeṣo'nukalpo
(48,148) devatādvandve ca pūrvasyāparaḥ saṃstavikaḥ || pūrvasyāparaḥ saṃstavikaḥ ||69 ||

(pariśiṣṭa_49. caraṇavyūhaḥ)
(49,1.1) omathātaścaraṇavyūhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(49,1.2) tatra catvāro vedā bhavanti | ṛgvedo yajurvedaḥ sāmavedo brahmavedaśceti ||
(49,1.3) tatra ṛgvedasyārthaśāstramupavedaḥ | yajurvedasya dhanurvedopavedaḥ | sāmavedasya gāndharvavedopavedaḥ | brahmavedasyāyurvedopavedaḥ | abhicārakārthaśāstramityucyate ||
(49,1.4) ṛgveda ātreyasagotro'gnirdevatā | yajurvedaḥ kāśyapasagotro vāyurdevatā | sāmavedo bhāradvājasagotro viṣṇurdevatā | brahmavedo vaitāyanasagotro brahmā devatā ||
(49,1.5) athāta ṛgvedaḥ pītavarṇaḥ padmapattrākṣaḥ suvibhaktagrīvaḥ kuñcitakeśaśmaśruḥ supratiṣṭhitajānujaṅghaḥ | pramāṇena sa vitastayaḥ pañca ||
(49,1.6) tatra ṛgvedasya sapta śākhā bhavanti | tadyathā | āśvalāyanāḥ | śāṅkhāyanāḥ | sādhyāyanāḥ | śākalāḥ | bāṣkalāḥ | audumbarāḥ | māṇḍūkāśceti ||
(49,1.7) teṣāmadhyayanam | ṛcāṃ daśa sahasrāṇi ṛcāṃ pañca śatāni ca | ṛcāmaśītiḥ pādaśca etatpāraṇamucyate ||
(49,2.1) tatra yajurvedasya caturviṃśatirbhedā bhavanti || tadyathā | kāṇvāḥ | mādhyaṃdināḥ | jābālāḥ | śāpeyāḥ | śvetāḥ | śvetatarāḥ | tāmrāyaṇīyāḥ | paurṇavatsāḥ | āvaṭikāḥ | paramāvaṭikāḥ | hauṣyāḥ | dhauṣyāḥ | khāḍikāḥ | āhvarakāḥ | carakāḥ maitrāḥ | maitrāyaṇīyāḥ | hāritakarṇāḥ | śālāyanīyāḥ | marcakaṭhāḥ | prācyakaṭhāḥ | kapiṣṭhalakaṭhāḥ | upalāḥ | taittirīyāśceti ||
(49,2.2) teṣāmadhyayanam | dve sahasre śate nyūne vede vājasaneyake | sakalaṃ parisaṃkhyātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tu caturguṇam ||
(49,2.3) aṣṭādaśa śatāni bhavanti | tānyeva triguṇamadhītya kramapāro bhavati | saptasu vīrāśceti ||
(49,2.4) śākhāstisro bhavanti | tadyathā | vārcikamarthādhyayanīyāḥ | pāraścaryāḥ | pāraśramaṇīyāḥ | pārakramavaṭaḥ | kramapāraśceti ||
(49,2.5) ṣaḍaṅgānyadhītya ṣaḍaṅgavidbhavati | śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ niruktaṃ chando jyotiṣamiti ṣaḍaṅgāni ||
(49,2.6) atha yajurvedaḥ prāṃśuḥ pralambajaṭharaḥ sthūlagalakapālo rakto varṇena prādeśāḥ ṣaḍdīrghatvena yajurvedasyaitadrūpaṃ bhavati ||
(49,3.1) tatra sāmavedasya śākhāsahasramāsīdanadhyāyeṣvadhīyānāḥ sarve te śakreṇa vinihatāḥ | [pravilīnās]
(49,3.2) tatra ke cidavaśiṣṭāḥ pracaranti | tadyathā | rāṇāyanīyāḥ | sādyamugrāḥ | kālapāḥ | mahākālapāḥ | kauthumāḥ | lāṅgalikāśceti ||
(49,3.3) kauthumānāṃ ṣaḍbhedā bhavanti | tadyathā | sārāyaṇīyāḥ | vātarāyaṇīyāḥ | vaitadhṛtāḥ | prācīnāstejasāḥ | aniṣṭakāśceti ||
(49,3.4) teṣāmadhyayanam | aṣṭau sāmasahasrāṇi sāmāni ca caturdaśa | sohyāni sarahasyāni etatsāmagaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
(49,3.5) atha sāmavedaḥ suvarcāḥ sugandhistejasvī mṛduvaktā brahmaṇyaḥ pralambabāhurduścarmī kṛṣṇo varṇena kātaraḥ svareṇeti ||
(49,3.6) ṣaḍaratniḥ pramāṇena ca smṛtaḥ | stuvantyṛṣayo brahmā sāmāni tiṣṭhati saṃnidhau sa bhagavān sāmavedo maheśvarabhaktaḥ ||
(49,4.1) tatra brahmavedasya nava bhedā bhavanti | tadyathā | paippalādāḥ | staudāḥ | maudāḥ | śaunakīyāḥ | jājalāḥ | jaladāḥ | brahmavadāḥ | devadarśāḥ | cāraṇavaidyāśceti |
(49,4.2) teṣāmadhyayanam | ṛcāṃ dvādaśa sahasrāṇyaśītistriśatāni ca | paryāyikaṃ dvisahasrāṇyanyāṃscaivārcikān bahūnity
(49,4.3) etadgrāmyāraṇyakāni ṣaṭsahasrāṇi bhavanti ||
(49,4.4) tatra brahmavedasyāṣṭāviṃśatirupaniṣado bhavanti | muṇḍakā praśnakā brahmavidyā kṣurikā cūlikā atharvaśiro atharvaśikhā garbhopaniṣanmahopaniṣadbrahmopaniṣatprāṇāgnihotraṃ māṇḍukyaṃ nādabindu brahmabindu amṛtabindu dhyānabindu tejobindu yogaśikhā yogatattvaṃ nīlarudraḥ pañcatāpinī ekadaṇḍī saṃnyāsavidhiḥ aruṇiḥ haṃsaḥ paramahaṃsaḥ nārāyaṇopaniṣadvaitathyaṃ ceti ||
(49,4.5) tatra gopathaḥ śataprapāṭhakaṃ brāhmaṇamāsīttasyāvaśiṣṭe dve brāhmaṇe pūrvamuttaraṃ ceti ||
(49,4.6) tatra ṣaḍaṅgānyadhītya ṣaḍaṅgavidbhavati ṣaḍaṅgāni bhavanti, śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ niruktaṃ chando jyotiṣamiti ||
(49,4.7) paKca kalpā bhavanti | nakṣatrakalpo vaitānakalpastṛtīyaḥ saṃhitāvidhiḥ | caturtha āṅgirasaḥ kalpaḥ śāntikalpastu pañcamaḥ ||
(49,4.8) lakṣaṇagranthā bhavanti | caturādhyāyikā prātiśākhyaṃ pañcapaṭalikā dantyoṣṭhavidhirbṛhatsarvānukramaṇī ceti ||
(49,4.9) tatra dvāsaptatiḥ pariśiṣṭāni bhavanti kauśikoktāni | kṛttikārohiṇī | rāṣṭrasaṃvargaḥ | rājaprathamābhiṣekaḥ | purohitakarmāṇi | puṣyābhiṣekaḥ | piṣṭarātryāḥ kalpaḥ | ārātrikam | ghṛtāvekṣaṇam | tiladhenuḥ | bhūmidānam | tulāpuruṣaḥ | ādityamaṇḍakaḥ | hiraṇyagarbhaḥ | hastirathaḥ | aśvarathaḥ | gosahasradānam | hastidīkṣā | aśvadīkṣā | vṛṣotsargaḥ | indrotsavaḥ | brahmayāgaḥ | skandayāgāḥ | saṃbhāralakṣaṇam | araṇilakṣaṇam | yajñapātralakṣaṇam | vedilakṣaṇam | kuṇḍalakṣaṇam | samillakṣaṇam | sruvalakṣaṇam | hastalakṣaṇam | jvālālakṣaṇam | lakṣahomaḥ | kāṅkāyanokto bṛhallakṣahomaḥ | koṭihomaḥ | gaṇamālā | ghṛtakambalam | anulomakalpaḥ | āsurīkalpaḥ | ucchuṣmakalpaḥ | samuccayaprāyaścittāni | brahmakūrcavidhiḥ | paiṭhīnasitaḍāgavidhiḥ | pāśupatavratavidhiḥ | saṃdhyopāsanavidhiḥ | snānavidhiḥ | tarpaṇavidhiḥ | śrāddhavidhiḥ | agnihotravidhiḥ | uttamapaṭalam | varṇapaṭalam | nighaṇtuḥ | caraṇavyūhaḥ | candraprātipadikam | grahayuddham | grahasaṃgrahaḥ | rāhucāraḥ | ketucāraḥ | ṛtuketulakṣaṇam | kūrmavibhāgaḥ | maṇḍalāni | digdāhalakṣaṇam | ulkālakṣaṇam | vidyullakṣaṇam | nirghātalakṣaṇam | pariveṣalakṣaṇam | bhūmikampalakṣaṇam | nakṣatragrahotpātalakṣaṇam | utpātalakṣaṇam | sadyovṛṣṭilakṣaṇam | gośāntiḥ | adbhutaśāntiḥ | svapnādhyāyaḥ | atharvahṛdayam |
bhārgavīyagārgyabārhaspatyośanasādbhutāni | mahādbhutāni bṛhatsarvānukramaṇī ceti ||
(49,4.10) tatra pañcadaśopaniṣado bhavanti | muṇḍakā | praśnakā | brahmavidyā | kṣurikā | cūlikā | atharvaśiraḥ | atharvaśikhā | garbhopaniṣat | mahopaniṣat | brahmopaniṣat | prāṇāgnihotram | māṇḍūkyam | vaitathyam | advaitam | alātaśāntiśceti ||
(49,4.11) tatra brahmavede'ṣṭādaśa vratāni cariṣyan sāvitrīvratam | vedavratam | vedottaravratam | mailavratam | mailottaravratam | mṛgāravratam | rohitavratam | viṣāsahivratam | yamavratam | śāntivratam | śikhivratam | gaṇavratam | śirovratam | śikhāvratam | marudvratam | adhivratam | aṅgirovratam | pāśupatavrataṃ caret ||
(49,4.12) kṛcchram | taptakṛcchram | atikṛcchram | sarvakṛcchram | maundabhāyaḥ | tulāpuruṣaḥ | sāṃtapanam | mahāsāṃtapanaṃ ceti ||
(49,5.1) yo vai brahmavedeṣūpanītaḥ sa sarvavedeṣūpanito
(49,5.2) yo vai brahmavedeṣvanupanītaḥ sa sarvavedeṣvanupanītaḥ ||
(49,5.3) anyavede dvijo yo brahmavedamadhītukāmaḥ sa punarupaneyo
(49,5.4) devāśca ṛṣayaśca brahmāṇamūcuḥ ||
(49,5.5) ko no [smo] jyeṣṭhaḥ | ka upanetā | ka ācāryaḥ | ko brahmatvaṃ ceti ||
(49,5.6) tān brahmābravīt ||
(49,5.7) atharvā vo jyeṣṭho'tharvopanetātharvācāryo'tharvā brahmatvaṃ ceti ||
(49,5.8) tadapyetadṛcoktam | brahmajyeṣṭheti etayā |
(49,5.9) iti tasyārhaṃ brahmavedaścaturṇāṃ vedānāṃ sāṅgopāṅgānām [tam] savākovākyānāṃ setihāsapurāṇānām ||
(49,5.10) athāto brahmavedaḥ kapilo varṇena tīkṣṇaḥ pracaṇḍaḥ kāmarūpī viśvātmā jitendriyaḥ | sa tasmin bhagavati durvārajvālaḥ |
(49,5.11) kṣudrakarmā sa ca bhagavān brahmavedaścaturmukho dvipakṣo dānto dharmī balavān prājñaḥ kṛtotthāpanīyaḥ krūraḥ ṣaḍrātrāṇi vimṛśī [ṣaḍrātrāṇi ṣaḍ] vaitāyano gotreṇa
(49,5.12) ya ekaikasmin vedānāṃ nāmavarṇagotrarūpapramāṇaṃ ca kīrtayedyo vidvān jātismaro bhavati mṛtaḥ sa brahmalokaṃ gacchati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_50. candraprātipadikam)
(50,1.1) oṃ vṛttaṃ prātaramāvāsyāṃ paścāddṛśyeta candramāḥ | tasya varṇaṃ gatiṃ rūpaṃ sthānaṃ caivoccanīcatām ||
(50,1.2) hrāsavṛddhiṃ ca śṛṅgāṇāṃ nakṣatraṃ yacca yojayet | tāni lakṣeta somasya varṣāvarṣaṃ bhayābhayam ||
(50,1.3) prathame darśane tvindoḥ samāsādya yadā graham | uttaraṃ vardhate śṛṅgaṃ nīcībhavati dakṣiṇam ||
(50,1.4) evameva śraviṣṭhābhyasteṣāmante ca candramāḥ | udyaccheddakṣiṇaṃ śṛṅgaṃ nīcībhavati cottaram ||
(50,1.5) anupaśyeta rāṣṭraṃ ca antargirimahāgirim | vidarbhānmadrakāṃścaiva bharatāṃścāpi sarvataḥ ||
(50,2.1) sārāṇāṃ vijarāṇāṃ ca samudre ye ca dakṣiṇe | etāñjanapadān hanti yadā syāduttaronnataḥ ||
(50,2.2) kāśmīrān daradān darvāñśūrasenān yayāvarān | śālvānāṃ ca virājānāṃ samudre ye ca paścime ||
(50,2.3) etājjanapadān hanti yadā syāddakṣinonnataḥ | puruṣaḥ strīnṛpaṃ hanti aparānto vinaśyati ||
(50,2.4) bālhikān yavanakāmbojāñśālvānmadrānuśīnarān | godhāṃśca bhadrakāṃścaiva madhyaṃ ca kurubhiḥ saha ||
(50,2.5) saurāṣṭrān sindhusauvīrān vāneyāṃścāpi siṃsakān | kṣudrakānmālavānmatsyānmlecchān saha pulindakaiḥ ||
(50,2.6) śastropajījikuḍyāṃśca brāhmaṇā yodhinaśca ye | etāñjanapadān hanti somaḥ puruṣalakṣaṇaḥ ||
(50,3.1) lakṣaṇādvā bhavetsthūlaḥ kāye śṛṅge ca hīyate | alpe śarīre durbhikṣaṃ bhayaṃ rogaṃ vinirdiśet ||
(50,3.2) yadā pratipadaścandraḥ prakṛtyā vikṛto bhavet | anudbhinno vilūno vā rājamṛtyuṃ vinirdiśet ||
(50,3.3) ṣaṣṭhyāṃ madhyaṃ yadā gacchedrājā vadhyeta pārthivaḥ | avantīnāṃ ca pūrvārdhaṃ māgadhāśca viśeṣataḥ ||
(50,3.4) paraṃ kumāreṣvaṣṭamyāṃ rājānaṃ daśamī param | evaṃ ca pakṣāpacaye madhye dṛśyeta dvādaśī ||
(50,3.5) hanti pañcanadaṃ tatra rājānaṃ sumahadbalam | sarvāṃśca kuryādrājñastu tasminnutpātadarśane ||
(50,4.1) adbhutāni ca dṛśyante tasminnutpātadarśane | vaiśvānarapathaṃ prāptaḥ samudramapi śoṣayet ||
(50,4.2) kṛttikānāṃ maghānāṃ ca rohiṇyāśca viśākhayoḥ | eteṣāmuttaro mārgo rājavīthīti tāṃ viduḥ ||
(50,4.3) yadīmaṃ mārgamāsthāya candramā vinivartate | nāvarṣā uttamā jñeyā yogakṣemaṃ tathaiva ca ||
(50,4.4) gajavīthīṃ nāgavīthīṃ yadi gacchati candramāḥ | ..... govīthīti tadāpyāhurgargasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
(50,4.5) aṣṭau sthānāni candrasya kroṣṭukiryāni veda vai | nausthāyī lāṅgalī caiva tṛtīyaścottaronnataḥ ||
(50,4.6) daṇḍasthāyī caturthastu daṇḍaśāyī tu pañcamaḥ | ṣaṣthastu yūpasthāyī syātpārśvaśāyī tu saptamaḥ ||
(50,4.7) aṣṭamo'vāṅśirāścaiva phalamasya nibodhata | rājānaḥ sveṣu rāṣṭreṣu yuktadaṇḍāḥ praśāsati ||
(50,5.1) lāṅgalī grasate lokān yugāntaṃ pratipādayet | mārīṃ samadhikāmāhuryadā syāduttaronnataḥ ||
(50,5.2) daṇḍasthāyī tvamātyānāṃ bhayaṃ rogaṃ vinirdiśet | śaktichedā granthichedā gostenāḥ pāradārikāḥ ||
(50,5.3) ete deśān vilumpanti daṇḍasthāyī yadā bhavet | daṇḍaśāyī tu viprāṇāṃ bhayaṃ tatra vinirdiśet ||
(50,5.4) yūpasthāyī tu dhānyānāṃ bhayaṃ tatra vinirdiśet | harite śarīre somasya paśūnāṃ vadhamādiśet ||
(50,5.5) kṛṣṇe śarīre somasya śūdrāṇāṃ vadhamādiśet | pīte śarīre somasya vaiśyānāṃ vadhamādiśet
(50,5.6) rakte śarīre somasya rājñāṃ tu vadhamādiśet | śukle śarīre somasya brahmavṛddhiṃ vinirdiśet ||
(50,6.1) snigdhaḥ pītaḥ suvarṇābhaḥ pakṣādau yadi candramāḥ | gosthāyī saṃpradṛśyeta vipravṛddhiṃ vinirdiśet ||
(50,6.2) uccasthāne yadā pītaḥ samaśṛṅgaḥ śaśī bhavet | nāgavīthīgataḥ snigdhaḥ sa sarvaguṇapūjitaḥ ||
(50,6.3) dhūmrābho lāṅgalasthāyī śrīmān salakṣmamaṇḍalaḥ | pakṣādau yadi dṛśyeta brahmakṣatrasukhāvaḥ ||
(50,6.4) rājavīthīṃ tu saṃprāpta ugradaṇḍī yadā bhavet | haridrākuṅkumābhaśca śmaśānamavalokayet ||
(50,6.5) mṛtyuṃ saṃyojayetsomo bālākṛtiravāṅchirāḥ | lākṣārudhirasaṃkāśo dhanuḥsthāyī yadā bhavet ||
(50,7.1) saṃgrāmaṃ yojayetsomo loke tu tumulaṃ bhayam | dvicandraṃ gaganaṃ dṛṣṭvā brūyādbrahmavadho mahān ||
(50,7.2) dvau sūryau vā yadā syātāṃ tadā kṣatravadho mahān | dṛṣṭvā tu caturaḥ sūryānuditān sarvatodiśam ||
(50,7.3) śastreṇa janamāreṇa tadyugāntasya lakṣaṇam | āditye pāṇḍuraṃ chattraṃ saṃdhyāvelāṃ yadā bhavet ||
(50,7.4) deśasya vidravaṃ sūryo rājamṛtyuṃ vinirdiśet | ādityasya rathaḥ śvetaḥ saṃdhyāvelāṃ yadā bhavet ||
(50,7.5) pratyāsannaṃ bhayaṃ vidyāttasminnutpātadarśane ||
(50,8.1) ādityaḥ sarvataśchinno dvaidhībhutaḥ pradṛśyate. deśasya vidravaṃ sūryo rājamṛtyuṃ vinirdiśet ||
(50,9.1) kṣemaṃ vikukṣile brūyātsthālīpiṭharasaṃsthite | saṃkṣipte kṣīyate loko durbhikṣaṃ vajrasaṃsthite ||
(50,9.2) divā hyasmin patatyulkā satataṃ kampate mahī | aparvāśaninirghoṣāḥ saṃdhyā ca jvalanacchavā ||
(50,9.3) nakṣatrapātasyotpattirdhūmasya rajaso'pi vā | śṛṅgaṃ bhavatyādityasya tṛṇakāṣṭhaṃ ca śuṣyati ||
(50,9.4) rājāno hyaśivāstatra citraṃ varṣati mādhavaḥ | dvādaśānāṃ tu māsānāṃ madhye naśyati pārthivaḥ ||
(50,9.5) kārttikyāṃ śuklapakṣasya bahulasya trayodaśīm | vidyāttu svātisaṃpātaṃ divasānekaviṃśatim ||
(50,9.6) saptāhaṃ tu bhavedgoṣu saptāhaṃ mṛgapakṣiṣu | mānuṣeṣu ca saptāhaṃ tataḥ śreyastu kalpayet ||

(pariśiṣṭa_51. grahayuddham)
(51,1.1) oṃ ke cidgrahā nārarānāśrayante ke cidgrahā [jyotiṣi] saṃgrahe ca | graho graheṇaiva hataḥ kathaṃ syādvijñāya tattvaṃ bhagavān bravītu ||
(51,1.2) evaṃ sa pṛṣṭo munibhirmahātmā provāca gargo grahayuddhatantram | parājayaṃ caiva jayaṃ ca teṣāṃ śubhāsubham(aśubhamñ) caiva jagaddhitāya ||
(51,1.3) arko jātaḥ kaliṅgeṣu yavaneṣu ca candramāḥ | aṅgārakastvavantyāyāṃ magadhāyāṃ budhastathā ||
(51,1.4) bṛhaspatiḥ saindhaveṣu mahārāṣṭre tu bhārgavaḥ | śanaiścaraḥ surāṣṭrāyāṃ rāhustu giriśṛṅgajaḥ | keturmalayake jāta ityetadgrahajātakam ||
(51,1.5) yasmin deśe tu yo jātaḥ sa grahaḥ pīḍyate yadā taṃ deśaṃ ghātitaṃ vidyāddurbhikṣeṇa bhayena vā ||
(51,2.1) divākaraścaiva śanaiścarastathā bṛhaspatiścaiva budhaśca nāgarāḥ | prajāpatiḥ keturathāpi candramāstathaiva rāhūśanasau ca yāyinaḥ ||
(51,2.2) yadā graho nagara eva nāgaraṃ vijeṣyate yāyyatha vāpi yāyinam | tadā nṛpo nāgara eva nāgaraṃ vijeṣyate vāyyatha vāpi yāyinam ||
(51,2.3) ārohaṇaṃ ca bhedaśca lekhanaṃ savyadakṣiṇam | raśmisaṃsarjanaṃ caiva grahayuddhaṃ caturvidham ||
(51,2.4) prasavye vigrahaṃ brūyātsaṃgrāmaṃ raśmisaṃgame | lekhane'mātyapūḍā syādbhedane tu janakṣayaḥ ||
(51,2.5) sarveṣāṃ nabhasi samāgame grahāṇāmuktṛṣṭo bhavati tathaiva raśmivān yaḥ | snigdhatvaṃ bhavati tu yasya [sa graho graheṇa] saṃyukto bhavati [tu yaḥ] parājayeta śeṣaḥ ||
(51,3.1) śyāmo vā vyapagataraśmimaṇḍalo vā rūkṣo vā vyapagataraśmivān kṛśo vā | ākrānto vinipatitastato'pasavyo vijñeyo hata iti sa graho graheṇa ||
(51,3.2) budhaśca bhaumaḥ śanibhārgavāṅgirāḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ yāti yadā niśākaram | anamayatvaṃ triṣu saukhyamuttamaṃ viparyaye cāpi mahāñjanakṣayaḥ ||
(51,3.3) dhanakanakarajatasaṃcayāśca sarve śamadamamantraparāścaye manuṣyāḥ | śakayavanatukhārabālhikāśca kṣayamupayānti divākarasya ghāte ||
(51,3.4) atha some hate vidyāddhrvaṃ rājño viparyayaḥ | saṃharanti ca bhūtāni bhūmipālāḥ pṛthakpṛthak ||
(51,3.5) parasparaṃ virudhyante kṣudbhayaṃ cāpi dāruṇām | anāvṛṣṭibhayaṃ ghoraṃ vidyātsomaviparyaye ||
(51,4.1) traigartāḥ kṣitipatayaḥ sayodhamukhyāḥ pīḍyante girinilayāgnijīvinaśca | saṃgrāmāḥ sarudhirapāṃsuvarṣamiśrā durbhikṣaṃ bhavati dharāsutasya ghāte ||
(51,4.2) sāgaranilayāḥ paurāḥ kṣayamupayānti narā vaṇikpradhānāḥ | bhavati tu [rājā] vijayī prayāyī budhabandhane prapatanti cātra sobhyāḥ ||
(51,4.3) daivajñāstapasi ciraṃ suniścitārthāḥ syurdāntā nṛpatigaṇaḥ purohitāśca | āganturjayati vadhaśca nāgarāṇāṃ trailokyam <ca> bhayamupaiti gurostu ghāte ||
(51,4.4) yo rājā prathitaparākramaḥ pṛthivyāṃ vaṅgāṅgādiṣu madadhāḥ saśūrasenāḥ | ye yodhāḥ samaraṇabhumilabdhaśabdāste sainyaiḥ kṣayamupayānti śukraghāte ||
(51,4.5) mahiṣakavṛṣabhāḥ sabhasmapauṇḍrāḥ kṛṣipaśupālyaratāśca ye manuṣyāḥ | vividhabhayasamāhitāstu sarve kṣayamupayānti śanaiścarasya ghāte ||
(51,5.1) ye ke cinnṛpatiṣu dāmbhikāḥ piśācāḥ kāryāṇāṃ vrataniyameṣu channapāpāḥ | ye cānye śabarapulindacedigādhā bādhyante yadi bhavate'tra rāhughātaḥ ||
(51,5.2) ākrāntaṃ samanubhavanti yāyisaṃghā vadhyante yadi bhavate [paras] paro'highātaḥ | saṃgrāmāḥ sarudhirapāṃsuvarṣamiśra durbhikṣaṃ bhavati tu ketupīḍanena ||
(51,5.3) yatkiṃ ciddivigatamantarikṣajaṃ vā bhaumaṃ vā bhavati nimittamapraśastam | tatsavaṃ stanitamahābhrabidyudvarṣaiḥ śāntaṃ syādbhavati sadakṣiṇaiśca homaiḥ ||
(51,5.4) ye deśā grahagaṇabhinnabhūmikampā yeṣāṃ vā graha upayātacandrasūryaḥ | tān deśān [grahagaṇabhinnabhumikampān] parjanyaḥ śamayati saptarātravṛṣṭyā ||
(51,5.5) prasavyastriṣu māseṣu saṃsargo māsikaḥ smṛtaḥ | lekhane pakṣa ityāhurbhedane saptarātrikam ||
(51,5.6) āgneyā vāsavāścaiva vāyavyā vāruṇāstathā | sarva eva śubhā jñeyā gargasya vacanaṃ yathā ||

(pariśiṣṭa_52. grahasaṃgrahaḥ)
(52,1.1) omatharvāṇaṃ namaskṛtya uvāca bhagavānṛṣiḥ | kīdṛśā grahaputrāśca kiyanto vā vadasva me ||
(52,1.2) pṛṣṭaha sa śaunakenātha brāhmaṇānāṃ hitāya vai | saṃkhyāmuvāca bhagavān padmayonimataṃ yathā ||
(52,1.3) dikcāriṇo divicarā bhūcarā vyomacāriṇaḥ | divācarā rātricarā divārātricarāścaye ||
(52,1.4) pṛthikcarāśca ye tatra te ca syuḥ saṃghacāriṇaḥ | carantyaparavīthīṣu ye ca vibhrāntamaṇḍalāḥ ||
(52,1.5) te grahāḥ saṃgraheṇāhaṃ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ | anekavidhasaṃsthānaṃ pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ ||
(52,2.1) gokṣīrakumudaprakhyāstīvreṇa vapuṣānvitāḥ | carantyantaravīthīṣu snigdhā vipulatejasaḥ ||
(52,2.2) ete visarpakā nāma arciṣmanto mahāprabhāḥ | vijñeyāścaturaśītiḥ śukraputrā mahāgrahāḥ ||
(52,2.3) śuklā nikarāḥ saurābhāstyajanta iva cārciṣaḥ | sphuranta iva cākāśe bimbakā raśmibhirvṛtāḥ ||
(52,2.4) prāyaśo dakṣiṇe mārge nīcairvibhrāntamaṇḍalāḥ | vikacāḥ pañcaṣaṣṭiste bṛhaspatisutāḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(52,2.5) ye śvetāḥ kiṃcidākṛṣṇā viśikhāḥ syurvitārakāḥ | te ṣaṣṭiḥ kanakā nāma śanaiścarasutā grahāḥ ||
(52,3.1) ekapaKcāśato jñeyāstaskarāḥ sūkṣmaraśmayaḥ | baudhāḥ kamalagarbhābhāḥ kiṃcitpāṇḍuratejasaḥ ||
(52,3.2) kauṅkumā lohitāṅgasya putrā vidrumatejasaḥ | triśikhā vā tribhāgā vā ṣaṣṭirityuttare pathi ||
(52,3.3) nānādhūmanibhā rūkṣā dhūmavyākularaśmayaḥ | śatamekādhikaṃ mṛtyoḥ putrāḥ syurdhūmaketavaḥ ||
(52,3.4) kṛṣṇābhāḥ kṛṣṇaparyantāḥ kaluṣākṛtiraśmayaḥ | rāhoḥ putrāstrayastriṃśadgrahāstāmasakīlakāḥ ||
(52,3.5) nānāvarṇāgnisaṃkāśā jvālāmālā visarpiṇaḥ | viśvarūpāḥ sutā agnergrahā viṃśaṃ śataṃ smṛtam ||
(52,4.1) aruṇāstu sutā vāyordāruṇāḥ saptasaptatiḥ | vāteritā bhramantīva rūkṣā vikīrṇaraśmayaḥ ||
(52,4.2) tārāpuñjapratīkāśāstārāmaṇḍalasaṃvṛtāḥ | prājāpatyā grahāstvaṣṭau gaṇakā nāmanāmataḥ ||
(52,4.3) catvārastārajā yuktāḥ sūkṣmāṇo rūparaśmayaḥ | brahmasaṃtānakā nāma dve śate caturuttare ||
(52,4.4) vaṃśagulmapratīkāśā [vaṃśagulmasaraśmayaḥ] | kākatuṇḍanibhābhiśca raśmibhiḥ kiṃcidāvṛtāḥ ||
(52,4.5) udakaṃ cotsṛjantīva snigdhatvātsaumyadarśanāḥ | ete nāmnā smṛtāḥ kaṅkāstriṃśaddvau vāruṇā grahāḥ ||
(52,5.1) muṇḍatārāḥ kabandhābhā rukmakeśāśca raśmayaḥ | kālaputrāḥ kabandhāste smṛtāḥ ṣaṇṇavatirgrahāḥ ||
(52,5.2) arciṣmāṃśca prabhāsaśca romaśo viṣamāṃstathā | asnigdhāścātikāyāśca kiṃśuko rājasāyakaḥ ||
(52,5.3) ṛṣakaiścaiva rādhātaḥ kumudaḥ phanako ghanaḥ | eṣāmaśveti vijñeyā ārāstu parisarpakāḥ ||
(52,5.4) nakṣatracāriṇo hyete bhṛguputrā mahābalāḥ | pāṇḍurābhiḥ sudīrghābhiḥ śikhābhiḥ śītaraśmayaḥ ||
(52,5.5) atisaṃtānakāśtvanye ṣaṣṭirvāyoḥ sutā grahāḥ | vikesarāḥ prakāśante kṛṣṇalohitaraśmayaḥ ||
(52,6.1) miśrībhūtāstu te jñeyā guṇṭhitā iva reṇunā | dhūmaketoḥ sutā jñeyāḥ śatamekādhikaṃ ca tat ||
(52,6.2) atyarthaṃ kanakāstvanye prataptakanakaprabhāḥ | antakaputrakāḥ ṣaṣṭirasnigdhā madhyacāriṇaḥ ||
(52,6.3) ye tu nakṣatravaṃśasya bhāgamuttaramāśritāḥ | ekatārā vapuṣmanto mahākāyāḥ prabhānvitāḥ ||
(52,6.4) vyālakasya tu ye putrāḥ saptaṣaṣṭiḥ samantataḥ | nāmato'dhikacā nāma tattvajñaiḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
(52,6.5) saṃtānakanibhā ye tu dṛśyante sūkṣmaraśmayaḥ | ekatārā dvitārā vā atha vā pañcatārakaḥ ||
(52,7.1) brahmarāśestu te putrā grahāḥ saṃtānasaṃsthitāḥ | saṃcaranti nabhaḥ sarvamutpanne puruṣakṣaye ||
(52,7.2) aṇavo lohitāstvanye prakāśante'dhikeśakāḥ | pañcaṣaṣṭistu te jñeyāḥ prājāpatyā grahāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
(52,7.3) pariveṣeṣu jāteṣu grahāṇāṃ maṇḍaleṣu ca | dṛśyante kārmukā nāma saptatyekā samāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
(52,7.4) adharmasaṃbhavāstvanye caturdaśa parikramāḥ | adhaḥśikhāḥ prakāśante vivarṇā ghoratārakāḥ ||
(52,7.5) karṇachidraprātīkāśāḥ kṛṣṇāste tārakākṛtau | kīlakā rāhuputrāstu candrasūryatalāśrayāḥ ||
(52,8.1) vajraḥ kavandhastriśirāḥ śaṅkhabhedī śikhāvataḥ | daṇḍāśca rāhuputrāḥ syurnāmabhistulyavarcasaḥ ||
(52,8.2) yathā somārkayorjyotermaṇḍalābhyāśasevinaḥ | rājanyatvātpradṛśyante prajānāṃ saṃkṣayāvahāḥ ||
(52,8.3) tatra mandaphalā jñeyāḥ śaśāṅkatalasevinaḥ | divākaratalābhyāśaṃ sevino bhṛśadāruṇāḥ ||
(52,8.4) pannagāstu caturviṃśatkṛṣṇā dvātriṃśatirgrahāḥ | dakṣiṇādyāsu vīkṣyante nīcairvibhrāntamaṇḍalāḥ ||
(52,8.5) kevalaṃ tārakākārā dṛśyante niḥprabhaprabhāḥ | pītaraktā grahāḥ pañca pūrvadakṣiṇataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(52,9.1) dakṣiṇāparataścāpi pītaraktau grahau smṛtau | uttarāparatastvekaḥ pītarakto grahaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(52,9.2) aiśānyāṃ śvetaraktābha ekastiṣṭhati sūryakaḥ | yaḥ saṃdhivelāsvarkābho dikṣu sarvāsu dṛśyate ||
(52,9.3) nātidūre raveḥ snigdhaḥ sa varṣāyābhayāya ca | yavakrītoṣaraibhyāśca nāradaḥ sarvatastathā ||
(52,9.4) karṇaśca raibhyasya putrau cārvāvasuparāvasū | saptaite sthāvarā jñeyāḥ saha sūryeṇa sarpiṇaḥ ||
(52,9.5) sthāvarāṇāṃ narendrāṇāṃ prācyānāṃ pakṣamāśritāḥ | svastyātreyo mṛgavyādha ṛmucuḥ pramṛcustathā ||
(52,10.1) prabhāsaścandrabhāsaśca tathāgastyaḥ pratāpavān | dṛḍhavratastriśaṅkuśca ajau vaiśvānare mṛḍaḥ ||
(52,10.2) aruṇaśca danuścaiva yāmyāyāṃ sthāvarāḥ smṛtāḥ | gautamo'trirvasiṣṭhaśca viśvāmitrastu kaśyapaḥ ||
(52,10.3) ṛcīkaputraśca tathā bharadvājaśca vīryavān | ete sapta mahātmāna udīcyāṃ sthāvarāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
(52,10.4) śiśumāreṇa sahitā dhruveṇa ca mahātmanā | pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ somo bhṛguraṅgirasā saha ||
(52,10.5) hāhāhūhū ca vijñeyau viṣṇośca padamuttamam | madhyāntasthāvarāṇāṃ tu niyatāviti buddhimān ||
(52,11.1) kūṭasthānāni sarvāṇi dikṣvetānyupadhārayet | prabhānvitāni śvetāni snigdhāni vimalāni ca ||
(52,11.2) arciṣmanti prasannāni tāni kuryuḥ prajāhitam | niḥprabhāṇi vivarṇāni nirarcīmṣyamalāni ca ||
(52,11.3) hrasvānyasnehayuktāni na bhāvāya bhavanti hi | yatkiṃcitsthāvaraṃ loke tatprasanneṣu vardhate ||
(52,11.4) kūṭastheṣvaprasanneṣu sthāvaraṃ parihīyate | ādityaścaiva śukraśca lohitāṅgastathaiva ca ||
(52,11.5) rāhuḥ somaḥ śanaiścaro bṛhaspatibudhau tathā | aindra āgneyo yāmyaśca nairṛto vāruṇastathā ||
(52,12.1) vāyavyaścaiva saumyaśca brāhmaścaivāṣṭamo grahaḥ | navamaścaiva vijñeyo dhūmaketurmahāgrahaḥ ||
(52,12.2) yugagrahā na cānye te tatrāṣṭau diggrahāḥ smṛtāḥ | sanakṣatreṣu mārgeṣu dṛśyante tu yugagrahāḥ ||
(52,12.3) vibhrāntamaṇḍalāḥ śeṣā dṛśyante khacarā grahaḥ | mṛtyorniśvāsajāścānye jñeyāḥ ṣoḍaśa ketavaḥ ||
(52,12.4) kūṣmāṇḍavadvisaṃchannastriṇavā dakṣiṇe pathi | ekādaśaiva vijñeyā dvādaśādityasaṃbhavāḥ ||
(52,12.5) sūryavarcanirīkṣāste tejodhātumayā grahāḥ | dakṣayajñe tu rudrasya krodhādanye tu niḥsṛtāḥ ||
(52,13.1) bhīmarūpā daśaikaśca jvalāṅkuśadharā grahāḥ | sapta paitāmahāstvanye tiryaggā jarjaragrahāḥ ||
(52,13.2) śikhāḥ sṛjanto vitatāstantuśuklapaṭopamāḥ | śvetaketava ityanye vyākhyātā daśa pañca ca ||
(52,13.3) uddālakarṣiputrāste nīcairvibhrāntamaṇḍalāḥ | te syuḥ śvetaśikhāḥ sarve saumyāḥ kāntāstanuprabhāḥ ||
(52,13.4) aṣṭādaśendunā sārdhaṃ mathyamāne purāmṛte | ketavaḥ kundapuṣpābhāḥ kṣīrodanabhasi smṛtāḥ ||
(52,13.5) viraśmayaśca viśikhā mahākāyā nirarciṣaḥ | raupyakumbhanibhāḥ saumyā grahāḥ syuḥ śītatejasaḥ ||
(52,14.1) brahmakopamayastveko viśvātmā sarvato grahaḥ | caturyugānte lokānāmudayastasya vidyate ||
(52,14.2) nakṣatrapathamutsṛjya nabhoaṃśāḥ pārśvacāriṇaḥ | pūrvato'bhyuditā vā syurnīcairuttaratastathā ||
(52,14.3) bhūmyāmabhyuditā vā syurhrasvasnehapariplutāḥ | sarva eva tu vijñeyā grahā mandaphalodayāḥ ||
(52,14.4) sarveṣāṃ paitṛkaṃ karma prajābhāgyodbhavaṃ mahat | sarve te sarvato hanyuraśubhaṃ yadvadanti ca ||
(52,14.5) tatkarmajanmamāhātmyaṃ śīlābhijanameva ca | tadrūpāṃstadguṇāṃścāpi tanmayāṃstatparigrahān ||
(52,15.1) sarva eva rogapradā mṛtyuśastrāgnitaskaraiḥ | paśusasyopaghātaiśca hanyuranyaiśca kāraṇaiḥ ||
(52,15.2) dhūpanātsparśanātsthānādudayāstamasaṃbhavāt | hanyuḥ pañcavidhaṃ sarve ketavo nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
(52,15.3) mṛdudhruvograkṣipreṣu sādhāraṇacareṣu ca | dāruṇeṣu ca ṛkṣeṣu sādhāraṇacareṣu ca | dāruṇeṣu ca ṛkṣeṣu vidyāttatsadṛśaṃ phalam ||
(52,15.4) yathādiṣṭaṃ yathāvarṇaṃ yathāvargaparigraham | sarva evoditā hanyuḥ sarva eva mahāgrahāḥ ||
(52,15.5) mṛtyoḥ kālasya sūryasya brahmaṇastryambakasya ca | bhaumasya rāhoragneśca prajātā ye sudāruṇāḥ ||
(52,16.1) prajāpateśca dharmasya somasya varuṇasya ca | pītādyāśca diśāṃ putrā vijñeyā mṛdudāruṇāḥ ||
(52,16.2) kaśyapasya ca marīceruddālakaṛṣestathā | putrā mandaphalā jñeyāsteṣāmamṛtasaṃbhavāḥ ||
(52,16.3) śukrādīnāṃ ca ye putrā grahāṇāṃ parikīrtitāḥ | teṣāṃ vīryāṇi jāniyātpitṛbhyaḥ sādhikāni tu ||
(52,16.4) nāradātreyagargāṇāṃ guroruśanasastathā | grahāṇāṃ saṃgraho hyevameṣa kārtsnyena kīrtitaḥ ||
(52,16.5) anekaśatasāhasra anekaśatalakṣaṇaḥ | devalabdhavarākāśe prāha sarvān pṛthakpṛthak ||
(52,16.6) etadutthe tu sarvasmin bhaye'tha samupasthite | mahāśāntiṃ prakurvīta rājā rāṣṭrasya rakṣaṇe ||
(52,16.7) tatprayāti śamaṃ sarvaṃ prajānāṃ tu sukhaṃ bhavet | rājāno muditāstatra pālayanti vasuṃdharām ||

(pariśiṣṭa_53. rāhucāraḥ)
(53,1.1) omupetya yaścandramasaṃ raviṃ vā gṛhṇāti sūnorasurasya putraḥ | nibodhataitasya śubhāśubhāni grahasya mṛtyoḥ praticeṣṭitāni ||
(53,1.2) yadā tu rāhuḥ śaśino raver<vā> [grahe] grahītukāmo bhavati prasahya | tadā karotyadbhutadarśanāni yairjñāyate rāhurupaiṣyatīti ||
(53,1.3) udvepate tadā candro yadā rāhuḥ pradṛśyate || pāṇḍurvā jāyate klībaḥ śaśo vāsya vivardhate ||
(53,1.4) rekhāntarāpurārāṇi kalmaṣāṇi divākare | vadanti ca bhaṛśaṃ śvāno vānti vātāśca bhīṣaṇāḥ ||
(53,1.5) saṃdhyayorubhayoścolkā gavāṃ prakṣīyate payaḥ | kṣīriṇāmaiva vṛkṣāṇāṃ tadaharnaśyate payaḥ ||
(53,2.1) apsu snigdhāni dṛśyante kākābadhnanti maṇḍalam | ūrdhvaṃ vadati gomāyuryadā rāhuḥ pradṛśyate ||
(53,2.2) candragrahanimittāni śuklapakśāṣṭamīparam | ā paurṇamāsyā dṛśyeran sūryasya ca tathobhayoḥ ||
(53,2.3) udito gṛhyamāṇastu hanti vedavido janān | bālāṃśca jaṭilān hanti ye ca kāṣāyavāsasaḥ ||
(53,2.4) yauvanasthāṃśca pūrvāhṇe hanti yajñavido janān | audakāni ca sarvāṇi nāgendrāścātra duḥkhitāḥ ||
(53,2.5) atha madhyapathaṃ prāptaḥ śūdrān hanti ca taskarān | parivṛkto nṛpaṃ hanti candraścaraṇacāraṇān ||
(53,3.1) pralambaḥ pramadān hanti kṣatraṃ rāṣṭraṃ ca sarvaśaḥ | traigartāścātra pīḍyante ye ca daṇḍabhṛto janāḥ ||
(53,3.2) uparakto yadodeti yadā vā pratitiṣṭhati | ayogakṣemamādiśyettriṣu lokeṣu dāruṇam ||
(53,3.3) śāradaṃ trīṇi varṣāṇi sasyaṃ jātaṃ na pacyate | naidāghenātra jīvanti prajā mūlaphalena vā ||
(53,3.4) sarvaṃ saṃgrasate jyotiściramantardadhāti vā | hanti sphītāni rāṣṭrāṇi pradhānaṃ ca mahīpatim ||
(53,3.5) yadi <tu> rāhurubhau śaśibhāskarau grasati pakṣamanantaramantataḥ | puruṣaśoṇitakardamavāhinī bhavati bhūrna ca varṣati mādhavaḥ ||
(53,4.1) gṛhītvā bhāskaraṃ pūrvaṃ gṛhṇāti śaśinaṃ yadi | taṃ tu somottaraṃ nāśastatra rdhyante hi devatāḥ ||
(53,4.2) vayasāṃ maraṇaṃ chidre prakīrṇe rakṣasāṃ vadhaḥ | nāgānāṃ tu mahānāge chidre devo na varṣati ||
(53,4.3) śveto varṇo brāhmaṇānāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu lohitaḥ | vaiśyānāṃ pītako varṇaḥ śūdrāṇāṃ kṛṣṇa ucyate ||
(53,4.4) eteṣāṃ yena varṇena rāhuścarati bhāskare | yā jātistasya varṇasya tāṃ sa pīḍayate grahaḥ ||
(53,4.5) śyāmo bhavati vātāya draukṣo bhavati vṛṣṭaye | haridraḥ sarvadhānyānāṃ kopaṃ sṛjati dāruṇam ||
(53,5.1) tāmro bhavati śastrāya śastrāya rūkṣo bhavati mṛtyave | bahvākārastu bhūtānāṃ ghoraṃ janayate jvaram ||
(53,5.2) dhūmavarṇo'gnivarṇo vā grāmeṣu nagareṣu vā | agnyutpātān gṛhasthānāṃ karotīha mahāgrahaḥ ||
(53,5.3) nīlalohitaparyanto rāhuścarati bhāskare | amātyo hanti rājānaṃ rājā vāmātyamātmaha ||
(53,5.4) yasya rājñaśca nakṣatre svarbhānuruparajyati | rājyabhraṃśaṃ suhṛnnāśaṃ maraṇaṃ cātra nirdiśet ||
(53,6.1) snigdhavarṇo yadārciṣmān parvasthaḥ snehavān grahaḥ | kṣipraṃ vāpyuditaṃ brūhi sarvabhūtabhayāya vai ||
(53,6.2) pradakṣiṇe tu somasya apasavye tu vigrahaḥ | raśmibhede bhayaṃ ghoramullekhe mantriṇāṃ vadhaḥ ||
(53,6.3) darśayitvā tu rūpāṇi yadā rāhurna dṛśyate | śastradurbhikṣasaṃpātairbhayaṃ ghoraṃ vinirdiśet ||
(53,6.4) gṛhītāstamitayostu na bhuñjīran dvijātayaḥ | ā punardarśanāttābhyāṃ japahomau vivartayet ||
(53,6.5) candrasūryagrahe nādyādadyātsnātvā tu muktayoḥ | amuktayorastaṃgayoradyātsnātvā pare'hani ||
(53,6.6) tataḥ ṣaṭsu tataḥ ṣaṭsu tato'dhyardheṣū vā punaḥ | ardhavarṣeṣu māseṣu ādityasya tato grahaḥ ||
(53,6.7) tataḥ ṣaṭsu tataḥ ṣaṭsu triṣu varṣeṣu vā punaḥ | etāvadeva rāhostu cāramāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_54. ketucārāḥ)
(54,1.1) oṃ bhārgavastu purovāca maharṣīn bhagavānṛṣiḥ | ketusaṃcāraṃ śṛṇuta utthānaṃ caiva yādṛśam ||
(54,1.2) nimittani ca vakṣyāmitasyoktāni hi yāni tu | tāni sarvāṇi jāniyādutpātajñānakovidaḥ ||
(54,1.3) viprāñśvetākṛtirhanti kṣatriyān hanti lohitaḥ | vaiśyāṃstu pītako hanti śūdrān hanti tathāsitaḥ ||
(54,1.4) itarān pīḍayetketuranyavarṇo yadā bhavet | ṣaṇmāsābhyantare rājño maraṇaṃ ca tadādiśet ||
(54,1.5) śvetaḥ śastrākulaṃ kuryāllohitastvagnito bhayam. kṣudbhayaṃ pītakaḥ kuryātkṛṣṇo rogamatholbaṇam ||
(54,2.1) yasmin deśe śirastasya sa deśaḥ pīḍyate bhṛśam | madhye tu madhyamā pīḍā yato pucchaṃ tato jayaḥ ||
(54,2.2) śaktyākāro'tināśāya duḥkhāya musalākṛtiḥ | dīrghaḥ sūkṣmaḥ sukhāyaiva hrasvaḥ sthūlo vināśakṛt ||
(54,2.3) utthānaṃ caiva ketūnāṃ vināśāyaiva hi smṛtam | tasmādātharvaṇairmantraiḥ śamanamārayedbudhaḥ ||
(54,2.4) māhendrūnanṛtān raydrūn vauśvadevīmathāpi vā | utpāteṣu mahāśāntiṃ kārayedbahudakṣiṇām ||
(54,2.5) ārādhitāḥ śamaṃ yānti tadutpātā na saṃśayaḥ | homairjapyaiśca vividhairdānaiścabahurūpakaiḥ ||
(54,2.6) tasya yatra śiro deśe tata utthāya vāvrajet | dhanaṃ vā sarvamutsṛjya mṛtyormucyetā vā na vā ||
(54,2.7) dattvā vā pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ rājā śāntiṃ niyacchati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_55. ṛtuketulakṣaṇam)
(55,1.1) omṛtuketūn pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ | yāvanto yasya putrāḥ syuḥ kuryuryaccoditā divi ||
(55,1.2) sarve te dharaṇījātā māsā ye devanirmitāḥ | ādityaraśmibhirbaddhāḥ saure tiṣṭhanti maṇḍale ||
(55,1.3) daśa vai vāruṇāstatra sūryaputrāstu viṃśatiḥ | caturviṃśatirāgneyā yamaputrā nava smṛtāḥ ||
(55,1.4) aṣṭādaśa ca kauberā vāyuputrāstu viṃśatiḥ | eṣā saṃkhyā tu ketūnāṃ śatamekottaraṃ smṛtam ||
(55,1.5) śrāvaṇaprauṣṭhapadayorvāruṇāṃstu vinirdiśet ||
(55,1.6) [ārādhitāḥ śamaṃ yānti tadutpātā na saṃśayaḥ | homairjāpaiśca vividhairdānaiśca bahurūpakaiḥ ||]
(55,1.7) āvāhayettato meghān pūrṇāṃ kuryādvasuṃdharām | unmattāḥ sarito yānti jalavegasamāhitāḥ ||
(55,1.8) dhānyaṃ samarghatāṃ yāti ītayo na bhavanti hi udaye vāruṇānāṃ tu etadbhavati lakṣaṇam ||
(55,2.1) aśvayuji kārttike ca sūryaputrān vinirdiśet | teṣāṃ caiva tu karmāṇi lakṣaṇaiḥ śṛṇu yādṛśaiḥ ||
(55,2.2) tato dahati dīptāṃśuḥ sarvānnāni divākaraḥ | mriyante ca tathā gāvaḥ śvāpadāśca viśeṣataḥ ||
(55,2.3) viṣaṃ ca prabalaṃ tatra sarvadamṣṭriṣu dāruṇam | udaye sūryaputrāṇāmetadbhavati lakṣaṇam ||
(55,3.1) mārgaśīrṣe ca pauṣe ca agniputrān vinirdiśet | teṣāṃ caiva tu karmāṇi lakṣaṇaiḥ śṛṇu yādṛśaiḥ ||
(55,3.2) agnirdahati rāṣṭrāṇi haritāni vanāni ca vidravanti tato deśāḥ samantādbhayapīḍitāḥ ||
(55,3.3) kasmiṃścijjāyate kṣemaṃ kasmiṃścijjāyate bhayam | udaye hyagniputrāṇāmetadbhavati lakṣaṇam ||
(55,4.1) māghaphālbunayormadhye yamaputrān vinirdiśet | teṣāṃ caiva tu karmāṇi lakṣaṇaiḥ śṛṇu yādṛśaiḥ ||
(55,4.2) śīghraṃ bhavati durbhikṣaṃ hāhābhūtamacetanam | chardijvarātisārāśca glāniścaivākṣivedanā ||
(55,4.3) udaye yamaputrāṇāmetadbhavati lakṣaṇam ||
(55,5.1) caitravaiśākhayormadhye kauberāṃstu vinirdiśet | teṣāṃ caiva tu karmāṇi lakṣaṇaiḥ śṛṇu yādṛśaiḥ ||
(55,5.2) ucchritairdhvajavedībhirucchritairdhvajatoraṇaiḥ | havirdhūmākulā tatra dṛśyate vasudhā tadā ||
(55,5.3) triviṣṭapaṃ samāpannastadā śakro mahīpatiḥ | evaṃ prajñāstu manyante kubere grahamāgate ||
(55,5.4) udaye tu kuberāṇāmetadbhavati lakṣaṇam ||
(55,6.1) jyaiṣṭhe caiva tathāṣāḍhe vāyuputrān vinirdiśet | teṣāṃ caiva tu karmāṇi lakṣaṇaiḥ śṛṇu yādṛśaiḥ ||
(55,6.2) vāyanti ca mahāvātā mahāyuddhaṃ mahābhayam | bhajyante ca mahāvṛkṣāstoraṇāṭṭālakāni ca ||
(55,6.3) gṛhāṇi rāmaṇīyāni kṣayaṃ yānti jalāni ca | udaye vāyuputrāṇāmetadbhavati lakṣaṇam ||
(55,6.4) ṛtugatamudayanameṣāṃ mahaujasāṃ vāruṇādiketūnām | jānāti yaḥ phalaṃ ca protkṛṣṭāḥ saṃpadastasya ||

(pariśiṣṭa_56. kūrmavibhāgaḥ)
(56,1.1) oṃ kṛttikārohiṇīsaumyaṃ madhyaṃ kūrmasya nirdiśet || śeṣānṛkṣavibhāge tu trikaṃ prati vinirdiśet ||
(56,1.2) sāketamithile mekalālayāvahichattranāgapuraṃ kāśipāriyātrakurupāñcālāḥ || atha kosalakauśāmbītīraṃ pāṭaliputraṃ kaliṅgapurapṛthivīmaṇḍalamadhye'bhihate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.3) aṅgavaṅgakaliṅgamāgadhamahendragavasamambaṣṭhāḥ || bhāgāḥ pūrvasamudrāḥ śirasyabhihate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.4) khaśabhadrā samataṭasamavardhamānakavaidehā gāndhārāḥ || kosalatosalaveṇātaṭasajjapurā mādreyatāmaliptā dakṣiṇapūrve hate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.5) āvantyakā vidarbhā matsyā cakorabhīma[gam]rathā yavanavalayakāntīsiṃhalalaṅkuṇanāsikyakarmaṇoyāmimahinarmadabhṛgukacchā dakṣiṇapaścāddhate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.6) sahyagirivaijayantī kuṇkuṇanāsikyakarmaṇoyāmimahinarmadabhṛgukacchā dakṣiṇapaścāddhate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.7) saurāṣṭrasindhusauvīramālavā rāmarāṣṭrakānvītān || ānartagacchayantrān pucche'bhihate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.8) sārasvatāṃstrigartānmatsyānnānvārabālhikān || mathurāpuraṃgadeśānuttarabhāge hate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.9) brahmāvartaṃ śatadruhimavantaṃ parvataṃ ca mainākaṃ kāśmīraṃ caiva tathā uttarapārśve hate'bhihanyāt ||
(56,1.10) nepālakāmarūpaṃ ca videhodumbaraṃ tathā || tathāvantyaḥ kaikayaśca uttarapūrve hate'bhihanyāt ||

(pariśiṣṭa_57. maṇḍalāni)
(57,1.1) viśākhe kṛttikāḥ puṣyaḥ pūrvau proṣṭhapadau tathā | bharaṇyaśca maghāścaiva phalgunyau prathame tathā ||
(57,1.2) yadyatra calate bhūmirnirghātolkāsta eva vā | aśarīrāśca nardante kampante daivatāni ca ||
(57,1.3) ādityo vātra gṛhyeta somo vāpyuparajyate | āgneyaṃ tadvijānīyāddurbhikṣaṃ cātra nirdiśet ||
(57,1.4) alpakṣīrāstathā gāvo agnervyādhiśca jāyate | purāṇi deśā grāmāśca pīḍyante hyagninā tadā ||
(57,1.5) pīḍyante cāgnikarmāṇo agniveśāśca ye narāḥ | pittajvarastathā śvāsaha prajāḥ pīḍayate tadā ||
(57,1.6) akṣirogāstathā ghorāḥ puruṣāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ | āpagāścātra śuṣyanti na ca sasyavatī mahī ||
(57,1.7) tapyate ca tadā bhūmirna ca devo'bhivarṣati | nīlalohitaparyaktā aphalāḥ pādapāstathā ||
(57,1.8) durbhikṣaṃ marako vyādhiḥ paracakrabhayaṃ tathā | etai rūpaistu vijñeyamāgneyaṃ caladarśanam ||
(57,2.1) hasto'śvinyau tathā citrā ṛkṣamaryamadaivatam | brāhmaṃ mṛgaśiraḥ svātirvāyavyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ smṛtam ||
(57,2.2) yadyatra calate bhūmirnirghātolkāsta eva vā | aśarīrāśca nardante kampante daivatāni ca ||
(57,2.3) ādityo vātra gṛhyeta somo vāpyuparajyate | vāyavyaṃ tadvijānīyādakṣemaṃ cātra nirdiśet ||
(57,2.4) pāṃsuvarṣaṃ tadā ghoraṃ kṣīre sarpirna vidyate | prāsādatoraṇādīni prapatanti mahītale ||
(57,2.5) madrakā yavanāścaiva śakāḥ kāmbojabālhikāḥ | gandhārāśca vinaśyanti etaiḥ sārdhaṃ tathā kila ||
