(57,2.6) gajā vājina uṣṭrāśca vṛkā nakulaceṭakāḥ | pīḍyante vyādhinā sarve ye ca śastropajīvinaḥ ||
(57,2.7) pure śreṣṭhā vinaśyanti gaṇeṣu guṇasaṃmitāḥ | gṛhāṇi ramaṇīyāni viśīryante ca sarvaśaḥ ||
(57,2.8) āyāsaścolbaṇastatra śastrabhrāmaḥ samantataḥ | etai rūpaistu vijñeyaṃ vāyavyaṃ caladarśanam ||
(57,3.1) ārdrāśleṣāstathā mūlan pūrvāṣāḍhāstathaiva ca | vāruṇaṃ revatī caiva sūryadaivatyameva ca ||
(57,3.2) yadyatra calate bhūmirnirghātolkāsta eva vā | aśarīrāśca nardante kampante daivatāni ca ||
(57,3.3) ādityo vātra gṛhyeta somo vāpyuparajyate | vāruṇaṃ tadvijānīyātsubhikṣaṃ cātra nirdiśet ||
(57,3.4) bahukṣīrāstathā gāvo nāgāśca phalinastathā | śāntārayaḥ prajāḥ sarvā gomino jaṅgalaṃ payaḥ ||
(57,3.5) jalopajīvinaḥ sarve prāpnuvantyṛddhimuttamām | khecarāścātra dṛśyante snigdhavarṇāḥ samantataḥ ||
(57,3.6) nyastaśastrāśca rājāno brahmakṣatraṃ ca vardhate | etai rūpaistu vijñeyaṃ vāruṇaṃ caladarśanam ||
(57,4.1) jyeṣṭhānurādhā śravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhāśca punarvasū | prājāpatyamāṣāḍhāśca māhendraṃ maṇḍalaṃ smṛtam ||
(57,4.2) yadyatra calate bhūmirnirghātolkāsta eva vā | aśarīrāśca nardante kampante daivatāni ca ||
(57,4.3) ādityo vātra gṛhyeta somo vāpyuparajyate | māhendraṃ tadvijānīyātsukṣemaṃ cātra nirdiśet ||
(57,4.4) gāvaḥ samagravatsāśca striyaḥ putrasamanvitāḥ | kīṭā vyālā mriyante ca ye cānye svedajantavaḥ ||
(57,4.5) vedādhyayanayajñeṣu brāhmaṇā niratāḥ sadā | viṭkṣatriyāḥ svakarmāṇaḥ śūdrāḥ śuśrūṣakārakāḥ ||
(57,4.6) viṃśatiśataṃ tvāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ navatiṃ calet | aśītiṃ calate tvaindraṃ vāruṇaṃ saptatiṃ calet ||
(57,4.7) āgneyo bhūmikampo yaḥ sa dvimāsādvipacyate | vāruṇastu phalaṃ sadyo vāyavyastu trimāsikaḥ || māhendrasya phalaṃ vidyānmāsamardhaṃ tathaiva ca ||

(pariśiṣṭa_58. digdāhalakṣaṇam)
(58,1.1) omata ūrdhvaṃ ca digdāhān kīrtyamānānnibodhata | yathā diśaḥ pradahyante tāsāṃ dāhaphalaṃ ca yat ||
(58,1.2) indro'gnirmarutaścaiva pradahanti diśo daśa | śubhāśubhāya lokānāṃ kṛtāntenābhicoditāḥ ||
(58,1.3) yadāstamita āditye vahnerjvālā pradṛśyate. diśāṃ dāhaṃ tu tadvidyādbhārgavasya vaco yathā ||
(58,1.4) nānārāgasamutthānāṃ nānāvidhaphalodayāḥ | pāṃśuneva ca saṃchannā digdāhāyogamāśritāḥ ||
(58,1.5) diśaḥ sarvāḥ pradahyante akṣemāya phalāya ca | avadāhādṛte dāhaṃ yadi snigdhāḥ pradarśanāḥ ||
(58,1.6) tamodhūmarajaskā ye dīptadvijamṛgāvṛtāḥ | pradīptalakṣaṇāḥ satyāḥ sarva evāhitāvahāḥ ||
(58,1.7) tathā kanakakiṃjalkataḍitkalpāḥ śivāśca ye | rūkṣāḥ kṛṣṇātha māñjiṣṭhā bandhujīvakavacca ye ||
(58,1.8) śvetā raktāśca pītāśca dāhāḥ kṛṣṇāśca varṇataḥ | brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdravināśāya prakīrtitāḥ ||
(58,1.9) raktāḥ śastrabhayaṃ kuryurpītā vyādhiprakopanāḥ | agnivarṇāstathā kuryuragniśastrabhayaṃ mahat ||
(58,1.10) sapītaparuṣaśyāmā ye ca vāruṇasaṃnibhāḥ | sarva eva kṣudhārogamṛtyuśastrāgnikopanāḥ ||
(58,1.11) ṛtau tu varṣaṃ tīvraṃ syātsainyavidravameva ca | bhṛśamuddyotanī saṃdhyā kurute vā grahāgamam ||
(58,1.12) dikṣu dagdhāsu pīḍyante yathādigdeśabhaktayaḥ | śakunajñānanirdiṣṭā ye ca tatrādhikārakāḥ ||
(58,1.13) yathoktā tu mahāśāntiryathoktavidhinā kṛtā | sarvaṃ digdāhajaṃ ghoraṃ śamayetsā sadakṣiṇā ||

(pariśiṣṭa_58 . ulkālakṣaṇam)
(58 ,1.1) omulkādayo hi nirdiṣṭā nirghātāstu purātra ye | teṣāmidānīṃ vakṣyāmi viśeṣāṃstu pṛthakpṛthak ||
(58 ,1.2) aṅgānāmāntarikṣāṇāṃ yadaṅgamabhipūjitam | tadulkālakṣaṇaṃ śrīmadaṅgaṃ kārtsnyena vakṣyate ||
(58 ,1.3) apradhṛṣyāṇi yāni syuḥ śarīrāṇīndriyairdṛḍhaiḥ | kṣamāvanti viśuddhāni satyavratarātāni ca ||
(58 ,1.4) tānyetāni prakāśante bhābhirvitimiraṃ nabhaḥ | samantājjvalayantīha yasmādasukaraṃ nabhaḥ ||
(58 ,1.5) tāni bhāvakṣayādbhūyaḥ pracyutāni nabhastalāt | kṣitau salakṣaṇānyeva nipatantīha bhārgava
(58 ,1.6) teṣāṃ nipatatāṃ tatra yatrayatropalakṣyate | tatratatraiva vividhaṃ prajānāṃ jāyate bhayam ||
(58 ,1.7) rūpavarṇaprabhāsnehapramaṇākṛtisaṃgamaiḥ | teṣāṃ balābalaṃ jñātvā guṇadoṣaḥ pravakṣyate ||
(58 ,1.8) tārā dhiṣṇyāstatholkāśca vidyuto'śanayastathā | vikalpāḥ pañcadhā caiṣāṃ parasparabalottarāḥ ||
(58 ,1.9) tatra śabdenba mahatā visvareṇa vikarṣiṇā | mahācakramivāgacchadāyatākṣā nabhastalāt ||
(58 ,1.10) manuṣyamṛgahastyaśvavṛkṣāśmapathaveśmasu | patantyaśanayo dīptāḥ sphoṭayantyo dharātalam ||
(58 ,2.1) sahasaivopapanneṣu bhṛśaṃ tadanuyāyinā | sattvavibhraṃśinātyarthaṃ śabdenodvegakāriṇā ||
(58 ,2.2) jvālābhāravisarpiṇyaḥ prakṛtyā duḥkhadarśanāḥ | vidyuto nipatantyāśu jīveṣu vanarāśiṣu ||
(58 ,2.3) tīkṣṇaśūlaviśālāgrā patantī cāpi vardhate | prakṛtyā pauruṣī tūlkā tasyā bhedānnibodhata ||
(58 ,2.4) kṛśā nārīva dīptā syācchikhā sāṅgāravarṣiṇī | uddyotayantī gaganaṃ kāñcanenaiva varmaṇā ||
(58 ,2.5) pītena pāṇḍunā vāpi dhūmadhūmāruṇena vā | viśīryatā mahābhreṇa mahatā cānuṣaṅgiṇā ||
(58 ,2.6) vaṃśagulmanibhāścāpi kāścidindradhvajopamāḥ | kāścidindrāyudhaprakhyāḥ kāścinmaṇḍalasaṃsthitāḥ ||
(58 ,2.7) chattravaccāpu dṛśyante cakravannipatanti ca | daṇḍavaccāpi tiṣṭhanti pradhāvanti ca sarpavat ||
(58 ,2.8) prakīrṇena kalāpena khe gacchantīva barhiṇaḥ | abhyucchritena pucchena yāti kā ciddharātalam ||
(58 ,2.9) tejāṃsi vikirantyanyāḥ pradhāvanti ca golavat ||
(58 ,3.1) pranṛttapretamārjātavarāhānugatāstathā | sasvanā niḥsvanāścāpi patanti dharaṇītale ||
(58 ,3.2) etāsāṃ phalamulkānāṃ pravakṣyāmi pṛthakpṛthak | tantunaiva hi saṃbaddhā uhyamāneva vāyunā ||
(58 ,3.3) patantī dṛśyate kā citkā cidbhramati cāmbare | ulkāsaṃghaiḥ parivṛtā kā cidyāti dharātalam ||
(58 ,3.4) sāmānyaṃ tu phalaṃ tāsāṃ tatsamāsena vakṣyate | varāhapretaśārdūlasiṃhamārjāravārāiḥ ||
(58 ,3.5) tulyā bhayāvahā ulkā nikṛṣṭāhinibhā ca yā | śūlapaṭṭiśaśaktyṛṣṭimudgarāsiparaśvadhaiḥ ||
(58 ,3.6) vṛṣṭyākāreṇa tulyāśca vṛkṣābhāśca vigarhitāḥ | padmaśaṅkhenduvajrāhimatsyadhvajanibhāḥ śubhāḥ ||
(58 ,3.7) śrīvṛkṣasvastikāvārtahaṃsadviradavarcasaḥ | jvalitāṅgārasaṃkāśā jihmagā atha śīghragāḥ ||
(58 ,3.8) vinā pucchāvakāśena hrasvenātikṛśena vā | daśāntarāṇi dhanuṣaḥ pucchaṃ cāpi pradṛśyate ||
(58 ,3.9) ulkāvikāro boddhavyo dhiṣṇya ityabhisaṃjñitaḥ | yastu śuklena varṇena vapuṣā pelavena vā ||
(58 ,3.10) padmatantunikāśena dhūmarājīnibhena vā | ulkāvikāraḥ so'pyuktastārakā nāmanāmataḥ ||
(58 ,3.11) evaṃ pañcavidhā hyetāḥ śaunakena prakīrtitāḥ | svargacyutānāṃ patatāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ puṇyakarmaṇām ||
(58 ,4.1) etāsāmindraśirasi patanaṃ nṛpaterbhayam | devatārcāsu patane rājarāṣṭrabhayaṃ bhavet ||
(58 ,4.2) puradvare purakṣobha indrakīle janakṣayaḥ | brahmāyatanaghāteṣu brāhmaṇānāmupadravaḥ ||
(58 ,4.3) caityavṛkṣābhighāteṣu satkṛtyānāṃ mahadbhayam | dvāre cāyuḥkṣayaṃ vidyādgṛhe tu svāmino bhayam ||
(58 ,4.4) goṣṭheṣu gomināṃ vidyātkarṣakāṇāṃ khaleṣu ca | gṛheṣu rājñāṃ jānīyādbheṣu tadbhaktināṃ bhayam ||
(58 ,4.5) āśāgrahopaghāteṣu taddeśyānāṃ tapasvinām | adhomukhīnṛpaṃ hanyādbrāhmaṇānūrdhvagāminī ||
(58 ,4.6) tiryaggā rājapatnīṃ ca śreṣṭhinaḥ pratilomanī | vaṃśagulmanibhā rāṣṭraṃ nṛpamindradhvajopamā ||
(58 ,4.7) gajamindrāyudhaprakhyā puraṃ maṇḍalasaṃsthitā | mantriṇaścakrasaṃsthānā chattrākārā purodhasam ||
(58 ,4.8) mayūrapucchānugatā kuryādulkā jalakṣayam | vilīyamānā nabhasi pibatyulkā payodharān ||
(58 ,4.9) sphuliṅgān visṛjantyo yāḥ pradhāvanti samantataḥ | golavacca pradhāvanti tāsu rāṣṭrabhayaṃ bhavet ||
(58 ,4.10) ulkāsaṃghaiḥ parivṛtā yāḥ patanti nabhastalāt | anusāriṇya ulkāstā rājarāṣṭrabhayāvahāḥ ||
(58 ,4.11) pretānugatamārgāśca varāhānugatāśca yāḥ | kravyāgnivyālarūpāśca tā janakṣayakārikāḥ ||
(58 ,4.12) kṣeveḍitāsphoṭitotkruṣṭā gītavāditranisvanāḥ | ulkāpāteṣu boddhavyā rājarāṣṭrabhayāvahāḥ ||
(58 ,4.13) sasvanā dāruṇāḥ saṃdhyāvāyośca pratilomagāḥ | nabho madhyaṃ ca yā yānti yāśca kuryurgatāgatam ||
(58 ,4.14) hinasti śuklā śirasā madhyena kṣatajaprabhā | pārśvābhyāṃ pītakā hanti kṛṣṇolkā pucchayoginī ||
(58 ,4.15) śuklā devanṛpān hanyātkṣatriyān kṣatajaprabhā | pītā vaiśyopaghātāya śūdrān hanyātsitetarā ||
(58 ,4.16) patantyo nopalakṣyante karma tāsāṃ prakāśate | kṣitāvaśanayo yatra tatra nāsti bhayāgamaḥ ||
(58 ,4.17) satārā nipatantyanyā mārutapratilomagāḥ | bhavanti vidyuto neṣṭā iṣṭāśca syurato'nyathā ||
(58 ,4.18) tārā dhiṣṇyāśca boddhavyāścirānmṛduphalodayāḥ | tāsāmapi ca bhūyiṣṭhaṃ patanaṃ doṣakārakam ||
(58 ,4.19) yatoyato vikārāḥ syurnipatantyatimātraśaḥ | tatastato nṛpo yāyāddaivo mārgaḥ sa ucyate ||
(58 ,4.20) nimitteṣu mahāśāntimulkāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ | kṛtvā siddhimavāpnoti ulkādoṣācca mucyate ||

(pariśiṣṭa_59. vidyullakṣaṇam)
(59,1.1) ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi vidyullakṣaṇamuttamam | varṇarūpavikārāṃśca deśabhāgāñśubhāśubhān ||
(59,1.2) caturthīṃ pañcamīṃ caiva pratīkṣeta sadā śuciḥ | āṣāḍhaśuddhe niyataṃ vidyuddarśanamadbhutam ||
(59,1.3) ativṛṣṭimanāvṛṣṭiṃ bhāvābhāvau tathaiva ca | sarvasasyeṣu niṣpattirvidyuto darśane naraḥ ||
(59,1.4) aindryāṃ cetsyandate vidyudaindrasthaścāpi mārutaḥ | subhikṣaṃ kṣemamārogyaṃ nirītiṃ ca vinirdiśet ||
(59,1.5) āgneyyāṃ cedubhau syātāṃ bhayaṃ śastrāgnivṛṣṭihtaḥ | yāmyāyāṃ viṣamāriśca vyādhimṛtyubhayaṃ tathā ||
(59,1.6) kanīyasī tu nairṛtyāṃ tathā bahvītikā samā | madhyamā sasyasaṃpattirvāruṇyāṃ vyādhisaṃkulā ||
(59,1.7) pataṃgadaṃśamaśakā vāyavyāṃ madhyasapadaḥ | ativāribhayaṃ vidyātsaumyāyāṃ bhūrisaṃpadaḥ ||
(59,1.8) nirītiḥ sasyasaṃpattu pradhānāiśyāṃ manoramā | pratilomeṣu vāteṣu ītibāhulyamādiśet ||
(59,1.9) anulomeṣu vāteṣu nirītiṃ tu samādiśet | śubhāyāṃ syandamānāyāmaniṣṭā syandate yadi ||
(59,1.10) saṃpadyate mahāsasyānmahāṃścetsyādavagrahaḥ | aśubhā syandate pūrvaṃ yadi paścācca śobhanā ||
(59,1.11) suvṛṣṭimeva tatrāhurna ca sasyaṃ samṛdhyati | yadā tu sarvāḥ syandante viṣamāṃ vṛṣṭimādiśet ||
(59,1.12) bahulāyāṃ vidyuti tu bahuvāribhayaṃ bhavet | savidyutaḥ sastanito darśayanti yadā śubhām ||
(59,1.13) pūrvottarāṃ diśaṃ meghāḥ suvṛṣṭiṃ tāṃ vijānate | pūrvataḥ pūrvavarṣeṣu dṛśyante yadi toyadāḥ ||
(59,1.14) pradakṣiṇāvartaśubhāḥ suvṛṣṭimiti nirdiśet | āgneyeṣvativṛṣṭiḥ syātsasyaṃ cāpi vipadyate ||
(59,1.15) viṣamā vṛṣṭiryāmyeṣu vyādhiṃ mṛtyuṃ ca nirdiśet | bahvītikā nairṛteṣu samūlaphaladāyinī ||
(59,1.16) vāruṇeṣu payodeṣu madhyamaṃ sasyamādiśet | vāyavyāṃ prathamaṃ varṣaṃ yatra varṣati vāsavaḥ ||
(59,1.17) tatrātivṛṣṭirbhavati svalpabījāni vāpayet | varṇasnebhopapannāstu pūrvavṛṣṭyāṃ payodharāḥ ||
(59,1.18) saumyāṃ yatra pravarṣeyustatra sarvavṛṣṭyāṃ payodharāḥ ||
(59,1.19) ityetatpūrvavarṣeṣu lakṣaṇaṃ vidyutāṃ sphuṭam | varṣārāvagataṃ sarvaṃ yathāvatparikīrtitam ||
(59,1.20) śubheṣvapi mahāśāntiravighātāya vocyate | aśubheṣu samarghāya tasmātsarveṣu śāntikamiti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_60. nirghātalakṣaṇam)
(60,1.1) aṣṭau bhavanti nirghātāsteṣāmindraḥ praśasyate | pūrveṇa vṛṣṭiṃ sasyaṃ ca rājavṛddhiṃ ca nirdiśet ||
(60,1.2) pūrvottare subhikṣaṃ tu bhūmilābhastathottare | aparottare caurabhayaṃ vājināṃ cāpyupadravaḥ ||
(60,1.3) paścimāyāṃ bhayaṃ rājño jalajātiśca pīḍyate | nairṛte sasyagostrīṇāṃ gaṇānāṃ ca mahadbhayam ||
(60,1.4) dakṣiṇe rājapīḍā syādāyuścātra vinirdiśet | ānartasya bhayaṃ vidyādyadi syātpūrvadakṣiṇaḥ ||
(60,1.5) sa yojanaparaḥ śabdo nirghātasya viśāmyati | sarvatra ca bhayaṃ vidyāttatra yatra viśāmyati ||
(60,1.6) bhaye raudrīṃ prakurvīta abhayāṃ vābhayapradām | tayā śāmyanti cotpātāḥ sukhaṃ cātyantikaṃ bhavet ||

(pariśiṣṭa_61. pariveṣalakṣaṇam)
(61,1.1) omathātaḥ pariveṣāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ caiva vakṣyate | vṛddhagargo yathā pūrvamuvāca mama suvrata ||
(61,1.2) svāyaṃbhuvaniyogena vikāraṃ kurute'malaḥ ||
(61,1.3) śvetaḥ śyāmo hariḥ kṛṣṇa iti varṇā vyavasthitāḥ | proktā megheṣu catvāro vyaktāḥ snigdhāḥ supūjitāḥ ||
(61,1.4) snigdheṣu pariveṣeṣu vaturṣveteṣu nārada | saṃdhyāyāmatra varṇeṣu vṛṣṭiṃ teṣvabhinirdiśet ||
(61,1.5) kācanīlāñjanāriṣṭāśanisarpanibheṣu ca | raupyadravasamābheṣu meghastriṣvapi varṣati ||
(61,1.6) nimagnā tu yadā saṃdhyā bhavatyetatsuvṛṣṭaye | ebhyaśca viparītā ye te'vṛṣṭibhayadā ghanāḥ ||
(61,1.7) varāhairmakarairuṣṭrairvṛkaiḥ kaṅkaistathā kharaiḥ | śaśakākṛtayaḥ kuryuḥ saṃdhyāyāṃ jaladā bhayam ||
(61,1.8) hemapāvakavarṇāśca vipulaṃ ca janakṣayam | śabdaṃ śvakharagomāyugṛdhravāyasasaṃsthitāḥ ||
(61,1.9) pūrvāparāsu saṃdhyāsu saṃgrāmaṃ prāhurunmukhāḥ | aśvasthā vāraṇasthāśca yeṣu yodhā narā iva ||
(61,1.10) megheṣu saṃpradṛśyante ye pāśāṅkuśasaṃnibhāḥ | tathā savāraṇāścaiva vinighnantaḥ parasparam ||
(61,1.11) kravyādbhirbhakṣyamāṇāśca gṛdhragomāyuvāyasaiḥ | udyudhyante yadā yuktā rāñjaḥ saṃśayakārakāḥ ||
(61,1.12) mayūrāṭṭālapadmendukāśanīlanibhāni tu | saṃdhyāsvabhrāṇi dṛśyante tīvraṃ varṣamupasthitam ||
(61,1.13) savidyutsadhanuṣkaśca saghoṣaḥ śikhisaṃnibhaḥ | saṃdhyāsvabhrāṇi dṛśyante tīvraṃ varṣamupasthitam ||
(61,1.14) nīlalohitaparyantaṃ kṛṣṇagrīvaṃ savidyutam | vivarṇaṃ parighaṃ dṛṣṭvā vidyādudakavāhakam ||
(61,1.15) trivarṇe parighe vāpi trivarṇairvā balāhakaiḥ | udayāstamayamiyādyadi sūryaḥ kadā cana ||
(61,1.16) pṛthivyāṃ rājavaṃśyānāṃ mahadbhayamupasthitam | lokakṣayakaraṃ vidyādyadi devo na varṣati ||
(61,1.17) matsyarūpī sādṛśyena yadyuttiṣṭheta bhāskaraḥ | sphuṭaraśmistadādityaḥ sa nirdahati medinīm ||
(61,1.18) etaddṛṣṭvā mahadrūpamāditye samupasthite | vispaṣṭaṃ jyotirvispaṣṭaṃ sadyovarṣasya lakṣaṇam ||
(61,1.19) grahasaṃchādanaṃ cāpi garjanaṃ pratigarjanam | paraspareṇa kurvanti meghā vegasamīritāḥ ||
(61,1.20) tasmiṃścaturvidhe yuddhe meghānāṃ vyomacārinām | utpadyante trayo bhāvāstanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
(61,1.21) garjamāneṣu megheṣu vāraṇaḥ pratigarjati | tāṃ diśaṃ yojayetsnenāṃ garjanaṃ yatra mīyate ||
(61,1.22) vidyuto'bhravikāreṣu śakrāyudhanibhāyudhāḥ | sphoṭitāśanighaṇṭāśca yāṃ diśaṃ meghavāraṇāḥ ||
(61,1.23) saṃghaṭṭeṣu samudbhutāḥ parasparajighāṃsavaḥ | tāṃ diśaṃ yojayetsenāṃ rājā jayati tāṃ diśam ||
(61,1.24) grahaṇāchādane caiva garjane pratigarjane | evameva vidhirjñeyaḥ sarvaścaiva viniścayaḥ ||
(61,1.25) parimāṇaṃ na śakyaṃ tatsamīritumaśeṣataḥ | aparābhravikārāṇāṃ ratnānāmiva sāgare ||
(61,1.26) saṃdhyā yojanabhākproktā stanitaṃ tu dviyojanam | parighaḥ pañcayojanyaḥ pratyādityastriyojanaḥ ||
(61,1.27) nirghātaḥ ṣaṭtathā vidyutpariveṣo dviṣaḍyataḥ | dāhaṃ yojanakaṃ cāpi ulkā tvamitabhāginī ||
(61,1.28) daśasaṃsthā samāptāni śāyāṅgāni pramāṇataḥ | aṅgāni tvāntarikṣāṇi vijñeyāni samāsataḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_62. bhūmikampalakṣaṇam)
(62,1.1) oṃ catvāro bhumikampāstu gargaḥ provāca buddhimān | agnirvāyustathāpaśca caturthastvindra ucyate ||
(62,1.2) teṣāṃ rūpaṃ vikārāṃśca vyākhyāsyāmo'nupūrvaśaḥ | yajjñātvā buddhimān dhīro nirdiśedvividhaṃ phalam ||
(62,1.3) prakampitāyāṃ bhūmau cetsaptāhābhyantareṇā tu | [bhaveyuratra saṃgrāmā rājñāṃ mṛtyubhayapradāḥ ||
(62,1.4) rājñāṃ virodho bhavati maraṇāni bhavanti ca |] tāmraḥ sūryaśca candraśca pītāśca mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ ||
(62,1.5) diśaḥ sarvā bhaveyuśca sūryodayasamaprabhāḥ | yadetallakṣaṇopetaṃ vidyādagniprakampitam ||
(62,1.6) tasmin bhavati nirdeśaḥ śaunakasya vaco yathā | hiraṇyaṃ ca suvarṇaṃ ca yaccānyadvidyate gṛhe ||
(62,1.7) sarvametatparityajya kartavyo dhānyasaṃgrahaḥ | rāṣṭrāṇi saṃdahedagnirgrāmāṃśca nagarāṇi ca
(62,1.8) saṃgrāmāścātra vartante māṃsaśoṇitakardamāḥ | rājānaśca virudhyante devaścātra na varṣati ||
(62,1.9) evametatprakampānāṃ garhitamagnikampitam ||
(62,2.1) prakampitāyāṃ bhūmau cediti ||
(62,2.2) atipracaṇḍo bahulo vāyurbhavati dāruṇaḥ | śarkarākarṣaṇaścāpi dikṣu caiva vidikṣu ca ||
(62,2.3) tadetallakṣaṇopetaṃ vidyādvāyuprakampitam | śastrairāvaraṇaṃkuryātprākāraṃ parikhāṃ tathā ||
(62,2.4) na tadā pravasedgrāmaṃ jñātvātmānaṃ tu gopayet | saṃgrāmāścātra vardhante māṃsaśoṇitakardamāḥ ||
(62,2.5) virudhyante ca rājāno maraṇāni bhavanti hi | rājaputrasahastrāṇāṃ bhūmiḥ pibati śoṇitam ||
(62,2.6) māsaṃ viṃśatirātraṃ vā devastatra na varṣati | dvābhyāṃ gatābhyāṃ māsābhyāṃ paraṃ syādbahulaṃ jalam ||
(62,2.7) daṣṭaṃ dūṣayate cātra kṣatabaddhāni cādhikam | eṣāmeva tu kampānāṃ garhitaṃ vāyukampitam ||
(62,3.1) prakampitāyāṃ bhūmau ||
(62,3.2) varṣantastu samāyānti mahāmeghāḥ samantataḥ | nakrāśca śiśumārāśca kūrmā makarasaṃsthitāḥ ||
(62,3.3) abhrākṛtiṣu dṛśyante grasantaścandrabhāskarau | tadetallakṣaṇopetaṃ vidyādambuprakampitam ||
(62,3.4) parvateṣu vapedbījamūṣare jāṅgale tathā | tatroptaṃ nandate bījamanyatra bhuvi naśyati ||
(62,3.5) udajāni tu puṣpāṇi mūlāni ca phalāni ca | gacchanti tatra vṛddhiṃ ca sattvānyudakajāni ca ||
(62,3.6) [kṣemaṃ subhikṣamārogyaṃ suvṛṣṭiṃ cātra nirdiśet] ||
(62,4.1) prakampitāyāṃ bhūmau ||
(62,4.2) gambhīraṃ garjamānastu megha āyāti pārthivaḥ | snigdho hyañjanasaṃkāśaḥ sumahatparvatopamaḥ ||
(62,4.3) vitrāsayan diśaḥ sarvā drutaṃ cāpi pravarṣati | indrāyudhaṃ bhaveccātra vidyutstanitameva ca ||
(62,4.4) suvṛṣṭiṃ kṣemamārogyaṃ subhikṣaṃ paramā mudaḥ | yajñodbhavaistu modante ānandairmoditāḥ prajāḥ ||
(62,4.5) eteṣāṃ bhūmikampānāṃ praśastaṃ hīndrakampanam | jānīyāllakṣaṇairetaiḥ sarvameva śubhāśubham ||
(62,4.6) eteṣu triṣu kampeṣu atharvā śāstrakovidaḥ | māhendrīmamṛtāṃ vāpi kuryācchāntiṃ sadakṣiṇām ||
(62,4.7) indrakampe tu vidhivadaindrairmantrairvidhānavit | tatohalasya pradhānārthaṃ juhuyācca japettathā ||

(pariśiṣṭa_63. nakṣatragrahotpātalakṣaṇam)
(63,1.1) omatha paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vakṣatreṣu graheṣu ca | pariveṣān bahuvidhānnānāvidhaphalodayān ||
(63,1.2) aindravāruṇakauberān raktapāṇḍuramecakān | pāṇḍūn babhrūṃśca pītāṃścānīlānalayamātmanaḥ ||
(63,1.3) prājāpatyāṃśca raudrāṃśca nairṛtyāṃścāpi bhārgava | hariśabalakāpotān pariveṣānuvāca ha ||
(63,1.4) navaite pariveṣāṇāṃ varṇā daivatayonayaḥ | bahutvamete gacchanti anyonyaguṇasaṃśrayāt ||
(63,1.5) gṛhītvābhrarajaḥ sūkṣmaṃ varṇayogsaṃnipatya ca | pitāmahaniyogena māturo maṇḍalīkṛtaḥ ||
(63,1.6) śubhāśubhārthaṃ lokānāṃ jyotīmṣyavaruṇaddhi saḥ | tasya rūpaṃ guṇaṃ jñātvā guṇadoṣaḥ pracakṣyate ||
(63,1.7) nakṣatratārakāṇāṃ ca parato viṣayasya ca niviṣṭo bhāva āgantuṃ pariveṣa iti smṛtaḥ ||
(63,1.8) dhṛtatīkṣṇārkakiraṇe prasannā mṛdumaṇḍale | prasnigdhe caikavarṇe ca māṃsale vyaktalakṣaṇe ||
(63,1.9) lohitākṣau kṣurakrānte saraśmau pītamaṇḍale | ā pradoṣādvimadhyāhnādā nakṣatrāntagāmini ||
(63,1.10) sahābhrabhārastanite pariveṣe prakāśini | anṛtāvapi jānīyānmahadbhayamupasthitam ||
(63,2.1) kṛṣṇanīhāratimire prakṛtyākrāntamaṇḍale | vikārairnābhasaiḥ kīrṇe sphuliṅgopacite'śubhe ||
(63,2.2) viṣame vigatasnehe vidhvastakaluṣābhrake | triṣu saṃdhiṣu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ darśanaṃ copagacchati ||
(63,2.3) dvitrinakṣatrage vāpi nakṣatrārdhagate'pi vā | pradīptairvā rasadbhiśca vīkṣyamāṇe mṛgadvijaiḥ ||
(63,2.4) pariveṣe vijānīyānnṛpādyānāmupasthitam | saptarātrādbhayaṃ ghoraṃ cauraśastrāgnimṛtyubhiḥ ||
(63,2.5) dhūmakarburamāñjiṣṭharaktapītāsitākṛtiḥ | bhavatyekatare pārśve rūpeṇāvilamaṇḍalaḥ ||
(63,2.6) tanunā cātra jālena samantātpariveṣṭitaḥ | muhurmuhuśca vilayaṃ saṃsthānaṃ cāpi gacchati ||
(63,2.7) so'pi vāyvātmako jñeyo mṛdumandadivākaraḥ | pariveṣo'lpaphalado vātavṛṣṭiḥ pravṛṃhate ||
(63,2.8) atha cedvātavṛṣṭistu trirātrānnāpajāyate | jalajvalanacaurāṇāṃ prādurbhāvaḥ prajāyate ||
(63,2.9) pariveṣagato'lkā syāddvimaṇḍalaparigrahe | dvābhyāṃ senāpatibhayaṃ yuvarājabhayaṃ tribhiḥ ||
(63,3.1) maṇḍalaiḥ purarodhaḥ syāttribhirabhyadhikairdhryvan | trāṇi yatrāvarudhyante nakṣatragrahacandramāḥ ||
(63,3.2) tryahādvarṣaṃ samācaṣṭe sa māsādvigrahaṃ vadet | senāpatikumārāṇāṃ senāyāścāpi vidravaḥ ||
(63,3.3) lohitāṅgapariveṣe śastrāgnyutpāta eva ca | sthāvarāḥ karṣakāścāpi kṣudradhānyaṃ ca pīḍyate ||
(63,3.4) vātavṛṣṭiṃ ca janayetpariviṣṭaḥ śanaiścaraḥ | rājyameva hi garbhāṃśca rāhuḥ pīḍayate dhruvam ||
(63,3.5) vyādhīṃścaiva prajanayetpariviṣṭaśca candramāḥ | kṣucśvāsāgnibhayaṃ ghoraṃ rājato mṛtyutastathā ||
(63,3.6) pariviṣṭo'mbare ketuḥ śikhinaśca hinasti saha | dvayoḥ saṃgrāmamācaṣṭe grahayoḥ pariviṣṭayoḥ ||
(63,3.7) kṣudbhayaṃ triṣu vijñeyaṃ varṣanigraha eva ca | caturbhirmriyate rājā sāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ ||
(63,3.8) yugānta iva jānīyātpatriviṣṭeṣu pañcasu | brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdrān hanyātpratipadādiṣu ||
(63,3.9) grāmān puraṃ ca kośaṃ ca pañcamyādiṣvatastriṣu | aṣṭamyāṃ yuvarājānāṃ camūpālān hinasti saha ||
(63,3.10) navamyāṃ ca daśamyāṃ ca ekādaśyāṃ ca pārthivān | trayodaśyāṃ balakṣobho dvādaśyāṃ rudhyate puram ||
(63,4.1) rājapatnīṃ caturdaśyāṃ pajcadaśyāṃ nṛpasya ca | purohitāmātyanṛpā hanyuranyonyameva tu ||
(63,4.2) purarodhaṃ vijānīyātpariviṣṭe bṛhaspatau | mantriṇo lekhakāścāpi rudhyante sthāvarāṇi ca ||
(63,4.3) vṛṣṭiṃ cāpi vijānīyātpariviṣṭe budhe grahe | yāyinaḥ kṣatriyāścāpi rājapakṣaśca pīḍyate ||
(63,4.4) dhānyārghaṃ ca priyaṃ kuryātpariviṣṭo bhṛgoḥ sutaḥ | tārāgrahapariveṣā nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca kevalam ||
(63,4.5) mahāgrahodayaṃ kuryānmaraṇaṃ vā mahīpateḥ | rakte pīte'site tāmre kṛṣṇe ca harite'ruṇe ||
(63,4.6) kṣucśastravyādhivarṣāgnimṛtyusasyānilānayoḥ | varṇānāṃ ca bhayaṃ jñeyaṃ yathā varṇaparigrahaḥ ||
(63,4.7) kāpotaḥ śabalaścāpi tiryagyonibhayāvahau | mayūragalaśaṅkhendumuktāgokṣīrapāṇḍurāḥ ||
(63,4.8) madhūkaghṛtamaṇḍābhā dūrvāśyāmāśca vṛṣṭaye | vimuktāriṣṭakākārāstailāmalakasaṃnibhāḥ ||
(63,4.9) snigdhāmalajalaprakhyā darpaṇābhāsca pūjitāḥ | babhravaḥ paruṣā rukṣā haridrāruṇasaṃnibhāḥ | vichinnā lohitā hrasvā vivarṇāśca śubhāvahāḥ ||
(63,4.10) yāyināṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ ca tathaivākrandasāriṇām | pariveṣān vijānīyādbāhyābhyantaramadhyataḥ ||
(63,5.1) saṃraktaśyāmakaluṣo yeṣāṃ bhāgo hataprabhaḥ | teṣāṃ parājayaṃ vidyātsnigdhe śvete ca vai jayaḥ ||
(63,5.2) yenayenābhravarṇena yoyo bhāgo'nurajyate | tattatteṣāṃ phalaṃ vidyāttadbhūtyādiṣu kīrtitam ||
(63,5.3) chidrāṇyetānyataścāhurmahānti vimalāni ca | tairdvāraiḥ pārthivo yāyātpanthānaste vikaṇṭakāḥ ||
(63,5.4) kālāmbudaparisrāvairgrahodayanimittakam | ityarthaṃ janma sarveṣāṃ śeṣamutpātalakṣaṇam ||
(63,5.5) raudrī sadakṣiṇā śāntirutpāteṣu prakīrtitā | samuccaye tu vijñeyā vaiśvadevyabhayā tathā ||
(63,5.6) atharvotpātahṛdayaṃ jñātvā svayamanāturaḥ | prayuñjīta mahāśāntiṃ sarvakalmaṣanāśinīm ||

(pariśiṣṭa_64. utpātalakṣaṇam)
(64,1.1) oṃ yān provācāṅgirāḥ pūrvaṃ yāṃśca vedo'śanāḥ kaviḥ | tānahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi utpātāṃstrividhānapi ||
(64,1.2) prakṛteranyathābhavo yatrayatropajāyate | tatratatra vijānīyātsarvamutpātalakṣaṇam ||
(64,1.3) pārthivaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca divyaṃ cotpātalakṣaṇam | nakṣatropadraveṣūktaṃ yathāvidhi tathaiva tat ||
(64,1.4) teṣūtpātagaṇeṣu āhū rasātalasamudbhavān | nirghātān bhūmikampāṃśca kīrtyamānānnibodhata ||
(64,1.5) vāruṇāgneyavāyavyāḥ kampayanti vasuṃdharām | śubhāśubhārthaṃ lokānāṃ rātrau ahani cakravat ||
(64,1.6) teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi kampānāṃ lakṣaṇāni phalāni ca | yatrovācāuśanāḥ khyātānnāradāya sma pṛcchate ||
(64,1.7) saptāhābhyantare kampe bhavedvajradharātmake | sasvanairāptaparyantaṃ svastikābhraghanairnabhaḥ ||
(64,1.8) saindracāpāyudhā kampādvidyudgaṇagavākṣakaiḥ | pāśorminagarākārairnaganāganibhairghanaiḥ ||
(64,1.9) nabhaso'ntaṃ ca sevinyo vidyutaḥ svārkasaṃnibhāḥ | prānte susaṃvṛtāścāpi śītāśītāśca mārutāḥ ||
(64,1.10) dhārāṅkuraparisrāvairnīlotpaladalaprabhaiḥ | svanadbhiśchādyate vyoma kampayedvaruṇaḥ svayam ||
(64,2.1) tārāpātairdiśaṃ dāhairulkāpātaiśca sasvanaiḥ | hāhākṛtamivābhūti pradīpitapathaṃ nabhaḥ ||
(64,2.2) saptāhābhyantare vāpi kṣitau vahniḥ prakupyate | sa āgneyo bhavetkampo rājarāṣtrabhayāvahaḥ ||
(64,2.3) niḥprakāśamivākāśe bhāskaro nātibhāskaraḥ | diśastu na prakāśante duḥkhāṛtā iva yoṣitaḥ ||
(64,2.4) saghoṣā mārutā rūkṣā vānti śarkarakarṣiṇaḥ | saptāhābhyantare kampe mārute'tibhayāvahe ||
(64,2.5) subhikṣakṣemadau kampau vijñeyāvaindravāruṇau | vāyavyāgneyajau kampau rājarāṣtrabhayāvahau ||
(64,2.6) yasyāṃ yasyāṃ diśi dharā virauti vikṛtasvarā | tasyāṃ tasyāṃ diśi bhayṃ sārdhaṃ syādadhikāridbhiḥ ||
(64,2.7) nirghātā bhūmikampāśca sasamāsamudāhṛtāḥ | ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śeṣamutpātalakṣaṇam ||
(64,2.8) prāgyāmyāparasaumyānāṃ gandharvanagaraṃ tathā | raktapītāśitaiścaiva varṇairdikṣu pradṛśyate ||
(64,2.9) rājñaḥ senāpateścāpi yuvarājapurodhasām | vyasanaṃ maraṇaṃ vāpi vijñeyamanupūrvaśaḥ ||
(64,2.10) varuṇāmāṇca bhayaṃ jñeyaṃ yathāvarṇaparigrahāt | vidikṣu ca vivarṇāsu pīḍā jñeyā vivarṇinām ||
(64,3.1) satataṃ dṛśyamāne ca rājarāṣṭrabhayāvaham | āśādhikārikāṇāṃ ca pīḍā jñeyā yathāvidhi ||
(64,3.2) viruddhayonigamanamanyasattvaprasūtayaḥ | hastapādākṣiśirasāmadhikānāṃ pradarśanam ||
(64,3.3) abhyaṅgatā ca saṃyoge gatihīnaṃ ca ceṣṭitam | viruddhānāṃ ca sattvānāmanyonyapratisaṃgamam ||
(64,3.4) calatvamacalānāṃ ca calānāmacalakṛyā | bhāṣitaṃ cāpi abhāṣāṇāmaśabdānāṃ ca bhāṣaṇam ||
(64,3.5) anagnau darśanaṃ cāgneḥ śītoṣṇasya viparyayaḥ | lohādīnāṃ plavaścāpsu nodake cāmbhasāṃ sravaḥ ||
(64,3.6) akālapuṣpaprasavaḥ sasyāḥ pañcacaturguṇāha | saṃyogo lāṅgalānāṃ ca prabhānāṃ ceṣṭitāni ca ||
(64,3.7) vicitrairdevatāsadbhirvṛkṣaprasravaṇāni ca | diśo dhūmāndhakārāśca dīptāśca mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ ||
(64,3.8) rajastamāśritaṃ vyoma kaluṣau candrabhāskarau | vastramāṃsāmbhasāṃ dīptirāgaprajvalitāni ca ||
(64,3.9) akasmādgopurāṭṭālaśailaprasādaveśmanām | daraṇaṃ jvalanaṃ vāpi kampo dhūmapravartanam ||
(64,3.10) abhīkṣṇā mārutāścaṇḍā vānti śarkarakarṣiṇaḥ | saṃhitā maṇḍalānāṃ ca nīlalohitapītakāḥ ||
(64,4.1) dhvajastambhendrakīlānāṃ śuṣkacaityādibhiḥ saha | chinne bhinne drumāṇāṃ ca skandhaśākhāṅkurodbhavaḥ ||
(64,4.2) gītānāṃ ca mṛdaṅgānāṃ vāditrāṇāṃ nisvanāḥ | bhaveyurākāśapathe sagandharvapurogamāḥ ||
(64,4.3) chāyādarśanamadravye virātre virutāni ca | divārātricarāṇāṃ ca viparītapracāratā ||
(64,4.4) nirabhravṛṣṭayaścaiva nirabhrastanitāni ca | sasvanānāmadhūmānāmulkānāṃ patanaṃ divā ||
(64,4.5) indorarkasya vā cāpi pāṃsvaśmādiṣu darśanam | abhīkṣṇapariveṣāśca kaluśā ravisomayoḥ ||
(64,4.6) mayūrakokilādīnāṃ madāvāptiranārtavā | vanānāṃ ca nagānāṃ ca devatānāṃ ca nirgamāḥ ||
(64,4.7) āraṇyānāṃ ca sattvānāṃ puragrāmaniveśanam | abhūtānāṃ pravṛtiśca pravṛttānāṃ ca nāśanam ||
(64,4.8) etadutpātajaṃ rājño yasyā deśe abhyudīryate | tasya deśo vinaśyeta kṣīyate ca sapārthivaḥ ||
(64,4.9) tyajanti vāpi yaṃ deśaṃ pāṣaṇḍā dvijadevatāḥ | vidveṣaṃ vāpi gacchanti so'pi deśo vinaśyati ||
(64,4.10) nartanaṃ ca kuśūlānāṃ dhānyarāśeśca kampanam | ulūkhālānāṃ saṃsarpo musalānāṃ praveśanam ||
(64,5.1) ceṣṭitaṃ rājadarvīṇāṃ mṛdbhāṇḍānāṃ tathaiva ca | dahanaṃ caiva śītānām [śabdā hyuttarāṇi ca] ||
(64,5.2) purīṣabhakṣaṇaṃcaiva dīnānāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām | grāmyāṇāṃ dīnavapuṣāṃ pradhānyastanitāni ca ||
(64,5.3) vālukāṅgāradhānyānāṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ vāpi vṛṣṭayaḥ | puradvāre ca bakavadvāyasānāṃ ca ceṣṭitam ||
(64,5.4) biḍālamatsyamajjānāṃ jantūnāṃ kṣudrasaṃjñinām | anyonyabhakṣaṇāni syurekasaṃsthāśca rātrayaḥ ||
(64,5.5) māṃsasasyānnavidveṣaḥ kriyāvyuparamastathā | yasmin deśe pradṛśyante tasmin kṣudbhayamādiśet ||
(64,5.6) śastrajvalanasaṃsarpaḥ sthūṇīsaraṇapūraṇam | chattravastradhvajānāṃ ca valmīkeṣu pradarśanam ||
(64,5.7) arke abhraparighādīnāṃ pariveṣo arkacandrayoḥ | lākṣālohitavarṇatvaṃ sarveṣāṃ ca vicāraṇam ||
(64,5.8) tvacmāṃsarudhirāsthīnāṃ medomajjāsthivṛṣṭayaḥ | nirabhravṛṣṭayaścāsya rajatakṣatasaprabham ||
(64,5.9) praghātākampanirghātāvidyutā cābhrapātanam | bhavecca devatādīnāṃ śiroabhiṣṭhānavarjanam ||
(64,5.10) strīṇāṃ nṛṇāṃ ca prasavaṃ tṛṇādīnāṃ ca mānuṣam | amānuṣāṇāṃ sattvānāṃ bhāṣitāni manuṣyavat ||
(64,6.1) vasāśoṇitagandhatvaṃ gajadaivatavājinām | yasmin deśe bhavettasmin śastrakopabhayaṃ mahat ||
(64,6.2) śoṇitāśruparisrāvāḥ prahāsodvīkṣaṇakriyā | nṛtyavāditragītāni sākrośābhāṣitāni ca ||
(64,6.3) prakampanaṃ devatānāṃ tathaiva jvalanāni ca | apāṃ śoṣavikārāśca ceṣṭitaṃ ca manuṣyavat ||
(64,6.4) daraṇaṃ rasanaṃ rājño vaikṛtyodvartanāni ca | kṣiteḥ kampaprahāsāśca rodanotkrośāani ca ||
(64,6.5) pīṭhikāvyañjanaṃ chattra[m]śasatrakīlakamaṇḍalau | nīlāṅgalohitatalau udye arkaniśāakarau ||
(64,6.6) candrārkolkāprabhedāśca bhāskarendudvayaṃ tathā | pratisrotavahā nadya iṣavaḥ pratikomagāḥ ||
(64,6.7) dantabhaṅgāḥ sakūrmāśca naravarṇavājinām | chattrabhaṅgāḥ sakūrmāśca naravāraṇavājinām ||
(64,6.8) māṃsatailavipākaśca caityatailaparisravāḥ | śakradhvajapatākānāṃ bhaṅgakravyadasvanam ||
(64,6.9) biḍalolūkyoryuddhaṃ nṛpaprāsādasaṃnidhau | pāṃsunā cāvṛtaṃ vyoma rajasā tamasāpi vā ||
(64,6.10) lohitāgniprabhākāśaṃ dīptā dvijamṛgastathā | vātāvartāstusaṃdhyāsu prasphuranto apasavyagāḥ ||
(64,7.1) maṇḍalāni samājāśca sarvato mṛgapakṣiṇām | kravyādairārasadbhiśca vyākulāḥ sarvato diśaḥ ||
(64,7.2) trirātrādaparaṃ vṛṣṭiḥ pranaṣṭendudivākarau | anṛtau cāpi dṛśyeta ghorastanitadīrghatā ||
(64,7.3) vajrādayo rāhuputrā vṛkṣāḥ śakunayastathā | maṇḍalābhyantarasthāśca bhavanti ravisomayoḥ ||
(64,7.4) ākāśe vā pradṛśyante prakampanti va parvatāḥ | viṣyeta ravisomau ca ābhīkṣaṃ tārakāstathā ||
(64,7.5) naradanaṃ ca biḍālāṇāṃ kṣīravṛkṣaniṣevaṇam | kharairdīptairulūkaiśca rasadbhiḥ saha vigrahaḥ ||
(64,7.6) siṃhāsanāni chattrāṇi bhṛṅgārāḥ śayanāstathā | kampanti akasmādbhajyante saṃsarpanti ārasanti ca ||
(64,7.7) rājñāṃ bhayakaraṃ sarvametadutpātalakṣaṇam | deśasya ca vijānīyādgargasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
(64,7.8) saṃdhyādaṇḍapariveṣā rajoarkaparighādayaḥ | maṇḍalānāṃ samūhāśca dikṣu pītāruṇaprabhāḥ ||
(64,7.9) kravyādā vānarā dvāri visphūrjanti ārasanti ca | tuṇḍaiśca vāyasā bhūmiṃ kuṭṭayanto ramanti ca ||
(64,7.10) mlāyate mālyamatyarthaṃ gandhāḥ kuṇapagandhinaḥ | vastreṣu bhakṣabhojyeṣu bhavati utpātalakṣaṇam ||
(64,8.1) kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaṃ ca dadhi ca prasravetprathitā drumāḥ | sārameyāḥ śmaśāneṣu rudanti viruvanti ca ||
(64,8.2) etadautpātikaṃ grāme yasmiṃśca dṛśyate pure | tasmin grāme pure vāpi vidyādatibhayaṃ mahat ||
(64,8.3) aśvatthodumbaraplakṣanyagrodhe kusumodbhavaḥ | śvetalohitapītāni kṛṣṇānīndrāyudhāni ca ||
(64,8.4) evaṃ varṇaduṇānāṃ ca patanaṃ devaveśmanām | brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdravināśo rājasaṃvṛtām ||
(64,8.5) rūkṣasrāvā citivṛkṣe tadbhayaṃ sumahadbhavet | ghṛtakṣīraphalāśrāve ghṛtakṣīrāmbhasāṃ kṣayaḥ ||
(64,8.6) surāśrāve mithobhedo rudhire rāṣṭravidravaḥ | rudhire goviṣāṇācca srute gobrāhmaṇakṣayaḥ ||
(64,8.7) phale phalaṃ yadā paśyetpuṣpe puṣpaṃ samāvṛtam | garbhāḥ sravanti nārīṇāṃ yuddhaṃ rājavadho api vā ||
(64,8.8) phaṇābhṛto mahatsarpānmaṇḍūlā atha vṛścikāḥ | maṇḍūkā grasate yatra tatra rājāvahanyate ||
(64,8.9) himapātānilotpātā vikṛtādbhutadarśanam | kṛṣṇāñjanābhramākāśaṃ tārolkāpātapiṅgalam ||
(64,8.10) citrā garbhodbhavāḥ strīṣu goajāśvamṛgapakṣiṣu | pattrāṅkuralatānāṃ ca vikārāḥ śiśire śubhāḥ ||
(64,9.1) vajrāśanimahīkampāḥ saṃdhyānirghātanisvanāḥ | pariveṣarajodhūmā raktārkāstamanodayāḥ ||
(64,9.2) drumebhyo annarasasnehamadhupuṣpaphalodgamāḥ | gopakṣiśabdavṛddhiśca śivāni madhumādhave ||
(64,9.3) tārolkāpātakaluṣaṃ kapilārkendumaṇḍalam | anagnijvalanasphoṭadhūmareṇvanilāhatam ||
(64,9.4) raktapītāruṇāṃ saṃdhyāṃ nabhaḥ saṃkṣubhītārṇavam | saritāṃ cāmbusaṃśoṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā grīṣme śubhaṃ vadet ||
(64,9.5) śakrāyudhaparīveṣavidyutśuṣkavirohaṇam | akasmādvarṇavaikṛtyaṃ rasanaṃ daraṇaṃ kṣiteḥ ||
(64,9.6) saronadyudapānānāṃ vṛddhirvā uttaraṇaplavāḥ | taraṇaṃ cārdravegānāṃ varṣāsu na bhayāvaham ||
(64,9.7) divyastrīgītagandharvavimānādbhutanisvanāḥ | grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ darśanaṃ ca divāmbare ||
(64,9.8) gītavāditranirghoṣo vanaparvatasānuṣu | sasyavṛddhī rasotpattirna pāpāḥ śaradi smṛtāḥ ||
(64,9.9) śītānilatuṣāratvaṃ nardanaṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām | rakṣoyakṣādisattvānāṃ darśanaṃ vāgamānuṣī ||
(64,9.10) dīptadhūmarajasdhvastā diṅnāgā vanaparvatāḥ | uccaistoyadasomārkā hemante śobhanāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
(64,10.1) ṛtusvabhāvā ete hi dṛṣṭāḥ svartau śubhapradāḥ | ṛtau anyatra ca utpātā dṛṣṭāḥ svartau śubhapradāḥ ||
(64,10.2) unmattānāṃ ca yā gāthā bālānāṃ ceṣṭitaṃ ca yat | striyaśca yatprabhāṣante tatra nāsti vyatikramaḥ ||
(64,10.3) pūrvaṃ vadati deveṣu paścādgacchati mānuṣe | nācoditā āgvadati satyā hi eṣā sarasvatī ||
(64,10.4) utpātāḥ sarva eva ete kadācidrājamṛtyave | jñeyā deśavināśāya rāhorāgamanāyā vā ||
(64,10.5) kālāmbudaparisrāvā grahāṇāmudayāya vā | svacakraparacakrebhyo bhaye vā samupasthite ||
(64,10.6) rāṣṭre senāpatau putre pure vātha purodhasi | amātye vāhane dāre nṛpatau vā palanti ca ||
(64,10.7) etān samutthitāṃ jñātvā rājā sabalavāhanaḥ | praṇipatya guruṃ brūyādbhagavan śamayasva me ||
(64,10.8) bhayamutpātajaṃ sarvaṃ brūhi kiṃ karavāṇi te | iti uktaḥ śraddadhānena rājñā svahitamicchatā ||
(64,10.9) nimittāni samālokya kṛtvā pāvanamāditaḥ | mahāśāntiṃ prayuñjīta sarvopadravanāśinīm ||
(64,10.10) sarvarogapraśamanīmutpātaphalanāśinīm | raudrīṃ kuryānmahāśāntiṃ śraddhayā bahudakṣiṇāṃ śraddhayā bahudakṣiṇām ||

(pariśiṣṭa_65. sadyovṛṣṭilakṣaṇam)
(65,1.1) omatha ato lakṣaṇopāṅge sadyovṛṣṭilakṣaṇaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(65,1.2) snigdhavimalataladarśane'rciṣmatyatitejasi sthūlaraśmau harijvalanasaṃnibhe savitari sadyo varṣati parjanyo viśuddhāsu ca dikṣu kākāṇḍavarṇeṣu giriṣvatirajaskandheṣu hradamagneṣu vimalavipulasnigdhaprasannahṛṣṭapradarśaneṣu nīcairiva jyotirgaṇeṣvanukūleṣu śive śite nīce mārute bhavati cātra ślokaḥ ||
(65,1.3) pūrvo'bhrajanamo vāyuritaro'bhravināśanaḥ | udagjanayate vṛṣṭiṃ varṣatyeva ca dakṣiṇaḥ ||
(65,1.4) abhreṣu timiramakaranaganāganakragrāhaśiṃśumāraśaṅkhadrumakūrmormijhaṣamahiṣavarāhadigdviradanavakumudakhaṇḍākṛtinalakalaśakuḍmalāpīḍatoraṇāvartasvastikavardhamānaravauhvarajatamadrāṇipatākāśvatātyāsthānavividhajalacarapakṣivirutacatuṣpadākāreṣu naktanīlotpalakamalapalāśakomaleṣu ||
(65,1.5) [muktā]sphaṭikarajatavaiḍuryāñjanabhramarasarpasaṃnikāśeṣu | kṣaudrakṣīrapalāśadhūma[dūrvā]rajatakanakavidrumaprabheṣ ||
(65,1.6) dviguṇatriguṇadarśaneṣu mūlavatsu viśikhareṣu mahāvarteṣu taralarathanemighoṣeṣu udadhijalanirghoṣasaṃhrādeṣu kṣubdhadundubhininādeṣu kiñjalkāravindasaṃnibheṣu vā kumudamayūragalakālakeṣu cābhrajālāvanādeṣu chinnābhreṣu vā chinnamūleṣu kāleṣu kāñcanamanahśiloupameṣu suvarṇapūrṇeṣu jaleṣvasminna cābhyantarato deśaśobhiteṣu dakṣiṇamāruteritaparitate grahāntargatastanitagambhīranisvaneṣu ardhāntareṣu sadyovarṣamādiśet || atra ślokau ||
(65,1.7) udayāstamaye meghā garbhabhūtā divākare | pradīptā iva citrāsu viṣamāsu khakoṭiṣu ||
(65,1.8) pañca mārutaparyaṅkā maṇayaḥ kāñcanā iva | yatrayatropalakṣyante tatratatra pravarṣati ||
(65,1.9) ghananicayaṃ virohaṇe vādhirohaṇāstagamane vā saviturdṛṣṭvā ca varṣadudadhijīvarādrariṣṭakavaiḍūryotpalakamalapalāśadhūmaśevālavadhrajabakasaṃnikāśasnigdhaghoṣagambhīragabhastividvanbhaiḥ pravṛddhaiḥ samārutān vañcibhiḥ pravṛddhaskandhaśākhānvitapāvanitalaruhān sadyovarṣamādiśet || atra ślokau ||
(65,1.10) antarājitadīptāgnikāñcanāmalasaṃnibhaiḥ | abhraiścotpalavaidūryaprabhāvāñjanasaṃnibhaiḥ ||
(65,1.11) nīlarśmiprarohantaḥ śākhāvanta iva drumāḥ | yatrayatra pradṛśyante dhruvaṃ tara pravarṣati ||
(65,2.1) saṃdhyā ca jvalanaravīndīvarakaraṇdatapanīyārkodayaharitālanīlotpalaghṛtamadhubandhujīvakajapāpuṣpakiṃśukarāśisaṃnikāśā tathā drutakanakavidrumasphaṭikavaiḍūryavarṇamuddyotayanti diśaḥ śāntamṛgaśakuniviśeṣāḥ ||
(65,2.2) kiṃtanā snigdhā ghanā gabhastimālini saṃprati saṃdhyāṃ dṛṣṭvā nīcairnirmalasnigdhaparidhipariveṣābhravṛkṣapratisūryakā lohitākṣapakṣiptā sārdhaṃ pañcakāvaliptaiśca mahiṣavṛṣavarāhādidviradajalagaṇairivācaritaviṣayā atra ślokaḥ ||
(65,2.3) sāṃdhyaiśca pariveṣaiśca pratighaiḥ pratisūryakaiḥ | jalajaiścāvṛtānindyaiḥ sadyaḥ saṃdhyā pravarṣati ||
(65,2.4) yathālakṣaṇaṃ śastrakāṃsyatāmrāyasānāṃ kledavatāṃ khadyotāni || tatra svedanti kāmahurdhunināśca uttaṭaprākāragopuragṛhāgādhirohaṇapāṃsusnānamaṇḍajānām
(65,2.5) pracaraṇe taḍāgakūpān setubandhākrītāśca śiśūnāṃ dṛṣṭvā prasaṃkhyāyāśca citrāviśākhāsvātibahulāṣāḍhāhirbudhnyayāmyasya saṃgrahasaṃpāteṣu mahadvarṣasaṃvṛte ca tryahādūrdhvaṃ cātra ślokau ||
(65,2.6) ākrīḍāścaiva matsyānāṃ gavāṃ dṛṣṭvāgamo gṛham | prācuryadaṃśamaśakairdhiṣṇyānāṃ cātha mokṣeṇe ||
(65,2.7) jalājalajasaṃtānānekatra bilavāsinām | pipīlikāṇḍasaṃkrāntirathoṣṇaṃ cāmbu vṛṣṭaye ||
(65,2.8) satkṛtya ca daivajñaṃ palvalakūpataḍāganadītīre || sāddālagṛheṣu deśeṣvārdravāsārdrapāṇiḥ prṛcchet || sadyovarṣamadiśet ||
(65,2.9) diśyaiśānyāṃ vā madhurasvarariṣṭavyāharaṇaṃ jalagotrasābhūtaṃ talliṅgānāmantarālaṃ bālānāṃ śrutvā dṛṣṭvā varṣatīti brūyāt ||
(65,2.10) rātristanito divā vidyudbhirvādyamākṣetre varṇaḥ snigdho dviguṇendracāpa darśane vyomni nirabhre paśuvirāvābhradaṇḍābhasvalpāñcābhrarājiprādurbhāvaiḥ sadyovṛṣṭiratra ślokāḥ ||
(65,2.11) pratisūryako bhavedyastu raveruttarato yadā | toyaṃ nivārayennityaṃ dakṣiṇe salilādbhayam ||
(65,2.12) tridhā nimittasaṃpannā vṛṣṭirbhavati pārthivī | nimitte tāvadekasmin pañcayojanikaṃ bhavet ||
(65,2.13) yeṣuyeṣu nimitteṣu nakṣatreṣu ca vartmani | praśastamiti teṣveva prādurbhūteṣu varṣati ||
(65,3.1) viparyayanimittāni pratibandhakarāṇi tu | teṣu śāntiṃ prakurvīta atharvā śamanāya vai ||
(65,3.2) samutpatantu sūktena pra nabhasveti cāpare | vaitasyaḥ samidho'nye tu śamīmayyo'pare viduḥ ||
(65,3.3) [samidhāṃ vaitasīnāṃ tu agnāvarkendhanāhute | ahorātrikahomaḥ syātparjanyo bahuvarṣadaḥ ||
(65,3.4) samutpatantu sūktena maruto yajate pākayajñavidhānena yathā varuṇaṃ vṛṣṭikāmaḥ || pra nabhasvetyṛcau dve maruto yajate vṛṣṭikāmo yathā varuṇaṃ juhoti ||]
(65,3.5) ādadhyātsamidhaḥ plākṣīḥ sakṣīrā ghṛtasaṃyutāḥ | tatastacchamamāyāti kṛtsnamutpātalakṣaṇam ||
(65,3.6) aindrīṃ vā vāruṇīṃ vāpi mahāśāntiṃ vidhānataḥ | varṣādau tu prayuñjīta avṛṣṭestu vināśanīm ||
(65,3.7) vṛṣṭeryāni nimittāni tānyapratihatāni tu | bhavanti vṛṣṭidāyīni sasyavṛddhikarāṇi tu ||
(65,3.8) vaitasānāṃ tu pattrāṇāṃ lakṣaṃ kṣīrasamāyutam | vratānte bhārgavo juhvadavarṣāsvapi varṣayediti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_66. gośāntiḥ)
(66,1.1) oṃ bhagavan devadeveśa surāsuranamaskṛta | gavāṃ sarveṣu rogeṣu pratijñāteṣu vai prabho ||
(66,1.2) kathaṃ śāntiṃ dvijaḥ kuryātkena mantreṇa prokṣaṇam | homamantrāśca ke proktāḥ kasmiṃstantre prayojayet ||
(66,1.3) uvāca paripṛṣṭaḥ san brahmā sarvajagatpatiḥ | śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve gośāntiṃ mahaduttamām ||
(66,1.4) atharvavihitāṃ samyaksarvarogavināśanīm | yāṃ śrutvā savarogāstu vidravanti sahasraśaḥ ||
(66,1.5) goṣṭhamadhye gṛhe vāpi govāṭe gokulāntile | ācāryastu śucirbhūtvā kārayenmaṇḍalaṃ śubham ||
(66,2.1) snātaścāhatavāsāśca ahorātroṣitaḥ śuciḥ | caturaśraṃ caturdvāramālikhettatra maṇḍalam ||
(66,2.2) tasya madhye tu deveśaṃ gomayena nidhāpayet | tataḥ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ caiva gugguluṃ candanāgurum ||
(66,2.3) puṣpāṇi ca sugandhīni tathā vai sarṣapāṃstilān | lājāśca samidhaścaiva samāhṛtya vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
(66,2.4) prāṇāṃstu tarpayettatra dadhikṣīraghṛtādibhiḥ | tataḥ śāntiṃ prayuñjita namaskṛtvā svayaṃbhuvam ||
(66,2.5) ājyabhāgāntājyatantramabhyātānāni caiva hi ||
(66,2.6) namojñāya sureśāya namaste viśvatomukha | namaḥ kālāya tīkṣṇāya [jaṭilāya] sarvabhūtahitāya ca ||
(66,3.1) tataḥ sarṣapatilalājā ūrdhvāḥ samidhaśca dadhimadhughṛtāktā juhuyāt ||
(66,3.2) yajāmi || kālāya svāhā || piṅgalāya tīkṣṇāya jaṭilāya babhrave oṃ bhūroṃ bhuva oṃ svaroṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svarjayavijayāya jayādhipataye kapardine karālāya vikaṭāya kaṭiramāṭarāyāṅgirasabārhaspatyakakapilamaṇḍalamuṇḍajaṭilakapāleśvarādhipataye kapardine svāheti ||
(66,3.3) eṣa karmasu tu gośānteḥ saṃsṛṣṭa ṛṣibhiḥ purā | proktā svayaṃbhuvā caiṣā gośāntistu hitāya vai ||
(66,3.4) yo vipraḥ paṭhatīmāṃ hi gokule cāpi hitāya vai | gāvastasya pravardhante mahatīṃ cāśnute śriyam ||

(pariśiṣṭa_67. adbhutaśāntiḥ)
(67,1.1) oṃ puruṣasputradāraṃ vā dhanadhānyamatha api vā | nimittairyairvinaśyeta śāntiṃ tatra nibodhata ||
(67,1.2) indrāyudhaṃ bhavetrātrau dṛśyate yasya kasya cit | darvī kare vā bhidyeta maṇiskumbhastathā eva ca ||
(67,1.3) chattraṃ śayyā āsanaṃ ca eva anyatvā api svayaṃ kva cit | strī hanyātca striyaṃ vā api gauravaghretulūkhalam ||
(67,1.4) śvā pidetgāmanaḍvāhaṃ kalissaṃpadyate kule | gajavājino mriyante vivādo rājakīyakas||
(67,1.5) kuṭumbamaśubhaṃ sarvamaindrāṇi etāni nirdiśet | śāmyanti yena sarvāṇi nirvapetpāyasaṃ carum ||
(67,1.6) samāvaoya ghṛtaṃ tatra āhutiṃ jihuyātimām | indramiddevatātaye sthālīpākasya homayet ||
(67,1.7) indrasśacīpatisśakro vajrapāṇissureāśvaras| sarvādbhutānāṃ śamano mahāvyāhṛtayastathā ||
(67,1.8) hutvā sviṣṭakṛtaṃ ca eva carutantraṃ samāpayet | vimuktotpātadoṣastu jīvettu śaradasśatam ||
(67,2.1) uddīpikā gṛhe yasya valmīkā madhujālakam | abjānāṃ maṇike śabde tailaṃ sthīyata eva vā ||
(67,2.2) aśubhā vikṛtirdadhnāṃ dugdhānāṃ vā yadā bhavet | akasmātca praroheyurbījāni kṛmayastathā ||
(67,2.3) kāryo varuṇayāgastu vāruṇīvidhipūrvakas| uduttamaṃ pradhānaṃ syātpañcājyāhutayastathā ||
(67,2.4) varuṇaspāśapāṇisca yadāsāṃ patireva ca | śe.aṃ tu pūrvavatca eva carutantraṃ samāpayet ||
(67,2.5) vimuktotpātadoṣastu jīvettu śaradasśatam ||
(67,3.1) gṛhe yasya patetgṛdhra ulūko vā kathaṃ cana | kapotaspraviśetca eva jīvā vā araṇyasaṃbhavās||
(67,3.2) dhuryau ca patato yuktau gostrījanma ca vaikṛtam | jāyante yamalāni eva ghorassvapnasca dṛśyate ||
(67,3.3) abhidravanti rakṣāṃsi yatra ca eva kumārakān | unnidrako atunidro vā atyalpamatibhojanam ||
(67,3.4) ālasya ca evameteṣāṃ devatā yama ucyate | nāke suparṇamiti etatsthālīpākasya homayet ||
(67,3.5) yamaspretapatisca eva daṇḍapāṇistathā īśvaras| śamanaḥ sarvādbhutānām... ||
(67,4.1) anagnirutthito yasya dhūmo vā api gṛhe kva cit | āmaṃ vā jvalate māṃsaṃ bhaveyurvisphuliṅgakās||
(67,4.2) chattradhvajapatākāsca jvalante toraṇāni ca || āsanaṃ ca eva ̭yyā ca vastrāṇi kusumāni ca ||
(67,4.3) hastyaśvānāṃ ca pucchāni varṣatyaṅgāravarṣaṇam | akāle ca diśāṃ dāha oṣadhīnāṃ ca pācanam ||
(67,4.4) hastinyasca eva madyante agnirūpaṃ tadadbhutam | agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe sthālīpākasya homayet ||
(67,4.5) agnirhiraṇyapatisca arciṣpāṇistathā īśvaras| śamanassarvādbhutānām... ||
(67,5.1) suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vajraṃ vaiḍūryaṃ mauktikāni ca | pravālavastranāśasca mitrāṇāṃ ca viparyayas||
(67,5.2) ārambhāsca vipadyante na soddhiskarmaṇāmapi | carurvaiśravaṇastatra abhi tyaṃ devamṛksmṛtā ||
(67,5.3) vaiśravaṇo yakṣapatisarthapāṇistathā īśvaras| śamanassarvādbhutānām... ||
(67,6.1) atha yasya svanakṣatre ulkā nirghāta eva vā | rāhurgrasati candrārkau kabandhaṃ darpaṇe bhavet ||
(67,6.2) patetsvayaṃ vā musalaṃ devatā vā kathaṃ cana | unmīlate ca eva yadā tathā ca api nimīlate ||
(67,6.3) prachidyate ca yadi vā tathā vā api prakampate | prayāto vā api dṛśyeta pratisroto nadī vahet ||
(67,6.4) vimale na eva arkachāyā pratīpā vā api tu sāyakās| pariveṣastu anabhreṣu dṛśyate candrasūryayos||
(67,6.5) kośātkhaḍgā nirgirante tūṇātca eva tu sāyakās| anāhatāni vādyante nadante śabdamāturam ||
(67,6.6) caruṇā vaiṣṇavena eṣāṃ yāgaskartavyaseva tu | idaṃ viṣṇuspradhānaṃ syātpañcājyāhutayastathā ||
(67,6.7) sarvabhūtapatisviṣnuscakrapāṇistathā īśvaras| śamanassarvādbhutānām... ||
(67,7.1) ativāto yatra bhavetrūpaṃ vā yatra vaikṛtam | kharakarabhamahiṣā varāhā vyāghrasiṃhakās||
(67,7.2) gṛdhrāsca tathā gomāyuskṛkalāsā vadanti ca | māṃsapeśaṃ ca rudhiraṃ pāṃsuvṛṣṭistathā eva ca ||
(67,7.3) vāyurūpamidaṃ sarvamadbhutaṃ parikīrtitam | vāta ā vātu bheṣajaṃ vāyau ā yāhi darśata iti sthālīpākasya homayet ||
(67,7.4) vāyurmahānnabhapatirvajrapāṇistathā īśvaras| śamanassarvādbhutānāṃ mahāvyāhṛtayastathā ||
(67,7.5) hutvā sviṣṭakṛtaṃ ca eva carutantraṃ samāpayet | vimuktotpātadoṣastu jīvettu śaradasśatam ||
(67,8.1) atha cedanyaśākhasu kartā bhavati vedavit | japtvā sa ṛgyajuḥsāmnaṃ śatamātraṃ samāhitas||
(67,8.2) gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japtvā yayajamānassamāhitas| vācayettamupādhyāyaṃ vastreṇa kanakena vā ||
(67,8.3) dṛṣṭāṃ ca eva adbhutaṃ yasminstatca api pratipādayet | etāstu dakṣiṇāssarvāsśaktiyukto na hāpayet ||
(67,8.4) yajamānastatsuto vā yassvayaṃ kartumarhati | brāhmaṇāya viśeṣeṇa dadyāttāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ śubhām ||
(67,8.5) japtvā atharvaśirasca eva kuryātvipreṣu pūjanam | śaktyā atha bhojanaṃ ca eva kuryātvipreṣu pūjanam ||
(67,8.6) etadevaṃ samākhyātamadbhutānāṃ viśodhanam | caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ yaskuryātśraddhayā anvitas||
(67,8.7) maraṇaṃ na bhavettasya na duḥkhaṃ na daridratā | sidhyanti sarvakāryāṇi dharme ca asya matirbhavet ||
(67,8.8) etatpuṇyaṃ paritraṃ ca devatāyāgapūjanam | sarvaśāntikaṃ ca eva pratipuruṣaṃ nibodhata || pratipuruṣaṃ nibodhata iti ||

(pariśiṣṭa_68. svapnādhyāyaḥ)
(68,1.1) omathātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi yaduktaṃ padmayoninā | upāṅgaṃ śukracārasta śubhāśubhanivedakam ||
(68,1.2) svapnādhyāyaṃ pravakṣyāmi kroṣṭukervacanaṃ yathā | śaśaṃsire purā yaṃ hi śaunakāya mahātmane ||
(68,1.3) nimittajñānakuśalāḥ sarvaṃ tasya tu pṛcchataḥ | grahā bhārgavabhaumārkāḥ paittikā dīptitejasaḥ ||
(68,1.4) kaphaprakṛtayo madhyā bṛhaspatibudhendavaḥ | vātaprakṛtayaḥ krūrā rāhuketuśanaiścarāḥ ||
(68,1.5) teṣāṃ tathā phalaṃ vidyātsaṃnipāte yathākramam | ete nava grahā jñeyā vātapittakaphātmakāḥ ||
(68,1.6) eṣāṃ prakṛtitulyānāṃ niṣiktānāṃ tu teṣu vai | saṃyogeṣu ca jātānāṃ tulya prakṛtitā bhavet ||
(68,1.7) arkenduprabhavā dehamupatiṣṭhanti dehinaḥ | tasmānniṣicyamāneṣu vātapittakapheṣu yaḥ ||
(68,1.8) eṣāmanyatamo deho yātiriktaḥ prakāśate | pracakṣate sā prakṛtiḥ prakṛtijñānakovidāḥ ||
(68,1.9) tatra ye mānisvāṅgāni ṛjavaḥ kalahapriyāḥ | uṣṇāḥ kapilaromāṇāaḥ svedanā anavekṣaṇāḥ ||
(68,1.10) bahvāśidurbhagāścaiva mṛdvaṅgāḥ śiśirapriyāḥ | lālanāḥ śithilāṅgāśca priyāśca priyāśca lavaṇāstathā ||
(68,1.11) tanutvaṅnakharomāṇastvācāryāstīkṣṇa eva ca | valīpalitabhūyiṣṭhāstathā khalatino narāḥ ||
(68,1.12) glayate śuṣyate caiṣāmāśu mālyānulepanam | dāhātmikāḥ śaśāṅke'pi pittaprakṛtayastu te ||
(68,1.13) svapne caiva prapaśyanti diśaḥ kanakapiṅgalāḥ | maṇḍalāni samūhāṃśca dikṣu pītāruṇaprabhān ||
(68,1.14) śṛṅgārimadirān deśāñśuṣkāṃ malajalāṃ mahīm | śuṣkagulmadrumalatā dahyamānaṃ mahadvanam ||
(68,1.15) viśuṣkāṇi ca vastrāṇi rudhirāṅgāṃstathaiva ca | dahanādīṃśca devāṃśca raktaminduṃ sugandhikān ||
(68,1.16) palāśāni ca puṣpāṇi karṇikāravanāni ca | digdāhavidyucchulkāśca dīpyamānaṃ ca pāvakam ||
(68,1.17) bhūyiṣṭhaṃ bhūṣitāścāpi pibanti subahūdakam | saritsaravanānteṣu kūpaprasravaṇeṣu ca ||
(68,1.18) uṣṇārtāḥ śītakāmāstu nimajjanti pibanti ca | kalahaṃ caiva kurvanti duḥkhānyanubhavanti ca ||
(68,1.19) strībhiścaiva vimānyante kṣayante klāmayanti ca | ityevaṃ paittikā jñeyāḥ prakṛtisvapnalakṣaṇe ||
(68,1.20) prakṛtisvapnabhāvaiśca śleṣmikāṇyapi me śṛṇu | snigdhakeśanakhaśmaśrutatatvagroma[bhāṣiṇaḥ] ||
(68,1.21) mahodarabhojoraskadīrghakeśanakha[dvijāñ] | vaiḍūryopalabaddhe tu saṃnibhairniyamaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
(68,1.22) sthiropacitasarvāṅgā bhavanti sukhabhāginaḥ | śirodarakaṭiskandhapakṣayorvimalekṣaṇāḥ ||
(68,1.23) priyāḥ priyamvadāḥ śūrāḥ kṛtajñā dṛḍhabhaktayaḥ | cirādgṛhṇanti suciraṃ gṛhītaṃ dhārayanti ca ||
(68,1.24) na krudhyanti cirātkruddhāḥ saṃbhavantyantakopamāḥ | pūjābhirvipulāṃ bhūmimāvahanti kulasya ca ||
(68,1.25) khyāpayanti ca sarvatra guṇaiśca vipulairyaśaḥ | māṃśoṣṇatātimadhurapayohārātha suprajāḥ ||
(68,1.26) na cirācchuṣyate caiṣāṃ toyamālyānulepanam | nimīlitāsyanayanā niḥśabdā niḥprakampinaḥ ||
(68,1.27) svapantyekena pārśvena ciraṃ sukhanibodhanāḥ | nātiduḥkhena jīvanti votpadyante sukhena tu ||
(68,1.28) śyāmāḥ śyāmāvadātāśca śrīmanto'dṛḍharogiṇaḥ | alpāśidīrghakāmāstu bhavantyarthasahiṣṇavaḥ ||
(68,1.29) kṣutpipāsāsahāścāpi kaphaprakṛtayo narāḥ | svapneṣu caiva paśyanti ramyaṃ candanakānanam ||
(68,1.30) vikuḍmalapalāśāni pauṇḍarīkavanāni ca | śubhāśca śiśiraprāyā nadyaḥ śubhajalāvahāḥ ||
(68,1.31) tuṣāreṇāvṛtāścāpi himavāghapaṭalāni ca | muktāmaṇisuvāśṛṅgā mṛṇālaphalakāni ca ||
(68,1.32) varāhakhaḍgamahiṣā mṛgāśca rathakuñjarāḥ | spaṣṭatāraṃ tu haṃsāśca vyopoḍhanti nabhastalam ||
(68,1.33) kundagokṣīragaurābhirindoḥ kīrṇagabhastiṣu | protphullakumudākārā vyomni sudhāmbusaprabhaiḥ ||
(68,1.34) rājahaṃsapratīkāśaṃ śaśāṅkaṃ cāmaladyutim | śubhrāṇi ca vimānāni phalāni madhurāṇi ca ||
(68,1.35) kṛtapuṣpopahārāṇi mahānti bhavanāni ca | brāhmaṇān yajñavādāṃśca dadhikṣīrāmṛtāni ca ||
(68,1.36) striyaśca paramodāktāḥ suveṣāḥ svabhyalaṃkṛtāḥ | madhuraśvetapītāni prāyaśaścirameva tu ||
(68,1.37) svapneṣu caivaṃ paśyanti kaphaprakṛtayo narāḥ | prakṛtisvapnabhāveṣu vātikānyapi lakṣayet ||
(68,1.38) calāśca calavikrāntāḥ kṣipramkṣipraṃ pralāpinaḥ | suptāḥ pralāpinastvanye kaṣāyakaṭukapriyāḥ ||
(68,1.39) tvagromanakhadantoṣṭhapāṇipādatalādiṣu | rūkṣasphuṭitadurdarśā durbalā duḥkhabhāginaḥ ||
(68,1.40) kaṭhinopacitāṅgāśca bhrāntacittāphutekṣaṇāḥ | lāpino mṛdavaḥ krūrā vidyādasthirabuddhayaḥ ||
(68,1.41) nṛtyagītakathāśīla jambhino duḥkhabhāginaḥ | hrasvalomāḥ suvapuṣo durbalā dhamanāstathā ||
(68,1.42) kṣāmā bhinnāḥ sadoṣāśca satataṃ vānavasthitāḥ | hastanakhatvagoṣṭhānāṃ pādānāṃ ca vikāriṇaḥ ||
(68,1.43) akasmātkopanāścāpi rodanā dhamānāstathā | paraprakṛtiśīlāśca valganāsphoṭanapriyāḥ ||
(68,1.44) durbalāḥ śiśirāścāpi vātaprakṛtayo naraḥ | svapneṣu caiva paśyanti vātābhravimalā diśaḥ ||
(68,1.45) mārutavegatuṅgāni bhuvanāni vānāni ca | śyāmatārāgrahagaṇaṃ vidhvastārkendumaṇḍalam ||
(68,1.46) dhārācaradbhirviśvābhaiḥ saṃkulaṃ gaganaṃ ghanaiḥ | bhramantaḥ pakṣisaṃghāśca mṛgāścodbhrāntayūthapāḥ ||
(68,1.47) anyāścāpu śambarāśca girigahvarakānanāḥ | bhramanti ghnanti dhāvanti ūrdhvebhyaḥ prapatanti ca ||
(68,1.48) svapneṣvetāni paśyanti vātaprakṛtayo narāḥ | miśrasvapnasvabhāveṣu saṃnipātātmakāḥ viduḥ ||
(68,1.49) etāstisraḥ prakṛtayaḥ saṃsṛṣṭāśca sarvaśaḥ ||
(68,1.50) samyakkaraṇavijñānaṃ tathā svapnādbhavetphalam | śubhaṃ vāpi aśubhaṃ vāpi nirdeṣṭavyamaśeṣataḥ ||
(68,1.51) yenayenendriyārthena viddhaḥ svapiti mānavaḥ | tasyatasyendriyārthasya suptaḥ karmāṇi paśyati ||
(68,1.52) prakṛtyākṛtasaṃkalpasaṃbhavā devatāsvapi | svapnamālāṃ tu yaḥ paśyedyāṃ tu dṛṣṭvā na tu smaret ||
(68,1.53) naite phalaṃ prayacchanti gargasya vacanaṃ yathā | prakṛtyānūkajānāhureke śubhaphalodayān ||
(68,1.54) sārasvataṃ yathānūkaṃ saṃghātasaṃśrayaṃ śṛṇu | dṛṣṭvā bhogamasuptavyaṃ tataḥ prāpya śubhaṃ phalam ||
(68,1.55) svapnaprakṛtibhāvaṃ tu jñātvā tattvaṃ samādiśet ||
(68,2.1) gṛhṇīta samudgendvindravāyvagnyarkanadīṃ kṣitim | samudraṃ vāhinīṃ dvīpaṃ laṅghayedvā vasuṃdharām ||
(68,2.2) vāhinīṃ caturaṅgāṃ ca jīvachattrapatākinīm | dorbhyāṃ ca pratigṛhṇīyāttathāketuvasuṃdharām ||
(68,2.3) ekapuṣkariṇīparṇe sauvarṇe bhāhane'pi vā | sarpiṣā pāyasaṃ bhuṅkte gāṃ duhan yaśca budhyati ||
(68,2.4) pariveṣaḥ svayaṃ candre yo'navastravasuṃdharām | parvatāgraṃ samāruhya kṣitiṃ yaścāvalokayet ||
(68,2.5) ā kaṇṭhaṃ majjate yo hi mānavaḥ śoṇitārṇave | rathena siṃhayuktena parvataṃ cādhirohati ||
(68,2.6) mahīṃ vā kampayedyastu cālayedvā punargirīm | śvetamaśvamathāruhya pāṇḍuraṃ vāpu yo gajam ||
(68,2.7) bhuṅkte puṣkariṇīparṇe pāyasaṃ vāpi sarpiṣā | aṅgavṛddhiṃ śirovṛddhiṃ prāpnuyādyastu mānavaḥ ||
(68,2.8) brāhmaṇo vāpi rājā vā svapne yadabhiṣecayet | rājā tu pārthivo jñeyaḥ kroṣṭukervacanaṃ yathā ||
(68,2.9) śiro vā chidyate yasya vimānaṃ śoṇitaṃ tathā | senāpatyaṃ mahaccāyurarthalābhaṃ tathaiva ca ||
(68,2.10) vibhūṣaṇaṃ ca vidyāṃ ca karṇachedamavāpnuyāt | hastachede labhetputraṃ bāhuchede dhanāgamam ||
(68,2.11) uraḥ sahasralābhaḥ syātpādachede tathaiva ca | uraḥprajananachede atyantaṃ sukhaṃ dedhate ||
(68,2.12) chatrādarśaphaloṣṇīṣaśuklamālyāgame tathā | matsyamāṃsadadhikṣīrarudhirāgama eva ca ||
(68,2.13) śaktyaṅkuśapatākānāṃ chattrāsidhanuṣāṃ tathā | vimalānāṃ jalānāṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ tu nidarśanam ||
(68,2.14) sūkarakharavāhyānāṃ vadhaścaikapaśorapi | narayuktasya yānasya nikṣiptasya gavasya ca ||
(68,2.15) darśanaṃ cāpyadṛṣṭānāmagamyāgamanaṃ tathā | kṣīriṇāṃ phalamvṛkṣāṇāṃ darśanārohaṇāni ca ||
(68,2.16) viṣadarśanadaṃsparśo dhānyenotsaṅgapūraṇam | dasyubhirhanyamānasya rudataḥ pratibodhanam ||
(68,2.17) dvijebhyo dadhimāṃsasya lābhaḥ piśitabhakṣane | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe cāpu śvetamālyānulepanam ||
(68,2.18) ghātanaṃ śvāpadānāṃ ca pāṇau ca rudhirāgamaḥ | arthalābhāya boddhavyaḥ suhṛnmitrasamāgamaḥ ||
(68,2.19) labhate nātra saṃdeho bhārgavasya vaco tathā | śuklāḥ sumanasaḥ kanyā dadhi gobrāhmaṇaṃ vṛṣam ||
(68,2.20) daivatāni nṛpādhyakṣāḥ pāṇḍurāṇi gṛhāṇi ca | suhṛdaḥ saphalā vṛkṣā nakṣatrāṇyamalaṃ jalam ||
(68,2.21) iṣṭakalyāṇaśabdāśca śuklāmbaradharāḥ striyaḥ | nabho vimalanakṣatraṃ pāvakaṃ viṣamārciṣam ||
(68,2.22) dṛṣṭvā yastatkṣaṇaṃ budhyettasya kalyāṇamādiśet | vṛkṣān gulmāṃśca vallīśca svagṛhe puṣpitā naraḥ ||
(68,2.23) śuklavāsāḥ striyaścāpi yaḥ paśyecchrīstu taṃ bhajet | viṣaśoṇitadigdhāṅgaḥ prītīmāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
(68,2.24) dīptāṅgo labhate bhūmiṃ vardhamānāṅga eva ca | parivāryābhirudito bāndhavaiḥ karuṇaṃ naraḥ ||
(68,2.25) śokārto labhate tuṣṭiṃ mṛtaścāyuravāpnuyāt | śuklamālyāmbaradharo dahyamānaḥ pralīyate ||
(68,2.26) yaḥ svapne saṃbhayedugraṃ pārakyaṃ so'rthamāpnuyāt | nāgadantakamudrāṃ ca vīṇāṃ mālāñjanaṃ tathā ||
(68,2.27) kāñcanaṃ paśyate yastu tathā strīṃ labhate naraḥ | uḍḍīyamānān vihagān tathā puṣkariṇīgatān ||
(68,2.28) mattaṃ kareṇumāruhya parastrīṃ labhate naraḥ | kumārīṃ labhate nārīmāyasairnigaḍairnaraḥ ||
(68,2.29) baddhvā navāṃ tu yo mālāmutpālānāṃ vibudhyate | kavāṭale ca saṃyukte tathaivotpalahastake ||
(68,2.30) bhṛṅgāro darpaṇo vāpi labdhvā putrāgamaṃ vadet | taḍāgārāmakūpānāṃ purārañjanayorapi ||
(68,2.31) pūrṇakumbhasya cādeśyaṃ varṣamuttaraṇādbhruvam | cipiṭaḥ kālako nagnaḥ śravaṇo mehate yadi ||
(68,2.32) vidikthaḥ sravate cārmiḥ svapne varṣaṃ samādiśet | sūkarīṃ mahiṣīṃ vāpi hastinīṃ śakunīṃ tathā ||
(68,2.33) svapne yadā prasūyeta subhikṣaṃ nirdiśettadā | śayanāsanayānāni gṛhagrāmapurāṇi ca ||
(68,2.34) yeṣāṃ svapne pralīyante teṣāṃ vṛddhimathādiśet | govṛṣaṃ puruṣaṃ vṛkṣaṃ hastinaṃ parvataṃ gṛham ||
(68,2.35) narasyārohaṇādvṛddhiḥ pāṇḍurāṇi viśeṣataḥ | daivatāni dvijā gāvaḥ pitaro liṅgino grahāḥ ||
(68,2.36) yadvadanti naraṃ svapne tattathaiva vinirdiśet | saritsarasamudrāṇāṃ taraṇe śokatāraṇam ||
(68,2.37) narasya śoṇitaṃ pītvā prakṛtāṇllabhate naraḥ | candrendradhvajasūryāṇāṃ patane nṛpaterbhayam ||
(68,2.38) mahārṇavamahendrāṇāṃ kṣobhe kṣobhaṃ vinirdiśet | keśaśmaśrunakhānāṃ ca patane śokasaṃbhavaḥ ||
(68,2.39) kṛmiṇatvaṃ bhaveddhanyaṃ kroṣṭukervacanaṃ yathā | kravyādairdamṣṭribhiścāpi vināśo bhūtavigrahe ||
(68,2.40) śastramuṣṭiprahāreṣu vijānīyājjvarāgamam | yadyadujjvalavaddravyaṃ tattatsukhakaraṃ bhavet ||
(68,2.41) yadyadvirudhyate vāpi svapne tattasya nirdiśet | [nopānena] prajātānāṃ darśane sthānamādiśet ||
(68,2.42) upānahabalachattradarśane ca grhe tathā | hasadbhirvā parivṛto nṛtyadbhiḥ svajanairapi ||
(68,2.43) saṃyuktaṃ sūkarakharairuṣṭraiḥ kṛṣṇacatuṣpadaiḥ | rathamāruhya yo yāyādakṣatastu yugaṃdharaḥ ||
(68,2.44) prakīrṇakeśo hriyate dakṣiṇenāpareṇa vā | dakṣiṇenāgatā kanyā kālikākulavāsinī ||
(68,2.45) nīyate puruṣairyaśca pāśahastairviśeṣataḥ | nirastānāṃ viṣamāṇāṃ pretenākuśalaṃ bhavet ||
(68,2.46) piṇyākasya tilānāṃ ca karṣāsu lavaṇasya ca | rūḍhaśmaśrunakhānāṃ ca duścelānāṃ ca vāsasām ||
(68,2.47) virāgavāsasāṃ vāpi vikṛtānāṃ tathaiva ca | sarīsṛpāṇāṃ vyālānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ cāpi darśanam ||
(68,2.48) kṛṣṇānāṃ vāpi sarveṣāṃ rājadvijavṛṣādṛte | darśanaṃ gamanaṃ vāpi śokamāyāsavedanam ||
(68,2.49) padmairvā jalabhāṇḍairvā krīḍitāyāsadarśanam | padmāni vāharetsvapne hastachedamavāpnuyāt ||
(68,2.50) prasanne tu dhruvaṃ śoko rajjuchede mriyeta saḥ | rūḍhasya srotasā śoko mṛtyuḥ sriotasi naśyataḥ ||
(68,2.51) dantā bāhuṃ tathā śīrṣṇo chinnāṃśadravyadarśanam | bhrātaraṃ pitaraṃ vāpi putraṃ vā nāśayanti te ||
(68,2.52) dvāre vā sārgale vāpi śayyāṃ śakhāṃ tathaiva ca | svapne yasya pranaśyanti bhāryā tasya vinaśyati ||
(68,2.53) kṛkalāso vṛko vāpi puruṣo vāpi piṅgalaḥ | śayyāṃ yasyādhirohanti bhāryā tasyāpi duṣyati ||
(68,2.54) svapne yo mārayetsarpaṃ śvetapītakalohitam | kṛṣṇasya vā śiraśchindyātputrastasya vinaśyati ||
(68,2.55) rājaputraśca coraśca rājabhṛṭyaśca yo bhavet | tasya svapnāḥ phalaṃ dadyureteṣu yadudāhṛtam ||
(68,2.56) yeṣāṃ lābhe bhavedvṛddhisteṣāṃ nāśe guṇo bhavet | yeṣāṃ lābhe bhaveddhānisteṣāṃ lābhe guṇo bhavet ||
(68,2.57) śubhaṃ dṛṣṭvā tu yaḥ svapne punaḥ paśyatyapūjitam | śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ vāpi yatpaścāttatphalaṃ labhet ||
(68,2.58) svapnāstu prathame yāme saṃvatsaravipākinaḥ | dvitīye'ṣṭasu māseṣu tṛtīye tu tadardhabhāk ||
(68,2.59) māsiko govisarge tu sadyaḥpākaḥ prabhātike. kālaḥ pañcasvavasthāsu śarvaryāḥ kīrtitaḥ pṛthak ||
(68,2.60) viprebhyaḥ śaktito dānaṃ śāntiḥ svaṣṭyayanādayaḥ | vināśayanti duḥsvapnaṃ prātaścāśvatthasevanam ||
(68,2.61) aśvatthasevā tilapātradānaṃ gosparśanaṃ brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ ca | śāntikriyā svastyayanakriyā ca duḥsvapnametāni vināśayanti ||
(68,2.62) vasnakanakadānadevapūjāgurugoṣṭhaniṣevitāni kuryuḥ | dvijavṛṣabḥgavāśvapārthivānāṃ darśanamitihāsamaṅgalāḥ syuḥ ||
(68,3.1) imān dṛṣṭvāśubhān svapnān prātarutthāya satvaraḥ | nadīsaṃgamatoyena mukhaṃ saṃmārjya tattvataḥ ||
(68,3.2) hiraṇyavarṇābhirudakaṃ śaṃtātīyamayobhuvā | abhimantrya prayatnena mukhaṃ saṃmārjya tattvataḥ ||
(68,3.3) yo na jīvaḥ paro'pehi vidma te svapna vedanam | rocanā sarṣapā mṛdā samitsakusumaṃ dadhi ||
(68,3.4) gāmajaṃ kanakaṃ sattvaṃ kumārīṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śubham | abhivandya nṛpo yāyātsuhṛdāṃśca manoharān ||
(68,3.5) yadā tu yātrāṃ nṛpatiḥ kartumicchedvidhānavit | atha svastyayanaiḥ [saumyaiḥ saumyaistamabhimantrayet ||
(68,3.6) tataḥ śuklāmbaradharo vāgyataḥ saṃyatendriyaḥ | tāṃ niśaṃ saṃviśedrājā bhūmau caivābhimantrayet ||
(68,3.7) [anyathaiva hi na svapnadarśanārthanidarśanam] | evamuktvā narapatiḥ prayatātmā tataḥ svapet ||
(68,3.8) praśastasvapnatāṃ dṛṣṭvā tato yāyānnarādhipaḥ | svapneṣu vāpraśastreṣu tataḥ śāntiṃ samārabhet ||
(68,3.9) māhendrīmamṛtāṃ raudrīṃ kuryādvāpyaparājitām | kauberīṃ vā prayuñjīta ādityāṃ vā sadakṣiṇām ||
(68,3.10) rajanikaradivākarau karābhyāṃ spṛśati yadā grasate'tha vā narendraḥ | lavaṇajalanidhiṃ nadīṃ ca dorbhyāṃ tarati hradāpadakardamaṃ tamo vā ||
(68,3.11) naraturagamahīruhānnagān vā bhavanacarānna virohayedgajān vā | jvalanamaraṇakālavṛddhayogān yadi nṛpa ātmagatāṃśca paśyatīha ||
(68,3.12) yadi ca nṛpatirātmano'bhracārairbhramati mahīṃ sapurāṃ parikṣipet | yadi ca sa ciramagnagātramātro bhramati nṛpo grasate'tha medinīṃ vā ||
(68,3.13) yadi ca jayati damṣṭriṇo narān vā yadi ca bhavetsitamālyadānadhārī | yadi ca ruditi caivamādi dṛṣṭvā paraviṣayaṃ hṛṣitastatastu yāyāt ||
(68,4.1) sa kaluṣasalilāvapāṃsumagno madhugh}ṛtatailavasāpradigdhagātraḥ | malinavasanajīrṇaraktavāsā yadi sumanobhiralaṃkṛtaḥ svayaṃ vā ||
(68,4.2) svapiti jayati khādati prahṛṣṭo vilapati nartati gāyanaprahāsaiḥ | bhavati ca mudito labheta kanyāṃ yadi nṛpatirnayaśo bhavejjayārthī ||
(68,4.3) mayakharasūkaravānarādhirūḍho hṛtamukuṭāṅgadavastracihnanagnaḥ | vinihataturagadvipo narendro yadi patitadhvajavāṃstato na yāyāt ||
(68,4.4) narapatiraparājitaḥ parairyo yadi ca paraiḥ parihasyate madadbhiḥ | yadi ca bhavati durdṛśograrūpo <atha> na sa īdṛśakaḥ parān prayāyāt ||
(68,4.5) svapnān dṛṣṭvā śubhān rājā japadbhirabhimantritaḥ | yuktaḥ sa śakunairbhūpa utpātagaṇavarjitaḥ ||
(68,4.6) sahāyavān susaṃnaddho nimittajñaiḥ samanvitaḥ | sumuhūrte sunakṣatre prayāyādvasudhādhipaḥ ||
(68,5.1) tailābhyaktaśca kṛsarāṃ bhuṅkte tailapariplutām | mātaraṃ praviśedyaśca jvalitaṃ ca hutāśanam ||
(68,5.2) prāsadātparvatāgrādvā patedyaścāpi mānavaḥ | magnaḥ kardamakūpeṣu jale yaścāpi naśyati ||
(68,5.3) drumamunmūlayedyastu paśyedrājñopasevakam | kumārīvadane yaśca vānarīṃ vādhigacchati ||
(68,5.4) raktakaṇṭhagate vāpi yasya kaṇṭhe visarjati | vivarṇo vāpi pāśairyo badhyate mriyate tu saḥ ||
(68,5.5) kāṃsyaṃ vā kāṃsyapātrīṃ vā yasya tejo'dhirohati | acireṇaiva kālena so'sinā vadhyate naraḥ ||
(68,5.6) yūpāgramadhiruhyātha nāvāgramadhirohati | acireṇaiva kālena śūlāgraṃ so'dhirohati ||
(68,5.7) muṇḍaḥ kāṣāyavāso vā śvetaraktapaṭo'pi vā | svapne yasyādhirohanti vyādhistasyādhirohati ||
(68,5.8) śvā vā ajagaro godhā tarakṣu śalyako'pi vā | kṛkalāso rururvyāghro dvīpī yasyādhirohati ||
(68,5.9) ahiśca raudrajaṭilaḥ śvetaraktapaṭo'pi vā | svapne yamupatiṣṭhanti vyādhistamupatiṣṭhati ||
(68,5.10) mahībhasmapradigdhāṅgo nirāvaraṇa eva ca | samasyānāṃ sajātānāmutsavānāṃ ca darśanam ||
(68,5.11) durgamadhvānagamanamanūpānāṃ ca sevanam | abhyaṅgaścaiva gātrāṇāṃ tilagomayakardamaiḥ ||
(68,5.12) suvarṇamaṇimuktāni bhūṣaṇaṃ rajatāni ca | darśanaṃ vāpyathaiteṣāṃ vyādhīnāṃ saṃpraveśanam ||
(68,5.13) gāyanaṃ nartanaṃ hāsyaṃ vivāhakaraṇaṃ tathā | ānandaśca pramodaśca vyasanasya ca darśanam ||
(68,5.14) purāṇaghṛtadigdhāṅgo naro maraṇamāpnuyāt | evamuktā mahotpātā vividhāḥ pūrvacoditaḥ ||
(68,5.15) ulkābhedāstathā pañca pariveṣā nava smṛtāḥ | digdāho'ṣṭavidhaḥ prokto vidyudaṣṭavidhā tathā ||
(68,5.16) catvāro bhūmikampāśca nirghāto'ṣṭavidhastathā | viṃśatī dve ca vijñeyā bhedā hyulkādiṣu smṛtāḥ ||
(68,5.17) mahotpātāśca bahavaḥ śāntiyogeṣu kīrtitāḥ | teṣu sarveṣu vidhivacchāntikāmo narādhipaḥ ||
(68,5.18) atharvāṇaṃ ca vṛṇuyātsarvaśāstravidaṃ nṛpaḥ | sa vṛto bhayabhītena śamanārthaṃ mahātmanā ||
(68,5.19) prajānāmabhayaṃ samyagdāpayetpṛthivīpatiḥ | anantaraṃ gavāṃ pūjā brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
(68,5.20) devatāyatane sadyo dohān bhūmau prakārayet | satataṃ cānulipyastu puṣpairdhūpairyathoditaiḥ ||
(68,5.21) pradīpairvividhaiḥ śubhraiḥ sarvadikṣuprakalpitaiḥ | tathā balyupahāraiśca pāyasāpūpasaṃyutaiḥ ||
(68,5.22) hṛdyairbahuvidhairbhakṣaiḥ sarvadikṣuprakalpitaiḥ | tasminnevāntare śānte goṣṭhe vā jalasaṃnidhau ||
(68,5.23) nirgatya nagarādvāpi śucau deśe samāhitaḥ | vṛṇuyācchāntitattvajñānutpātavihitāñśubhān ||
(68,5.24) ṣoḍaśāṣṭau vṛtāste ca puraścaraṇaśodhitāḥ | aṅgāni kuryuranye ca śatasaṃkhyā dvijottamāḥ ||
(68,5.25) udayāste sukhāsīnā japaṃ kuryuratandritāḥ | te sadasyā iti proktā vācane yajñakarmaṇi ||
(68,5.26) teṣāṃ variṣṭhaḥ śāntijña upadraṣṭā manoharaḥ | sarvakarmasu vettā ya ānayetso'pyathādarāt ||
(68,5.27) bhūmiṃ saṃśodhya vidhivatkṛtvā tatra ca maṇḍapam | vidhivatkalpayedvediṃ yajñapātrāṇi ca svayam ||
(68,5.28) evaṃ yathoktavidhinā agnimanthanapūrvakām | mahāśāntiṃ prayuñjīta sarvopadravanāśinīm ||
(68,5.29) annairvastraiśca vividhaiḥ saṃyuktāṃ bahudakṣiṇām | kārayitvā mahāśāntiṃ varaṃ gāṃ ca nivedayet ||
(68,5.30) gṛhamābharaṇaṃ chattramanaḍudvājinaṃ tathā | kuñjaraṃ vā tathā dattvā ghaṇtābharaṇabhūṣitam ||
(68,5.31) mahatsukhamavāpnoti kāryasiddhiṃ ca vindati | kāryasiddhiṃ ca vindati ||

(pariśiṣṭa_69. atharvahṛdayam)
(69,1.1) omupasaṃgamya munayaḥ sarvajñaṃ śāntamānasam | apṛcchan gatamātsyaryaṃ bhṛguṃ brahmavidāṃ varam ||
(69,1.2) kāmā hi bahavo loke saṃsthitā bhinnasādhanāḥ | ekameva paraṃ teṣāṃ samyaktvaṃ brūhyasaṃśayam ||
(69,1.3) samāsena pravakṣyāmi yena sarvaṃ pradadyate | atipraśno'yamudgīrṇastathāpi kathayāmyaham ||
(69,1.4) sarvārthasādhanārthāya śrutirāṅgirasī hitā | svatejasā prajvalantī hṛdayaṃ tadathārvaṇām ||
(69,1.5) prabhāvaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi upariṣṭādyathāvidhi | duḥsādhyāni nimittāni tāni vedmi hi kāni cit ||
(69,2.1) akāle yasya jāyante dantāḥ keśairvivarjitaḥ | prabhūtalambakeśo vā tathā hīnādhikāṅguliḥ ||
(69,2.2) dvidantaścāpi jāyeta tasya karma svaśākhikam | svaśākhāyāṃ tu yatproktaṃ kuryādvātharvaṇaṃ vidhim ||
(69,2.3) dvimūrdhā vā trinetro vā tathaikākṣirdvināsikaḥ | hīnahasto'paro hyarthe na tvatharvaśirāḥ sa ca ||
(69,2.4) kṛtopacāraḥ pañca sapta śuddhātmā sādhyasatkṛtaḥ | sa śāntyudakamācamya śāntavṛkṣasamīpataḥ ||
(69,2.5) śāntavṛkṣasamidbhistu tilaistrimadhuraistathā | homaṃ kuryādatharvā tu tena nandati satkulam ||
(69,3.1) na labhedyadyatharvāṇaṃ kuryāddaśaguṇaṃ svayam | mahāvyāhṛtihomaṃ ca sāvitraṃ japameva ca ||
(69,3.2) vikṛtāṅgo'dhikāṅgo vā jāto hīnāṅgo eva vā | kulasyādbhutamatyarthaṃ tadatharvā śamaṃ nayet ||
(69,3.3) kapyuṣṭrebhagavādīnāṃ jāyante'ṅgamukhaiḥ samāḥ | yasmin rāṣṭre nṛpastatra ṣaṇmāsāddhi vinaśyati ||
(69,3.4) kapyādayo va jāyante anyasya tulyagātrakāḥ | vṛkṣe'nyavṛkṣajaṃ puṣpaṃ jāyate phalameva vā ||
(69,3.5) dvijottamamatharvāṇaṃ tatrecchecchāntimātmanaḥ | kārayeta mahāśāntiṃ rāṣṭrasya ca purasya ca ||
(69,4.1) upasthite rājyanāśe mahāraurava eva vā | durbhikṣe marake vāpi anāvṛṣṭibhaye'pi vā ||
(69,4.2) sarvaṃ rāṣṭre vinaśyeta sasyaṃ śalabhamūṣakaiḥ | akasmānnirjalā vā syādaśoṣā vā mahāsarit ||
(69,4.3) tathānyeṣvapu anukteṣu ghoreṣūpasthiteṣu ca | kuryuḥ śāntimatharvāṇo dvijā hyeteṣu bheṣajam ||
(69,4.4) labhate rājyayogyo'pi na rājyaṃ rajanandanaḥ | paṭhanna labhate vidyāṃ dvijaḥ śṛṇvannapi śrutam ||
(69,4.5) ādhitsurapi nādhānaṃ kuryādāvāsameva ca | kanyā pariṇinīṣurvā kāmyeṣviṣṭapatiṃ na ca ||
(69,5.1) vandhyā vā mṛtavatsā yā durbhagā strīprasū ca yā | sakṛtprasūtā yā nārī garbhaṃ gṛhṇāti naiva ca ||
(69,5.2) sūtikāle'pyatikrānte garbhe sphuraṇavatyapi | na sūtiṃ labhate yā tu bahuputrīyate ca yā ||
(69,5.3) kṛṣīvalaḥ kṛṣīvalaṃ jayatyāyudhajīvyapi | jayāpsurvyavahāre vā saubhāgyaṃ sārvabhautikam ||
(69,5.4) athāpahantuṃ bhayamevamādikaṃ yadābhilaṣyetphalamuktameva vā | tadāṅgirasyaṃ varamantrasaṃpadā sphurantamuccaiḥ śaraṇaṃ vrajeddvijam ||
(69,5.5) rātrau dvichāyavṛkṣaṃ vā kusvapnaṃ vāpi riṣṭadam | divā grahānnirīkṣeta bhūmikampaivamādikam ||
(69,6.1) jvālādbhutānyatha proktāni yāni ulkādibhedā gaditāstathā ye | svapnādbhutānyapi vānyādbhutāni gṛheṣu yānyarthavido vadanti ||
(69,6.2) eteṣu śāntiṃ kurvīta amṛtāṃ vā sadakṣiṇām | raudrīṃ vā vaiśvadevīṃ vā abhayāṃ vāparājitām ||
(69,6.3) gobhūhiraṇyavastrānnaistilairvā saphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ | upānacchattrasaṃyuktāṃ gurvābharaṇasaṃyutām ||
(69,6.4) pratipatīyathoktaṃ vā yaḥ kurvīta vidhānvit | etadutpātajaṃ sarvaṃ mahāśāntyā pralīyate ||
(69,6.5) yasya rājño janapade atharvā śāntipāragaḥ | nivasatyapi tadrāṣṭraṃ vardhate nirupadravam ||
(69,7.1) yasya rājño janapade sa nāsti vividhairbhayaiḥ | pīḍyate tasya tadrāṣṭraṃ paṅle gauriva majjati ||
(69,7.2) tasmādrājā viśeṣeṇa atharvāṇaṃ jitendriyam | dānasaṃmānasatkārairnityaṃ samabhipūjayet ||
(69,7.3) nityaṃ ca kārayecchāntiṃ grahaṛkṣāṇi pūjayet | bhūmidohān prakurvīta devatāyataneṣu ca ||
(69,7.4) catuṣpatheṣu goṣṭheṣu tīrtheṣvapsu ca kārayet | gotarpaṇaṃ ca vidhivatsarvadoṣavināśanam ||
(69,7.5) evaṃ tu khyāpayan rājā sarvakālaṃ jitendriyaḥ | anantaṃ sukhamāpnoti kṛtsnāṃ bhuṅkte vasuṃdharām ||
(69,8.1) upasthitaṃ mṛtyumapi dvijottamaḥ śamaṃ nayet | adhītyātharvāṅgirasastādṛśamādhṛtavrataḥ ||
(69,8.2) dyutiṃ prabhāṃ sadā sphuranmantrapavitravānnaraḥ | nṛpe dhanini cānyatra śāntyāptvā dakṣiṇāṃ budhaḥ ||
(69,8.3) sīdan kuṭumbakaḥ poṣaṃ gṛhītvānyatsamutsṛjet | triḥ saṃhitāṃ haviṣyādyaṃ japetkṛcchraṃ ca śuddhaye ||
(69,8.4) sāvitrīlakṣamayutaṃ sahasaramatha cottaram | japtvā daśāṃśako homaḥ kāryo doṣānusārataḥ ||
(69,8.5) śatīranirmalo yastu nārcito'pi dvijottaram | amatsarī nitāntaṃ yaḥ so'tra śāntiṃ samārabhet ||
(69,8.6) evaṃvidho'ṅgirā yatra yāni sādhyāni sādhayet | na nyūnaṃ tatra kiṃcitsyāditi tadbhṛgubhāṣitam ||
(69,8.7) laghuśāntyudakavidhinā gāyatryā vāpu atharvakaḥ | kuryātsarvamidaṃ jānannatharvahṛdayaṃ budhaḥ ||
(69,9.1) ye na jānantyadhītā api śrutimāṅgirasīṃ dvijāḥ | atharvahṛdayaṃ cāpi na te vedavidaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
(69,9.2) atharvahṛdayaṃ vettā atharvā paramaḥ smṛtaḥ | nātharvaṇe'pyidaṃ deyaṃ gurorvidveṣayāyini ||
(69,9.3) anyaśākhyaṃ dvijo mohātpāṭhayan pravilīyate | atharvahṛdayaṃ buddhvā yaḥ paṭhedbhaktipūrvakam ||
(69,9.4) atharvā nādbhutaṃ tasya śāntireva sadā bhavet | śāntireva sadā bhavet ||

(pariśiṣṭa_70. bhārgavīyāṇi)
(70,1.1) oṃ saṃpūjya vidhivatprājñaṃ vidvāṃso munayaḥ purā | apṛcchan bhṛgumavyagraṃ sarvasattvahite ratam ||
(70,1.2) lokatrayaniviṣṭānāmutpātānāmanekadhā | bhinnānāṃ śamanaṃ noktaṃ vada tvasaṃśayaṃ mune ||
(70,1.3) pratyuvāca bhṛgurviprān śṛṇutāhitamānasaḥ | utpātaśamanatritvaṃ kathyamānamasaṃśayam ||
(70,1.4) tatra viprān pravakṣyāmi duriṣṭaśamanakṣamān | atharvāṅgiraso vede vidhijñān sarvakarmaṇām ||
(70,1.5) ahiṃsāsatyadākṣiṇyaśaucaśraddhāsamanvitāḥ | śrutismṛtisadācārāḥ kulaśīlavayo'nvitāḥ ||
(70,1.6) teṣāmekaḥ pradhānatve yaḥ śānto dvijasattamaḥ | bhṛgvaṅgirovidatyarthaṃ śuciḥ syātsādhusaṃmataḥ ||
(70,1.7) brahmāṇaṃ taṃ nṛpaḥ kuryāddhotāraṃ sarvavedinam | evamukte bhṛguṃ viprāḥ procurvigatakalmaṣam ||
(70,1.8) hotāro bhūmibhartṝṇāṃ mahāśānteḥ prakīrtitāḥ | nanu kṣīṇe vidvan svadharmapracyute'pi vā ||
(70,1.9) tatra śāntau prayuktāyāṃ kasya śāntiphalaṃ bhavet | nṛpo'pyadhārmikaḥ kuryādbrahmaṇastarpaṇaṃ purā ||
(70,1.10) tataḥ kṛtā mahāśāntī rājānaṃ pāti sarvataḥ | sa vṛtaḥ pāvanaṃ gaccheddvijānāṃ pāvanāya vai ||
(70,1.11) dvādaśāhaṃ vrataṃ tatra payomūlaphalāśanaiḥ ||
(70,2.1) trīṇi tryahāṇi kurvīta payomūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ | anaśnaṃśca tryahaṃ dhīraḥ sa puraścaraṇo bhavet ||
(70,2.2) tathaikonaṃ śataṃ nṝṇāṃ śuśrūṣūṇāmakalmaṣam | anuktavacca tryahaṃ tatkarmaṇaḥ karaṇe kṣamam ||
(70,2.3) kṛcchraṃ cāpi hitaṃ kṛtvā kuryuḥ karma samāhitāḥ | śuddhātmāno japairhomairvaidikairvītamatsarāḥ ||
(70,2.4) tataḥ paraṃ purodhaḥsu divyaṃ tatramavāpnuyāt | grahātithyaṃ ca saṃrabhya diśāṃ yāgaṃ ca sarvataḥ ||
(70,2.5) nakṣatreṣu ca sarveṣu yāgaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ | tato'mṛtamahāśāntyā sthāpayetpadmasaṃbhavam ||
(70,3.1) sāvitryā lakṣahomaṃ tu bhaume tiṣṭhedviśāradāḥ | kuryurdeyaṃ ca dānānāṃ viprebhyo yasya yatpriyam ||
(70,3.2) gobhūmikāñcanāśvānāṃ ratnānāṃ dhānyavāsasām | rathānāṃ vāraṇānāṃ ca dānaṃ kāmamataḥ param ||
(70,3.3) tuṣyeyuryena vā viprāḥ saṃbhavo yasya yasya hi | tattatsarvamupādeyameṣa dānavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
(70,3.4) dadyācca gurave grāmaṃ dhenuṃ vāsoyugaṃ tathā | alaṃkāraiśca saṃpūjya prīṇayetprītamānasaḥ ||
(70,3.5) anena vidhinā bhaumamadbhutaṃ śamayedguruḥ | eṣa eva vidhirjñeyo viyatye'pyadbhutāśraye ||
(70,4.1) viśeṣo'yaṃ tu sāvitryā daśalakṣāṃstu homayet | homasamāhitamanāḥ kuryācca ghṛtakambalam ||
(70,4.2) dhenūnāṃ dvādaśaṃ deyaṃ śataniṣkasamanvitam | gurave dīyamānaṃ tacchamayatyambarādbhutam ||
(70,4.3) divyādbhuteṣu kartavyaḥ koṭihomasamanvitaiḥ | gosahasraṃ ca dātavyaṃ gurave dakṣiṇāvidhiḥ ||
(70,4.4) eṣa prokto vidhiḥ samyagdivyāniṣṭavipatkare | subhikṣakṣemasaṃpattyā prajānāṃ puṣṭivardhanaḥ ||
(70,4.5) koṭihomeṣu sarveṣu dravyabhedāśrayaṃ phalam | śāntipuṣṭyabhicārārthaṃ tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
(70,4.6) saumyavṛkṣāśrayāḥ kāryāḥ samidhaḥ śāntimicchatā | arkakāśmaryanimbānāṃ samidbhiḥ śatruśātanam ||
(70,4.7) durnāmakaṇṭakambūnāṃ samidbhiśca viśeṣataḥ | bhagnasphuṭitavṛkṣāṇāṃ phalaṃ śatrunibarhaṇam ||
(70,4.8) bilvapadmotpalānāṃ tu śucideśaprarohiṇām | sarvadā sarvakāmāṃstu homaiḥ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
(70,4.9) tilavrīhiyavādīnāṃ dadhno madhughṛtasya ca | payogodhūmaśālīnāṃ hotā śāntiṃ samārabhet ||
(70,4.10) sarveṣāṃ haviṣāṃ caiva ghṛtaṃ śāntikaraṃ smṛtam | sarvadravye ghṛtaṃ tasmāddhome prakṣepamarhati ||
(70,5.1) yajñopavītinā kāryaṃ śāntikarma vipaścitā | upavītaṃ tu pitryeṣu sarveṣveva samārabhet ||
(70,5.2) madhvājyadadhidugdheṣu bhakṣyamāṇe vilepane | yantravāhanaśastreṣu bhavaneṣvāyudheṣu ca ||
(70,5.3) darpaṇe bhaktapātre ca maṇimuktāphaleṣu ca | bhūṣaṇeṣu tathānyeṣu śayyāyāmāsaneṣu ca ||
(70,5.4) kākolūkakapotānāṃ madhorvā darśanaṃ bhavet | anyeṣāṃ cāpraśastānāmāgamo mṛgapakṣiṇām ||
(70,5.5) aśvetānāṃ ca puṣpāṇāṃ sarīsṛpagaṇasya ca | vasālohitamāṃsānāmasthimajjāśiroruhām ||5 ||
(70,6.1) akasmāccaiva saṃghāte darśane nakhabhasmanām | rasānyatve rasānāṃ ca durgandhe vānimittaje ||
(70,6.2) padmapuṣpākṛtiryatra dṛśyate madhusarpiṣi | kṛsarāpāyase caiva kṣayastasya dhanāyuṣoḥ ||
(70,6.3) ghṛte vā madhudadhni vā yadā padmākṛtirbhavet | svastiko vāpi dṛśyeta tadā maraṇamādiśet ||
(70,6.4) vikāro yatra dṛśyeta kṣīrodanahavihṣu vā | śrotriyāya tu taddadyādbhāvaiva śamayennaraḥ ||
(70,6.5) yatrasthaṃ cādbhutaṃ paśyettatrāpi pratipādayet | kuryādvā vāruṇīṃ śāntiṃ parameṇa samādhinā ||
(70,7.1) anyākṛtiṣu vāpyetadye sthāne śāntikārakāḥ | teṣāmathākṣayaṃ vidyādāyuṣārthadhanena vā ||
(70,7.2) calite jvalite pāte sphurita utpatite tathā | mahājanagajāśvānāṃ sthāne vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(70,7.3) tatra yudhyanti jātīnāṃ bhayaṃ tatsyādasaṃśayam | tatrāpi cārthanicayaiḥ paśubhirvidyayāpi vā ||
(70,7.4) utpātaśamanārthaṃ tu ye kriyā na prayuñjate | narāḥ kṣipraṃ vinaśyante sānvayāḥ saparicchadāḥ ||
(70,7.5) viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ kāryaṃ sahasrasyāyutasya vā | balipuṣpopahāraiśca devatānāṃ prasādanam ||
(70,7.6) kartavyaṃ ca yathānyāyaṃ śāntikarma vipaścitā | evaṃ kṛte bhayaṃ sarvaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati ||
(70,8.1) śubhāśubhasthitaṃ caiva munayo bhṛgumabruvan | sa pratyuvāca puruṣu saṃvatsarasamāśraye ||
(70,8.2) śītoṣṇavṛṣṭikāleṣu vītadoṣeṣu sarvadā | saṃvatsarākhyaḥ puruṣo nirupadrava ucyate ||
(70,8.3) yadi nirghātabhūkampadigdāhādivivarjitaḥ | ketubhiścaiva yujyeta yadi vādityakīlakaiḥ ||
(70,8.4) anyairvā lokanāśārthairadbhutairnāśanākulaiḥ | tata eṣa viśuddhātmā puruṣaḥ sukhamedhate ||
(70,8.5) atha cetsa bahuvidhairadbhutaiḥ parisaṃyutaḥ | saṃvatsaraṃ bhavecchīghraṃ kuryustacchamanaṃ budhāḥ ||
(70,9.1) tatra śāntiṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm | divyatantravidācāryo yayā phalamavāpnuyāt ||
(70,9.2) pūrvaṃ tāvadviśuddhātmā sa puraścaraṇo bhavet | devatānāṃ tato yāgaṃ yathāśruti samācaret ||
(70,9.3) yāgaṃ kṛtvā grahāṇāṃ tu nakṣatrāṇāṃ tataḥ param | ṛtūnathārtavāṃścaiva mahādevagaṇādhipān ||
(70,9.4) diśaśca vidiśaścaiva yamendravaruṇāṃstathā | viśveśvaraṃ ca viṣṇuṃ ca yajetādbhutakarma ca ||
(70,9.5) sūryācandramasāvagniṃ sarvān grahagaṇāṃstathā | vāyuṃ tathāśvinau caiva mahāśāntiṃ vidhānataḥ ||
(70,9.6) kuryāddevādṛto dhīmānevaṃ doṣaḥ praśāmyati ||
(70,10.1) śvetaṃ vā bhavati payo vilohitaṃ vā pītaṃ vā bhavati hi kṛṣṇapiṅgalaṃ vā | utpātaḥ phalati yathā catuḥprakārastatsarvaṃ śṛṇuta samāsato mayoktam ||
(70,10.2) viprāṇāṃ bhavati hi śuklamambudoṣe rājānaṃ sapadi nihanti lohitaṃ ca | pītaṃ cedbhavati nihanti vaiśyavargaṃ śūdrāṇāṃ bhavati hi kṛṣṇapiṅgadoṣe ||
(70,10.3) bījaṃ yatra praroheta phalamatha pramādataḥ | etadatyadbhutaṃ nāma dampatyostu vināśanam ||
(70,10.4) apūjanāttu pūjyasya tathāpūjyasya pūjanāt | antaḥkaraṇadoṣācca hetoḥ śāntirna jāyate ||
(70,10.5) tasmādvedārthaśāstrajñān vītarāgānamatsarān | paricārakamukhyāṃśca kārayetkuśalān dvijān ||
(70,11.1) vicāritaṃ ca vidvadbhirniścitaṃ sudhiyā punaḥ | deśakālasamāyuktaṃ karma kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||
(70,11.2) codite karmaṇyanyasminnānyatkuryādvidhānavit | na ca prārabhya karmāṇi sthātavyaṃ kva cidantare ||
(70,11.3) nāpi kurvīta matimān kadā citkarmasaṃkaram | kurvaṃstu na tathā karma doṣamutpādayedbhṛśam ||
(70,11.4) anyathākaraṇe doṣān samīkṣya tu bahūniha | saṃkalpavānna samāptiḥ [sāvīryākṛtikarmaṇī] ||
(70,11.5) dṛṣṭvādbhutaṃ tu kārtsnyena tataḥ śāntiṃ samārabhet | asamīkṣya tu kurvāṇo na śāntiṃ labhate naraḥ ||
(70,12.1) vātikasya yathā vaidyaḥ paittikasya ca niścaye | rogasya bhaiṣaje datte karmasiddhimavāpnuyāt ||
(70,12.2) tasmāttāvatparīkṣeta yāvanniṣpannamadbhutam | asadasyasadasyānāṃ kartuḥ kārayitustathā ||
(70,12.3) viguṇaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ tu karma kuryādupadravam | viśeṣato nihanyeta kartāraṃ saparicchadam ||
(70,12.4) hetuśrutaṃ ca dṛṣṭaṃ ca tasmātsāguṇyamācaret | saguṇe ca kṛte tasmin sarvasaṃpadbhaveddhruvam ||
(70,12.5) aśokapuṣpairhome tu madhukṣīrasamanvitaiḥ | prāpnoti sukṛtairvipro gandharvapadamuttamam ||
(70,12.6) brahmādistambaparyantaṃ yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ samīhate | tattatprāpnotyayatnena satyametaddvijottamāḥ ||

(pariśiṣṭa_70 . gārgyāṇi)
(70 ,1.1) oṃ praṇamya viṣṭarāsīnāṃ brahmāṇaṃ kavisattamam | praṇamya śirasā devaṃ gautamaḥ paryapṛcchata ||
(70 ,1.2) adbhutāni suraśreṣṭha prajānāmahitāya vai | śamanaṃ ca tathā teṣāṃ prabrūhi vinayena me ||
(70 ,1.3) tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ | abravītparamaḥ prītaḥ sarvotpātapratikriyām ||
(70 ,1.4) śṛṇu vatsa yathānyāyaṃ dvādaśādhyāyasaṃgraham | pracyamānamaśeṣaṃ taṃ vātavaikṛtanoditam ||
(70 ,1.5) yasmācca vāyurvalavāñśreṣṭhaḥ sarvādbhutodbhavaḥ | tasmāttameva prathamaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvidhi ||
(70 ,2.1) yānti yānānyayuktāni vinā nṛbhistathā | yuktāni vā na gacchanti narendrāṇāṃ mahadbhayam ||
(70 ,2.2) bheryo mṛdaṅgāḥ paṭahā vādyante vāpyanāhatāḥ | āhatāśca na vādyante acalāni calanti vā ||
(70 ,2.3) araṇye tūryanirghoṣo yadi śrūyeta nābhasaḥ | śarīraṃ vyathate tatra yadi vā veśmani śrutaḥ ||
(70 ,2.4) śrūyante ca mahāśabdā gītagāndharvanisvanāḥ | śarīraṃ bādhyate tatra vyādhirvā sumahān bhavet ||
(70 ,2.5) koṣṭhe vā patate yatra hastāddarvī kadā cana | patate musalaṃ cāpi śūrpaṃ vā dhūyate yadi ||
(70 ,3.1) golāṅgalānāṃ saṃsargo vikāraścandrasūryayoḥ | nārīṃ vā dhayate nārī jāyate tumulaṃ bhayam ||
(70 ,3.2) pratyāharanti sarpanti stambhaprāsādapādapāḥ | śayanāsanayānāni niyataṃ nṛpatervadhaḥ ||
(70 ,3.3) vāti cākālilo vāyurghoraḥ śarkarakarṣaṇaḥ | pātayan vṛkṣaveśmāni kalpānta iva bhīṣaṇaḥ ||
(70 ,3.4) saptāhamatha vā pakṣaṃ nibadhnātyatidāruṇam | tryahādyadi na varṣeta ghoraṃ śastrabhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,3.5) vāyavyeṣveva nṛpatirvāyuṃ saptabhirarcayet | dvāvimāviti tisro hi japtavyāḥ prayatairdvijaiḥ ||
(70 ,4.1) bahvannadakṣiṇo homaḥ kartavyo'tiprayatnataḥ | vāyavyāmeva śāntau ca vāyoḥ saviturāvapet ||
(70 ,4.2) ādāvante ca madhye ca tathaivamanuyojayet | gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādvāyavīśāntisiddhaye ||
(70 ,4.3) yamakaṃ jāyate puṣpaṃ phalaṃ vā yamakaṃ yadi | kumudotpalapadmāni ekanāle bahūnyapi ||
(70 ,4.4) bahuśīrṣā dviśīrṣā vā tathānyaprasavā api | yavā vā vrīhayo vāpi svāmino maraṇāya te ||
(70 ,4.5) ekavṛkṣe ca saṃpaśyennānātvaṃ phalapuṣpayoḥ | vyatyāsamanyathātvaṃ vā paracakrāgamo bhavet ||
(70 ,5) [ṭhe manuscriptscontainno khaṇḍikā five.]
(70 ,6.1) anṛtu phalapuṣpaṃ vā na yathartu phalanti vā | oṣadhīvīrudho vāpi janamārabhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,6.2) atha dhānyaviparyāse abhadraṃ cāpi śaṃsati | tilā vā samatailāḥ syuḥ surātailā bhavanti vā ||
(70 ,6.3) agrāmyaṃ kārayetpuṣpaṃ phalaṃ vā vikṛtaṃ nṛpaḥ | dhānyānāṃ vaikṛte kṣetraṃ saha sasyena dāpayet ||
(70 ,6.4) sauryaṃ caruṃ puṣpaphale vikṛte paśumeva ca | kṣaitrapatyaṃ ca bhaumaṃ ca nirvapetsasyavaikṛte ||
(70 ,6.5) sauryī śāntiḥ prayoktavyā sauryairmantrairyathāvidhi | uccā patantamityṛgbhyāṃ garbhaṃ tu parikīrtitam ||
(70 ,7.1) bhaumena cānuvākena garbhayetsasyavaikṛte | sadakṣiṇairdvijairbhuktaiḥ kartāraṃ cārcayettataḥ ||
(70 ,7.2) pureṣu yeṣu dṛśyante pādapā devacoditāḥ | rudanto vā hasanto vā sravanto vā bahūn rasān ||
(70 ,7.3) arogā vā nivāte ca śākhā muñcantyasaṃbhrame | phalaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā bālā darśayantīti hāsanam ||
(70 ,7.4) sarvāvasthāṃ darśayantaḥ phalapuṣpamanārtavam | [kṣipraṃ tatra bhayaṃ ghoraṃ ghoraṃ pravaryteta caturvidham ||
(70 ,7.5) sarpānmatsyān pakṣiṇo vā yatra devaḥ pravarṣati | tatra sasyopaghātaḥ syādbhayaṃ vātipravartate ||
(70 ,7.6) surāsavaṃ tathā kṣaudraṃ sarpistailaṃ tathā dadhi | yatra varṣati parjanyaḥ kṣudragastatra jāyate ||
(70 ,7.7) ulkātārāśca dhiṣṇyeṣu yadāṅgārāṃśca varṣati | tadā vyādhibhayaṃ ghoraṃ teṣu deśeṣu nirdiśet ||
(70 ,7.8) nārācāḥ śaktayaḥ khaḍgāḥ pradīpyante yadā muhuḥ | tadā śastrabhayaṃ ghoraṃ teṣu deśeṣu nirdiśet ||
(70 ,7.9) pumānaśvo gajo vāpi yadā yatra pradīpyate | naśyanti sevakāstatra pradhānaśca vinaṅkṣyati ||
(70 ,7.10) yatra sraveccaityavṛkṣaḥ sahasā vividhān rasān | pṛthakpṛthaksamastān vā tatpravakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
(70 ,7.11) ghṛte madhuni dugdhe ca ghṛte dugdhe tathāmbhasi | kṣaudre madhuni pānīye ghṛte caivāparaṃ payaḥ ||
(70 ,7.12) yatraitacca mahotpātaṃ vṛkṣeṣu syātsudāruṇam | surāsave mithobhedaḥ śoṇite śastrapātanam ||
(70 ,7.13) taile pradhānā vadhyante bhakte kṣudbhayamādiśet | anṛtau cetphalaṃ yatra puṣpaṃ vā dṛśyate drume ||
(70 ,7.14) dhruvaṃ syāddaśame māsi rājñastatra viparyayaḥ | puṣpe puṣpaṃ bhavedyatra phale caiva tathā phalam ||
(70 ,7.15) parṇe parṇaṃ vijānīyāttatra nānāvidhaṃ bhayam | śuklena vāsasā yatra caityavṛkṣaḥ samāvṛtaḥ ||
(70 ,7.16) brāhmaṇānāṃ bhayaṃ ghoraṃ tadā tīvraṃ vinirdiśet | raktavastrāvṛtaiścānyaiḥ kṣatraiyāṇāṃ mahābhayam ||
(70 ,7.17) pītavastraiśtu vaiśyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ kṛṣṇavāsasaiḥ | nīlaiḥ sasyopaghātāya miśraistu mṛgapakṣiṇām ||
(70 ,7.18) vivarṇairvāyavastīvrāḥ paraṃ syurdaśamāsataḥ | daivatāni pralapanti yasya rāṣṭre hasanti vā ||
(70 ,7.19) udīkṣante puro vāpi tatra vidyānmahadbhayam | vihasanti nimīlanti liṅgāni vikṛtāni ca ||
(70 ,7.20) māsāntareṇa jānīyāttatra tatra mahadbhayam | yatra citramudīkṣeta gāyate ceṣṭate muhuḥ ||
(70 ,7.21) eteṣvaṣṭasu māseṣu rājño maraṇamādiśet | citrāṇi yatra liṅgāni tathaivāyatanāni ca ||
(70 ,7.22) vikāraṃ kuryuratyarthaṃ tatra vidyānmahābhayam | utpāṭanaṃ tadāgānāṃ saraso vā firestathā ||
(70 ,7.23) samuddeśe pradīpyante vidyāttatra bhayaṃ mahat | yatra vṛkṣā akālīnaṃ darśanaṃ phalapuṣpayoḥ] ||
(70 ,7.24) kṣīraṃ snehaṃ surāṃ raktaṃ madhu toyaṃ sravanti vā | śūṣyantyarogāḥ sahasā śuṣkā ruhanti vā punaḥ ||
(70 ,7.25) uttiṣṭhanti niṣīdanti tatpravakṣyānyataḥ param ||
(70 ,8.1) hasane dehanāśaḥ syādyodhā naśyanti śakhayā | saṃbhramo deśanāśāya phalī śilpikṣayāya ca ||
(70 ,8.2) bālānāṃ maraṇaṃ kuryādbālānāṃ phalapuṣpatā | svarāṣṭrabhedaṃ kurute phalapuṣpamanārtavam ||
(70 ,8.3) kṣayaḥ kṣīrasrave jñeyaḥ snehe durbhikṣalakṣaṇam | vāhanāpacayaṃ madye rakte saṃgrāmamādiśet ||
(70 ,8.4) madhusrāve bhavedvyādhirjalasrāve na varṣati | arogā yadi śuṣyante vidyāddurbhikṣalakṣaṇam ||
(70 ,8.5) bhedaḥ svapatitotthāne rudatsvannakṣayo bhavet | jalpane dhananāśaḥ syādgulmavallīlatāsu ca ||
(70 ,9.1) pūjitānāṃ jalasrutau rājño mṛtyuṃ samādiśet | ācchādayitvā taṃ vṛkṣāṃ gandhamālyairvibhūṣayet ||
(70 ,9.2) bhojanaṃ cātra viprāṇāṃ madhusarpiḥsamanvitam | chattradhvajaṃ ca dātavyaṃ parṇahomastathā param ||
(70 ,9.3) mantrairauṣadhasaṃyuktairbhvoradābanataḥ param | baliṃ caivopahārāṃśca gītanṛtyaṃ samantataḥ ||
(70 ,9.4) gandhamālyaṃ ca dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ dadyāttathaiva ca | bhakṣabhojyānnapānaṃ ca rudrasyopaharenniśi ||
(70 ,9.5) pākaśca daśame māsi śukrasya vacanaṃ yathā | bṛhaspatistathāditye bruvete yattathaiva tat ||
(70 ,10.1) raudrī caivātra kartavyā vṛkṣādbhutavināśinī | durave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyānniṣkaṃ bhūmiṃ ca tatra vai ||
(70 ,10.2) akālaprasuvo nāryaḥ kālātītāḥ prajāstathā | saṃbaddhayugmaprasavā dviyugmaprasavā api ||
(70 ,10.3) amānuṣāṇi ruṇḍāni saṃjāyante yadā striyām | atyaṅgāni anaṅgāni hīnāṅgānyatha vā punaḥ ||
(70 ,10.4) catuṣpatpakṣisadṛśānyardhamānuṣavanti ca | vināśastatsya deśasya kulasya ca vinirdiśet ||
(70 ,10.5) aprāptavayaso garbho dvicatuṣpatstriyo'pi vā | vidhvastaṃ vikṛtaṃ cāpi prajāyeta bhayāya tat ||
(70 ,11.1) tānyāśu parabhūmiṣu tyaktavyāni śubhārthibhiḥ | śāntiścātra prakartavyā brāhmaṇairbrahmavādibhiḥ ||
(70 ,11.2) vaḍavā hastinī gaurvā yadi yugmaṃ prasūyate | vijātaṃ vikṛtaṃ vāpi ṣaṇmāsairmriyate nṛpaḥ ||
(70 ,11.3) apatyāni ca yūthebhyastyājyāni parabhūmiṣu | svāmino nagaraṃ yūthamanyathā tu vināśayet ||
