(70 ,11.4) viyoniṣu yadā yānti ṃśrībhāvaḥ prajāsvapi | kharoṣṭrahayamātaṅgāḥ pakṣiṇo vā na sādhu tat ||
(70 ,11.5) akāle vāpi mādyante kāle vāpu amadā yadi | śivoṣṭrahayamātaṅgāḥ pakṣiṇo vā na sādhu tat ||
(70 ,12.1) athānaḍvānanaḍvāhaṃ dhenurdhenuṃ pibedyadi | śunī vā dhayate dhenuṃ śunīṃ dhenurathāpi vā ||
(70 ,12.2) [tiryagyonau mānuṣī vā paracakrāgamo bhavet | amānuṣā mānuṣāṇi jalapante prāṇino yadi ||
(70 ,12.3) viceṣṭāṃ vā virāvaṃ vā māsena mriyate nṛpaḥ] | catuṣpatpakṣibhujagānmānuṣī janayedyadi ||
(70 ,12.4) tiryagyonau mānuṣaṃ vā paracakrāgamo bhavet | jaṅgame sthāvaraṃ jātaṃ sthāvare vātha jaṅgamam ||
(70 ,12.5) tasmin yoniviparyāse paracakrāgamo bhavet | tyāgo vivāso dānaṃ vā dattvāpyāśu śubhaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,13.1) sthālīpākena yaṣṭavyaṃ paśunā vā purohitaḥ | prājāpatyena mantreṇa yajedbahvannadakṣiṇām ||
(70 ,13.2) yāmyākarmaprayogastu prathamaṃ tatra dṛśyate | prājāpatyāṃ tataḥ śāntiṃ prājārthī kārayennṛpaḥ ||
(70 ,13.3) ādāvante ca madhye ca śāntāvuktastu tadgaṇaḥ | ārogyaṃ ca śivaṃ caiva deśe tasminnṛpe bhavet ||
(70 ,13.4) yatrādbhutāni dṛśyante vicitrāṇi samantataḥ | susamṛddho'pi deśaḥ sa kṣiprameva vinaśyati ||
(70 ,13.5) rājaveśmasu vaikṛtye prāsādadhvajatoraṇe | autpātikāni dṛśyante rājñastatra mahadbhayam ||
(70 ,14.1) prāsādatoraṇāṭṭāladvāraprāsādaveśmanām | akasmātpatanaṃ teṣāṃ rājamṛtyukaraṃ smṛtam ||
(70 ,14.2) devarājadhvajānāṃ ca patanaṃ bhaṅga eva vā | niṣevaṇaṃ vā kravyādaiḥ..........prabhraṣṭairvītaraśmikaiḥ ||
(70 ,15.1) prabhraṣṭagrahanakṣatrairdiśaḥ sarvāḥ samākulāḥ | saṃdhyā cobhayathā dīptā tatra vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(70 ,15.2) yadi vā dīryate'kasmādbhūmiśchidrībhavedyadi | prakampate'timātraṃ vā sarveṣu ca bhayāya tat ||
(70 ,15.3) rakṣaḥpataṃgaiḥ panthāno na vahanti bhayānvitāḥ | rakṣorūpāṇi dṛśyante na ca rakṣā gṛheṣvapi ||
(70 ,15.4) saṃpraviṣṭaiḥ piśācairvā rakṣobhirvāpi tannibhaiḥ | acirānnagaraṃ tatra janamāreṇa māryate ||
(70 ,15.5) ṛtavastu viparyastā brāhmaṇāśca vidharmiṇaḥ | nakṣatrāṇi viyogīni bhayamīdṛkpradarśanam ||
(70 ,16.1) apūjyā yatra pūjyante na pūjyante ca pūjitāḥ | pūjyeṣvadānaniṣṭhā ca bhayamīdṛkpradarśanam ||
(70 ,16.2) nādīyante na pūjyante brāhmaṇā balibhiḥ surāḥ | na caivātmīyadharmeṣu ratiṃ kurvantyadharmataḥ ||
(70 ,16.3) bhinnāḥ kauṭilyabahulā gajāḥ puruṣavājinaḥ | kalahe syurnirutsāhāḥ sasatyāḥ satyavarjitāḥ ||
(70 ,16.4) śīlācāravihīnāśca madyamāṃsānṛtapriyāḥ | nagnapāṣaṇḍabhūyiṣṭhā vināśe paryupasthite ||
(70 ,16.5) mahābaliṃ mahāśāntiṃ bhojyāni sumahānti ca | prājāpatyaṃ mahendraṃ ca mahādevamathāpi vā ||
(70 ,17.1) aindrasthāne tu māhendrīṃ raudre raudrīṃ prayojayet | gavāmaṣṭaśataṃ dadyādviprebhyo manujādhipaḥ ||
(70 ,17.2) gurave tu śataṃ niṣkaṃ prajāsvevaṃ śivaṃ bhavet | anāvṛṣṭyātivṛṣṭyā vā durbhikṣeṇa bhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,17.3) akālavarṣo rogāya ativṛṣṭirbhayāya ca | anabhraṃ varṣate'kasmādvaidyutaṃ garjitaṃ tathā ||
(70 ,17.4) anabhre vāpi nirghātaḥ patito rājamṛtyave | tīkṣṇaṃ ca varṣatyanṛtau ṛtuṣveva na varṣati ||
(70 ,17.5) yadā coṣṇe bhavecchītaṃ śīte coṣṇaṃ tathaiva ca | dṛṣṭo bhāvastu vikṛto na yathartu svarūpakaḥ ||
(70 ,17.6) anārogyaṃ bhaveccaiva prajānāmiti nirdiśet ||
(70 ,18.1) saptarātraṃ yadā varṣetprabaddhaṃ pākaśāsanaḥ | anṛtau tasya deśasya pradhānasya vadho dhruvam ||
(70 ,18.2) śoṇitaṃ varṣate yatra tatra śastrabhayaṃ bhavet | majjāsthisnehamāṃsaṃ vā janamārībhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,18.3) aṅgārapāṃsuvṛṣṭestu nagaraṃ tadvinaśyati | phalaṃ puṣpaṃ śamīdhānyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ vā bhayāya tat ||
(70 ,18.4) jantavo dīnavikṛtāḥ palālo'pi vināśanaḥ | chidrāvartāḥ plavaṅgaśca sasyānāmativardhanam ||
(70 ,18.5) anabhre vā divā rātrau śvetamindrāyudhaṃ bhavet | pūrvapaścāduttare vā dakṣiṇe vāpi dṛśyate ||
(70 ,18.6) susamṛddhamapi sthānaṃ durbhikṣeṇa vinaśyati ||
(70 ,19.1) yadyanabhre'pi vimale sūryachāyā na dṛśyate | na nirabhre pratīpā vā tatra deśabhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,19.2) sūryendravāyuparjanyā yaṣṭavyā varṣavaikṛte | annāni sahiraṇyāni dhānyaṃ gāvaśca dakṣiṇāḥ ||
(70 ,19.3) vaiśvadevī ca kartavyā sarvādbhutavināśinī | gurave ca hayaḥ śvetaḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ ||
(70 ,19.4) śataṃ niṣkaṃ suvarṇasya dātavyaṃ vā gavāṃ śatam ||
(70 ,19.5) athāto'gnivaikṛtamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmo yathovāca bhagavāñśukraḥ ||
(70 ,19.6) anindhano'gnirdīpyeta yatra tūrṇamaghasvanaḥ | na dīpyate sendhano vā sarāṣṭraṃ pīḍayennṛpam ||
(70 ,19.7) prajvaleddadhi māṃsaṃ vā tathā dūrvāpi kiṃ cana | agniṃ vinā yadāśuṣkaṃ niyataṃ nṛpatervadhaḥ ||
(70 ,20.1) prāsādaṃ toraṇaṃ dvāraṃ prākāraṃ kāśyapaṃ gṛham | śayanāsanayānaṃ ca dhvajaṃ chattraṃ sacāmaram ||
(70 ,20.2) anagninā yadi dahedvidyutā vāpi nirdahet | saptāhābhyantare tatra niyataṃ nṛpatervadhaḥ ||
(70 ,20.3) aniśāyāṃ tamāṃsi syuryadi vā pāṃsavo rajaḥ | dhūmāścānagnijā yatra tatra vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(70 ,20.4) rātrau divā cānabhre vā yadi jvālā pradṛśyate | garhitaṃ jyotiṣāṃ caiva darśanaṃ vā bhavenniśi ||
(70 ,20.5) purāṇāṃ caiva patanaṃ jvalatāṃ ca muhurmuhur | dṛśyate'nyacca sahasā tatrāpyagnibhayaṃ vadet ||
(70 ,21.1) prāsādādiṣu caityeṣu yadi dhūmo vināgninā | bhavatyagniradhūmo vā tathaivātibhayāvahaḥ ||
(70 ,21.2) jvalanti yadi śastrāṇi vinamantyunnamanti vā | kośebhyo vāpi niryānti saṃgrāmastumulo bhavet ||
(70 ,21.3) pradīpyante ca sahasā catuṣpatpakṣimānuṣāḥ | vṛkṣā vā parvatā vāpi tatra vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(70 ,21.4) śayanāsanayāneṣu keśaprāvaraṇeṣu ca | dṛśyate'tīva sahasā tatrāpyagnibhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,21.5) garjantyāyudhaśastrāṇi vinamantyunnamanti vā | dhanunā saha vā bāṇāḥ saṃgrāmastumulo bhavet ||
(70 ,22.1) samidbhiḥ kṣīraṛkṣāṇāṃ sarṣapaistu ghṛtena ca | hotavyo'gniḥ svakairmantraiḥ mantraiḥ suvarṇaṃ cātra dakṣiṇā ||
(70 ,22.2) pāyasaṃ sarpiṣā miśraṃ dvijātīn bhojayettataḥ | tebhya eva yathāśaktyā dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayennṛpaḥ ||
(70 ,22.3) agnirbhūmyāmiti tribhirāgneyaṃ tatra kārayet | gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyānniṣkamaśvaṃ ca sundaram ||
(70 ,23.1) gārgyeṇoktaṃ pravakṣyāmi kṛtsnamutpātalakṣaṇam | bhūmikampo bhavedyatra devatāpratimā haset ||
(70 ,23.2) devatā bhramate yatra mṛtyustatra vinirdiśet | garjanaṃ vāpi kūpānāmupasargastu jāyate ||
(70 ,23.3) pratisrotavahā nadyo bhavanti ca kathaṃ cana | ṣaḍbhirmāsairvijānīyātparacakrābhimarśanam ||
(70 ,23.4) akālajaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ śītoṣṇatvamakālajam | anyaṃ svāminamicchanti nadyaścākālasaṃbhavāḥ ||
(70 ,23.5) acalaṃ ca calaṃ yatra calaṃ vā<py> acalaṃ bhavet | rājā vinaśyate tara deśo vāpi vinaśyati ||
(70 ,23.6) divā tārā yatra paśyecchvetaḥ pakṣyatha vā bhavet | rātrau cendrāyudhaṃ paśyeddeśabhaṅgaṃ vinirdiśet ||
(70 ,23.7) śaśakaṃ jambukaṃ vāpi sūkaraṃ hariṇaṃ tathā | sthānamadhye yadā paśyecchūnyaṃ bhavati niścayam ||
(70 ,23.8) araṇyamṛgajātīyāḥ svayaṃ yānti nṛpālayam | tatsthānaṃ tu bhavecchūnyaṃ bhagnaprākāratoraṇam ||
(70 ,23.9) prākāraveśmabhiṭṭīṣu toraṇe gokule'pi vā | madhūni yatra dṛśyante tatra vai kasya kiṃ phalam ||........................
(70 ,23.10) kālanaṣṭapathaṃ sīmāṃ tṛṇavallīsamākulām | sa deśo mānuṣairmukto mṛgāṇāṃ gocaro bhavet ||
(70 ,23.11) pratyādityaṃ yadā paśyetpure devakule'pi vā | api śakrasamo rājā abdamadhye vinaśyati ||
(70 ,23.12) vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu nadyāṃ vā tarate śilā | rājabhaṅgaṃ bhaveccaiva cauravyādhibhayaṃ tathā ||
(70 ,23.13) rājagāmiṣu puṣpeṣu vastreṣvābharaṇeṣu ca | anagninā yadi dahetparighaṃ tatra vai dhruvam ||
(70 ,23.14) tatpātaparityakta kadā cidapi budhasyādayaṃ bhavati | dahanaṃ pavanajalamaraṇarogarakṣakṣayāya buddhivākkaroti budhaḥ ||
(70 ,23.15) tatra kuryānmahāśāntimamṛtāṃ viśvabheṣajīm ||

(pariśiṣṭa_70 . bārhaspatyāni)
(70 ,22.1) omāsīnaṃ tu himavati bṛhaspatiṃ sukhāvaham | gautamaḥ paripṛcchati vinayātsaṃśitavrataḥ ||
(70 ,22.2) kathamagniḥ parīkṣyo'yaṃ mantrakarmaṇi śobhanaḥ | svarūpaṃ jñāpaya tvaṃ hi śubhāśubhanibodhane ||
(70 ,23.1) bṛhaspatiḥ pratyāha taṃ gautam || śvetaḥ sugandhiḥ padmābho nirbhūmo dundubhisvanaḥ | asakto'muṭitaśikhaḥ snigdhotthāyī pradakṣiṇaḥ ||
(70 ,23.2) hūyamānaḥ pradīptaḥ syāddīptatejāḥ sukhapradaḥ | śāntikarmaṇi yatrāgnirniyataṃ siddhilakṣaṇam ||
(70 ,23.3) svastikā vardhamānā ca śrīvatsā ca pradakṣiṇā | jvālārūpeṇa dṛśyeta sā vai śrīḥ sarvatomukhī ||
(70 ,23.4) yadā hotra prasannena hūyamāno yathā śikhī | ghoṣamutpādayan snigdhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tadvinirdiśet ||
(70 ,23.5) dīptaśca ratnasaṃkāśaḥ kṣemo dundubhivadghanaḥ | dhūmaḥ praśasto bhavati svārthasiddhikaro nṛṇam ||
(70 ,24.1) snigdhaghoṣo'lpadhūmaśca gauravarṇo mahān bhavet | piṇḍitārcirvapuṣmān vā pāvakaḥ siddhikārakaḥ ||
(70 ,24.2) yadā tvagniḥ sarvadikthā jvālāgraiḥ spṛśate haviḥ | tadāsya nṛpatiḥ śīghraṃ pararāṣṭraṃ ca mardati ||
(70 ,24.3) tiṣṭhantaṃ sthāvaraṃ snigdhaṃ śrūyate yatra gītakam | vācaḥ prasannā homeṣu maṅgalyāścaiva siddhaye ||
(70 ,24.4) kokilasya māyurasya bhāsasya kuralasya ca | homeṣu śravaṇaṃ caiva prādakṣiṇyaṃ ca śasyate ||
(70 ,24.5) śatapattrā rudantī ca cāṣasya nandanaṃ tathā | rambhaṇaṃ caiva dhenūnāṃ havaneṣu praśasyate ||
(70 ,25.1) padmavaiḍūryanikāśā vāditrāṇāṃ ca nisvanāḥ | gāvaḥ savarṇavatsā ca dṛṣṭā home praśasyate ||
(70 ,25.2) vikāsipadmasadṛśaḥ prasannārcirhutāśanaḥ | susamānābhirarcābhiḥ snigdhābhiranupūrvaśaḥ ||
(70 ,25.3) gambhīraṃ nardate yatra tadagryaṃ siddhilakṣaṇam | akṣatān phalapuṣpāṇi vardhamānamapāṃ ghaṭam ||
(70 ,25.4) dṛṣṭvā vā yadi vā śrutvā karmasiddhiṃ samādiśet | pīṭhachattradhvajanibhā jvālā vāraṇasaṃnibhāḥ ||
(70 ,25.5) praśastā ujjvalāścaiva vajrakuṇḍalasaṃnibhāḥ | pradakṣiṇagatiḥ śrīmānagniḥ karturmanoharaḥ | yasya syādvijayaṃ kuryātkṣipraṃ narapaterdhruvam ||
(70 ,26.1) bhūmyāṃ meghābhivṛṣṭānāṃ madhupāyasasarpiṣām | kṛṣṇavartmā sugandhiḥ syājjayaṃ kṣitipatervadet ||
(70 ,26.2) śaṅkhasvastikarūpāṇi cakrarūpaṃ tathā gadā | śiromālā ca dṛśyeta tadvai vijayalakṣaṇam ||
(70 ,26.3) ghṛtavarṇanibhastvagniḥ snigdhaghoṣo mahāsvanaḥ | citrabhānuḥ prasanno vā niyataṃ siddhilakṣaṇam ||
(70 ,26.4) mṛgapakṣiṇa āraṇyāḥ praviśanti yadā puram | grāmyā vā tyaktvā nagaramaraṇyaṃ yānti nirbhayāḥ ||
(70 ,26.5) divā rātricarā vāpi rātrau vāpi divācarāḥ | divā vā puramadhyasthā ghoraṃ vāśyanti nirbhayāḥ ||
(70 ,27.1) rājadvāre puradvāre śivā vāpyaśubhaṃ vadet | [tyaktvāraṇyaṃ ca tiṣṭhanti nagaraṃ mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ] ||
(70 ,27.2) āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe vāpi śūnyaṃ bhavati tatpuram | [tyaktvā siṃhāḥ sahariṇā mūṣikaṃ sūkaraṃ rurum ||
(70 ,27.3) dṛṣṭvā praviṣṭānnagare śūnyaṃ bhavati tatpuram] | abhivācaṃ vadante ca paśavyā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ ||
(70 ,27.4) śyenā gṛdhrā bakāḥ kākāḥ sarve maṇḍalacāriṇaḥ | vāśante bhairavaṃ yatra tadapyāśu vinaśyati ||
(70 ,27.5) niśāyāṃ bahavaḥ śvāno roruvanti yadā tu te | hanyamānā na gacchanti tatra vāso na rocate ||
(70 ,27.6) prāsādadhvajaśālāsu prākāradvāratoraṇaiḥ | gardabhaṛśyabhāsānāṃ piṇḍān dṛṣṭvā puraṃ tyajet ||
(70 ,27.7) pūrvamukhaśca saṃdhyāyāmapraśāntasvaro mṛgaḥ | grāmīṇaghātaṃ śaṃsetsa grāmaṇyapraticārataḥ ||
(70 ,27.8) grāmadvāre ca vāśyeta vanādāgatya jambukaḥ | tīkṣṇasvareṇa mahatā diṣṭo grāmavadho hi saḥ ||
(70 ,27.9) yadyāti veśma kapotaḥ praviśeta viśeṣataḥ | rājaveśmanyulūko vā tattyājyamacirādgṛham ||
(70 ,27.10) akasmādveśmaprākāre prāsāde toraṇe dhvaje | patanti bahavo gṛdhrāḥ kākolūkā bakaiḥ saha ||
(70 ,28.1) athāpyeteṣu sthāneṣu madhu saṃjāyate yadā | nalinī caiva valmīkaḥ ṣaṇmāsairmriyate nṛpaḥ ||
(70 ,28.2) mṛgaḥ paśurvā pakṣī vā sūkaro vāpi vāśyate | yadi cotthāya śṛṇute sa manuṣyo vinaśyati ||
(70 ,28.3) kākamūṣikamārjārāñśvapataṃgān bhayāvahān | atīva bahuśo dṛṣṭvā durbhikṣeṇa kṣayaṃ vadet ||
(70 ,28.4) śvānaḥ śivābhirvāśyanto bhramantaḥ puramadhyataḥ | asthīni vā mṛtādīnāṃ janamārabhayaṃkarāḥ ||
(70 ,28.5) kāṣṭhaṃ vā yadi vā śṛṅgaṃ gṛhītvā śunakaḥ svayam | grāmamadhyena dhāvan syāttathaivāhurmahadbhayam ||
(70 ,29.1) purohitastu kurvīta kāpotīṃ śāntimuttamām | devāḥ kapota iti ca sūktaṃ tatra samādiśet ||
(70 ,29.2) āvāpe vyatiṣaṅge ca upariṣṭācca hūyate | kāmikāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādgururvā yena tuṣyati ||
(70 ,29.3) devatārcāḥ pranṛtyanti dīpyanti prajvalanti vā | udvijanti rudante vā prasvidyante hasanti vā ||
(70 ,29.4) uttiṣṭhanti niṣīdanti pradhāvanti pibanti vā | ejanti vikṣipante vā gātrapraharaṇadhvajān ||
(70 ,29.5) avāṅmukhā vadante vā sthānātsthānaṃ vrajanti vā | vapante vāgnimudakaṃ snehaṃ raktaṃ payo vasām ||
(70 ,30.1) jalpanti vā niśvasanti viceṣṭante rudanti vā | citraṃ saṃvīkṣyate yatra gātrairvāpi viceṣṭitaiḥ ||
(70 ,30.2) yatraite saṃpradṛśyante vikārāḥ sahasotthitāḥ | liṅgāyatanacaityeṣu tatra vāso na rocate ||
(70 ,30.3) rājño vā vyasanaṃ tatra sa vā deṣaḥ pralīyate | kṣucśastramaraṇairvāpi kiṃ cittatrābhiśasyate ||
(70 ,30.4) devatāyatanairvāpi prayātāḥ sumahotsavaiḥ | japahomaśca kalpantā sīdatāṃ ca same pathi ||
(70 ,30.5) same pātamakasmācca udāsīnāṃ tathaiva ca | dṛśyate tadvināśāya rājño janapadasya vā ||
(70 ,31.1) yatra prasthāni bhūtāni liṅgasyāyatanāni ca | tatra śāmyanti ghorāṇi japahomaśca kalpate ||
(70 ,31.2) prasādaḥ puṇḍarīkaṃ vā viśīryeta pateta vā | vātavajrahato vāpi puramukhye bhayaṃ bhavet ||
(70 ,31.3) pitāmahasya dharmeṣu yannimittaṃ dvijeṣu tat | aśvakrāntāgniyāneṣu yāni tāni purohite ||
(70 ,31.4) paśūnāṃ rudrajaṃ jñeyaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ lokapālajam | jñeyaṃ māṇḍalikānāṃ ca yattatskandaviśākhayoḥ ||
(70 ,31.5) laukikaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ jñeyaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ ca sarvadā | senāpatau gaṇeśānāṃ gāndharvaṃ saciveṣu ca ||
(70 ,31.6) devapreṣyaṃ nṛpapreṣye devastrīṇāṃ nṛpastriyām | kāśyapaṃ yantraprāsāde vāstoṣpatyaṃ pure sthitam ||
(70 ,31.7) kumārīṣu kumārījaṃ kumāreṣu kumārajam | yakṣarākṣasanāgaiśca yathoktaiḥ pānakarma ca ||
(70 ,32.1) athātaḥ sarvasarvasamucchayaḥ ekamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmo yathovāca bhagavān bṛhaspatiḥ ||
(70 ,32.2) yaddvādaśabhiradhyāyairvyākhyānaṃ parikīrtitam | tatsamāsena bhūyo'pi śṛṇu paryāyamāgatam ||
(70 ,32.3) parājito rāhunipīḍitamaṇḍalo vivarṇaḥ saṃdhyāvikṛto niḥprabho yadā | astamanaṃ yāti divākaraḥ tadāśu vidyātsubrahmajanakṣayam ||
(70 ,32.4) gṛhīto rāhuṇā sārdhamuttiṣṭhati divākaraḥ | tadā dharmaphalaṃ kṣīṇaṃ kalimāviśate prajā ||
(70 ,32.5) amukto rāhuṇā sārdhamuttiṣṭhati yadā śaśī | tadā dharmaphalaṃ kṣīṇaṃ kalimāviśate prajā ||
(70 ,32.6) amukto rāhuṇā sārdhamastaṃ gacchati candramāḥ | tadā tato bhayaṃ vidyānmṛtyumāviśate prajā ||
(70 ,32.7) avādyamānāḥ paṭahāḥ pravadanti muhurmuhuḥ | śastrāṇi vāhanāni ca jvalantyaśubhadāruṇam ||
(70 ,32.8) vātaprakopo rajasānuviddhā diśaśca saṃdhyā ca ghanānuyātā | drakṣanti saṃdhyā yadi pañcavarṇā bhayāni rājñaḥ prativedayanti ||
(70 ,32.9) anabhre stanate yatra nabhogulma gulmāyate | kṣipraṃ vidravate rāṣṭraṃ daśavarṣāṇi pañca ca ||
(70 ,32.10) anabhre patate vidyuddarśayedvāghanotthitām | anabhre vāpi nirghātaḥ patito rājamṛtyave ||
(70 ,32.11) yadyahni vāteṣu mahendrarekhā mahendracāpaḥ samudeti rātrau | tadā bhayaṃ pārthivamaṇḍalānāṃ vadanti śāstrārthavido dvijendrāḥ ||
(70 ,32.12) nikalkayukto niśi sendracāpo vivardhamānaḥ samudeti rātrau | viśīryamāṇā patate tatholkā tadā bhayaṃ pārthivamaṇḍalānām ||
(70 ,32.13) muñcanti nāgā rudhiraṃ karaiśca lomāni dīpyanti turaṃgamāṇām | dīpyanti khaḍgāni ca khecarāṇi cihnāni rājñaḥ prativedayanti ||
(70 ,32.14) girivarapatanaṃ svabhūmicālaḥ pratibhayatā ca tathaiva mānuṣāṇām | vikṛtajananamuktimugravācā mahati bhaye mṛgapakṣiṇo vadanti ||
(70 ,32.15) chattre gṛhe vāsarathe dhvaje ca dhūmaḥ samuttiṣṭhati yasya cāgniḥ | sa pārthivaḥ kṣīnamanuṣyakośaḥ prāpnoti nāśaṃ ca janakṣayaṃ ca ||
(70 ,32.16) mahormibhiḥ svairvitatairjaloghairnadyaḥ svakūlācca haranti vṛkṣān | yadi pratisrotavahāstadā syurvināśanā deśaparā nṛpasya ||
(70 ,32.17) yadā tu ghāte ca divākaraprabhāḥ svareṇubhirvāpi vidhūmasaṃbhramāḥ | na tasya vāsaṃ viṣaye vadanti āhurgaṇānāṃ ca vivṛddhināśaḥ ||
(70 ,32.18) hutāśanasyjvalanaṃ niredhaṃ tathā na caiva jvalate ca sedhmā | .......... bhayāni rājñaḥ prativedayanti ||
(70 ,32.19) śiloccayānāṃ ca śilānipātaḥ puradrumāṇāṃ ca viṣāṇapātaḥ | caityadrumāṇāṃ ca tathaiva pāto bhayāni rājñaḥ prativedayanti ||
(70 ,32.20) acālyavatsāḥ puragopureṣu bhramanti gāvaḥ kṛtaraudraśabdāḥ | mṛṇālabāddhāśca gajā bhavanti bhayāni rājñaḥ prativedayanti ||
(70 ,32.21) prāsādagopuramukhāśca patanti yatra indradhvajotthitavanaspativājināṃ ca | teṣāṃ vadanti pacanāni sukhāvahāni saumyādi saṃprabhayatā ca tathādiśanti ||
(70 ,32.22) ūrdhvaṃ vilokya nagaraṃ pratisaṃniviṣṭāḥ sūryodaye khalu rudanti śivātiraudram | gṛdhrāśca maṇḍalasamutpatitā bhramanti prāptaṃ bhayaṃ janapadasya samādiśanti ||
(70 ,32.23) daṇḍāśaniḥ patati yatra savisphuliṅgā bhūḥ kampate dinakarasya bhavetpraśāntiḥ | candhre ca yatra vikṛtaṃ ca bhavedaśāntaṃ māsātsamudbhavati tatra bhayo'tighoraḥ ||
(70 ,32.24) caityadrumāṇāṃ rudhiraprakopāḥ kabandhayānāni bhavanti yatra | saṃdhyāsu rakṣo'dhipaterjanānāṃ prabhūti rājño'tibhaye bhavanti ||
(70 ,32.25) vṛṣṭiryadā varṣati reṇuvarṣaistatopariṣṭāddharitālavarṣam | tataḥ paraṃ varṣati śailavarṣaṃ tadā balaṃ naśyati pārthivasya ||
(70 ,32.26) āraṇyo grāmavāsī mṛgaśakunigaṇo grāmavāsī vanānte gṛdhrāṇāṃ saṃnipāto narapatibhavane gopure vā pure vā | yatra syānmānuṣīṇāṃ kharakarabhamukhānekarūpā prasūtistatsthāne jīvitārthī sthitimati kurute naiva pātaiḥ praduṣṭe ||
(70 ,32.27) prayānti devāḥ sahasāyatasthā vanāni vā yatra patanti bhūmau | sthānāni mucyanti nadanti ke cittathā paraṃ śoṇitajagdhagātrāḥ ||
(70 ,32.28) utpātasaṃghairatyugraiḥ kṣātrahāniḥ prajāyate | lokānāṃ pīḍanaṃ caiva rogacaurāgnisaṃbhavam ||
(70 ,32.29) agnīnāṃ saṃpradoṣāḥ pratibhayajananā dīpyamānā diśaśca madhyāhne cāntarikṣe grahagaṇakhacitā gṛdhrasaṃghaiḥ prakīrṇāḥ | nirghātaiḥ pāṃsuvarṣaiḥ satatamalinatā bhūpracālaśca ghoro devānāṃ cāśrupāto nṛpatibhayakarā rāṣṭranāśāya caite ||
(70 ,32.30) śivādaye yatra divākarasya jvālāvimucyūrdhvamukhī praroditi | samāvṛtā vāyasagṛdhrasaṃghaistadā bhayaṃ vedavido vadanti ||
(70 ,32.31) rudanti nāgāstu vimuktahastā vimuktadantāsturagā rudanti | rudanti nāryaśca samāgame ca tadā bhayaṃ syācchrutiliṅgamūlam ||
(70 ,32.32) yadā tu vastrāṇi varadrumāṇāṃ prakāśavṛṣṭyā nipatanti mūrdhni | samīkṣya pātaṃ ca yathārthadṛṣṭaṃ bhayāya rāṣṭrasya nṛpasya vidyāt ||
(70 ,32.33) śakaṭādyāni yānāni yadāyuktāni saṃcalet | tadā janapade vidyānmahābhayamupasthitam ||
(70 ,32.34) yathaiva nityaṃ dṛśyante tathaiva samudāharet | na tasyātikramaḥ kaścidakṛte śāntikarmaṇi ||
(70 ,32.35) kṣayo janapadastrīṇāṃ vidyādgajapurohite | japaṃ homaṃ ca śāntiṃ ca utpāteṣu prayojayet ||
(70 ,32.36) viśeṣeṇāmṛtāṃ kuryādbṛhaspativaco yathā | homaṃ lakṣamitaṃ kuryājjapedvā vedasaṃhitām | dānāni tu hiraṇyāni śāntikarmaṇi yojayet ||

(pariśiṣṭa_71. auśanasādbhutāni)
(71,1.1) papracchośanasaṃ kāvyaṃ nāradaḥ paryavasthitaḥ | divyāṃścaivāntarikṣāṃśca utpātān pārthivāṃstathā ||
(71,1.2) ṛtūnāṃ ca viparyāse tathaiva mṛgapakṣiṇām | amānuṣāṇāṃ vyāhāre sthāvarāṇāṃ vyatikrame ||
(71,1.3) yonivyatikare caiva māṃsaśonitavarṣaṇe | anagnijvalane caiva tathā yānānusarpaṇe ||
(71,1.4) śastraprajvalane caiva caityaśuṣkavirohaṇe | liṅgāyatanacitrāṇāṃ rodane garjane tathā ||
(71,1.5) udapānataḍāgābān jvalane garjane'pi vā | matsyasarpadvijātīnāṃ rasānāṃ ca pravarṣaṇe ||
(71,2.1) āyudhānāṃ prajvalane garjane ca viśeṣataḥ | puṣpe phale ca vṛkṣāṇāmakāle ca virohaṇe ||
(71,2.2) prāsādādrivimānānāṃ prākārāṇāṃ ca kampane | gītavāditraśabdāśca yatra syuranimittataḥ ||
(71,2.3) ye cānye ke cidutpātā jāyante vikṛtātmakāḥ | teṣāṃ phalaṃ ca kālaṃ ca tattvenācakṣva bhārgava ||
(71,2.4) sa tasmai pṛcchate samyaṅnāradāyāśanāḥ kaviḥ | trividhānapyathotpātān vyākhyātumupacakrame ||
(71,2.5) yadā śīte bhavatyuṣṇamuṣṇe śītamatīva ca | navamāsātparaṃ vidyātteṣu deśeṣu vai bhayam ||
(71,3.1) yatrānṛtau prabaddhena tryahādūrdhvaṃ pravarṣati | tasmin deśe pradhānasya puruṣasya vadho bhavet ||
(71,3.2) kokilāśca mayūrāśca akāle madabhāginaḥ | saṃsargaṃ vāpi gaccheyurvidyājjānapadaṃ bhayam ||
(71,3.3) ruravaścaiva raudrāśca pṛṣatā hariṇāstathā | yeṣu deśeṣu dṛśyante tānaraṇyāya nirdiśet ||
(71,3.4) pradhānāścaiva vadhyante pakṣe saptadaśe tathā | tasmiñjanapade caiva mahadutpadyate bhayam ||
(71,3.5) gāvo'śvāḥ kuñjarāḥ śvānaḥ kharoṣṭrā vānaroragāḥ | nakulāḥ pakṣiṇo vyālāḥ sūkarā mahiṣā mṛgāḥ ||
(71,4.1) sattvānyetāni jalpanti yeṣu deśeṣu mānuṣam | teṣu deśeṣu rājā tu ṣaṣṭhe māsi vinaśyati ||
(71,4.2) utpātā vikṛtātmāno dṛśyante yatra tatra vai | deśe bhavati śīghraṃ hi ṣaṇmāsādbhayamuttamam ||
(71,4.3) āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ yadā yatra prasarpati | vipakṣāttatra tatsvāmī bhayaṃ prāpnoti dāruṇam ||
(71,4.4) dhānyakoṣṭhāyudhāgārāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ kūpaparvatāḥ | etāni yatra sarpanti vikṛtāni vadanti ca ||
(71,4.5) bahu vā jāyate tīvraṃ tasmin deśe bhayaṃ mahat | trīnmāsān parakāle tu śeṣe saumyātikaṃ phalam ||
(71,5.1) deśe vā yadi vā grāme yonivyatikaro bhavet | tatra saṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ mahadutpadyate bhayam ||
(71,5.2) gauraśvaṃ vaḍavā vāpi yasmin deśe prasūyate | abhyantareṇa tadvarṣādrājño maraṇamādiśet ||
(71,5.3) mānuṣī janayedyatra tṛṇādān vividhān paśūn | ṣaṇmāsotthaṃ bhayaṃ tīvraṃ tatra tūtpadyate mahat ||
(71,5.4) paracakrāgamaṃ caiva nirdiśediha śāstravit | saṃgrāmāścātra vipulā jāyante vikṛtātmakāḥ ||
(71,5.5) sarpaṃ vā pakṣiṇaṃ vāpi janayedyatra mānuṣī | pracalastasya deśasya ṣaṇmāsāttu paraṃ bhavet ||
(71,6.1) uṣṭraṃ vā yā prasūyeta vānaraṃ vāpi mānuṣī | anyadvā jaṅgamaṃ kiṃ citsthāvaraṃ vāpi kiṃ cana ||
(71,6.2) rogeṇa śastrapātena durbhikṣeṇa ca pīḍitaḥ | sa deśo vyathate śīghraṃ rājā tatra vinaśyati ||
(71,6.3) amānuṣī mānuṣaṃ vā mānuṣī vāpyamānuṣam | prasūyate tu jānīyātparacakrāgamaṃ dhruvam ||
(71,6.4) caturakṣaṃ dviśīrṣaṃ vā gātrairnyūnādhikaistathā | vyañjanaiścopasaṃpannaṃ mānuṣī yā prasūyate ||
(71,6.5) dvisaṃvatsaraparyantādrājā tatra vinaśyati | uṣṭro vṛṣo vāpyaśvo vā gajo vā yatra jāyate ||
(71,6.6) pakṣānmāsācca bhavati rājñastatra bhayaṃ mahat | paracakrasamutthaṃ vā sa deśo bhayamṛcchati ||
(71,7.1) yonivyatikaraṃ yatra kuryurevaṃvidhaṃ striyaḥ | gaurvā sūyettathānyāni tatra rājyaṃ vinaśyati ||
(71,7.2) vasanti yeṣu deśeṣu teṣu vidyānmahadbhayam | tasmādetāni sattvāni rājā kṣipraṃ pravāsayet ||
(71,7.3) aśvā kiśoraṃ janayecchṛṅgiṇaṃ yatra tatra tu | ādiśenmaraṇaṃ rājño varṣābhyantara eva hi ||
(71,7.4) māghe budhe ca mahiṣī śrāvaṇe vaḍavā divā | siṃhe gāvaḥ prasūyante svāmino mṛtyudāyakāḥ | iti śāstrasamuccayāt ||
(71,7.5) nārī kharavṛṣoṣṭrāśvāñśunaḥ sūkaragardabhān | rākṣasān vā piśācān vā yadāpyevaṃ prasūyate ||
(71,7.6) vyāpadyante'tra dhānyāni sasyāni ca dhanāni ca | caturvidhaṃ bhayaṃ ghoraṃ kṣipraṃ tatra pravartate ||
(71,8.1) vadhyante hi pradhānāstu sārdhamāsāṣṭame tathā | vyādhīṃśca teṣu deśeṣu trīṇi varṣāṇi nirdiśet ||
(71,8.2) anagnirjvalate yatra deśe tūrṇamanindhanaḥ | yo rājā tasya deśasya sadeśaḥ sa vinaśyati ||
(71,8.3) māṃsavarṣeṇa maghavā yatra deśe pravarṣati | asthīni rudhiraṃ majjāṃ vasāṃ caiteṣu vai dhruvam ||
(71,8.4) paracakrāgamaḥ śīghraṃ vijñeyastu mahadbhayam | āhavāścātra jāyante vipulā vikṛtātmakāḥ ||
(71,8.5) aṅgāravālukādhānyaṃ yatra devaḥ pravarṣati | kṣipraṃ tatra bhayaṃ ghoraṃ pravarteta caturvidham ||
(71,9.1) sarpānmatsyān pakṣiṇo vā yatra devaḥ pravarṣati | tatra sasyopaghātaḥ syādbhayaṃ cātipravartate ||
(71,9.2) surāsavaṃ tathā kṣaudraṃ sarpistailaṃ payo dadhi | yatra varṣati parjanyaḥ kṣudrogastatra jāyate ||
(71,9.3) ulkātārāśca dhiṣṇyeṣu yadāṅgārāṃśca varṣati | tadā vyādhibhayaṃ ghoraṃ teṣu deśeṣu nirdiśet ||
(71,9.4) pumānaśvo gajo vāpi yadā yatra pradīpyate | daśamāsātparaṃ tatra jānīyādrāṣṭrasaṃplavam ||
(71,9.5) nārācāḥ śaktayaḥ khaḍgāḥ pradīpyante yadā muhuḥ | tadā śastrabhayaṃ ghoraṃ teṣu deśeṣu nirdiśet ||
(71,10.1) caityavṛkṣāḥ prabhajyante visvaraṃ vinadanti ca | prahasanti prasarpanti gāyanti ca rudanti ca ||
(71,10.2) āgamaḥ paracakrasya teṣu cāpadyate tvaram | sacakrā vāpi naśyanti pradhānaścātra vadhyate ||
(71,10.3) yatra sraveccaityavṛkṣaḥ sahasā vividhān rasān | pṛthakpṛthaksamastān vā tatpravakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
(71,10.4) ghṛte madhuni dugdhe ca ghṛte dugdhe tathāmbhasi | kṣaudre madhuni taile vā vyādhayaḥ syuḥ sudāruṇāḥ ||
(71,10.5) surāsave mithobhedaḥ śoṇite śastrapātanam | taile pradhānā vadhyante bhakṣe kṣudbhayamādiśet ||
(71,11.1) anṛtau cetphalaṃ yatra puṣpaṃ vā sūyate drumaḥ | vidyāddvādaśame māsi rājñastatra viparyayam ||
(71,11.2) puṣpe puṣpaṃ bhavedyatra phale vā syāttathā phalam | parṇe parṇaṃ vijānīyāttatra jānapadaṃ bhayam ||
(71,11.3) śuklena vāsasā yatra caityavṛkṣaḥ samāvṛtaḥ | brāhmaṇānāṃ bhayaṃ ghoramāśu tīvraṃ vinirdiśet ||
(71,11.4) raktavastrāvṛtaiścānyaiḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ mahadbhayam | pītavastraistu vaiśyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ kṛṣṇavāsasaiḥ ||
(71,11.5) nīlaiḥ sasyopaghātaḥ syāccitraistu mṛgapakṣiṇām | vivarṇairvyādhayastīvrāḥ paraṃ syurdaśamāsataḥ ||
(71,12.1) daivatāni prasarpanti yatra rāṣṭre hasanti vā | udīkṣante'tha rodhāṃsi tatra vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(71,12.2) vihasanti nimīlanti gāyanti vikṛtāni ca | māṃsaśonitagandhāni yatra tatra mahadbhayam ||
(71,12.3) yatra citramudīkṣeta gāyate ceṣṭate muhuḥ | eteṣvaṣṭasu māseṣu rājño maraṇamādiśet ||
(71,12.4) citrāṇi yatra liṅgāni tathaivāyatanāni ca | vikāraṃ kuryuratyarthaṃ tatra vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(71,12.5) udapānaṃ taḍāgaṃ vā saraḥ parvata eva vā | samuddeśeṣu dīpyante vidyādbhayamupasthitam ||
(71,13.1) [prahaseyuḥ staneyurvā] śvā vā mārjāravadvadet | tasya deśasya rājā tu pīḍāmāpnoti dāruṇām ||
(71,13.2) śaṅkhavaiṇavatūryāṇāṃ dundubhīnāṃ ca nisvanaḥ | deśe yatra bhṛśaṃ tatra rājadaṇḍo nipātyate ||
(71,13.3) yasya rājño janapade nityodvignāḥ prajāḥ kṣayam | gacchanti na citrāttatra vināśamapi nirdiśet ||
(71,13.4) yasya rājño janapade nityameva gavāṃ kṣayaḥ | bhayaṃ tatra vijānīyādacirātsamupasthitam ||
(71,13.5) yasya rājño janapade nadī vahati kardamam | kāṣṭhaṃ tṛṇaṃ copalaṃ vā mṛtamatsyān grahāṃstathā ||
(71,14.1) madyaṃ kṣaudraṃ ca māṃsaṃ ca sarpistailaṃ payo dadhi | anyarājāgamabhayaṃ tatra deśe samādiśet ||
(71,14.2) yasya rājño janapade pratisroto nadī vahet | māsāṣṭakājjānapadaṃ bhayaṃ syācchastrapāṇinaḥ ||
(71,14.3) kūpo vā garjate yatra yadā vāpyavadīryate | lohitaṃ vātha pūyaṃ vā bhayaṃ tatra vinirdiśet ||
(71,14.4) āyudhāni pradhāvanti tīvraṃ pratyāharanti ca | tūṇīrātsahasā bāṇā udgiranti nadanti ca ||
(71,14.5) svabhāvataśca pūryante dhanūmṣi prajvalanti ca | saṃgrāmo dāruṇastatra deśe bhavati biścitaḥ ||
(71,15.1) akāle puṣpavantaśca phalavantaśca pādapāḥ | dṛśyante yasya rāṣṭreṣu tasya nāśo vibhāvyate ||
(71,15.2) vṛkṣā vallyaśca taruṇā yatra syuḥ phalapuṣpadāḥ | akāle cāpi dṛśyeyustatra vidyānmahadbhayam ||
(71,15.3) prāsādāni vimānāni prajvalanti tu yatra vai | dṛḍhāni ca viśīryante yasya sa mriyate'cirāt ||
(71,15.4) vadantyaraṇye tūryāṇi śrūyante vyomni nityaśaḥ | nivaseta tadā rājā samāgamya diśo daśa ||
(71,15.5) yasya veśmani śrūyante gītavāditranisvanāḥ | akasmānmriyate samyagdhanaṃ cāsya vilupyate ||
(71,15.6) śaṅkhavaiṇavavīṇāśca bherīmurajagomukhāḥ | vādyamānāḥ pradṛśyante deśe yatrāpyaghaṭṭitāḥ ||
(71,15.7) saṃbhṛtyaiva tato bhāramanyaṃ janapadaṃ vrajet | mṛgavāṃstu sa deśo hi vāyuścātropajāyate ||
(71,15.8) anāhatā dundubhayo vāditrāṇi vadanti ca | chidrāṇi ca gṛhe yasya sa śīghraṃ bhayamṛcchati ||
(71,15.9) devarājadhvajānāṃ ca patanaṃ bhaṅga eva vā | kravyādānāṃ praveśaṃ ca rājñaḥ pīḍākaraṃ bhavet ||
(71,15.10) vājivāraṇamukhyānāmakasmānmaraṇaṃ bhavet | itarakṣamāpatestatra vijñeyā satvarāgatiḥ ||
(71,16.1) aśvatthe puṣpite kṣatraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ cāpyudumbare | plakṣe vaiśyāśtu pīḍyante nyagrodhe dasyavastathā ||
(71,16.2) śvetamindrāyudhaṃ viprān raktaṃ kṣatriyanāśanam | vaiśyānāṃ pītakaṃ rātrau kṛṣṇaṃ śūdravināśanam ||
(71,16.3) nirghāte bhūmikampe ca caityaśuṣkavirohaṇe | deśapīḍāṃ vijānīyātpradhānaścātra vadhyate ||
(71,16.4) indrayaṣṭirbhajyate vā viśasto vā paśurvrajet | yadā tadā vijānīyādrājñaḥ pīḍāmupasthitām ||
(71,16.5) pitāmahe vāsudeve somadharmāryameṣvapi | nimittamaśubhaṃ yatra brāhmaṇānāṃ bhayāvaham ||
(71,17.1) bṛhaspatau vā śukre vā pāvake pākaśāsane | yāni rūpāṇi dṛśyante vidyāttāni purohite ||
(71,17.2) mahādeve kubere ca tathā skandaviśākhayoḥ | nimittaṃ tatpārthiveṣu vijñeyaṃ saṃpravartitam || [akasmāddṛśyate yattu nimittaṃ saṃprakīrtitam ||]
(71,17.3) devānāṃ pārthivānāṃ ca ratho yatra nimajjati | bhayaṃ tatra vijānīyātpārthivasyāśuradbhutam ||
(71,17.4) some ca vāsudeve ca varuṇe pākaśāsane | yadbhayaṃ dṛśyate taddhi jñeyaṃ bhāṇḍādhike kane ||
(71,17.5) vāte prājāpatau caiva viśvakarmaṇi caiva hi | pravartate yannimittaṃ tajjānapadikaṃ bhavet ||
(71,17.6) kumārīṣu kumārīṇāṃ kumārāṇāṃ kumārajam | tathā preṣyeṣu sarveṣu kalpayecchāstrataḥ phalam ||
(71,17.7) indrāṇi varuṇānī ca bhadrakālī mahābalā | vīramātā ca yadbrūyustadrājamahiṣībhayam ||
(71,17.8) ekaivāsāṃ tathā cānyā yāścānyā devatāḥ striyaḥ | kuryurnimittaṃ tatstrīṇāṃ pradhānānāṃ ca nirdiśet ||
(71,17.9) gandharveṣu nimittaṃ yattadanyeṣu pradṛśyate | senāpatīnāṃ bhayakṛtsacivānāṃ bhayāya ca ||
(71,17.10) rakṣapannagayakṣeṣu liṅgasyāyataneṣu ca | yathārūpaṃ yathākarma puruṣeṣu vyavasthitam ||
(71,18.1) dakṣiṇeṣu śarīreṣu devatānāṃ ca veśmasu | sarveṣvaṅgeṣu nārīṇāṃ tulyaṃ syādubhayorbhayam ||
(71,18.2) svaśarīre yathotpātā vihitā daivacintakaiḥ | tathaiva parisaṃkhyeyaṃ sarvatraiva śubhāśubham ||
(71,18.3) māṇibhadrādayo yakṣā gandharvāścitrasenayaḥ | tadbhayaṃ tu pradhānānāmamātyānāṃ vibhāvayet ||
(71,18.4) yeṣu deśeṣu dṛśyeta daivateṣu śubhāśubham | te ca deśā vinaśyanti rājā vātha vinaśyati ||
(71,18.5) brāhmaṇā yatra vadhyante grāme rāṣṭre'tha vā pure | rājadhānīṣu vā yatra tadabhāvasya lakṣaṇam ||
(71,19.1) yatrābalaṃ vadhyamānaṃ rājā naivābhirakṣati | tatra daivakṛto daṇḍo nipatatyāśu rājani ||
(71,19.2) chattradhvajapatākāsu devasthāne gṛheṣu ca | dvārāṭṭālakaharmyeṣu [kārayeddhomavācanam] ||
(71,19.3) yatra prakṛtibhūtāni liṅgāni vikṛtāni ca | devatāścāpi nadyaśca kṣarakṣāmamahīruhāḥ ||
(71,19.4) senā caiva na dṛśyeta hastyaśvaiśca padātibhiḥ | hīnāṅgā vikṛtāṅgā vā pralayaṃ tatra nirdiśet ||
(71,19.5) stambhavṛkṣā dhvajā yatra sraveyū rudhirāmbu ca | dhūmayeyurjvaleyurvā mantriṇāṃ tatra vai vadhaḥ ||
(71,19.6) jagatsvāmini jānīyādyadi ceddivi jāyate | āntarikṣaṃ tu deśe syādbhaumaṃ sasyopatiṣṭhati ||
(71,19.7) bhāryāyāṃ vāhane putre kośe senāpatau pure | purohite narendre vā patate daivamaṣṭadhā ||
(71,19.8) māhendrīmamṛtāṃ raudrīṃ vaiśvadevīmathāpi vā | utpāteṣu mahāśāntiṃ kārayedbahudakṣiṇām ||
(71,19.9) śāmyanti yena ghorāṇi yogakṣemaṃ ca jāyate | rājāno muditāstatra pālayanti vasuṃdharām ||

(pariśiṣṭa_72. mahādbhutāni)
(72,1.1) atha mahādbhutāni vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
(72,1.2) kṣipravipākīnyamoghāni ghorāṇi grahopahatamulkābhihataṃ grastaṃ nirastamupadhūpitaṃ vā yadā syājjanmanakṣatraṃ karmanakṣatramabhiṣecanīyajanapadanakṣatram
(72,1.3) eteṣu kṣiprameva mahāśāntimamṛtāṃ kārayedrājāṣṭame ca candramasaḥ sthāne <vajre> ca devopasṛṣṭe skambhe vā
(72,1.4) atha vā nānāvarṇe bahurūpe śṛṅgiṇi cāditye kīlavati c[ādbhutāny] ulkābhihate
(72,1.5) kacandha eva niśvasati hasati bhramati
(72,1.6) hāse bhāse nāde śabde vāsane ca vaiśvānare'prajvalite'ntarikṣe bhasmāsthyaśmāṅgārā vīthī cendradhanuṣi rātrau vīdhra eva tu ||
(72,2.1) candrārkau yasya rāṣṭre pariviṣyetātāṃ tān vipakṣān parolakasaṃsthāñjanapadāṃs
(72,2.2) tathaiva kākakapotakaṅkagṛdhrayakṣarākṣasapiśācaśvāpadeṣu naktaṃ vadatsvabhivadatsu gāyatsu rāyatsu vā cakradhvajaveśmāvasathaprāsādāgre
(72,2.3) vāpīkūpa udapāne codgirati nadati vidyotati vā
(72,2.4) rathayantravāraṇapravahaṇavāditrādiṣūlkādayo'ṅgārā dhūmo'rcirvā prādurbhāve
(72,2.5) liṅgaṃ viliṅge rājñaḥ
(72,2.6) kālolūkakṛkalāsaśyenanipatite rājachattre bhagne dhvaje cakrasya rājño daṇḍe rājñaśca dante
(72,2.7) hastinyāṃ ca mattāyāṃ grāme ca prasūtāyām
(72,2.8) rājarathaśca rājādhirūḍho bhagnākṣaḥ saptarātrādrājño hanti purohitamamātyaṃ senāpatiṃ jāyāṃ hastinaṃ mahiṣīṃ kumāraṃ rājānameva vā ṛdhnuyādya evaṃ veda
(72,2.9) dvādaśaṃ śataṃ gavāṃ dhenūnāṃ kaṃsavasanaṃ hiraṇyaṃ niṣko'śva etāśca dakṣiṇāḥ ||
(72,3.1) nānutpanneṣu daiveṣu rājñāṃ śāntirvidhīyate | asthāneṣu kṛtā śāntirnimittāyopapadyate | tasmātsthānaṃ samuddiśya kārayecchāntimātmanaḥ ||
(72,3.2) sarpasamitau vāyusaṃbhrame udakaprādurbhāvagamaneṣu
(72,3.3) dhanuḥsaṃdhyolkāpariveṣavidyuddaṇḍāśaniparighaparidhinirghāte
(72,3.4) rajovarṣamupalavarṣaṃ dadhimadhughṛtakṣīravarṣaṃ majjārudhira<varṣam> varṣati
(72,3.5) hīnagabhastī dve mārge vīthyau vittakṣaye somasya kṣaye'pūrṇapūraṇe kṣayasyāvabhāsāḥ sadyo'pararātrāddigdāhopadhūpanam
(72,3.6) grahavaiṣamyamārohaṇamākramaṇaṃ gandharvanagaraṃ mārutaprakopastithikaraṇamuhūrtanakṣatragrahādīnāṃ somaviyogaḥ
(72,3.7) pratisrotogāminyo nadyaḥ prāsādatoraṇadhvajeṣu vāyasasamavāyā vṛkaśakaṭārohaṇaṃ vṛṣadaṃśātimārjanamulūkapratigarjanaṃ śyenagṛdhrādīnāṃ dhvajābhilapanam
(72,3.8) vikṛtāśca mānuṣāmānuṣaprabhavāḥ strībālavṛddhapralāpāḥ pradīptendrayaṣṭipādabhagne'dravyeṣvekavṛkṣe dvichāye pratichāye parivṛktam
(72,3.9) ata ūrdhvam [chāyo]'kasmāccaityavṛkṣastambhapatane virohatsvaviroheṣvachinnaparṇaprapātācchuṣkaśākhino drumā dhūmarajodakaprādurbhāvagamaneṣu vanaspatiṣu
(72,3.10) bahuśastrabhaṅga indrakīlagopurāṭṭālakadhvajādīnāṃ bhaṅga ucitānāṃ vyucchedane'nucitānāṃ pravardhane dṛḍhabhaṅgesu
(72,3.11) śuṣkavirohe gṛhe valmīke śayanadeśe darhastambotpattau mitravirodhe'mitraprītau ca devatārcayo tathachedane
(72,3.12) yatra rājāprasādamukhaḥ paureṣu ca bhṛtyādiṣu bhavati bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ
(72,3.13) yadā tu pratipatsomo vikṛtyā vikṛto bhavet | anudbhinno vilūno vā rājño maraṇamādiśet ||
(72,3.14) āyudhākārarūpāṇi śvetavarṇākṛtīni ca | pañcavarṇāni cābhrāṇi tathā daṇḍanibhāni ca ||
(72,3.15) yadā candrārkayormadhye kṛṣṇaṃ bhavati maṇḍalam | sa śaṅkuriti vijñeyo grahaḥ paramadāruṇaḥ ||
(72,3.16) tatra rājño vadhaṃ vidyātsarvabhūtabhayāvaham | tatra kuryānmahāśāntimamṛtāṃ viśvabheṣajīmiti ||
(72,4.1) atha yasminneva janapade gobrāhmaṇasūtasāṃvatsaravaidyānāṃ parivrājakacāraṇavānaprasthabrahmacāriṇāṃ vāpi saṃkaraḥ pravartate tadadbhutaṃ vidyāt
(72,4.2) karmasaṃkaraṃ yajñasaṃkaraṃ vyavahārasaṃkaraṃ ca yatra ca dharmo'dharmeṣa pīḍyate tadadbhutaṃ vidyāt
(72,4.3) teṣāmajñātaprāyaścittaṃ yadajñātamanāmnātamiti madhye juhuyātpuruṣasūktaṃ ca teṣvakṛtaprāyaścitteṣu mahādbhutāni prādurbhavanti ||
(72,4.4) divyānītyācakṣate devagṛheṣvatha hasanti gāyanti rudanti krośanti prasvidyanti pradhūmāyanti prajvalanti prakampantyunmīlayanti nimīlayanti lihitaṃ sravanti parivartayanti vā
(72,4.5) teṣāṃ prādurbhāvagamaneṣvanyarājāgamanaṃ vā vidyādudagraṃ vā[ṅgegam] avṛṣṭiśastrabhayaṃ bubhukṣāmāraṃ jānapadamamātyānāṃ rājño vināśam
(72,4.6) teṣu sarveṣu bhṛgvaṅgirovidamityuktaṃ sa catuṣpatha īśānaṃ prapadyeta || oṃ prapadye bhūḥ prapadye bhuvaḥ prapadye svaḥ prapadye janatprapadya iti prapadyeta
(72,4.7) kapilānāmaṣṭaśatasya kṣīreṇa pāyasaṃ śrapayitvā kapilāsvalabhyamānāsu dogdhrīṇāṃ śatasya kṣīreṇa pāyasaṃ śarapayitvā prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya paristīrya barhī raudreṇa gaṇena śāntā juhuyāt || sarpirjuhuyātpāyasaṃ juhuyācchuklāḥ sumanasa upaharedbrāhmaṇān bhaktenopepasanti tā eva gā dadyādrājyaṃ vā parimitakālaṃ tasya parituṣṭaye gosahasraṃ kartre dadyādgrāmavaraṃ ca ||
(72,5.1) atha yatraitacchayane vātha vastre vā jāyate yaddhutāśanaḥ | etadatyadbhutaṃ nāma sarvakṣayakaraṃ nṛṇām ||
(72,5.2) atra brāhmīṃ mahāśāntiṃ kārayedbahudakṣiṇām | bahvannāṃ bahusaṃbhārāmanūcānasudakṣiṇām | rājyakāmo'rthakāmo vā pūjayettu bṛhaspatim ||
(72,5.3) sṛjanti devā divyādbhutāni prāgupasargātpratibodhanārtham | kāryāṇi vighnāni tathā janānāṃ karmākule varṇasamākule ca ||
(72,5.4) daivyopasṛṣṭena balena kāryaṃ kārya ca śāntiḥ praṇipatya devān | tatopasargādvighnātpramucyate divi cedaniṣṭaṃ na punaḥ sa kuryāt ||
(72,5.5) pṛthivyāmantarikṣe ca divi cāpyupalakṣayet | ceṣṭitaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ rutaṃ ca mṛgapakṣiṇām ||
(72,6.1) grāme kule vā yadi vāpi deśe rājanyamātyeṣu tathā dvijeṣu | bhāvaḥ paśūnāṃ vikṛto virūpastadadbhutaṃ tasya deśasya vidyāt ||
(72,6.2) amātyabhedo vividhaikaśīrṣa ekadviśīrṣe bhavati dvirājyam | apādahaste mriyate hyamātyo jāte kavandhe nṛpatirvinaśyet ||
(72,6.3) yadādhikāṅgo yadi vāṅgahīno bhavetpaśūnāṃ vikṛto virūpaḥ | strīṇāṃ tathaiva vikṛto virūpastadadbhutaṃ tasya deśasya vidyāt ||
(72,6.4) anāsyaṃ vāpyanoṣṭhaṃ vā jāyate cedvidūlakam | arūpamasarūpaṃ vā jāyate cedvidūlakam ||
(72,6.5) adharādīnnacakṣurvā jāyate cedvidūlakam | etadatyadbhutaṃ nāma rāṣṭre rājyakṣayamkaram ||
(72,6.6) tamadbhiḥ snātaṃ surabhiṃ sugandhiṃ gatāsumagnau juhuyādghṛtāktam | ganeṇa raudreṇa ghṛtaṃ ca hutvā tathā mahātmā śivamasya kuryāt ||
